*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  SELECT_T001W_PARA
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       プラント名称の取得
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_select_t001w_para.

* 変数初期化
  CLEAR v_name1.

* プラント/支店の検索
  SELECT SINGLE
         name1              "名称１
    FROM t001w
    INTO v_name1
   WHERE werks = p_werks.   "プラント

ENDFORM.                    " SELECT_T001W_PARA
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  MAIN_WORK_DIC
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       直納分メイン処理
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM main_work_dic .

* 各伝票タイプの入力値の制御
  PERFORM check_type_dic.

* 処理対象データの取得
  PERFORM select_data_dic.

* 直納分_直納一覧画面データの編集
  PERFORM edit_data_dic.

* 直納分_直納一覧画面の呼び出し
  PERFORM call_9200.

ENDFORM.                    " MAIN_WORK_HUB
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  SELECT_DATA_DIC
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       処理対象データの取得
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM select_data_dic .

* 購買依頼データの取得
  PERFORM select_eban.

* プラント名称の取得
  PERFORM select_t001w.

* 品目マスタの取得
  PERFORM select_mara_9200.

* 品目名称の取得
  PERFORM select_makt_9200.

ENDFORM.                    " SELECT_DATA_DIC
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  SELECT_EBAN
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       購買依頼データの取得
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM select_eban .

* 購買依頼伝票タイプがブランクの場合

  IF s_bst_b[] IS INITIAL AND v_del_created IS INITIAL.

*  「対象データがありません」
    MESSAGE s089(/ccej/otc_atp).
*   処理を終了する
    LEAVE LIST-PROCESSING.
  ENDIF.

* 初期化
  REFRESH it_eban.

* 購買依頼の検索
*****Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1 Conversion **begin of change by EJX9007359 2026.05.22  for ATC
*  SELECT banfn        "購買依頼番号
*         bnfpo        "購買依頼の明細番号
*         badat        "購買依頼日/要求日
*         txz01        "テキスト(短)
*         matnr        "品目コード
*         werks        "プラント
*         lgort        "保管場所
*         reswk        "在庫転送オーダーにおける供給(出庫)プラント
*         menge        "購買依頼数量
*         meins        "購買依頼数量単位
*         lfdat        "明細納入期日
*         flief        "固定仕入先
*         beswk        "調達元プラント
*    FROM eban
*    INTO TABLE it_eban
*   WHERE banfn IN s_banfn
*     AND bsart IN s_bst_b
*     AND matnr IN s_matnr
*     AND loekz =  space
*     AND frgkz <> c_frg_2
*     AND frgkz <> space
*     AND flief IN s_lifnr
*     AND ebeln =  space.
  SELECT PurchaseRequisition      AS banfn,   "購買依頼番号
         PurchaseRequisitionItem  AS bnfpo,   "購買依頼の明細番号
         CreationDate             AS badat,   "購買依頼日/要求日
         PurchaseRequisitionItemText AS txz01, "テキスト(短)
         Material                 AS matnr,   "品目コード
         Plant                    AS werks,   "プラント
         StorageLocation          AS lgort,   "保管場所
         SupplyingPlant           AS reswk,   "供給プラント
         RequestedQuantity        AS menge,   "購買依頼数量
         BaseUnit                 AS meins,   "購買依頼数量単位
         DeliveryDate             AS lfdat,   "明細納入期日
         FixedSupplier            AS flief   "固定仕入先
    INTO TABLE @it_eban
    FROM I_PurchaseRequisitionItemAPI01
   WHERE PurchaseRequisition     IN @s_banfn    "購買依頼番号
     AND PurchaseRequisitionType IN @s_bst_b    "購買依頼伝票タイプ
     AND Material                IN @s_matnr    "品目コード
     AND IsDeleted               =  @space      "購買伝票の削除フラグ
     AND ReleaseCode             <> @c_frg_2    "承認区分
     AND ReleaseCode             <> @space      "承認区分
     AND FixedSupplier           IN @s_lifnr    "固定仕入先
     AND PurchasingDocument      =  @space.     "購買発注番号
* beswk(調達元プラント) は I_PurchaseRequisitionItemAPI01 に未対応のため個別取得
  IF it_eban IS NOT INITIAL.
    SELECT banfn AS banfn,
           bnfpo AS bnfpo,
           beswk AS beswk
      INTO TABLE @DATA(lt_eban_ext)
      FROM eban
      FOR ALL ENTRIES IN @it_eban
     WHERE banfn = @it_eban-banfn
       AND bnfpo = @it_eban-bnfpo.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      SORT lt_eban_ext BY banfn bnfpo.
      LOOP AT it_eban ASSIGNING FIELD-SYMBOL(<ls_eban>).
        READ TABLE lt_eban_ext
          WITH KEY banfn = <ls_eban>-banfn
                   bnfpo = <ls_eban>-bnfpo
          BINARY SEARCH
          ASSIGNING FIELD-SYMBOL(<ls_eban_ext>).
        IF sy-subrc = 0.
          <ls_eban>-beswk = <ls_eban_ext>-beswk.
        ENDIF.
      ENDLOOP.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.
*****Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1 Conversion **End of change by EJX9007359 2026.05.22  for ATC

* データが取得出来なかった場合
  IF sy-subrc <> 0 AND v_del_created IS INITIAL.
*  「対象データがありません」
    MESSAGE s089(/ccej/otc_atp).
*   処理を終了する
    LEAVE LIST-PROCESSING.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    " SELECT_EBAN
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  SELECT_T001W
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       プラント名称の取得
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM select_t001w .

* ローカル定義
  DATA:
    l_it_taihi TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_eban.   "退避用テーブル


  CHECK it_eban IS NOT INITIAL.

* データ退避
  l_it_taihi = it_eban.

* ソート処理
  SORT l_it_taihi BY werks.   "プラント

* 重複データ削除
  DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM l_it_taihi
    COMPARING werks.    "プラント

* 初期化
  REFRESH it_t001w.
  IF l_it_taihi[] IS NOT INITIAL.
* プラント/支店
    SELECT werks   "プラント
           name1   "名称
      FROM t001w
      INTO TABLE it_t001w
      FOR ALL ENTRIES IN l_it_taihi
     WHERE werks = l_it_taihi-werks.    "プラント

* データが取得出来た場合
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
*   ソート処理
      SORT it_t001w BY werks.   "プラント
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    " SELECT_T001W
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  SELECT_MARA_9200
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       品目マスタの取得
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM select_mara_9200 .

* ローカル定義
  DATA:
    l_it_taihi TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_eban.   "退避用テーブル


  CHECK it_eban IS NOT INITIAL.


* データ退避
  l_it_taihi = it_eban.

* ソート処理
  SORT l_it_taihi BY matnr.   "品目コード

* 重複データ削除
  DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM l_it_taihi
    COMPARING matnr.    "品目コード

* 初期化
  REFRESH it_mara_9200.
  IF l_it_taihi[] IS NOT INITIAL.
* 一般商品データの検索
*****Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1 Conversion **begin of change by EJX9007359 2026.05.22  for ATC
*    SELECT matnr   "品目コード
*           meins   "基本数量単位
*      FROM mara
*      INTO TABLE it_mara_9200
*      FOR ALL ENTRIES IN l_it_taihi
*     WHERE matnr = l_it_taihi-matnr.
    SELECT Product  AS matnr,   "品目コード
           BaseUnit AS meins   "基本数量単位
      INTO TABLE @it_mara_9200
      FROM I_Product
      FOR ALL ENTRIES IN @l_it_taihi
     WHERE Product = @l_it_taihi-matnr.    "品目コード
*****Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1 Conversion **End of change by EJX9007359 2026.05.22  for ATC

* データが取得出来た場合
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
*   ソート処理
      SORT it_mara_9200 BY matnr.   "品目コード
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    " SELECT_MARA_9200
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  SELECT_MAKT_9200
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       品目名称の取得
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM select_makt_9200 .

* ローカル定義
  DATA:
    l_it_taihi TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_eban.   "退避用テーブル


  CHECK it_eban IS NOT INITIAL.


* データ退避
  l_it_taihi = it_eban.

* ソート処理
  SORT l_it_taihi BY matnr.    "品目コード

* 重複データ削除
  DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM l_it_taihi
    COMPARING matnr.    "品目コード

* 初期化
  REFRESH it_makt.
  IF l_it_taihi[] IS NOT INITIAL.
* 品目テキストの検索
    SELECT matnr   "品目コード
           maktx   "品目テキスト
      FROM makt
      INTO TABLE it_makt
      FOR ALL ENTRIES IN l_it_taihi
     WHERE matnr = l_it_taihi-matnr   "品目コード
       AND spras = sy-langu.          "言語キー

* データが取得出来た場合
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
*   ソート処理
      SORT it_makt BY matnr.   "品目コード
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    " SELECT_MAKT_9200
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  EDIT_DATA_DIC
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       直納分_直納一覧画面データの編集
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM   edit_data_dic .

* ローカル定義
  DATA:
    l_it_text  TYPE ty_tbl_text,   "テキスト用内部テーブル
    l_wa_text  TYPE bapiebantx,    "テキスト用構造
    l_wa_eban  TYPE ty_eban,       "購買依頼データ
    l_wa_t001w TYPE ty_t001w,      "プラント名称
    l_wa_mara  TYPE ty_mara_9200,  "品目マスタ
    l_wa_makt  TYPE ty_makt,       "品目テキスト
    l_wa_9200  TYPE ty_9200.       "直納分_直納一覧画面

* 初期化
  REFRESH it_9200.

* ソート処理
  SORT it_eban BY banfn.   "購買依頼番号

  LOOP AT it_eban INTO l_wa_eban.

*   初期化
    CLEAR l_wa_9200.

*   購買依頼番号が変わった場合
    AT NEW banfn.   "購買依頼番号

*     初期化
      REFRESH l_it_text.   "テキスト用内部テーブル

*     明細テキスト取得
      PERFORM get_detail
        USING    l_wa_eban-banfn   "購買依頼番号
        CHANGING l_it_text.        "テキスト用内部テーブル

    ENDAT.

*   テキストの編集
    LOOP AT l_it_text INTO l_wa_text
      WHERE preq_no   = l_wa_eban-banfn   "購買依頼番号
        AND preq_item = l_wa_eban-bnfpo   "購買依頼明細番号
        AND text_id   = c_tid.           "テキストID
*        AND TEXT_FORM = c_TFRM.         "タグ列

*     複数レコードある場合は半角スペースで結合する
      IF ( sy-tabix = 1 ).
        l_wa_9200-text = l_wa_text-text_line.   "画面表示テキスト
      ELSE.
        CONCATENATE l_wa_9200-text               "画面表示テキスト
                    l_wa_text-text_line          "明細行テキスト
               INTO l_wa_9200-text               "画面表示テキスト
               SEPARATED BY space.
      ENDIF.
    ENDLOOP.

*   プラント名称の読み込み
    READ TABLE it_t001w INTO l_wa_t001w BINARY SEARCH
      WITH KEY werks = l_wa_eban-werks.   "プラント

*   品目マスタの読み込み
    READ TABLE it_mara_9200 INTO l_wa_mara BINARY SEARCH
      WITH KEY matnr = l_wa_eban-matnr.   "品目コード

*   品目テキストの読み込み
    READ TABLE it_makt INTO l_wa_makt BINARY SEARCH
      WITH KEY matnr = l_wa_eban-matnr.   "品目コード

*   画面表示項目の編集(明細テキストは上記で編集済)

*    PERFORM EXIT_ALPHA_OUTPUT
*      USING    L_wa_EBAN-BANFN               "購買依頼
*      CHANGING L_wa_9200-BANFN.              "購買依頼
    l_wa_9200-banfn     = l_wa_eban-banfn.

    l_wa_9200-bnfpo     = l_wa_eban-bnfpo.   "明細番号
    l_wa_9200-kunnr     = l_wa_eban-werks.   "出荷先
    l_wa_9200-name1     = l_wa_t001w-name1.  "出荷先名称

*    PERFORM EXIT_MATN1_OUTPUT
*      USING    L_wa_EBAN-MATNR               "品目コード
*      CHANGING L_wa_9200-MATNR.              "品目コード

    l_wa_9200-matnr     = l_wa_eban-matnr.

    l_wa_9200-maktx     = l_wa_makt-maktx.   "品目名称
    l_wa_9200-edacode   = l_wa_eban-edacode. "枝番
    l_wa_9200-syomi     = l_wa_eban-syomi.   "賞味期限
    l_wa_9200-meins_eb  = l_wa_eban-meins.   "購買依頼数量単位
    l_wa_9200-meins     = l_wa_mara-meins.   "出荷単位


    l_wa_9200-werks     = l_wa_eban-beswk.   "出荷プラント
    l_wa_9200-menge     = l_wa_eban-menge.   "購買依頼数量

*    L_wa_9200-MEINS_EB  = L_wa_EBAN-MEINS.   "購買依頼数量単位

    l_wa_9200-lfdat     = l_wa_eban-lfdat.   "納入期日
    l_wa_9200-badat     = l_wa_eban-badat.   "購買依頼日/要求日
    l_wa_9200-lgort     = l_wa_eban-lgort.   "保管場所
    l_wa_9200-matnr_s   = l_wa_eban-matnr.   "品目コード
    APPEND l_wa_9200 TO it_9200.

  ENDLOOP.


  FREE :
    it_eban.


ENDFORM.                    " EDIT_DATA_DIC
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_DETAIL
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       明細テキスト取得
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->I_BANFN     購買依頼番号
*  <--O_IT_TEXT   テキスト用内部テーブル
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_detail USING    i_banfn   TYPE eban-banfn
                CHANGING o_it_text TYPE ty_tbl_text.

* ローカル定義
  DATA:
    l_it_items TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF bapieban.   "ダミーテーブル

* 購買依頼詳細の照会
" Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_A Conversion **BEGIN OF CHANGE BY EJX9007359 03.06.2026  FOR ATC
  CALL FUNCTION 'BAPI_REQUISITION_GETDETAIL'"#EC CI_USAGE_OK[2438131]
" Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_A Conversion * *END OF CHANGE BY EJX9007359 03.06.2026 FOR ATC
    EXPORTING
      number            = i_banfn      "購買依頼番号
      item_texts        = 'X'
    TABLES
      requisition_items = l_it_items   "明細データ
      requisition_text  = o_it_text.    "テキスト

ENDFORM.                    " GET_DETAIL

ENDFORM.                    " PARA_WORK_HUB
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  LOCK_TRAN
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       トランザクションロック
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM lock_tran .

* ローカル定義
  DATA:
    l_v_param1 TYPE funfd."NA zmm70-param1.
  DATA:
    l_it_9100   TYPE SORTED  TABLE OF ty_9100
                     WITH NON-UNIQUE KEY matnr,
    l_wa_9100   TYPE ty_9100,
    l_v_param2 TYPE funfd."NA zmm70-param2.

*  Begin of Change by S197005 MOD-021
  DATA : l_v_username TYPE xubname ,
         l_v_bname TYPE xubname ,
         l_v_lockuser TYPE char100,
         l_v_name_textc TYPE char80.
*  End of Change by S197005 MOD-021

  l_it_9100[] = it_9100[].
  DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM l_it_9100 COMPARING matnr.

  LOOP AT s_werks.

* 型合わせ
    l_v_param1 = s_werks-low."p_werks.   "プラント

*    LOOP AT l_it_9100 INTO l_wa_9100.
    LOOP AT i_jscale INTO wa_jscale.

*     BOC for MOD-033
      "Remove Lock on Materials Starting with A
      CHECK wa_jscale-matnr+0(1) NE c_a.
*     EOC for MOD-033

*     BOC for MOD-036
      "Check Deletion Flag
      CHECK wa_jscale-lvorm NE c_on.
*     EOC for MOD-036

      l_v_param2 = wa_jscale-matnr."l_wa_9100-matnr.

* トランザクションロック(単一ユーザ)
      CALL FUNCTION '/CCEJ/OTC_TCODE_LOCK'
        EXPORTING
          im_tcode              = sy-tcode      "トランザクションコード

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  SELECT_VBAK
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       受注伝票ヘッダの取得
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM select_vbak .


* ローカル変数定義
  DATA:
    l_it_vbak_0  TYPE TABLE OF ty_vbak_0,
    l_wa_vbak_0  TYPE ty_vbak_0,
    l_it_vbpa_0  TYPE TABLE OF ty_vbpa_0,
    l_wa_vbpa_0  TYPE ty_vbpa_0,
    l_wa_vbak    TYPE ty_vbak.

  DATA:
    l_it_vbuk    TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_vbuk.


* 初期化
  REFRESH it_vbak.

  SELECT vbeln                             "販売伝票
         erdat                             "レコード登録日
         auart                             "伝票タイプ
         kunnr
         vkorg vsbed audat                           "受注先
    INTO TABLE l_it_vbak_0
    FROM vbak
   WHERE vbeln IN s_vbeln                  "販売伝票番号
     AND erdat IN s_erdat                  "レコード登録日
     AND auart IN s_auart.                 "販売伝票タイプ

  IF l_it_vbak_0[] IS NOT INITIAL.
    SORT l_it_vbak_0 BY vbeln.
    DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM l_it_vbak_0 COMPARING vbeln.

    SELECT vbeln
      FROM vbuk
      INTO TABLE l_it_vbuk
       FOR ALL ENTRIES IN l_it_vbak_0
     WHERE   vbeln = l_it_vbak_0-vbeln
       AND  "gbstk = c_gbstk_a.
      ( gbstk = space OR           "全伝票明細の拒否ステータス
             gbstk = c_gbstk_a OR
             gbstk = c_gbstk_b ).

    IF l_it_vbuk[] IS NOT INITIAL.
      SORT l_it_vbuk BY vbeln.
      DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM l_it_vbuk COMPARING vbeln.

      SELECT vbeln
             kunnr
        INTO TABLE l_it_vbpa_0
        FROM vbpa
         FOR ALL ENTRIES IN l_it_vbuk
       WHERE vbeln = l_it_vbuk-vbeln
         AND posnr = c_posnr_hdr
         AND parvw = c_parvw_we
         AND kunnr IN s_kunnr.
    ENDIF.



    SORT l_it_vbpa_0 BY vbeln.
    SELECT vkorg vkokl FROM tvko INTO TABLE i_tvko
      FOR ALL ENTRIES IN l_it_vbak_0
      WHERE vkorg = l_it_vbak_0-vkorg.

    SELECT mandt auart vsbed lead_time
      FROM /scl/otc_leadt
      INTO TABLE i_leadt
      FOR ALL ENTRIES IN l_it_vbak_0
     WHERE auart = l_it_vbak_0-auart
       AND vsbed = l_it_vbak_0-vsbed.


  ENDIF.

*
  LOOP AT l_it_vbak_0 INTO l_wa_vbak_0.

*
    CLEAR:
      l_wa_vbak,
      l_wa_vbpa_0.

*
    READ TABLE l_it_vbpa_0 INTO l_wa_vbpa_0
         WITH KEY vbeln = l_wa_vbak_0-vbeln
                  BINARY SEARCH.

*   取得できる場合
    IF sy-subrc = 0.

      l_wa_vbak-vbeln   = l_wa_vbak_0-vbeln.        "販売伝票
      l_wa_vbak-erdat   = l_wa_vbak_0-erdat.        "レコード登録日
      l_wa_vbak-auart   = l_wa_vbak_0-auart.        "受注伝票タイプ
      l_wa_vbak-kunnr   = l_wa_vbak_0-kunnr.        "受注先
      l_wa_vbak-kunnr2  = l_wa_vbpa_0-kunnr.        "取引先の得意先コード
      l_wa_vbak-vkorg   = l_wa_vbak_0-vkorg.
      l_wa_vbak-vsbed   = l_wa_vbak_0-vsbed.
      l_wa_vbak-audat   = l_wa_vbak_0-audat.

      APPEND l_wa_vbak TO it_vbak.

    ENDIF.

  ENDLOOP.

* ソート処理
  SORT it_vbak BY vbeln.   "販売伝票



ENDFORM.                    " SELECT_VBAK

* 販売伝票: 明細データ、販売伝票: 納入日程行データの検索
  SELECT sditem~SalesDocument              AS vbeln  , "販売伝票
         sditem~SalesDocumentItem          AS posnr  , "販売伝票明細
         sditem~Plant                      AS werks  , "プラント
         sditem~StorageLocation            AS lgort  , "保管場所
         sditem~Material                   AS matnr  , "品目コード
         sditem~TargetToBaseQuantityNmrtr  AS umziz  , "販売単位から基本単位への換算係数(分子)
         sditem~TargetToBaseQuantityDnmntr AS umzin  ,"販売単位から基本単位への換算係数(分母),
         sditem~BaseUnit                   AS meins  , "基本数量単位
         sditem~OrderQuantity              AS kwmeng , "累積受注数量(販売単位)
         sditem~OrderQuantityUnit          AS vrkme  , "販売単位
*        sditem~/scl/atwrt                 AS atwrt   "特性値 ※S/4標準CDS未対応(拡張フィールド)
*        sditem~zzssyomi                              "希望賞味期限 ※NA
         sdsl~DeliveryDate                AS edatu  , "納入日程日付
         sdsl~GoodsIssueDate              AS wadat  , "出庫日付
         sdsl~ConfdOrderQtyByMatlAvailCheck AS bmeng
    INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE @it_vbap
    FROM I_SalesDocumentItem AS sditem
    INNER JOIN I_SalesDocumentScheduleLine AS sdsl
      ON  sditem~SalesDocument     = sdsl~SalesDocument      "販売伝票番号
      AND sditem~SalesDocumentItem = sdsl~SalesDocumentItem  "販売伝票明細番号
*BOC MOD-004 (VBUP結合不要: SdProcessStatusはI_SalesDocumentItemに含まれる)
    FOR ALL ENTRIES IN @it_vbak
   WHERE sditem~SalesDocument  =  @it_vbak-vbeln   "販売伝票
     AND sditem~Plant          IN @s_werks          "プラント
     AND sditem~Material       IN @s_matnr          "品目コード
     AND sdsl~DeliveryDate     IN @s_edatu          "納入日程日付
     AND sdsl~GoodsIssueDate   IN @s_wadat          "出庫日付(出荷日)
     AND sdsl~ConfdOrderQtyByMatlAvailCheck <> 0
*BOC MOD-004
     AND ( sditem~SdProcessStatus =  @space
        OR sditem~SdProcessStatus =  @c_gbstk_a
        OR sditem~SdProcessStatus =  @c_gbstk_b ).
*EOC MOD-004

* 拡張フィールド補完: /scl/atwrt を VBAP から直接取得
  IF it_vbap IS NOT INITIAL.

    SELECT vbeln,
           posnr,
           /scl/atwrt
      INTO TABLE @DATA(lt_vbap_ext)
      FROM vbap
      FOR ALL ENTRIES IN @it_vbap
      WHERE vbeln = @it_vbap-vbeln
        AND posnr = @it_vbap-posnr.

    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      SORT lt_vbap_ext BY vbeln posnr.

      LOOP AT it_vbap ASSIGNING FIELD-SYMBOL(<ls_vbap>).
        READ TABLE lt_vbap_ext
          WITH KEY vbeln = <ls_vbap>-vbeln
                   posnr = <ls_vbap>-posnr
          BINARY SEARCH
          ASSIGNING FIELD-SYMBOL(<ls_ext>).
        IF sy-subrc = 0.
          <ls_vbap>-/scl/atwrt = <ls_ext>-/scl/atwrt.
        ENDIF.
      ENDLOOP.
    ENDIF.

  ENDIF.

* 販売伝票フローの検索
*****Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1 Conversion **begin of change by EJX9007359 2026.05.22  for ATC
*  SELECT vbelv   "先行販売管理伝票
*         posnv   "販売管理伝票前明細
*    FROM vbfa
*    INTO TABLE it_vbfa

*   WHERE vbelv   = it_vbap-vbeln

*     AND vbtyp_n = c_vbtyp_j.
  SELECT PrecedingDocument     AS vbelv,   "先行販売管理伝票
         PrecedingDocumentItem AS posnv   "販売管理伝票前明細
    INTO TABLE @it_vbfa
    FROM I_SDDocumentMultiLevelProcFlow

   WHERE PrecedingDocument     = @it_vbap-vbeln   "先行販売管理伝票
     AND PrecedingDocumentItem = @it_vbap-posnr   "販売管理伝票前明細

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  DELETE_VBAP
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       対象データの絞り込み
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM delete_vbap .

* ローカル定義
  DATA:

    l_wa_vbfa TYPE ty_vbfa,   "出荷伝票
    l_wa_vbkd TYPE ty_vbkd,
    l_wa_vbap TYPE ty_vbap.

  LOOP AT it_vbfa INTO l_wa_vbfa.

    DELETE it_vbap WHERE vbeln = l_wa_vbfa-vbelv   "販売伝票番号
                     AND posnr = l_wa_vbfa-posnv.  "販売伝票の明細番号


  ENDLOOP.

  IF it_vbap[] IS NOT INITIAL.
    SELECT vbeln posnr bstdk_e
      FROM vbkd INTO TABLE i_vbkd
      FOR ALL ENTRIES IN it_vbap
      WHERE vbeln = it_vbap-vbeln
        AND posnr = it_vbap-posnr.
    IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
      SORT i_vbkd BY vbeln posnr.
      LOOP AT it_vbap INTO l_wa_vbap.
        READ TABLE i_vbkd INTO l_wa_vbkd WITH KEY vbeln = l_wa_vbap-vbeln posnr = l_wa_vbap-posnr BINARY SEARCH.
        IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL AND l_wa_vbkd-bbe IS NOT INITIAL.
          l_wa_vbap-syomi = l_wa_vbkd-bbe.
        ENDIF.
        MODIFY it_vbap FROM l_wa_vbap.
        CLEAR:l_wa_vbap,l_wa_vbkd.
      ENDLOOP.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.



ENDFORM.                    " DELETE_VBAP

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  SELECT_EKKO
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       購買伝票ヘッダの取得
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM select_ekko .


  DATA: l_v_ekorg  TYPE t001w-ekorg,
        l_v_cond   TYPE char03.
* 購買組織の取得
  SELECT SINGLE ekorg
    INTO l_v_ekorg
    FROM t001w
   WHERE werks = p_werks.

* 上記で取得した購買組織の上2桁＋"*"
  CONCATENATE l_v_ekorg+0(2)
              c_percent
         INTO l_v_cond.


* 初期化
  REFRESH it_ekko.

* 購買伝票ヘッダの検索
*****Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1 Conversion **begin of change by EJX9007359 2026.05.22  for ATC
*  SELECT ebeln   "購買伝票番号
*         aedat   "レコード登録日
*         bsart   "購買伝票タイプ
*         reswk   "在庫転送オーダーにおける供給(出庫)プラント
*    FROM ekko
*    INTO TABLE it_ekko
*   WHERE reswk IN s_werks
*     AND bstyp =    c_bstyp_f
*     AND ebeln IN   s_ebeln
*     AND bsart IN   s_bst_k
*     AND bedat IN   s_aedat.
  SELECT PurchaseOrder     AS ebeln,   "購買伝票番号
         CreationDate      AS aedat,   "レコード登録日
         PurchaseOrderType AS bsart,   "購買伝票タイプ
         SupplyingPlant    AS reswk   "供給(出庫)プラント
    INTO TABLE @it_ekko
    FROM I_PurchaseOrderAPI01
   WHERE SupplyingPlant    IN @s_werks    "供給プラント
     AND PurchaseOrder     IN @s_ebeln    "購買伝票番号
     AND PurchaseOrderType IN @s_bst_k    "購買伝票タイプ
     AND PurchaseOrderDate IN @s_aedat.   "購買伝票日付
* bstyp(購買伝票カテゴリ) は I_PurchaseOrderAPI01 が PO 専用のため条件不要
*****Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1 Conversion **End of change by EJX9007359 2026.05.22  for ATC


* データが取得出来た場合
  IF sy-subrc = 0.
*   ソート処理
    SORT it_ekko BY ebeln.  "購買伝票番号
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    " SELECT_EKKO
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  SELECT_EKPO
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       購買伝票明細の取得
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM select_ekpo .

* ヘッダデータがある場合に下記の処理を行う
  CHECK it_ekko IS NOT INITIAL.

* 初期化
  REFRESH it_ekpo.

* 購買伝票明細、分納契約納入日程行、購買伝票明細の在庫転送用出荷データ
* の検索
  SELECT ekpo~ebeln        "購買伝票番号
         ekpo~ebelp        "購買伝票の明細番号
         ekpo~matnr        "品目コード
         ekpo~werks        "プラント
         ekpo~lgort        "保管場所
         ekpo~menge        "購買発注量
         ekpo~meins        "発注単位
         ekpo~umrez        "発注単位から基本単位への換算式の分子
         ekpo~umren        "発注単位から基本単位への換算式の分母
         ekpo~lmein        "基本数量単位
         ekpo~reslo        "出庫保管場所: 在庫転送オーダー
         ekpo~/scl/atwrt   "特性値
*         ekpo~zzssyomi     "希望賞味期限"NA
         eket~eindt        "納入期日
         eket~wadat        "出庫日付
         ekpv~kunnr                                         "得意先コード1

         ekpo~afnam                 "購買依頼者名/要求者名
*         EKPO~BEDNR AS ZZBEDNR      "購買依頼追跡番号
         ekpo~banfn                 "購買依頼番号
         ekpo~bnfpo                 "購買依頼の明細番号
         ekpo~insmk                 "在庫タイプ
         ekpo~/scl/charg            "ロット番号
         ekpo~/scl/prueflos         "品質検査ロット番号
         ekpo~/ccej/bbd
         eket~mbdat                 "品目供給/利用可能日
         eket~tddat                 "輸送計画日付
         eket~lddat                 "積載日付
         eket~eldat                 "入庫終了日付

    INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE it_ekpo
    FROM ekpo
    INNER JOIN eket
    ON  ekpo~ebeln = eket~ebeln   "購買伝票番号
    AND ekpo~ebelp = eket~ebelp   "購買伝票の明細番号
    INNER JOIN ekpv
    ON  ekpo~ebeln = ekpv~ebeln   "購買伝票番号
    AND ekpo~ebelp = ekpv~ebelp   "購買伝票の明細番号
    FOR ALL ENTRIES IN it_ekko
   WHERE ekpo~ebeln =  it_ekko-ebeln   "購買伝票番号
     AND ekpo~elikz <> c_on          "納入完了"区分
     AND ekpo~loekz <> c_loelz       "購買伝票の削除フラグ
     AND ekpo~matnr IN s_matnr         "品目コード
     AND ekpv~kunnr IN s_kunnr                              "得意先コード1

*     AND EKET~EINDT IN S_EDATU.        "納入期日
     AND eket~eindt IN s_edatu         "納入期日
     AND eket~wadat IN s_wadat.        "出庫日付(出荷予定日)

** データが取得出来た場合
*  IF SY-SUBRC = 0.
**   ソート処理
*    SORT IT_EKPO BY EBELN   "購買伝票番号
*                    EBELP.  "購買伝票の明細番号
*  ENDIF.


ENDFORM.                    " SELECT_EKPO
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  DELETE_EKKO
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       対象データの絞り込み
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM delete_ekko .

* ローカル定義
  DATA:

    l_wa_ekbe TYPE ty_ekbe.   "購買伝票履歴

  LOOP AT it_ekbe INTO l_wa_ekbe.


    DELETE it_ekpo WHERE ebeln = l_wa_ekbe-ebeln   "購買伝票番号
                     AND ebelp = l_wa_ekbe-ebelp.  "購買伝票の明細番号


  ENDLOOP.

ENDFORM.                    " DELETE_EKKO
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  EDIT_9100
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       引当対象データの編集
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM edit_9100 .

* ローカル定義
  DATA:
    l_v_menge TYPE eban-menge,   "変換数量
    l_wa_vbak  TYPE ty_vbak,      "受注伝票ヘッダ

    l_wa_ekpo  TYPE ty_ekpo,      "購買伝票明細

    l_wa_leadt TYPE /scl/otc_leadt,

  DATA :
    l_v_bednr          TYPE ekpo-bednr.    " 購買依頼追跡No.

*BOC for MOD-033
  FIELD-SYMBOLS:<l_fs_jscale> TYPE ty_jscalecode.
*EOC for MOD-033

* 初期化

  CLEAR   v_wadat.

  REFRESH it_9100.

* 受注データの値設定
  LOOP AT it_vbap INTO l_wa_vbap.

*   伝票番号が変わるたびにヘッダ情報取得
    AT NEW vbeln.    "伝票番号
      READ TABLE it_vbak INTO l_wa_vbak BINARY SEARCH
        WITH KEY vbeln = l_wa_vbap-vbeln.     "伝票番号
    ENDAT.

    l_wa_9100-kbn       = text-001.             "処理区分（テキスト）
    l_wa_9100-vbeln     = l_wa_vbap-vbeln.      "伝票番号
    l_wa_9100-posnr     = l_wa_vbap-posnr.      "明細
    l_wa_9100-matnr     = l_wa_vbap-matnr.      "品目コード
    " BOC | MOD-016| S180184 | Gap 3389 | Subcode changes | 29/07/2016
*      l_wa_9100-edacode   = l_wa_vbap-atwrt.      "枝番
    l_wa_9100-edacode   = l_wa_vbap-/scl/atwrt.      "枝番
    " EOC |MOD-016 | S180184 | Gap 3389 | Subcode changes | 29/07/2016

    l_wa_9100-syomi     = l_wa_vbap-syomi.      "賞味期限

*   基本数量単位変換ロジック
    l_v_menge = l_wa_vbap-kwmeng.
    PERFORM conv_meins
         USING l_wa_vbap-matnr     "品目コード
               l_wa_vbap-vrkme     "基本数量単位（変更前）
               l_wa_vbap-meins     "基本数量単位（変更後）
               l_v_menge          "引当数量（CS）
      CHANGING l_v_menge.         "引当数量（CS）
    l_wa_vbap-kwmeng = l_v_menge.

    l_wa_9100-menge_cs  = l_wa_vbap-kwmeng.     "引当数量（CS）
    WRITE l_wa_vbap-kwmeng UNIT l_wa_vbap-meins "引当数量（一覧表示用）
      TO l_wa_9100-menge_out
      RIGHT-JUSTIFIED.

*    PERFORM EXIT_ALPHA_OUTPUT
*      USING    L_wa_VBAK-KUNNR2                 "出荷先コード
*      CHANGING L_WA_9100-KUNNR2.                "出荷先コード
    l_wa_9100-kunnr2 = l_wa_vbak-kunnr2.        "出荷先コード

    l_wa_9100-werks     = l_wa_vbap-werks.      "プラント
    l_wa_9100-lgort     = l_wa_vbap-lgort.      "出荷保管場所

    l_wa_9100-lgort_org = l_wa_vbap-lgort.      "伝票の保管場所
    l_wa_9100-auart     = l_wa_vbak-auart.      "伝票タイプ

    l_wa_9100-edatu     = l_wa_vbap-edatu.      "納入日付
    l_wa_9100-kunnr     = l_wa_vbak-kunnr.      "受注先
    l_wa_9100-meins     = l_wa_vbap-meins.      "基本数量単位
    l_wa_9100-vrkme     = l_wa_vbap-vrkme.      "販売/発注単位


*   *--- 一覧で賞味期限ﾌﾞﾗﾝｸに変更して仮引当を実施した場合に使用するために保持
    l_wa_9100-syomi_org   = l_wa_vbap-syomi.    "賞味期限(伝票)

    l_wa_9100-erdat     = l_wa_vbak-erdat.      "受注伝票登録日
    l_wa_9100-wadat     = l_wa_vbap-wadat.      "出庫日付
    l_wa_9100-matnr_s   = l_wa_vbap-matnr.      "品目コード(ソート用)
    l_wa_9100-kunnr2_s  = l_wa_vbak-kunnr2.     "出荷先コード(ソート用)



*   *--- 取置区分
    PERFORM set_zsztorikbn_rtn_001
                             USING     l_wa_9100-kunnr2

                                       space

                             CHANGING  l_wa_9100-zsztorikbn.

*Check the Customer required delivery date
    SORT i_tvko BY vkorg.
    SORT i_leadt BY auart vsbed.
    CLEAR:l_wa_tvko,l_wa_leadt,l_v_doc_date.
    READ TABLE i_tvko INTO l_wa_tvko WITH KEY vkorg = l_wa_vbak-vkorg BINARY SEARCH.
    IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
      READ TABLE i_leadt INTO  l_wa_leadt WITH KEY auart = l_wa_vbak-auart vsbed = l_wa_vbak-vsbed BINARY SEARCH.
      IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
        CALL FUNCTION 'BKK_ADD_WORKINGDAY'
          EXPORTING
            i_date      = l_wa_vbak-audat
            i_days      = l_wa_leadt-lead_time
            i_calendar1 = l_wa_tvko-vkokl
          IMPORTING
            e_date      = l_v_doc_date.
        l_wa_9100-audat = l_v_doc_date.
        IF l_v_doc_date NE l_wa_9100-edatu.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
    APPEND l_wa_9100 TO it_9100.

*   *--- 最新の出荷予定日を取得
    IF v_wadat <= l_wa_vbap-wadat.
      v_wadat = l_wa_vbap-wadat.
    ENDIF.

    CLEAR l_wa_9100.

  ENDLOOP.


* CCW個別社のサマリ処理
  PERFORM f_ekpo_edit_ccw_005.


* 購買データの値設定
  LOOP AT it_ekpo INTO l_wa_ekpo.

*   伝票番号が変わるたびにヘッダ情報取得
    AT NEW ebeln.    "伝票番号
      READ TABLE it_ekko INTO l_wa_ekko BINARY SEARCH
        WITH KEY ebeln = l_wa_ekpo-ebeln.     "伝票番号
    ENDAT.

    l_wa_ekpo-syomi  = l_wa_ekpo-/ccej/bbd.
    l_wa_9100-kbn       = text-002.             "処理区分（テキスト）
    l_wa_9100-vbeln     = l_wa_ekpo-ebeln.      "伝票番号
    l_wa_9100-posnr     = l_wa_ekpo-ebelp.      "明細
    l_wa_9100-matnr     = l_wa_ekpo-matnr.      "品目コード

*    L_WA_9100-WERKS     = L_wa_EKPO-WERKS.      "プラント
    l_wa_9100-werks     = l_wa_ekko-reswk.      "供給プラント
    l_wa_9100-rec_plnt  = l_wa_ekpo-werks."MOD-011

    l_wa_9100-lgort     = l_wa_ekpo-lgort.      "出荷保管場所

    l_wa_9100-lgort_org = l_wa_ekpo-reslo.      "伝票の保管場所
    l_wa_9100-auart     = l_wa_ekko-bsart.      "伝票タイプ
    " BOC | MOD-016| S180184 | Gap 3389 | Subcode changes | 29/07/2016
*  l_wa_9100-edacode   = l_wa_ekpo-atwrt.      "枝番
    l_wa_9100-edacode   = l_wa_ekpo-/scl/atwrt.      "枝番
    " EOC |MOD-016 | S180184 | Gap 3389 | Subcode changes | 29/07/2016

    l_wa_9100-syomi     = l_wa_ekpo-syomi.      "賞味期限
    l_wa_9100-reslo     = l_wa_ekpo-reslo.      "出庫保管場所

    l_wa_9100-werks_ekpo = l_wa_ekpo-werks.     "伝票の出荷先ﾌﾟﾗﾝﾄ
    l_wa_9100-lgort_ekpo = l_wa_ekpo-lgort.     "伝票の出荷先保管場所


*   基本数量単位変換ロジック
    l_v_menge = l_wa_ekpo-menge.
    PERFORM conv_meins
         USING l_wa_ekpo-matnr     "品目コード
               l_wa_ekpo-meins     "基本数量単位（変更前）
               l_wa_ekpo-lmein     "基本数量単位（変更後）
               l_v_menge          "引当数量（CS）
      CHANGING l_v_menge.         "引当数量（CS）
    l_wa_ekpo-menge = l_v_menge.

    l_wa_9100-menge_cs  = l_wa_ekpo-menge.      "引当数量（CS）
    WRITE l_wa_ekpo-menge UNIT l_wa_ekpo-lmein  "引当数量（一覧表示用）
      TO l_wa_9100-menge_out
      RIGHT-JUSTIFIED.

*    L_WA_9100-KUNNR2    = L_wa_EKPO-WERKS.      "出荷先
    l_wa_9100-kunnr2    = l_wa_ekpo-kunnr.

    l_wa_9100-edatu     = l_wa_ekpo-eindt.      "納入日付
    l_wa_9100-meins     = l_wa_ekpo-lmein.      "基本数量単位
    l_wa_9100-vrkme     = l_wa_ekpo-meins.      "販売/発注単位

*    CALL FUNCTION 'CONVERSION_EXIT_CUNIT_OUTPUT'

    l_wa_9100-syomi_org   = l_wa_ekpo-syomi.     "賞味期限(伝票)

    l_wa_9100-wadat     = l_wa_ekpo-wadat.      "出庫日付
    l_wa_9100-matnr_s   = l_wa_ekpo-matnr.      "品目コード(ソート用)
*   10桁でソートするためプラントに前ゼロ付与

*    L_WA_9100-KUNNR2_S = L_WA_9100-WERKS.

    l_wa_9100-kunnr2_s = l_wa_9100-kunnr2.
    l_wa_9100-kunnr    = l_wa_ekpo-kunnr.

                             USING     l_wa_ekpo-ebeln

    IF v_routine_005 = c_kosha.

      l_wa_9100-afnam  = l_wa_ekpo-afnam.
    ENDIF.

*Check the Customer required delivery date

    APPEND l_wa_9100 TO it_9100.

*   *--- 最新の出荷予定日を取得
    IF v_wadat <= l_wa_ekpo-wadat.
      v_wadat = l_wa_ekpo-wadat.
    ENDIF.

    CLEAR l_v_bednr.


  ENDLOOP.

    it_vbap,
    it_ekko,
    it_ekpo.

* 引当対象データが0件の場合
  IF it_9100 IS INITIAL AND v_del_created IS INITIAL..

    MESSAGE s089(/ccej/otc_atp).

    LEAVE LIST-PROCESSING.

  ELSE.
    REFRESH it_9100_lock.
*   *--- トランザクションロック

                             CHANGING  it_9100
                                       it_9100_lock.

      WHEN OTHERS.
*        PERFORM lock_tran."Lock
    ENDCASE.

  ENDIF.

*-----Find Jscale Code & Subcode.....

  IF it_9100[] IS NOT INITIAL.
*****Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1 Conversion **begin of change by EJX9007359 2026.05.22  for ATC
*    SELECT bismt normt matnr zeivr
*      /scl/psize /scl/ptype /scl/cal matkl mtart
*      mhdhb lvorm
*      FROM mara INTO TABLE i_jscale
*      FOR ALL ENTRIES IN it_9100
*      WHERE matnr = it_9100-matnr.
    SELECT ProductOldId            AS bismt,
           IndustryStandardName    AS normt,
           Product                 AS matnr,
           ProductDocumentVersion  AS zeivr,
           ProductGroup            AS matkl,
           ProductType             AS mtart,
           IsMarkedForDeletion     AS lvorm
      INTO TABLE @i_jscale
      FROM I_Product
      FOR ALL ENTRIES IN @it_9100
     WHERE Product = @it_9100-matnr.
* /scl/psize, /scl/ptype, /scl/cal, mhdhb は I_Product 未対応のため個別取得
    IF i_jscale IS NOT INITIAL.
      SELECT matnr AS matnr,
             /scl/psize AS /scl/psize,
             /scl/ptype AS /scl/ptype,
             /scl/cal   AS /scl/cal,
             mhdhb      AS mhdhb
        INTO TABLE @DATA(lt_mara_scl1)
        FROM mara
        FOR ALL ENTRIES IN @i_jscale
       WHERE matnr = @i_jscale-matnr.
      IF sy-subrc = 0.
        SORT lt_mara_scl1 BY matnr.
        LOOP AT i_jscale ASSIGNING FIELD-SYMBOL(<ls_jscale1>).
          READ TABLE lt_mara_scl1 WITH KEY matnr = <ls_jscale1>-matnr
            BINARY SEARCH ASSIGNING FIELD-SYMBOL(<ls_scl1>).
          IF sy-subrc = 0.
            <ls_jscale1>-/scl/psize = <ls_scl1>-/scl/psize.
            <ls_jscale1>-/scl/ptype = <ls_scl1>-/scl/ptype.
            <ls_jscale1>-/scl/cal   = <ls_scl1>-/scl/cal.
            <ls_jscale1>-mhdhb      = <ls_scl1>-mhdhb.
          ENDIF.
        ENDLOOP.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
*****Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1 Conversion **End of change by EJX9007359 2026.05.22  for ATC
  ENDIF.
  SORT i_jscale BY matnr.
  LOOP AT it_9100 INTO l_wa_9100.
    READ TABLE i_jscale INTO wa_jscale WITH KEY matnr = l_wa_9100-matnr BINARY SEARCH.
    IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
      " BOC | MOD-016| S180184 | Gap 3389 | Subcode changes | 29/07/2016
      IF wa_jscale-zeivr IS INITIAL.
        " EOC |MOD-016 | S180184 | Gap 3389 | Subcode changes | 29/07/2016
        l_wa_9100-edacode = wa_jscale-subcode.
        " BOC | MOD-016| S180184 | Gap 3389 | Subcode changes | 29/07/2016
      ENDIF.
      " EOC |MOD-016 | S180184 | Gap 3389 | Subcode changes | 29/07/2016
      l_wa_9100-zeivr = wa_jscale-zeivr.
      l_wa_9100-mtart = wa_jscale-mtart."MOD-019
      l_wa_9100-jscale_code = wa_jscale-jscale_code.

*BOC MOD-003
      l_wa_9100-/scl/psize = wa_jscale-/scl/psize.
      l_wa_9100-/scl/ptype = wa_jscale-/scl/ptype.
      l_wa_9100-/scl/cal = wa_jscale-/scl/cal.
      l_wa_9100-matkl = wa_jscale-matkl."MOD-013
*EOC MOD-003
    ENDIF.
    MODIFY it_9100 FROM l_wa_9100 TRANSPORTING edacode jscale_code /scl/psize /scl/ptype /scl/cal matkl zeivr mtart.
    CLEAR l_wa_9100.
  ENDLOOP.
*Find all material belongs to Jscale Code
  CLEAR i_jscale[].
  IF it_9100[] IS NOT INITIAL.
*****Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1 Conversion **begin of change by EJX9007359 2026.05.22  for ATC
*    SELECT bismt normt matnr zeivr
*      /scl/psize /scl/ptype /scl/cal matkl mtart
*      mhdhb lvorm
*      FROM mara INTO TABLE i_jscale
*      FOR ALL ENTRIES IN it_9100
*      WHERE bismt = it_9100-jscale_code.
    SELECT ProductOldId            AS bismt,
           IndustryStandardName    AS normt,
           Product                 AS matnr,
           ProductDocumentVersion  AS zeivr,
           ProductGroup            AS matkl,
           ProductType             AS mtart,
           IsMarkedForDeletion     AS lvorm
      INTO TABLE @i_jscale
      FROM I_Product
      FOR ALL ENTRIES IN @it_9100
     WHERE ProductOldId = @it_9100-jscale_code.
* /scl/psize, /scl/ptype, /scl/cal, mhdhb は I_Product 未対応のため個別取得
    IF i_jscale IS NOT INITIAL.
      SELECT matnr AS matnr,
             /scl/psize AS /scl/psize,
             /scl/ptype AS /scl/ptype,
             /scl/cal   AS /scl/cal,
             mhdhb      AS mhdhb
        INTO TABLE @DATA(lt_mara_scl2)
        FROM mara
        FOR ALL ENTRIES IN @i_jscale
       WHERE matnr = @i_jscale-matnr.
      IF sy-subrc = 0.
        SORT lt_mara_scl2 BY matnr.
        LOOP AT i_jscale ASSIGNING FIELD-SYMBOL(<ls_jscale2>).
          READ TABLE lt_mara_scl2 WITH KEY matnr = <ls_jscale2>-matnr
            BINARY SEARCH ASSIGNING FIELD-SYMBOL(<ls_scl2>).
          IF sy-subrc = 0.
            <ls_jscale2>-/scl/psize = <ls_scl2>-/scl/psize.
            <ls_jscale2>-/scl/ptype = <ls_scl2>-/scl/ptype.
            <ls_jscale2>-/scl/cal   = <ls_scl2>-/scl/cal.
            <ls_jscale2>-mhdhb      = <ls_scl2>-mhdhb.
          ENDIF.
        ENDLOOP.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
*****Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1 Conversion **End of change by EJX9007359 2026.05.22  for ATC
  ENDIF.



  LOOP AT i_jscale INTO wa_jscale.
    l_wa_9100-zeivr = wa_jscale-zeivr.
    l_wa_9100-matnr = wa_jscale-matnr.
    l_wa_9100-edacode = wa_jscale-subcode.
    l_wa_9100-jscale_code = wa_jscale-jscale_code.
    l_wa_9100-replace = c_on.
*BOC MOD-003
    l_wa_9100-/scl/psize = wa_jscale-/scl/psize.
    l_wa_9100-/scl/ptype = wa_jscale-/scl/ptype.
    l_wa_9100-/scl/cal = wa_jscale-/scl/cal.
    l_wa_9100-matkl = wa_jscale-matkl."MOD-013
*EOC MOD-003
    APPEND l_wa_9100 TO it_9100.
    CLEAR l_wa_9100.
  ENDLOOP.

  DELETE it_9100 WHERE matkl NOT IN s_matg.

*  IF c_mult IS INITIAL."MOD-033
  PERFORM lock_tran.
*  ENDIF.

*BOC for MOD-033
  "Search materials starting with A and replace with leading zero
  LOOP AT i_jscale ASSIGNING <l_fs_jscale> WHERE matnr+0(1) = c_a.
    <l_fs_jscale>-matnr+0(1) = c_kosha.
    "adding leading zeros
    PERFORM exit_alpha_input  USING <l_fs_jscale>-matnr
                       CHANGING <l_fs_jscale>-matnr.

    IF <l_fs_jscale>-zeivr IS INITIAL.
      READ TABLE i_jscale TRANSPORTING NO FIELDS
      WITH KEY matnr = <l_fs_jscale>-matnr
               zeivr = abap_true.
      IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
        <l_fs_jscale>-zeivr = abap_true.
      ENDIF.

    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.
  SORT i_jscale BY matnr.
  DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM i_jscale COMPARING matnr.

*EOC for MOD-033

ENDFORM.                                                    " EDIT_9100

*****Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1 Conversion **begin of change by EJX9007359 2026.05.22  for ATC
*  SELECT ebeln    "購買伝票番号
*         ebelp    "購買伝票の明細番号
*    FROM ekbe
*    INTO TABLE it_ekbe
*    FOR ALL ENTRIES IN it_ekpo
*   WHERE ebeln =  it_ekpo-ebeln
*     AND ebelp =  it_ekpo-ebelp
*     AND vgabe =  c_vgabe_8
*     AND menge <> 0.
  SELECT PurchaseOrder     AS ebeln,   "購買伝票番号
         PurchaseOrderItem AS ebelp   "購買伝票の明細番号
    INTO TABLE @it_ekbe

   WHERE PurchaseOrder     =  @it_ekpo-ebeln   "購買伝票番号
     AND PurchaseOrderItem =  @it_ekpo-ebelp   "購買伝票の明細番号
     AND PurchasingHistoryDocumentType = @c_vgabe_8  "購買発注履歴取引タイプ
     AND Quantity          <> 0.                     "数量
*****Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1 Conversion **End of change by EJX9007359 2026.05.22  for ATC

*    PERFORM select_zstsd0003.

*  ELSE.
*   WM管理品の在庫データ取得(在庫断面情報参照OFF)
  PERFORM select_lqua.
*  ENDIF.

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  SEPARATE_MARC
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       在庫の取得元の切り分け（ロット管理品/非ロット管理品）
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM separate_marc .

* ローカル定義
  DATA:
    l_it_taihi    TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_9100,   "退避用テーブル
    l_wa_taihi    TYPE ty_9100,                     "退避用テーブル
    l_wa_marc     TYPE ty_marc,                     "品目プラントデータ
    l_wa_marc_non TYPE ty_marc.                     "非ロット管理品取得用

* データ退避
  l_it_taihi = it_9100.

* ソート処理
  SORT l_it_taihi BY matnr.   "品目コード

* 重複データ削除
  DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM l_it_taihi
    COMPARING matnr.   "品目コード

* 初期化
  REFRESH:
    it_marc_lot,    "ロット管理品取得用
    it_marc_non.    "非ロット管理品取得用

* 引当対象内部テーブルを非ロット管理品取得用内部テーブルに退避
  LOOP AT l_it_taihi INTO l_wa_taihi.
    l_wa_marc_non-matnr = l_wa_taihi-matnr.   "品目コード
*    l_wa_marc_non-werks = p_werks.            "プラント
*    APPEND l_wa_marc_non TO it_marc_non.
    LOOP AT s_werks.
      l_wa_marc_non-werks = s_werks-low.            "プラント
      APPEND l_wa_marc_non TO it_marc_non.
    ENDLOOP.

  ENDLOOP.

* ソート処理
  SORT it_marc_non BY matnr   "品目コード
                      werks.  "プラント
  DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM it_marc_non COMPARING matnr werks.
  LOOP AT it_marc INTO l_wa_marc
    WHERE xchpf <> space.          "ロット管理所要量区分

*   非ロット管理品取得用内部テーブルの読み込み
    READ TABLE it_marc_non INTO l_wa_marc_non BINARY SEARCH
      WITH KEY matnr = l_wa_marc-matnr   "品目コード
               werks = l_wa_marc-werks.""p_werks.          "プラント

*   データが存在する場合
    IF sy-subrc = 0.

*     ロット管理品取得用内部テーブルに格納する
      APPEND l_wa_marc_non TO it_marc_lot.

*     非ロット管理品取得用内部テーブルから削除する
      DELETE TABLE it_marc_non FROM l_wa_marc_non.

    ENDIF.

  ENDLOOP.

ENDFORM.                    " SEPARATE_MARC

*&      Form  SELECT_MARD
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       非ロット管理品の在庫データ取得
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM select_mard .

* 非ロット管理品取得用テーブルにデータがある場合に下記の処理を行う
  CHECK it_marc_non IS NOT INITIAL.

* 初期化
  REFRESH it_mard.

* 品目の保管場所データの検索
*****Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1 Conversion **begin of change by EJX9007359 2026.05.22  for ATC
*  SELECT matnr   "品目コード
*         werks   "プラント
*         lgort   "保管場所
*         labst   "利用可能評価在庫
*    FROM mard
*    INTO TABLE it_mard
*    FOR ALL ENTRIES IN it_marc_non
*   WHERE matnr = it_marc_non-matnr
*    AND werks = it_marc_non-werks.
  SELECT Product         AS matnr,   "品目コード
         Plant           AS werks,   "プラント
         StorageLocation AS lgort   "保管場所
    INTO TABLE @it_mard
    FROM I_ProductStorageLocationBasic
    FOR ALL ENTRIES IN @it_marc_non
   WHERE Product = @it_marc_non-matnr   "品目コード
     AND Plant   = @it_marc_non-werks.  "プラント
* labst(利用可能評価在庫) は I_ProductStorageLocationBasic 未対応のため個別取得
  IF it_mard IS NOT INITIAL.
    SELECT matnr AS matnr,
           werks AS werks,
           lgort AS lgort,
           labst AS labst
      INTO TABLE @DATA(lt_mard_ext)
      FROM mard
      FOR ALL ENTRIES IN @it_mard
     WHERE matnr = @it_mard-matnr
       AND werks = @it_mard-werks
       AND lgort = @it_mard-lgort.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      SORT lt_mard_ext BY matnr werks lgort.
      LOOP AT it_mard ASSIGNING FIELD-SYMBOL(<ls_mard>).
        READ TABLE lt_mard_ext
          WITH KEY matnr = <ls_mard>-matnr
                   werks = <ls_mard>-werks
                   lgort = <ls_mard>-lgort
          BINARY SEARCH
          ASSIGNING FIELD-SYMBOL(<ls_mard_ext>).
        IF sy-subrc = 0.
          <ls_mard>-labst = <ls_mard_ext>-labst.
        ENDIF.
      ENDLOOP.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.
*****Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1 Conversion **End of change by EJX9007359 2026.05.22  for ATC

ENDFORM.                    " SELECT_MARD

    LOOP AT i_im_data INTO wa_im_data.
      wa_im_data-matnr = l_wa_marc-matnr.

      CLEAR wa_im_data.
    ENDLOOP.
    CLEAR:l_wa_marc.
  ENDLOOP.

  SORT i_im_data1 BY matnr werks lgort.
  DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM i_im_data1 COMPARING ALL FIELDS.

  SELECT matnr   "品目コード

         cumlm

    FOR ALL ENTRIES IN i_im_data1"it_marc_lot
   WHERE matnr =  i_im_data1-matnr"it_marc_lot-matnr    "品目コード
     AND werks = i_im_data1-werks"IN rng_werks_im"s_werks"=  p_werks              "プラント
     AND lgort = i_im_data1-lgort."IN rng_lgort_im.        "保管場所



ENDFORM.                    " SELECT_MCHB

*&      Form  SELECT_LQUA
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       WM管理品の在庫データ取得(在庫断面情報参照OFF)
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM select_lqua .


* ローカル定義
  DATA:
    l_it_lagp    TYPE TABLE OF ty_lagp,
    l_it_marc_wm TYPE TABLE OF ty_marc,
    l_it_lqua    TYPE TABLE OF ty_lqua,
    l_wa_lqua    TYPE ty_lqua,
    l_v_tabix   TYPE i.

  DATA:
    l_it_tmp     TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_marc,
    l_it_t320    TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF t320,
    l_wa_t320    TYPE t320,
    l_rng_lgnum  TYPE RANGE OF t320-lgnum,
    l_wa_lgnum   LIKE LINE OF l_rng_lgnum,
    l_i_strg TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF /ccej/t_atp_strg,
    l_wa_strg TYPE /ccej/t_atp_strg.



* WM管理品取得用内部テーブルにデータがある場合に以下の処理を行う
  CHECK it_marc_wm IS NOT INITIAL.

* 保管場所レンジテーブル(WM)にデータがある場合に以下の処理を行う
  CHECK rng_lgort_wm IS NOT INITIAL.

* 初期化
  REFRESH it_lqua.



* 倉庫番号/複合倉庫検索条件の作成
  l_it_tmp[] = it_marc_wm[].
  SORT l_it_tmp BY werks.
  DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM l_it_tmp COMPARING werks.
  IF lines( l_it_tmp ) > 0.
    SELECT mandt werks lgort lgnum obest lgntb slgor obtyp lgref censy
      FROM t320
      INTO TABLE l_it_t320
*       FOR ALL ENTRIES IN l_it_tmp
     WHERE werks IN rng_werks_wm"s_werks "= l_it_tmp-werks    "プラント
       AND lgort IN rng_lgort_wm      "保管場所
    .
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      LOOP AT l_it_t320 INTO l_wa_t320.
        l_wa_lgnum-sign    =  c_sign_i.
        l_wa_lgnum-option  =  c_opt_eq.
        l_wa_lgnum-low     =  l_wa_t320-lgnum.
        APPEND l_wa_lgnum TO l_rng_lgnum.
        APPEND l_wa_lgnum TO rng_lgnum.
      ENDLOOP.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

*Check Storage Type Control Table
  CLEAR l_i_strg[].
  SELECT mandt ktopl werks lgnum lgtyp
    FROM /ccej/t_atp_strg
    INTO TABLE l_i_strg
    WHERE werks IN rng_werks_wm"s_werks
     AND lgnum IN rng_lgnum.
*
  l_it_marc_wm[]   = it_marc_wm[].
  SORT l_it_marc_wm BY matnr werks.
  DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM l_it_marc_wm
                  COMPARING matnr werks.
  IF l_it_marc_wm[] IS NOT INITIAL.
* 保管ロット、棚番、保管単位ヘッダレコードの検索
    SELECT werks   "プラント
           lgort   "保管場所
           matnr   "品目コード
           letyp   "保管単位タイプ
           bestq   "倉庫管理システムにおける在庫カテゴリ
           lgnum   "倉庫番号/複合倉庫
           lqnum   "保管ロット
           charg   "ロット番号
           lgtyp   "保管域タイプ
           meins   "基本数量単位
           gesme   "合計数量
           lgpla   "棚番
      INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE it_lqua
      FROM lqua
      FOR ALL ENTRIES IN l_it_marc_wm
     WHERE lqua~matnr   =  l_it_marc_wm-matnr   "品目コード

     AND lqua~werks   IN rng_werks_wm"s_werks"=  L_IT_MARC_WM-WERKS   "プラント
*     AND LQUA~LGORT   IN RNG_LGORT_WM         "保管場所
       AND lqua~lgnum   IN  l_rng_lgnum         "倉庫番号/複合倉庫

       AND lqua~bestq   IN (c_bestq, space)
           .
  ENDIF.
*
  IF it_lqua[] IS NOT INITIAL.
*
    l_it_lqua[] = it_lqua[].
*
    SORT l_it_lqua BY lgnum lgtyp lgpla.
    DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM l_it_lqua
           COMPARING lgnum lgtyp lgpla.

    SELECT lgnum   "倉庫番号/複合倉庫
           lgtyp   "保管域タイプ
           lgpla   "棚番
      INTO TABLE l_it_lagp
      FROM lagp
       FOR ALL ENTRIES IN l_it_lqua
     WHERE lgnum   = l_it_lqua-lgnum     "倉庫番号/複合倉庫
       AND lgtyp   = l_it_lqua-lgtyp     "保管域タイプ
       AND lgpla   = l_it_lqua-lgpla     "棚番
       AND skzua   = space.              "ブロック区分: 在庫払出(ユーザ)

    SORT l_it_lagp BY lgnum lgtyp lgpla.

  ENDIF.

  SORT l_i_strg BY werks lgnum lgtyp.
  LOOP AT it_lqua INTO l_wa_lqua.

*
    l_v_tabix  = sy-tabix.
*
    READ TABLE l_it_lagp TRANSPORTING NO FIELDS
               WITH KEY lgnum   = l_wa_lqua-lgnum     "倉庫番号/複合倉庫
                        lgtyp   = l_wa_lqua-lgtyp     "保管域タイプ
                        lgpla   = l_wa_lqua-lgpla     "棚番
                        BINARY SEARCH.

    IF sy-subrc <> 0.

      DELETE it_lqua INDEX l_v_tabix.

    ENDIF.

    IF l_i_strg[] IS NOT INITIAL.
      READ TABLE l_i_strg INTO l_wa_strg WITH KEY werks   = l_wa_lqua-werks     "倉庫番号/複合倉庫
                                                  lgnum   = l_wa_lqua-lgnum     "保管域タイプ
                                                  lgtyp   = l_wa_lqua-lgtyp    "棚番
                                                  BINARY SEARCH.
      IF sy-subrc <> 0.
        DELETE it_lqua INDEX l_v_tabix.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.


  ENDLOOP.


ENDFORM.                    " SELECT_LQUA
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*

FORM select_stock_yotei .


* *--- 最終入荷予定日算出
  v_wadat = v_wadat - v_factr.


* 発注入荷予定データの取得
  PERFORM select_ekpo_y.

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  SELECT_EKPO_Y
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       発注入荷予定データの取得
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM select_ekpo_y .

* ローカル定義
  DATA:
    l_it_taihi TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_9100.   "退避用テーブル

  DATA:
    l_it_ekpo_y  TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_ekpo_y,
    l_wa_ekpo_y  TYPE ty_ekpo_y,
    l_it_eket    TYPE SORTED TABLE OF ty_eket
                 WITH NON-UNIQUE KEY ebeln ebelp etenr,
    l_wa_eket    TYPE ty_eket.



* 引当対象データがある場合に当処理を行う
  CHECK it_9100 IS NOT INITIAL.


* データ退避
  l_it_taihi = it_9100.

* ソート処理
  SORT l_it_taihi BY matnr.    "品目コード

* 重複データ削除
  DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM l_it_taihi
    COMPARING matnr.    "品目コード

* 初期化
  REFRESH it_ekpo_y.

  IF l_it_taihi[] IS NOT INITIAL.
* 購買伝票明細、分納契約納入日程行、購買伝票ヘッダの検索
    SELECT ekpo~ebeln   "購買伝票番号
           ekpo~ebelp   "購買伝票の明細番号
           ekpo~matnr   "品目コード
           ekpo~werks   "プラント
           ekpo~lgort   "保管場所
           ekpo~menge   "購買発注量
           ekpo~meins   "発注単位
           ekpo~lmein   "基本数量単位
           ekko~bsart   "購買伝票タイプ
           ekpo~/scl/atwrt   "枝番
           ekpo~/ccej/bbd
      INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE l_it_ekpo_y
      FROM ekpo
      INNER JOIN ekko
      ON  ekpo~ebeln = ekko~ebeln   "購買伝票番号
      FOR ALL ENTRIES IN l_it_taihi
     WHERE ekpo~matnr =  l_it_taihi-matnr   "品目コード
       AND ekpo~werks IN s_werks"=  p_werks            "プラント
       AND ekpo~elikz <> c_on             "納入完了区分
       AND ekpo~loekz <> c_elikz          "購買伝票の削除フラグ

           .
  ENDIF.
  IF l_it_ekpo_y[] IS NOT INITIAL.

*
    SELECT ebeln           "購買伝票番号
           ebelp           "購買伝票の明細番号
           etenr           "納入日程行カウンタ
           eindt           "納入期日※出荷予定日
           wemng           "入庫数量
      INTO TABLE l_it_eket
      FROM eket
       FOR ALL ENTRIES IN l_it_ekpo_y
     WHERE ebeln = l_it_ekpo_y-ebeln   "購買伝票番号
      AND  ebelp = l_it_ekpo_y-ebelp   "購買伝票の明細番号
      AND  eindt <= v_wadat           "納入期日(入荷予定日)
           .

  ENDIF.

*
  LOOP AT l_it_ekpo_y INTO l_wa_ekpo_y.

*
    LOOP AT l_it_eket INTO l_wa_eket
            WHERE ebeln = l_wa_ekpo_y-ebeln   "購買伝票番号
             AND  ebelp = l_wa_ekpo_y-ebelp   "購買伝票の明細番号
             .

*
      l_wa_ekpo_y-eindt    = l_wa_eket-eindt.   "納入期日※出荷予定日
      l_wa_ekpo_y-wemng    = l_wa_eket-wemng.   "入庫数量
*     BOC MOD-020
      CLEAR  wa_jscale.
      READ TABLE i_jscale INTO  wa_jscale
           WITH KEY matnr = l_wa_ekpo_y-matnr.
      IF sy-subrc = 0 AND wa_jscale-zeivr IS INITIAL.
        l_wa_ekpo_y-/scl/atwrt = l_wa_ekpo_y-matnr+14(4).
      ENDIF.
*     EOC MOD-020

      APPEND l_wa_ekpo_y TO it_ekpo_y.

    ENDLOOP.

  ENDLOOP.


ENDFORM.                    " SELECT_EKPO_Y
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  SELECT_AFPO_Y
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       製造入庫予定データの取得
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM select_afpo_y .

* ローカル定義
  DATA:
    l_it_taihi TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_9100,   "退避用テーブル
    l_wa_art_s TYPE ty_auart,                    "指図タイプ
    l_v_dauat TYPE afpo-dauat.                  "指図タイプ

  DATA:l_wa_afko TYPE ty_afko_y,
       l_wa_jest TYPE ty_jest,
       l_wa_tj02t TYPE ty_tj02t.

  CONSTANTS:l_c_dlfl TYPE tj02t-txt04 VALUE 'DLFL',
            l_c_i0076 TYPE j_status VALUE 'I0076',"DLFL
            l_c_i0045 TYPE j_status VALUE 'I0045',"TECO MOD-008
            l_c_i0012 TYPE j_status VALUE 'I0012'."DLV MOD-008

* BOC MOD-018
  FIELD-SYMBOLS:<l_fs_afpo> TYPE ty_afpo_y,
                <l_fs_afko> TYPE ty_afko,
                <l_fs_stko> TYPE ty_stko.
* EOC MOD-018

* 引当対象データがある場合に当処理を行う
  CHECK it_9100 IS NOT INITIAL.


* データ退避
  l_it_taihi = it_9100.

* ソート処理
  SORT l_it_taihi BY matnr.    "品目コード

* 重複データ削除
  DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM l_it_taihi
    COMPARING matnr.    "品目コード

* 初期化
  REFRESH it_afpo_y.
  CLEAR:it_afko_y[],it_jest[],it_tj02t[].
*  LOOP AT it_art_s INTO l_wa_art_s.

*   退避
  l_v_dauat = l_wa_art_s-zzdata1.
  IF l_it_taihi[] IS NOT INITIAL.
    SELECT afpo~aufnr       "指図番号
           afpo~posnr       "指図明細番号
           afpo~psmng       "指図明細数量
           afpo~wemng       "指図明細の品目入庫数量
           afpo~amein       "内製単位
           afpo~meins       "基本数量単位
           afpo~matnr       "指図の品目コード
           afpo~pwerk       "指図の計画プラント
           afpo~lgort       "保管場所
           afpo~umrez       "基本数量単位への換算分子
           afpo~umren       "基本数量単位への換算時の分母
           afpo~dwerk       "プラント
           afpo~dgltp       "基準終了日付
           afpo~dglts       "計画終了
           afpo~verid       "製造バージョン
      FROM afpo
      APPENDING CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE it_afpo_y
      FOR ALL ENTRIES IN l_it_taihi
     WHERE afpo~matnr =  l_it_taihi-matnr  "指図の品目コード
       AND afpo~pwerk IN s_werks"=  p_werks           "指図の計画プラント
       AND afpo~elikz <> c_on            "納入完了区分
       AND afpo~dauty = c_prod_40
       AND afpo~dauat =  c_prod_zpi2"l_v_dauat        "指図タイプ
       AND afpo~dgltp <= v_wadat         "基準終了日付
       AND afpo~xloek =  space.          "削除フラグ

    IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
      SELECT aufnr objnr FROM aufk
        INTO TABLE it_afko_y
        FOR ALL ENTRIES IN it_afpo_y
        WHERE aufnr = it_afpo_y-aufnr
         AND auart = c_prod_zpi2
         AND autyp = c_prod_40 .

      IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.

        SELECT objnr stat
          FROM jest
          INTO TABLE it_jest
          FOR ALL ENTRIES IN it_afko_y
          WHERE objnr = it_afko_y-objnr
          AND stat IN (l_c_i0076, l_c_i0045, l_c_i0012)" DLFL,TECO,DLV MOD-008
          AND inact = space.

*BOC MOD-018
*Get Subcode Details from BOM
        CLEAR i_afko[].
        SELECT aufnr stlty stlnr stlal sdatv
          FROM afko INTO TABLE i_afko
          FOR ALL ENTRIES IN it_afko_y
          WHERE aufnr = it_afko_y-aufnr.
        IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
          SORT i_afko BY aufnr.
          " get subcode from the bom
          SELECT stlty
                 stlnr
                 stlal
                 stkoz
                 datuv
                 sub_code
           FROM stko
           INTO TABLE i_stko
           FOR ALL ENTRIES IN i_afko
           WHERE stlty  = i_afko-stlty
           AND   stlnr = i_afko-stlnr
           AND   stlal = i_afko-stlal
           AND   datuv =  i_afko-sdatv.
          IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
            SORT i_stko BY stlty stlnr stlal datuv.
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.
*EOC MOD-018

      ENDIF.

    ENDIF.


  ENDIF.

*Delete Orders which is having Status Deleted
  LOOP AT it_afko_y INTO l_wa_afko.
    READ TABLE it_jest INTO l_wa_jest WITH KEY objnr = l_wa_afko-objnr.
    IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
      DELETE it_afpo_y WHERE aufnr = l_wa_afko-aufnr.
    ENDIF.
    CLEAR:l_wa_afko,l_wa_jest,l_wa_tj02t.
  ENDLOOP.
*  ENDLOOP.

*BOC MOD-018
  LOOP AT it_afpo_y ASSIGNING <l_fs_afpo>.
    READ TABLE i_afko ASSIGNING <l_fs_afko>
    WITH KEY aufnr = <l_fs_afpo>-aufnr
    BINARY SEARCH.
    IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
      READ TABLE i_stko ASSIGNING <l_fs_stko>
      WITH KEY stlty = <l_fs_afko>-stlty
                stlnr = <l_fs_afko>-stlnr
                stlal = <l_fs_afko>-stlal
                datuv = <l_fs_afko>-sdatv
       BINARY SEARCH.
      IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
        <l_fs_afpo>-sub_code = <l_fs_stko>-sub_code.
*     BOC MOD-020
        CLEAR  wa_jscale.
        READ TABLE i_jscale INTO  wa_jscale
             WITH KEY matnr = <l_fs_afpo>-matnr.
        IF sy-subrc = 0 AND wa_jscale-zeivr IS INITIAL.
          <l_fs_afpo>-sub_code = <l_fs_afpo>-matnr+14(4).
        ENDIF.
*     EOC MOD-020
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.
*EOC MOD-018


ENDFORM.                    " SELECT_AFPO_Y

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  SELECT_LIPS
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       出荷伝票情報取得
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM select_lips .

* ローカル定義
  DATA:
    l_it_taihi TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_9100.   "退避用テーブル

*    L_wa_TAIHI TYPE TY_9100.

  DATA:
    l_wa_lips           TYPE ty_lips,
    l_v_tabix          TYPE i.

  DATA:
    l_it_tmp    TYPE ty_it_vbup,
    l_wa_tmp    TYPE ty_vbup,
    l_it_vbuk   TYPE ty_it_vbuk,
    l_it_vbup   TYPE ty_it_vbup.

  DATA : l_i_open    TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF /ccbji/t_opendlv, " MOD-039 +
         l_wa_opdlv  TYPE /ccbji/t_opendlv,                   " MOD-039 +
         l_wa_tvarvc TYPE tvarvc,                             " MOD-039 +
         l_v_varkey  TYPE vim_enqkey,                         " MOD-039 +
         l_v_numb    TYPE tvarv_numb,                         " MOD-039 +
         l_v_flag    TYPE flag.                               " MOD-039 +

  FIELD-SYMBOLS : <l_fs_lips> TYPE ty_lips.                     " MOD-039 +

  l_it_taihi = it_9100.

* ソート処理
  SORT l_it_taihi BY matnr.    "品目コード

* 重複データ削除
  DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM l_it_taihi
    COMPARING matnr.    "品目コード

* 初期化
  REFRESH it_lips.

*   販売伝票: 出荷伝票: 明細データ
*  SELECT lips~vbeln         "出荷伝票
*         lips~posnr         "出荷明細
*         lips~matnr         "品目コード
*         lips~werks         "プラント
*         lips~lgort         "保管場所
*         lips~lgmng   AS lfimg       "出荷数量実績(在庫単位)
*         lips~meins         "基本数量単位
*         lips~vrkme         "販売単位
*         lips~umvkz         "販売数量単位から基本数量単位への変換係数の分子
*         lips~umvkn         "販売数量を在庫単位に換算する分母 (除数)
*         lips~vgbel         "参照伝票
*         lips~vgpos         "参照明細
*         lips~vgtyp         "伝票カテゴリ
**         lips~zzseda        "枝番"NA
**         lips~zzskiboshom   "賞味期限"NA
**         lips~zzsplcode     "パレット種別"NA
*         likp~wadat         "出庫予定日付
*
**         lips~zzskyoseikb   "強制引当区分"NA

*    APPENDING CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE it_lips
*    FROM lips
*    INNER JOIN likp
*    ON lips~vbeln = likp~vbeln   "出荷伝票
*   WHERE lips~werks =  p_werks             "プラント
*     AND likp~lfart IN rng_lfart           "除外出荷伝票タイプ
*     AND lips~lgort IN rng_lgort.          "保管場所
*   販売伝票: 出荷伝票: 明細データ

  " BOC | MOD-039 | S420011 | Gap 1000002261

  SELECT COUNT(*)
    FROM tvarvc
   WHERE name = c_name1
     AND type = sy-abcde+18(1)
     AND low  = p_werks.

  IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.

    SELECT *
      FROM /ccbji/t_opendlv
      INTO TABLE l_i_open
     WHERE werks IN s_werks[].

    IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.

      SELECT vbuk~vbeln
             lips~posnr
             lips~matnr
             lips~werks
             lips~lgort
             lips~charg
             lips~lgmng AS lfimg
             lips~meins
             lips~vrkme
             lips~umvkz
             lips~umvkn
             lips~vgbel
             lips~vgpos
             lips~vgtyp
             lips~vfdat
             lips~/lot/tempkz
             lips~/scl/atwrt
             likp~wadat
             likp~lfdat
             likp~kunnr
   APPENDING CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE it_lips
        FROM vbuk
       INNER JOIN vbup ON vbup~vbeln = vbuk~vbeln
       INNER JOIN lips ON lips~vbeln = vbup~vbeln
                      AND lips~posnr = vbup~posnr
       INNER JOIN likp ON likp~vbeln = vbuk~vbeln
         FOR ALL ENTRIES IN l_i_open
       WHERE vbuk~vbeln = l_i_open-vbeln
         AND likp~lfart IN rng_lfart                           " MOD-040 +
         AND lips~werks IN s_werks
         AND lips~lgort IN rng_lgort
         AND vbuk~wbstk IN (space, c_wbstk_a, c_wbstk_b)
         AND vbuk~abstk IN (space, c_abstk_a, c_abstk_b)
         AND vbuk~vbtyp  =  c_vbtyp_j
         AND vbuk~vbobj  =  c_vbobj_l
         AND vbup~wbsta IN (space, c_wbsta_a, c_wbsta_b)
         AND vbup~absta IN (space, c_absta_a, c_absta_b).
    ENDIF.
  ELSE.
    " EOC | MOD-039 | S420011 | Gap 1000002261

*****Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1 Conversion **begin of change by EJX9007359 2026.05.22  for ATC
*    SELECT lips~vbeln lips~posnr lips~matnr lips~werks lips~lgort
*           lips~lgmng AS lfimg lips~meins lips~vrkme lips~umvkz lips~umvkn
*           lips~vgbel lips~vgpos lips~vgtyp lips~vfdat
*           lips~/lot/tempkz lips~/scl/atwrt
*           likp~wadat likp~lfdat likp~kunnr
*      APPENDING CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE it_lips
*      FROM lips INNER JOIN likp ON lips~vbeln = likp~vbeln
*      INNER JOIN vbuk ON vbuk~vbeln = likp~vbeln
*      INNER JOIN vbup ON vbup~vbeln = lips~vbeln AND vbup~posnr = lips~posnr
*     WHERE lips~werks IN s_werks AND likp~lfart IN rng_lfart
*       AND lips~lgort IN rng_lgort AND vbuk~vbtyp = c_vbtyp_j
*       AND vbuk~wbstk IN (space,c_wbstk_a,c_wbstk_b)
*       AND vbuk~abstk IN (space,c_abstk_a,c_abstk_b)
*       AND vbuk~vbobj = c_vbobj_l
*       AND vbup~wbsta IN (space,c_wbsta_a,c_wbsta_b)
*       AND vbup~absta IN (space,c_absta_a,c_absta_b).
    SELECT dlvitem~DeliveryDocument          AS vbeln,   "出荷伝票
           dlvitem~DeliveryDocumentItem      AS posnr,   "出荷明細
           dlvitem~Material                  AS matnr,   "品目コード
           dlvitem~Plant                     AS werks,   "プラント
           dlvitem~StorageLocation           AS lgort,   "保管場所
           dlvitem~ActualDeliveredQtyInBaseUnit AS lfimg, "出荷数量実績(在庫単位)
           dlvitem~BaseUnit                  AS meins,   "基本数量単位
           dlvitem~DeliveryQuantityUnit      AS vrkme,   "販売単位
           dlvitem~DeliveryToBaseQuantityNmrtr AS umvkz, "分子
           dlvitem~DeliveryToBaseQuantityDnmntr AS umvkn, "分母
           dlvitem~ReferenceSDDocument       AS vgbel,   "参照伝票
           dlvitem~ReferenceSDDocumentItem   AS vgpos,   "参照明細
           dlvitem~ReferenceSDDocumentCategory AS vgtyp, "伝票カテゴリ
           dlvitem~ShelfLifeExpirationDate   AS vfdat,   "賞味期限日
           dlvdoc~PlannedGoodsIssueDate      AS wadat,   "出庫予定日付
           dlvdoc~DeliveryDate               AS lfdat,   "納入日
           dlvdoc~ShipToParty               AS kunnr   "得意先
      APPENDING CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE @it_lips
      FROM I_DeliveryDocumentItem AS dlvitem
      INNER JOIN I_DeliveryDocument AS dlvdoc
        ON dlvitem~DeliveryDocument = dlvdoc~DeliveryDocument
     WHERE dlvitem~Plant               IN @s_werks         "プラント
       AND dlvdoc~DeliveryDocumentType IN @rng_lfart        "出荷伝票タイプ
       AND dlvitem~StorageLocation     IN @rng_lgort        "保管場所
       AND dlvdoc~SdDocumentCategory   =  @c_vbtyp_j       "伝票カテゴリ
       AND dlvdoc~OverallGoodsMovementStatus IN ( @space, @c_wbstk_a, @c_wbstk_b ) "在庫移動ステータス
       AND dlvitem~GoodsMovementStatus  IN ( @space, @c_wbsta_a, @c_wbsta_b ). "明細在庫移動ステータス
* abstk/absta/vbobj は CDS 未対応のため ABAP で後フィルタ
* /lot/tempkz, /scl/atwrt は LIPS から個別取得
    IF it_lips IS NOT INITIAL.
      SELECT vbeln AS vbeln,
             posnr AS posnr,
             /lot/tempkz AS /lot/tempkz,
             /scl/atwrt  AS /scl/atwrt
        INTO TABLE @DATA(lt_lips_ext)
        FROM lips
        FOR ALL ENTRIES IN @it_lips
       WHERE vbeln = @it_lips-vbeln
         AND posnr = @it_lips-posnr.
      IF sy-subrc = 0.
        SORT lt_lips_ext BY vbeln posnr.
        LOOP AT it_lips ASSIGNING FIELD-SYMBOL(<ls_lips>).
          READ TABLE lt_lips_ext
            WITH KEY vbeln = <ls_lips>-vbeln
                     posnr = <ls_lips>-posnr
            BINARY SEARCH
            ASSIGNING FIELD-SYMBOL(<ls_lips_ext>).
          IF sy-subrc = 0.
            <ls_lips>-/lot/tempkz = <ls_lips_ext>-/lot/tempkz.
            <ls_lips>-/scl/atwrt  = <ls_lips_ext>-/scl/atwrt.
          ENDIF.
        ENDLOOP.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
*****Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1 Conversion **End of change by EJX9007359 2026.05.22  for ATC

    " BOC | MOD-039 | S420011 | Gap 1000002261

    IF it_lips[] IS NOT INITIAL.

      " Update Open Deliveries
      LOOP AT it_lips ASSIGNING <l_fs_lips>.

        CLEAR : l_v_varkey, l_v_flag, l_wa_opdlv.

        l_wa_opdlv-werks = <l_fs_lips>-werks.
        l_wa_opdlv-vbeln = <l_fs_lips>-vbeln.

        CONCATENATE sy-mandt <l_fs_lips>-werks <l_fs_lips>-vbeln
               INTO l_v_varkey.

        DO 3 TIMES.
          CALL FUNCTION 'ENQUEUE_E_TABLE'
            EXPORTING
              mode_rstable   = c_mode
              tabname        = c_tname2
              varkey         = l_v_varkey
            EXCEPTIONS
              foreign_lock   = 1
              system_failure = 2
              OTHERS         = 3.
          IF sy-subrc IS NOT INITIAL.
            WAIT UP TO 1 SECONDS.
          ELSE.
            l_v_flag = abap_true.
            EXIT.
          ENDIF.
        ENDDO.

        IF l_v_flag EQ abap_true.

          MODIFY /ccbji/t_opendlv FROM l_wa_opdlv.

          CALL FUNCTION 'DEQUEUE_E_TABLE'
            EXPORTING
              mode_rstable = c_mode
              tabname      = c_tname2
              varkey       = l_v_varkey.

        ENDIF.
      ENDLOOP.

      " Update TVARVC
      SELECT MAX( numb )
        FROM tvarvc
        INTO l_v_numb
       WHERE name = c_name1
         AND type = sy-abcde+18(1).

      IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
        l_v_numb = l_v_numb + 1.
      ENDIF.

      CLEAR : l_v_varkey, l_v_flag.

      l_wa_tvarvc-name = c_name1.
      l_wa_tvarvc-type = sy-abcde+18(1).
      l_wa_tvarvc-numb = l_v_numb.
      l_wa_tvarvc-low  = p_werks.

      CONCATENATE sy-mandt c_name1 sy-abcde+18(1) l_v_numb
             INTO l_v_varkey.

      DO 3 TIMES.

        CALL FUNCTION 'ENQUEUE_E_TABLE'
          EXPORTING
            mode_rstable   = c_mode
            tabname        = c_tname1
            varkey         = l_v_varkey
          EXCEPTIONS
            foreign_lock   = 1
            system_failure = 2
            OTHERS         = 3.
        IF sy-subrc IS NOT INITIAL.
          WAIT UP TO 1 SECONDS.
        ELSE.
          l_v_flag = abap_true.
          EXIT.
        ENDIF.
      ENDDO.

      IF l_v_flag EQ abap_true.

        MODIFY tvarvc FROM l_wa_tvarvc.

        CALL FUNCTION 'DEQUEUE_E_TABLE'
          EXPORTING
            mode_rstable = c_mode
            tabname      = c_tname1
            varkey       = l_v_varkey.

      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.
  " EOC | MOD-039 | S420011 | Gap 1000002261

*    APPEND l_wa_tmp TO l_it_tmp.
*  ENDLOOP.
** 伝票ステータスの取得
*  PERFORM f_get_vbukp USING     l_it_tmp      "出荷伝票
*                      CHANGING  l_it_vbuk     "ヘッダステータス
*                                l_it_vbup.    "明細ステータス

  LOOP AT it_lips INTO l_wa_lips.

*
    l_v_tabix  = sy-tabix.

*    ENDIF.

**   明細ステータスのチェック

               WITH KEY matnr =  l_wa_lips-matnr    "品目ｺｰﾄﾞ
                        BINARY SEARCH.

    IF sy-subrc <> 0.

      DELETE it_lips INDEX l_v_tabix.

    ENDIF.

  ENDLOOP.

  SORT it_lips   BY matnr       ASCENDING

*&      Form  SELECT_MAKT_9100
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       品目テキストの取得
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM select_makt_9100 .

* ローカル定義
  DATA:

*    L_wa_TAIHI TYPE TY_9100,                    "退避用テーブル

    l_it_taihi TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_9100.
*
*  FIELD-SYMBOLS :
*    <L_FS_TAIHI> TYPE TY_9100.



  CHECK it_9100 IS NOT INITIAL.


* データ退避
  l_it_taihi = it_9100.

* ソート処理
  SORT l_it_taihi BY matnr.   "品目コード

* 重複データ削除
  DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM l_it_taihi
    COMPARING matnr.    "品目コード



* 初期化
  REFRESH it_makt.
  IF l_it_taihi[] IS NOT INITIAL.
* 品目テキストの検索
    SELECT matnr   "品目コード
           maktx   "品目テキスト
      FROM makt
      INTO TABLE it_makt
      FOR ALL ENTRIES IN l_it_taihi
     WHERE matnr = l_it_taihi-matnr   "品目コード
       AND spras = sy-langu.          "言語キー

* データが取得出来た場合
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
*   ソート処理
      SORT it_makt BY matnr.   "品目コード
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    " SELECT_MAKT_9100

  IF l_it_9100[] IS NOT INITIAL.
*****Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1 Conversion **begin of change by EJX9007359 2026.05.22  for ATC
*    SELECT matnr meins matkl rmatp magrv
*      FROM mara INTO TABLE it_mara_9100

      " BOC | s180184| MOD-023 | Gap 4276 | 21/07/2017

        l_wa_zaiko-hsdat     = l_wa_lqua-hsdat.     "製造日付

*     品質検査中在庫の場合予定在庫フラグオン
        IF l_wa_lqua1-bestq = c_bestq.
          l_wa_zaiko-flg_y   = c_on.

          CLEAR l_wa_zaiko-jitu_cs.
          l_wa_zaiko-yotei_cs  = l_v_sum.            "予定在庫引当可能数量
          l_wa_zaiko-stck_qrem = l_v_sum.

        ENDIF.

        l_wa_zaiko-verab = l_wa_lqua-verab.         " MOD-026 +
        APPEND l_wa_zaiko TO it_zaiko.
*      APPEND l_wa_zaiko TO it_scheduled.

*     初期化
        CLEAR:

          l_wa_zaiko,

          l_v_sum.   "集計した合計数量

      ENDAT.

    ENDLOOP.

  ENDIF.


*  CLEAR:it_lqua[].
*  LOOP AT it_lqua1 INTO l_wa_lqua1.
*    MOVE-CORRESPONDING l_wa_lqua1 TO l_wa_lqua.
*    APPEND l_wa_lqua TO it_lqua.
*    CLEAR:l_wa_lqua,l_wa_lqua1.
*  ENDLOOP.
*  CLEAR:it_lqua1[].
*EOC MOD-016

** ソート処理
*  SORT it_lqua BY werks        "プラント
*                  lgort        "保管場所
*                  matnr        "品目コード
*                  letyp        "保管単位タイプ
*                  edacode      "枝番
*                  kiboshom     "賞味期限
*                  bestq.       "在庫カテゴリ
*
*  LOOP AT it_lqua INTO l_wa_taihi.
*
**   ブレイク処理のため退避
*    l_wa_lqua = l_wa_taihi.
*
**   合計数量の集計
*    l_v_sum = l_v_sum + l_wa_lqua-gesme.
*
*    AT END OF bestq.  "在庫カテゴリ
*
**     在庫内部テーブルの作成
*      l_wa_zaiko-werks     = l_wa_lqua-werks.     "プラント
*      l_wa_zaiko-lgort     = l_wa_lqua-lgort.     "保管場所
*      l_wa_zaiko-matnr     = l_wa_lqua-matnr.     "品目コード
*      l_wa_zaiko-letyp     = l_wa_lqua-letyp.     "保管単位タイプ
*      l_wa_zaiko-eda       = l_wa_lqua-edacode.   "枝番
*      l_wa_zaiko-kiboshom  = l_wa_lqua-kiboshom.  "賞味期限
*      l_wa_zaiko-menge_cs  = l_v_sum.            "引当可能数量(実在庫＋予定在庫－引当済数量)
*      l_wa_zaiko-jitu_cs   = l_v_sum.            "実在庫引当可能数量(実在庫－引当済数量)
*      l_wa_zaiko-bestq     = l_wa_lqua-bestq.     "在庫カテゴリ
*      PERFORM edit_bestq_txt
*        USING    l_wa_lqua-bestq                  "在庫カテゴリ
*        CHANGING l_wa_zaiko-bestq_txt.            "在庫カテゴリテキスト
*      l_wa_zaiko-charg     = l_wa_lqua-charg.     "ロット番号
*      l_wa_zaiko-hsdat     = l_wa_lqua-hsdat.     "製造日付
*
**     品質検査中在庫の場合予定在庫フラグオン
*      IF l_wa_lqua-bestq = c_bestq.
*        l_wa_zaiko-flg_y   = c_on.
*
*        CLEAR l_wa_zaiko-jitu_cs.
*        l_wa_zaiko-yotei_cs  = l_v_sum.            "予定在庫引当可能数量
*
*      ENDIF.
*
*      APPEND l_wa_zaiko TO it_zaiko.
**      APPEND l_wa_zaiko TO it_scheduled.
*
**     初期化
*      CLEAR:
*
*        l_wa_zaiko,
*
*        l_v_sum.   "集計した合計数量
*
*    ENDAT.
*
*  ENDLOOP.

ENDFORM.                    " SEPARATE_MARC_WM
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_LOT_INFO
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       ロット情報の取得
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->I_MATNR      品目コード
*      -->I_WERKS      プラント
*      -->I_CHARG      ロット番号
*      <--O_wa_MM0001  ロット情報用構造
*      <--O_FLG_ERR    エラーフラグ
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_lot_info  USING    i_matnr     TYPE lqua-matnr
                            i_werks     TYPE lqua-werks
                            i_charg     TYPE lqua-charg
                   CHANGING o_wa_mm0001 TYPE /ccej/slss_lot
                            o_flg_err   TYPE c.

* ロット情報取得
*  CALL FUNCTION 'ZS_ZSMM004_GET_LOT_INFO'
*    EXPORTING
*      im_matnr       = i_matnr       "品目コード
*      im_werks       = i_werks       "プラント
*      im_charg       = i_charg       "ロット番号
*    IMPORTING
*      ex_zssmm0001   = o_wa_mm0001   "ロット情報用構造
*    EXCEPTIONS
*      mcha_not_found = 1
*      bapi_error     = 2
*      OTHERS         = 3.

* ロット情報取得
  CALL FUNCTION '/CCEJ/OTC_GET_LOT_INFO'
    EXPORTING
      im_matnr       = i_matnr       "品目コード
      im_werks       = i_werks       "プラント
      im_charg       = i_charg       "ロット番号
    IMPORTING
      ex_lot_info    = o_wa_mm0001   "ロット情報用構造
    EXCEPTIONS
      mcha_not_found = 1
      bapi_error     = 2
      OTHERS         = 3.



* 取得に失敗した場合エラーフラグオン
  IF sy-subrc <> 0.
    o_flg_err = c_on.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    " GET_LOT_INFO
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GRAYOUT_PARA_HUB
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       入力不可項目の制御(出荷ハブ選択時)
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_grayout_para_hub .

  LOOP AT SCREEN.
    IF screen-name = 'S_LIFNR-LOW'   "仕入先コード
    OR screen-name = 'S_LIFNR-HIGH'  "仕入先コード
    OR screen-name = 'S_BST_B-LOW'   "購買依頼伝票タイプ
    OR screen-name = 'S_BADAT-LOW'   "購買依頼登録日
    OR screen-name = 'S_BADAT-HIGH'  "購買依頼登録日
    OR screen-name = 'S_BANFN-LOW'   "購買依頼伝票番号
    OR screen-name = 'S_BANFN-HIGH'. "購買依頼伝票番号
      screen-input = 0.
      MODIFY SCREEN.
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.

*BOC for MOD-030
*  Batch Check
  PERFORM f_batch_check.   " MOD-026 +
  IF v_valid IS INITIAL.
    LOOP AT SCREEN.
      IF screen-group1 = 'NDY'.
        screen-input = 0.
        screen-invisible = 1.
        MODIFY SCREEN.
      ENDIF.
    ENDLOOP.
  ENDIF.
*ECO for MOD-030

ENDFORM.                    " GRAYOUT_PARA_HUB

*       ステータス画面の設定
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE m_set_status_9200 OUTPUT.

* 初期表示の場合、初期表示ソートボタンを隠す
  IF ( cnt_open = 0 ).
    v_fcode = c_init.
    cnt_open = cnt_open + 1.
  ENDIF.

* 処理件数取得
  DESCRIBE TABLE it_9200 LINES cnt_data.

* ステータス設定
  SET PF-STATUS 'ST02' EXCLUDING v_fcode.
  SET TITLEBAR  'T02' WITH cnt_data.

  CLEAR: sy-ucomm.

ENDMODULE.                 " M_SET_STATUS_9200  OUTPUT
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Module  M_EXIT_COMMAND_9200  INPUT
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       EXITコマンド
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE m_exit_command_9200 INPUT.

* 初期化
  CLEAR:
    v_question,   "出力メッセージ
    v_ans.        "リターンコード

* 出力メッセージの編集(改行考慮)
  v_question+0(38)  = text-s01.    "引当処理を終了します。
  v_question+39(16) = text-s02.    "よろしいですか？

* 確認ポップアップを表示
*「引当処理を終了します。よろしいですか？」
  PERFORM display_popup
    USING    text-s03           "MESSAGE
             v_question        "出力メッセージ
    CHANGING v_ans.            "リターンコード

* ポップアップで「はい」が選択された場合
  IF v_ans = c_ans.
*   初期画面に戻る
    LEAVE TO SCREEN 0.
  ENDIF.

ENDMODULE.                 " M_EXIT_COMMAND_9200  INPUT
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  DISPLAY_POPUP
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       ポップアップ表示
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->I_TITLE     ダイアログボックス表題
*      -->I_QUESTION  ダイアログボックス内の質問テキスト
*      <--O_ANS       リターンコード: '1'、'2'、'A'
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM display_popup  USING    i_title    TYPE c
                             i_question TYPE c
                    CHANGING o_ans      TYPE c.

* 標準ダイアログポップアップ
  CALL FUNCTION 'POPUP_TO_CONFIRM'
    EXPORTING
      titlebar       = i_title      "ダイアログボックス表題
      text_question  = i_question   "質問テキスト
      text_button_1  = text-s04     "はい
      text_button_2  = text-s05     "いいえ
      default_button = c_btn      "カーソル位置
    IMPORTING
      answer         = o_ans        "リターンコード
    EXCEPTIONS
      text_not_found = 1
      OTHERS         = 2.
  IF sy-subrc <> 0.
    MESSAGE ID sy-msgid TYPE sy-msgty NUMBER sy-msgno
            WITH sy-msgv1 sy-msgv2 sy-msgv3 sy-msgv4.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    " DISPLAY_POPUP

  v_matnr  =  wa_9200-matnr.

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Module  M_USER_COMMAND_9200  INPUT
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       ユーザコマンド(9200)
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE m_user_command_9200 INPUT.

  CASE sy-ucomm.
*   ダブルクリック
    WHEN c_pick.
      PERFORM f_command_pick_9200.
*   全選択ボタン押下時
    WHEN c_alsele.
      PERFORM f_command_allselect_9200.
*   全選択解除ボタン押下時
    WHEN c_desele.
      PERFORM f_command_delselect_9200.
*   ソート：初期表示ボタン押下時
    WHEN c_init.
      PERFORM f_command_init_9200.
*   ソート：品目コードボタン押下時
    WHEN c_matnr.
      PERFORM f_command_sort_m_9200.
*   ソート：出荷先コードボタン押下時
    WHEN c_kunnr.
      PERFORM f_command_sort_k_9200.
*   確定ボタン押下時
    WHEN c_exec.
      PERFORM f_command_exec_9200.
    WHEN OTHERS.
  ENDCASE.

ENDMODULE.                 " M_USER_COMMAND_9200  INPUT
