*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  SELECT_T001L
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       出荷プラントに紐付く保管場所取得
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM select_t001l .

* ローカル定義
  DATA:
    l_wa_t001l TYPE ty_t001l,                     "保管場所
    l_it_t001l TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_t001l.   "保管場所

* 初期化
  REFRESH rng_lgort.   "保管場所レンジテーブル

  " BOC | S180184 | MOD-038 | GAP 1346  | 16-Sep-2019
  DATA : l_wa_t320 TYPE ty_t320.
  " EOC | S180184 | MOD-038 | GAP 1346  | 16-Sep-2019


* 保管場所の検索
  SELECT
    werks   "プラント
    lgort   "保管場所
    FROM t001l
    INTO TABLE l_it_t001l
    WHERE werks IN s_werks."werks = p_werks.   "プラント

* 保管場所レンジテーブル作成
  LOOP AT l_it_t001l INTO l_wa_t001l.

    wa_lgort-low    = l_wa_t001l-lgort.
    wa_lgort-sign   = c_sign_i.
    wa_lgort-option = c_opt_eq.
    APPEND wa_lgort TO rng_lgort.


    wa_werks_imwm-low    = l_wa_t001l-werks.
    wa_werks_imwm-sign   = c_sign_i.
    wa_werks_imwm-option = c_opt_eq.

    " BOC | S180184 | MOD-038 | GAP 1346  | 16-Sep-2019
*    READ TABLE it_t320 TRANSPORTING NO FIELDS
    READ TABLE it_t320 INTO l_wa_t320
" EOC | S180184 | MOD-038 | GAP 1346  | 16-Sep-2019
    WITH KEY werks = l_wa_t001l-werks
             lgort = l_wa_t001l-lgort.

    IF sy-subrc = 0.

      " BOC | S180184 | MOD-038 | GAP 1346  | 16-Sep-2019
      READ TABLE i_t340d
      WITH KEY lgnum = l_wa_t320-lgnum
      BINARY SEARCH
      TRANSPORTING NO FIELDS.
      IF sy-subrc IS NOT INITIAL.

        " EOC | S180184 | MOD-038 | GAP 1346  | 16-Sep-2019

        APPEND wa_lgort TO rng_lgort_wm. "WM管理保管場所用レンジテーブル
        APPEND wa_werks_imwm TO rng_werks_wm.
*      APPEND wa_lgort TO rng_lgort_im.
*      APPEND wa_lgort TO rng_lgort_wm.

        " BOC | S180184 | MOD-038 | GAP 1346  | 16-Sep-2019
      ELSE.
        APPEND wa_lgort TO rng_lgort_im. "非WM管理保管場所用レンジテーブル
        APPEND wa_werks_imwm TO rng_werks_im.
        wa_im_data-werks = l_wa_t001l-werks.
        wa_im_data-lgort = l_wa_t001l-lgort.
        APPEND wa_im_data TO i_im_data.
        CLEAR wa_im_data.
      ENDIF.
      " EOC | S180184 | MOD-038 | GAP 1346  | 16-Sep-2019
    ELSE.
      APPEND wa_lgort TO rng_lgort_im. "非WM管理保管場所用レンジテーブル
      APPEND wa_werks_imwm TO rng_werks_im.
      wa_im_data-werks = l_wa_t001l-werks.
      wa_im_data-lgort = l_wa_t001l-lgort.
      APPEND wa_im_data TO i_im_data.
      CLEAR wa_im_data.
*      APPEND wa_lgort TO rng_lgort_wm.
    ENDIF.

  ENDLOOP.

  SORT rng_werks_wm.
  DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM rng_werks_wm COMPARING ALL FIELDS.

  SORT rng_werks_im.
  DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM rng_werks_im COMPARING ALL FIELDS.

ENDFORM.                    " SELECT_T001L
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  SELECT_KNA1_F
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       出荷先名称の取得
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->I_KUNNR       出荷先
*      <--O_KUNNR_TXT   出荷先名称
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM select_kna1_f  USING    i_kunnr     TYPE kna1-kunnr
                    CHANGING o_kunnr_txt TYPE c.


** ローカル定義
*  DATA:
*    L_v_KUNNR TYPE KNA1-KUNNR.   "出荷先コード
*
** 前ゼロ付与
*  PERFORM EXIT_ALPHA_INPUT
*    USING    I_KUNNR       "出荷先
*    CHANGING L_v_KUNNR.   "出荷先


* 得意先マスタ: 一般データの検索
  SELECT SINGLE
         name1   "名称１
    FROM kna1
    INTO o_kunnr_txt

*   WHERE KUNNR = L_v_KUNNR.   "得意先コード
   WHERE kunnr = i_kunnr.      "得意先コード


ENDFORM.                    " SELECT_KNA1_F
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  CHANGE_DATA_9100
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       出荷伝票登録データ編集
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM change_data_9100 .

  LOOP AT it_9100 INTO wa_9100
                  WHERE icon = v_icon_checked.   "信号機
    APPEND wa_9100 TO it_ins_9100.
  ENDLOOP.

ENDFORM.                    " CHANGE_DATA_9100
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Module  M_SET_STATUS_9400  OUTPUT
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       ステータス画面の設定
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE m_set_status_9400 OUTPUT.

* Dynpro出力中止
  SUPPRESS DIALOG.

* GUIステータス・タイトル設定
  SET PF-STATUS 'ST04'.
  SET TITLEBAR  'T04'.

* Dynproからレポート処理に遷移
*  LEAVE TO LIST-PROCESSING AND RETURN TO SCREEN 9400.

* 在庫一覧画面データ編集

  IF v_valid IS INITIAL."MOD-030
    PERFORM edit_9400.
  ELSEIF v_valid = c_on."MOD-030
    PERFORM edit_9400_batch."MOD-030
  ENDIF.

* *--- CCW･四国･仙台
  IF v_routine_001 = c_kosha.
    PERFORM edit_9400_rtn_001.
* *--- CCW･四国･仙台以外
  ELSE.
    IF v_valid IS INITIAL."MOD-030
      PERFORM edit_9400.
    ELSEIF v_valid = c_on."MOD-030
      PERFORM edit_9400_batch."MOD-030
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.


* 在庫一覧画面出力
  PERFORM write_9400.

* 画面終了
  SET SCREEN 0.
  LEAVE SCREEN.

ENDMODULE.                 " M_SET_STATUS_9400  OUTPUT
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  WRITE_9400
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       在庫一覧画面出力
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM write_9400 .

  DATA: l_i_fieldcat  TYPE slis_t_fieldcat_alv,
        l_wa_fiedcat TYPE slis_fieldcat_alv,
  l_v_layout    TYPE slis_layout_alv,
  l_i_stk TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF /ccej/slss_stock,
  l_wa_stk TYPE /ccej/slss_stock,
  l_v_title TYPE string .

  CLEAR:i_stk[].

* 在庫データがない場合も一覧画面を表示する
  IF it_9400 IS INITIAL.
    WRITE / space.
    RETURN.
  ENDIF.


  LOOP AT it_9400 INTO wa_9400.

    CLEAR:wa_jscale.
    READ TABLE i_jscale INTO wa_jscale WITH KEY matnr = wa_9400-matnr BINARY SEARCH.
    IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
      l_wa_stk-jscalecode = wa_jscale-jscale_code.
    ENDIF.

    PERFORM exit_matn1_output
                             USING     wa_9400-matnr
                             CHANGING  wa_9400-matnr.

*    WRITE:/001(018) wa_9400-matnr,       "品目コード
*           021(004) wa_9400-edacode,     "枝番
*           027(010) wa_9400-syomi,       "賞味期限
*           039(019) wa_9400-zaiko_suu,   "在庫数
*           060(019) wa_9400-zan_suu,     "引当残数
*           081(006) wa_9400-bestq_txt,   "STATE
*           089(012) wa_9400-werks,       "出荷プラント
*           103(012) wa_9400-lgort.       "出荷保管場所
    l_wa_stk-matnr = wa_9400-matnr.
    l_wa_stk-subcode = wa_9400-edacode.
    l_wa_stk-bbe = wa_9400-syomi.
    l_wa_stk-invstk = wa_9400-zaiko_suu.
    l_wa_stk-remstk = wa_9400-zan_suu.
    l_wa_stk-state = wa_9400-bestq_txt.
    l_wa_stk-werks = wa_9400-werks.
    l_wa_stk-lgort = wa_9400-lgort.
    l_wa_stk-charg = wa_9400-charg."MOD-029

    " BOC | MOD-026 | S420011 | Gap 1000000917
    l_wa_stk-hsdat = wa_9400-hsdat.
    l_wa_stk-verab = wa_9400-verab.
    IF l_wa_stk-verab IS NOT INITIAL.
      IF l_wa_stk-verab GT sy-datlo.
        l_wa_stk-freiz = l_wa_stk-verab - sy-datlo.
      ELSE.
        l_wa_stk-freiz = 0.
      ENDIF.
    ELSE.
      CLEAR l_wa_stk-freiz.
    ENDIF.
    " EOC | MOD-026 | S420011 | Gap 1000000917

    APPEND l_wa_stk TO i_stk.
    CLEAR l_wa_stk.


  ENDLOOP.


  CALL FUNCTION 'REUSE_ALV_FIELDCATALOG_MERGE'
    EXPORTING
      i_structure_name       = '/CCEJ/SLSS_STOCK'
    CHANGING
      ct_fieldcat            = l_i_fieldcat
    EXCEPTIONS
      inconsistent_interface = 1
      program_error          = 2
      OTHERS                 = 3.
  IF sy-subrc <> 0.
    MESSAGE ID sy-msgid TYPE sy-msgty NUMBER sy-msgno
            WITH sy-msgv1 sy-msgv2 sy-msgv3 sy-msgv4.
  ENDIF.
  LOOP AT l_i_fieldcat INTO l_wa_fiedcat.
    IF l_wa_fiedcat-fieldname = 'SUBCODE'.
      l_wa_fiedcat-seltext_l = text-h02.
      l_wa_fiedcat-seltext_m = text-h02.
      l_wa_fiedcat-seltext_s = text-h02.

    ENDIF.

*   BOC for MOD-029
    IF l_wa_fiedcat-fieldname = 'BATCH'.
      l_wa_fiedcat-edit = c_on.
      l_wa_fiedcat-checkbox = c_on.
      l_wa_fiedcat-input = c_on.
*      l_wa_fiedcat-seltext_l = text-h02.
*      l_wa_fiedcat-seltext_m = text-h02.
*      l_wa_fiedcat-seltext_s = text-h02.
    ENDIF.
*   EOC for MOD-029

    IF l_wa_fiedcat-fieldname = 'BBE'.
      l_wa_fiedcat-seltext_l = text-h03.
      l_wa_fiedcat-seltext_m = text-h03.
      l_wa_fiedcat-seltext_s = text-h03.

    ENDIF.
    IF l_wa_fiedcat-fieldname = 'INVSTK'.
      l_wa_fiedcat-seltext_l = text-h04.
      l_wa_fiedcat-seltext_m = text-h04.
      l_wa_fiedcat-seltext_s = text-h04.

    ENDIF.
    IF l_wa_fiedcat-fieldname = 'REMSTK'.
      l_wa_fiedcat-seltext_l = text-h05.
      l_wa_fiedcat-seltext_m = text-h05.
      l_wa_fiedcat-seltext_s = text-h05.

    ENDIF.
    IF l_wa_fiedcat-fieldname = 'STATE'.
      l_wa_fiedcat-seltext_l = text-h06.
      l_wa_fiedcat-seltext_m = text-h06.
      l_wa_fiedcat-seltext_s = text-h06.
      l_wa_fiedcat-outputlen = 25.
    ENDIF.
    IF l_wa_fiedcat-fieldname = 'WERKS'.
      l_wa_fiedcat-seltext_l = text-h07.
      l_wa_fiedcat-seltext_m = text-h07.
      l_wa_fiedcat-seltext_s = text-h07.
      l_wa_fiedcat-outputlen = 15.

    ENDIF.
    IF l_wa_fiedcat-fieldname = 'LGORT'.
      l_wa_fiedcat-seltext_l = text-h08.
      l_wa_fiedcat-seltext_m = text-h08.
      l_wa_fiedcat-seltext_s = text-h08.
      l_wa_fiedcat-outputlen = 15.
    ENDIF.

    IF l_wa_fiedcat-fieldname = 'JSCALECODE'.
      l_wa_fiedcat-seltext_l = text-h09.
      l_wa_fiedcat-seltext_m = text-h09.
      l_wa_fiedcat-seltext_s = text-h09.
      l_wa_fiedcat-outputlen = 15.
    ENDIF.

    " BOC | MOD-026 | S420011 | Gap 1000000917
    IF v_valid IS INITIAL.
      IF l_wa_fiedcat-fieldname = c_verab OR
         l_wa_fiedcat-fieldname = c_hsdat OR
         l_wa_fiedcat-fieldname = c_freiz OR
         l_wa_fiedcat-fieldname = c_charg OR
        l_wa_fiedcat-fieldname = c_batch.
        l_wa_fiedcat-no_out = abap_true.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
    " EOC | MOD-026 | S420011 | Gap 1000000917

    MODIFY l_i_fieldcat FROM l_wa_fiedcat.
    CLEAR l_wa_fiedcat.
  ENDLOOP.

  l_v_title = text-017.

  CALL FUNCTION 'REUSE_ALV_POPUP_TO_SELECT'
    EXPORTING
      i_title               = l_v_title
*     i_selection           = ' '
      i_tabname             = 'I_STK'
      i_screen_start_column = 2
      i_screen_start_line   = 2
      i_screen_end_column   = 160
      i_screen_end_line     = 12
      i_checkbox_fieldname  = 'CHECK'
*     i_structure_name      = '/CCEJ/SLSS_STOCK'
      it_fieldcat           = l_i_fieldcat
    TABLES
      t_outtab              = i_stk
    EXCEPTIONS
      program_error         = 1
      OTHERS                = 2.



  PERFORM f_replace_material.

ENDFORM.                    " WRITE_9400
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  WRITE_9400_HEADER
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       在庫一覧のヘッダ部出力
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_write_9400_header .

* 在庫一覧画面が呼び出された場合に当処理を行う
  CHECK sy-dynnr = c_dynnr_9400.

  FORMAT COLOR 1.
  WRITE:/001(018) text-h01,   "品目コード
         021(004) text-h02,   "枝番
         027(010) text-h03,   "賞味期限
         039(019) text-h04,   "在庫数
         060(019) text-h05,   "引当残数
         081(006) text-h06,   "STATE
         089(012) text-h07,   "出荷プラント
         103(012) text-h08.   "出荷保管場所
  FORMAT RESET.

ENDFORM.                    " WRITE_9400_HEADER
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  EDIT_9400
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       在庫一覧画面データ編集
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM edit_9400_batch.

* ローカル定義
  DATA:
   l_it_sum       TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_sum,   "合算テーブル
   l_it_taihi     TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_zaiko, "退避用テーブル
   l_wa_sum       TYPE ty_sum,                     "合算テーブル
   l_wa_zaiko     TYPE ty_zaiko,                   "在庫内部テーブル
   l_wa_taihi     TYPE ty_zaiko,                   "退避用テーブル
   l_wa_mara      TYPE ty_mara_9100,               "品目マスタ
   l_v_zaiko     TYPE lqua-gesme,                 "在庫数量合計
   l_v_hiki      TYPE lqua-gesme,                 "引当数量合計
   l_v_zaiko_suu TYPE lqua-gesme,                 "在庫数
   l_v_matnr     TYPE mara-matnr,                 "品目コード
* Begin of changes by S204711 for defect 9000001705        MOD-027
   l_i_zaiko     TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_tzaiko,
   l_wa_tzaiko   TYPE ty_tzaiko,
   l_wa_ztaihi   TYPE ty_tzaiko.
* End of changes by S204711 for defect 9000001705        MOD-027
  " BOC | MOD-016 | S180184 | Gap 3389 | Subcode changes | 29/07/2016
  DATA :l_wa_jscale TYPE ty_jscalecode.
  " EOC | MOD-016 | S180184 | Gap 3389 | Subcode changes | 29/07/2016

* ソート処理
  SORT it_zaiko BY matnr      "品目コード
                   werks      "プラント
                   lgort      "保管場所
                   eda        "枝番
                   kiboshom   "賞味期限
                   hsdat      "MOD-030
                   verab      " MOD-026 +
                   letyp.     "保管単位タイプ
* Begin of changes by S204711 for defect 9000001705        MOD-027
** 品目コード/プラント/保管場所/枝番／賞味期限単位で数量合算
*  LOOP AT it_zaiko INTO l_wa_taihi.
*    l_wa_zaiko = l_wa_taihi.
*
**   数量合算(品質検査中在庫、過去の賞味期限、予定在庫は除く)
*    IF  l_wa_zaiko-bestq <> c_bestq_q
**    AND l_wa_zaiko-kiboshom >= wa_9100-syomi
*    AND l_wa_zaiko-flg_y IS INITIAL.
*      l_v_zaiko = l_v_zaiko + l_wa_zaiko-menge_cs.   "在庫数量合計
*      l_v_hiki  = l_v_hiki  + l_wa_zaiko-sumi_cs.    "引当数量合計
*    ENDIF.
*
**   AT END OF kiboshom.  " MOD-026 -
*    AT END OF verab.     " MOD-026 +
*      l_wa_sum-matnr    = l_wa_zaiko-matnr.          "品目コード
*      l_wa_sum-werks    = l_wa_zaiko-werks.          "プラント
*      l_wa_sum-lgort    = l_wa_zaiko-lgort.          "保管場所
*      l_wa_sum-eda      = l_wa_zaiko-eda.            "枝番
*      l_wa_sum-kiboshom = l_wa_zaiko-kiboshom.       "賞味期限
*      l_wa_sum-verab    = l_wa_zaiko-verab.          " MOD-026 +
*      l_wa_sum-zaiko_cs = l_v_zaiko.                "在庫数量合計
*      l_wa_sum-hiki_cs  = l_v_hiki.                 "引当数量合計
*      APPEND l_wa_sum TO l_it_sum.
*
**     初期化
*      CLEAR:
*        l_v_zaiko,   "在庫数量合計
*        l_v_hiki.    "引当数量合計
*    ENDAT.
*
*  ENDLOOP.
  CLEAR: l_i_zaiko[].
  LOOP AT it_zaiko INTO l_wa_taihi.
    MOVE-CORRESPONDING l_wa_taihi TO l_wa_ztaihi.
    APPEND l_wa_ztaihi TO l_i_zaiko.
    CLEAR: l_wa_ztaihi.
  ENDLOOP.
  IF l_i_zaiko[] IS NOT INITIAL.
    SORT l_i_zaiko BY matnr      "品目コード
                     werks      "プラント
                     lgort      "保管場所
                     eda        "枝番
                     kiboshom   "賞味期限
                     hsdat "MOD-030
                     verab      " MOD-026 +
                     letyp.     "保管単位タイプ

* 品目コード/プラント/保管場所/枝番／賞味期限単位で数量合算
    LOOP AT l_i_zaiko INTO l_wa_ztaihi.
      l_wa_tzaiko = l_wa_ztaihi.

*   数量合算(品質検査中在庫、過去の賞味期限、予定在庫は除く)
      IF  l_wa_tzaiko-bestq <> c_bestq_q
*    AND l_wa_zaiko-kiboshom >= wa_9100-syomi
      AND l_wa_tzaiko-flg_y IS INITIAL.
        l_v_zaiko = l_v_zaiko + l_wa_tzaiko-menge_cs.   "在庫数量合計
        l_v_hiki  = l_v_hiki  + l_wa_tzaiko-sumi_cs.    "引当数量合計
      ENDIF.

      AT END OF verab.     " MOD-026 +
        l_wa_sum-matnr    = l_wa_tzaiko-matnr.          "品目コード
        l_wa_sum-werks    = l_wa_tzaiko-werks.          "プラント
        l_wa_sum-lgort    = l_wa_tzaiko-lgort.          "保管場所
        l_wa_sum-eda      = l_wa_tzaiko-eda.            "枝番
        l_wa_sum-kiboshom = l_wa_tzaiko-kiboshom.       "賞味期限
        l_wa_sum-verab    = l_wa_tzaiko-verab.          " MOD-026 +
        l_wa_sum-hsdat    = l_wa_tzaiko-hsdat.          " MOD-030 +
        l_wa_sum-zaiko_cs = l_v_zaiko.                "在庫数量合計
        l_wa_sum-hiki_cs  = l_v_hiki.                 "引当数量合計
        APPEND l_wa_sum TO l_it_sum.

*     初期化
        CLEAR:
          l_v_zaiko,   "在庫数量合計
          l_v_hiki.    "引当数量合計
      ENDAT.

    ENDLOOP.
    CLEAR: l_wa_tzaiko, l_wa_ztaihi.
  ENDIF.
* End of changes by S204711 for defect 9000001705        MOD-027
* データ退避
  l_it_taihi = it_zaiko.

* 品質検査中在庫は非表示
  DELETE l_it_taihi
    WHERE bestq = c_bestq_q.
* Begin of changes by S204711 for defect 9000001705        MOD-027
* Delete the data based on flag since it has not been considered later
  DELETE l_it_taihi WHERE flg_y = c_on.
* End of changes by S204711 for defect 9000001705        MOD-027
* 在庫カテゴリありなしのデータが混在することは業務的にない想定だが
* 重複データがあれば削除する
  DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM l_it_taihi
    COMPARING matnr      "品目コード
              werks      "プラント
              lgort      "保管場所
              eda        "枝番
              kiboshom  "賞味期限
              hsdat    "MOD-030
              verab.    " MOD-026 +


* ソート処理
  SORT l_it_sum BY     matnr      "品目コード
                       werks      "プラント
                       lgort      "保管場所
                       eda        "枝番
                       kiboshom  "賞味期限
                       hsdat    "MOD-030
                       verab     ." MOD-026 +

  SORT it_mara_9100 BY matnr.     "品目コード

* 初期化
  REFRESH it_9400.

* 編集処理
  LOOP AT l_it_taihi INTO l_wa_taihi.
    l_wa_zaiko = l_wa_taihi.

*   品目コードが変わるたびに基本数量単位取得
    AT NEW matnr.
      READ TABLE it_mara_9100 INTO l_wa_mara BINARY SEARCH
        WITH KEY matnr = l_wa_zaiko-matnr.   "品目コード
    ENDAT.


**   前ゼロ付与
*    PERFORM EXIT_MATN1_INPUT
*      USING    WA_9100-MATNR   "品目コード
*      CHANGING L_v_MATNR.     "品目コード
    l_v_matnr = wa_9100-matnr.


*   品目コード、枝番が一致しない在庫は非表示
*Check Replace Material----
    CHECK l_wa_zaiko-matnr = l_v_matnr
" Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_APS Conversion **BEGIN OF CHANGE BY EJX9007359 11.06.2026  for ATC
*       AND l_wa_zaiko-eda = wa_9100-edacode.
      AND l_wa_zaiko-eda = wa_9100-edacode. "#EC CI_FLDEXT_OK[0002250183]
" Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_APS Conversion * *END OF CHANGE BY EJX9007359 11.06.2026 for ATC

*   在庫の賞味期限が一覧の賞味期限より前の場合は非表示
*    IF  wa_9100-syomi <> c_syomi_0
*    AND l_wa_zaiko-kiboshom < wa_9100-syomi AND wa_9100-syomi IS NOT INITIAL.
*      CONTINUE.
*    ENDIF.

*   MARDに対応するため賞味期限が初期値の場合は賞味期限が初期値以外の在庫は非表示
*    IF  wa_9100-syomi = c_syomi_0
*    AND l_wa_zaiko-kiboshom <> c_syomi_0 AND wa_9100-syomi IS NOT INITIAL.
*      CONTINUE.
*    ENDIF.

*   引当残数の読み込み
    READ TABLE l_it_sum INTO l_wa_sum BINARY SEARCH
      WITH KEY matnr    = l_wa_zaiko-matnr      "品目コード
               werks    = l_wa_zaiko-werks      "プラント
               lgort    = l_wa_zaiko-lgort      "保管場所
               eda      = l_wa_zaiko-eda        "枝番
               kiboshom = l_wa_zaiko-kiboshom   "賞味期限
               hsdat    = l_wa_zaiko-hsdat     " MOD-030 +
               verab    = l_wa_zaiko-verab.     " MOD-026 +

*   引当数量より引当残数が小さい在庫は非表示
*BOC for MOD-025
*    CHECK l_wa_sum-zaiko_cs >= wa_9100-menge_cs."Check
    CHECK l_wa_sum-zaiko_cs <> 0.
*EOC for MOD-025

    wa_9400-matnr     = l_wa_zaiko-matnr.      "品目コード
    wa_9400-edacode   = l_wa_zaiko-eda.        "枝番
    wa_9400-syomi     = l_wa_zaiko-kiboshom.   "賞味期限
    l_v_zaiko_suu = l_wa_sum-zaiko_cs
                   + l_wa_sum-hiki_cs.
    WRITE l_v_zaiko_suu UNIT l_wa_mara-meins  "在庫数
        TO  wa_9400-zaiko_suu.

*    l_wa_sum-zaiko_cs = l_v_zaiko_suu - wa_9100-menge_cs.
*    CHECK l_wa_sum-zaiko_cs GE 0.

    WRITE l_wa_sum-zaiko_cs UNIT l_wa_mara-meins   "引当残数
        TO  wa_9400-zan_suu.
    wa_9400-bestq     = l_wa_zaiko-bestq.      "在庫カテゴリ
    wa_9400-bestq_txt = l_wa_zaiko-bestq_txt.  "在庫カテゴリテキスト
    wa_9400-werks     = l_wa_zaiko-werks.      "プラント
    wa_9400-lgort     = l_wa_zaiko-lgort.      "保管場所
    wa_9400-hsdat     = l_wa_zaiko-hsdat.      " MOD-026 +
    wa_9400-verab     = l_wa_zaiko-verab.      " MOD-026 +
    wa_9400-charg     = l_wa_zaiko-charg.      " MOD-029 +
    APPEND wa_9400 TO it_9400.

    CLEAR wa_9400.

  ENDLOOP.
  " BOC | MOD-016 | S180184 | Gap 3389 | Subcode changes | 29/07/2016
  " get the stock for new subcode materials as well

  " check if material is with new approach
  CLEAR l_wa_jscale.
  READ TABLE i_jscale INTO l_wa_jscale
  WITH KEY matnr = wa_9100-matnr
  .

  IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL .
    IF l_wa_jscale-zeivr IS INITIAL.
      " EOC | MOD-001 | S180184 | Gap 3389 | Subcode changes | 29/07/2016
*Add all Jscale Code  relavant material and show in stock search
      LOOP AT i_jscale INTO wa_jscale WHERE jscale_code = wa_9100-jscale_code AND matnr <> wa_9100-matnr.
        LOOP AT l_it_taihi INTO l_wa_taihi WHERE matnr = wa_jscale-matnr AND flg_y <> c_on..
          l_wa_zaiko = l_wa_taihi.

          AT NEW matnr.
            READ TABLE it_mara_9100 INTO l_wa_mara BINARY SEARCH
              WITH KEY matnr = l_wa_zaiko-matnr.   "品目コード
          ENDAT.

          l_v_matnr = wa_jscale-matnr.

*   在庫の賞味期限が一覧の賞味期限より前の場合は非表示
*      IF  wa_9100-syomi <> c_syomi_0
*      AND l_wa_zaiko-kiboshom < wa_9100-syomi AND wa_9100-syomi IS NOT INITIAL.
*        CONTINUE.
*      ENDIF.

*   MARDに対応するため賞味期限が初期値の場合は賞味期限が初期値以外の在庫は非表示
*      IF  wa_9100-syomi = c_syomi_0
*      AND l_wa_zaiko-kiboshom <> c_syomi_0 AND wa_9100-syomi IS NOT INITIAL.
*        CONTINUE.
*      ENDIF.

*   引当残数の読み込み
          READ TABLE l_it_sum INTO l_wa_sum BINARY SEARCH
            WITH KEY matnr    = l_wa_zaiko-matnr      "品目コード
                     werks    = l_wa_zaiko-werks      "プラント
                     lgort    = l_wa_zaiko-lgort      "保管場所
                     eda      = l_wa_zaiko-eda        "枝番
                     kiboshom = l_wa_zaiko-kiboshom   "賞味期限
                     hsdat    = l_wa_zaiko-hsdat     " MOD-030 +
                     verab    = l_wa_zaiko-verab.     " MOD-026 +

*   引当数量より引当残数が小さい在庫は非表示
*BOC for MOD-025
*          CHECK l_wa_sum-zaiko_cs >= wa_9100-menge_cs."Check
          CHECK l_wa_sum-zaiko_cs <> 0.
*EOC for MOD-025
          wa_9400-matnr     = l_wa_zaiko-matnr.      "品目コード
          wa_9400-edacode   = l_wa_zaiko-eda.        "枝番
          wa_9400-syomi     = l_wa_zaiko-kiboshom.   "賞味期限
          l_v_zaiko_suu = l_wa_sum-zaiko_cs
                         + l_wa_sum-hiki_cs.

          wa_9400-bestq_txt = l_wa_zaiko-bestq_txt.  "在庫カテゴリテキスト
          wa_9400-werks     = l_wa_zaiko-werks.      "プラント
          wa_9400-lgort     = l_wa_zaiko-lgort.      "保管場所

          wa_9400-verab     = l_wa_zaiko-verab.      " MOD-026 +
          wa_9400-charg     = l_wa_zaiko-charg.      " MOD-029 +
          APPEND wa_9400 TO it_9400.

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  EDIT_9400
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       在庫一覧画面データ編集
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM edit_9400 .

* ローカル定義
  DATA:
   l_it_sum       TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_sum,   "合算テーブル
   l_it_taihi     TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_zaiko, "退避用テーブル
   l_wa_sum       TYPE ty_sum,                     "合算テーブル
   l_wa_zaiko     TYPE ty_zaiko,                   "在庫内部テーブル
   l_wa_taihi     TYPE ty_zaiko,                   "退避用テーブル
   l_wa_mara      TYPE ty_mara_9100,               "品目マスタ
   l_v_zaiko     TYPE lqua-gesme,                 "在庫数量合計
   l_v_hiki      TYPE lqua-gesme,                 "引当数量合計
   l_v_zaiko_suu TYPE lqua-gesme,                 "在庫数
   l_v_matnr     TYPE mara-matnr.                 "品目コード
  " BOC | MOD-016 | S180184 | Gap 3389 | Subcode changes | 29/07/2016
  DATA :l_wa_jscale TYPE ty_jscalecode.
  " EOC | MOD-016 | S180184 | Gap 3389 | Subcode changes | 29/07/2016

* ソート処理
  SORT it_zaiko BY matnr      "品目コード
                   werks      "プラント
                   lgort      "保管場所
                   eda        "枝番
                   kiboshom   "賞味期限
                   letyp.     "保管単位タイプ

* 品目コード/プラント/保管場所/枝番／賞味期限単位で数量合算
  LOOP AT it_zaiko INTO l_wa_taihi.
    l_wa_zaiko = l_wa_taihi.

*   数量合算(品質検査中在庫、過去の賞味期限、予定在庫は除く)
    IF  l_wa_zaiko-bestq <> c_bestq_q
*    AND l_wa_zaiko-kiboshom >= wa_9100-syomi
    AND l_wa_zaiko-flg_y IS INITIAL.
      l_v_zaiko = l_v_zaiko + l_wa_zaiko-menge_cs.   "在庫数量合計
      l_v_hiki  = l_v_hiki  + l_wa_zaiko-sumi_cs.    "引当数量合計
    ENDIF.

    AT END OF kiboshom.
      l_wa_sum-matnr    = l_wa_zaiko-matnr.          "品目コード
      l_wa_sum-werks    = l_wa_zaiko-werks.          "プラント
      l_wa_sum-lgort    = l_wa_zaiko-lgort.          "保管場所
      l_wa_sum-eda      = l_wa_zaiko-eda.            "枝番
      l_wa_sum-kiboshom = l_wa_zaiko-kiboshom.       "賞味期限
      l_wa_sum-zaiko_cs = l_v_zaiko.                "在庫数量合計
      l_wa_sum-hiki_cs  = l_v_hiki.                 "引当数量合計
      APPEND l_wa_sum TO l_it_sum.

*     初期化
      CLEAR:
        l_v_zaiko,   "在庫数量合計
        l_v_hiki.    "引当数量合計
    ENDAT.

  ENDLOOP.

* データ退避
  l_it_taihi = it_zaiko.

* 品質検査中在庫は非表示
  DELETE l_it_taihi
    WHERE bestq = c_bestq_q.

* 在庫カテゴリありなしのデータが混在することは業務的にない想定だが
* 重複データがあれば削除する
  DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM l_it_taihi
    COMPARING matnr      "品目コード
              werks      "プラント
              lgort      "保管場所
              eda        "枝番
              kiboshom.  "賞味期限

* ソート処理
  SORT l_it_sum BY     matnr      "品目コード
                       werks      "プラント
                       lgort      "保管場所
                       eda        "枝番
                       kiboshom.  "賞味期限
  SORT it_mara_9100 BY matnr.     "品目コード

* 初期化
  REFRESH it_9400.

* 編集処理
  LOOP AT l_it_taihi INTO l_wa_taihi.
    l_wa_zaiko = l_wa_taihi.

*   品目コードが変わるたびに基本数量単位取得
    AT NEW matnr.
      READ TABLE it_mara_9100 INTO l_wa_mara BINARY SEARCH
        WITH KEY matnr = l_wa_zaiko-matnr.   "品目コード
    ENDAT.

*    PERFORM EXIT_MATN1_INPUT

    l_v_matnr = wa_9100-matnr.

    CHECK l_wa_zaiko-matnr = l_v_matnr

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  MODIFY_BAPIMSG
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       処理メッセージの更新(BAPIエラー)
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM modify_bapimsg .

* ローカル定義
  DATA:
    l_v_vbeln TYPE vbap-vbeln,    "伝票番号
    l_tabix    TYPE sy-tabix.      "内部テーブルの索引

* BAPIのエラーが1件でもある場合に下記の処理を行う
  CHECK flg_err = c_on.

  LOOP AT it_9100 INTO wa_9100.

    l_tabix = sy-tabix.


**   伝票番号前ゼロ付与
*    PERFORM EXIT_ALPHA_INPUT
*      USING    WA_9100-VBELN     "伝票番号
*      CHANGING L_v_VBELN.       "伝票番号
    l_v_vbeln = wa_9100-vbeln.


    READ TABLE it_mod
          INTO wa_mod
      WITH KEY number  = l_v_vbeln
               itemnum = wa_9100-posnr.
    IF ( sy-subrc = 0 ).
      CLEAR : wa_9100-check.
      wa_9100-message = wa_mod-message.
    ELSE.
      CLEAR : wa_9100-check.
    ENDIF.
    MODIFY it_9100
      FROM wa_9100
     INDEX l_tabix.
  ENDLOOP.

ENDFORM.                    " MODIFY_BAPIMSG
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  CHECK_HIKITEDATA
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       引当済データの存在チェック
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM check_hikitedata .

* 初期化
  CLEAR cnt_data.

* 信号機がチェック記号のデータをチェックする

*  LOOP AT IT_INS_9100 INTO WA_9100
  LOOP AT it_ins_9100 TRANSPORTING NO FIELDS

                WHERE icon = v_icon_checked.   "信号機
    cnt_data = cnt_data + 1.
    EXIT.
  ENDLOOP.

* チェック記号のデータがない場合
  IF cnt_data = 0.
*  「引当済データが存在しません」
    MESSAGE s055(/ccej/otc_atp) WITH text-s18.   "引当済データ
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    " CHECK_HIKITEDATA
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  EDIT_EDACODE
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       枝番マスタの存在チェック
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      <--O_WA_9100  引当対象データ
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM edit_edacode  CHANGING o_wa_9100 TYPE ty_9100.

* ローカル定義
  DATA:
    l_it_zmm88   TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_zmm88,   "枝番マスタ
    l_wa_zmm88   TYPE ty_zmm88,                     "枝番マスタ
    l_v_zaisitu TYPE char3."NA zmm58-zaisitu.             "材質

  DATA :
    l_v_lifnr   TYPE lifnr."NA zmm58-lifnr.


* 枝番が初期値の場合
  IF o_wa_9100-edacode IS INITIAL.


*   *--- 出荷元ﾌﾟﾗﾝﾄを得意先の型(前ｾﾞﾛあり10桁)に変換
    CALL FUNCTION 'CONVERSION_EXIT_ALPHA_INPUT'
      EXPORTING
        input  = p_werks
      IMPORTING
        output = l_v_lifnr.

*   材質マスタ存在チェック
*------------------NA---------------------
*    SELECT SINGLE
*           zaisitu   "材質
*      FROM zmm58
*      INTO l_v_zaisitu

**     WHERE LIFNR = P_WERKS           "得意先コード１
*     WHERE lifnr = l_v_lifnr        " 得意先

*       AND matnr = o_WA_9100-matnr.  "品目コード
*
**   データが取得出来た場合
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
*
**     枝番マスタ存在チェック
*      SELECT youki   "容器種別（材質）
*             eda     "枝番
*        FROM zmm88
*        INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE l_it_zmm88
*       WHERE matnr     = o_WA_9100-matnr   "品目コード
*         AND youki     = l_v_zaisitu      "容器種別（材質）
*         AND promsyu1 IN s_pack            "プロモ種別（オンパック）
*         AND promsyu2 IN s_seal            "プロモ種別（シール）
*         AND promsyu3 IN s_dsgn            "プロモ種別（デザイン）
*         AND date_sta <= sy-datlo          "有効開始日
*         AND date_end >= sy-datlo          "有効終了日
*         AND zdelflg   = space.            "削除フラグ

*     データが取得出来た場合
      IF sy-subrc = 0.

*       ソート処理
        SORT l_it_zmm88 BY eda.   "枝番

*       複数のレコードが取得された場合、番号の小さい枝番を保持する
        READ TABLE l_it_zmm88 INTO l_wa_zmm88 INDEX 1.

        o_wa_9100-youki   = l_wa_zmm88-youki.   "容器種別（材質）
        o_wa_9100-edacode = l_wa_zmm88-eda.     "枝番

      ENDIF.

    ENDIF.

* 枝番が初期値以外の場合
  ELSE.

*   材質の取得
*    SELECT youki   "容器種別（材質）
*      FROM zmm88
*      INTO o_WA_9100-youki
*      UP TO 1 ROWS
*     WHERE matnr     = o_WA_9100-matnr   "品目コード
*       AND eda       = o_WA_9100-edacode "枝番
*       AND promsyu1 IN s_pack            "プロモ種別（オンパック）
*       AND promsyu2 IN s_seal            "プロモ種別（シール）
*       AND promsyu3 IN s_dsgn            "プロモ種別（デザイン）
*       AND date_sta <= sy-datlo          "有効開始日
*       AND date_end >= sy-datlo          "有効終了日
*       AND zdelflg   = space.
*    ENDSELECT.
*------------------NA---------------------
  ENDIF.


** 枝番が存在しない場合
*  IF SY-SUBRC <> 0.
*
**   処理メッセージの編集
**  「枝番 XXX が存在しません」
*    MESSAGE E074(/ccej/otc_atp)
*      WITH TEXT-E02            "枝番
*           O_WA_9100-EDACODE   "一覧画面の枝番
*      INTO O_WA_9100-MESSAGE.
*
**   信号機の編集
*    O_WA_9100-ICON     = v_ICON_RED.     "信号機
*    PERFORM GET_SORT_ICON  USING    O_WA_9100-ICON
*                           CHANGING O_WA_9100-SORT_ICON.
*    O_WA_9100-STATUS   = TEXT-010.        "処理ステータス
*    PERFORM GET_SORT_STAUS USING    O_WA_9100-STATUS
*                           CHANGING O_WA_9100-SORT_STA.
*
*  ENDIF.


ENDFORM.                    " EDIT_EDACODE

*       引当対象外プロモ種別の考慮
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->I_MATNR    品目コード
*      -->I_EDACODE  枝番
*      <--O_FLG_ERR  エラーフラグ
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM check_pack  USING    i_matnr   TYPE matnr
                          i_edacode TYPE char4 "NA zeeda
                 CHANGING o_flg_err TYPE c.

* ローカル定義
  DATA:
    l_v_promsyu1 TYPE char3."NA zmm88-promsyu1.   "プロモ種別（オンパック）

ENDFORM.                    " CHECK_PACK

*&      Form  F_CHECK_LINE_9100
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       カーソル行チェック(9100)
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      <--O_LINE  カーソル行
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_check_line_9100  CHANGING o_line TYPE sy-tabix.

* カーソル行取得
  PERFORM f_get_line CHANGING o_line.
  IF o_line = 0.
    IF sy-ucomm <> c_pick.
*     データをカーソルで選択して下さい
      MESSAGE s070(/ccej/otc_atp) DISPLAY LIKE c_w.
    ENDIF.
    EXIT.
  ENDIF.

  o_line = tc9100-top_line + o_line - 1.

ENDFORM.                    " F_CHECK_LINE_9100

FORM change_karidata_9100_fil .

* フィルタ条件を無視し、信号機が変更あり(注意記号)の全データを仮引当の対
* 象とするためデータの入れ替え
  LOOP AT it_9100_save INTO wa_9100
                       WHERE icon = v_icon_warning.   "信号機
    APPEND wa_9100 TO it_kari_9100.
  ENDLOOP.

ENDFORM.                    " CHANGE_KARIDATA_9100_FIL

                       WHERE icon = v_icon_warning.   "信号機
    APPEND wa_9100 TO it_kari_9100.
  ENDLOOP.


ENDFORM.                    " CHANGE_KARIDATA_9100
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  RETURN_ZAIKO
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       仮引当データの引き剥がし処理
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM return_zaiko .



* 複数回変更が行われた場合を考慮して重複データを削除する
  SORT it_9100_bef BY vbeln   "伝票番号
                      posnr.  "明細
  DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM it_9100_bef
    COMPARING vbeln   "伝票番号
              posnr.  "明細

* *--- CCW･仙台･四国の場合
  IF v_routine_001 = c_kosha.
    PERFORM return_zaiko_rtn_001.
    RETURN.
  ENDIF.

*BOC for MOD-034
  IF it_kari_9100[] IS NOT INITIAL.
    LOOP AT it_9100_bef INTO wa_9100_bef.
      READ TABLE it_kari_9100 TRANSPORTING NO FIELDS
      WITH KEY vbeln = wa_9100_bef-vbeln
               posnr = wa_9100_bef-posnr
               icon = v_icon_warning.
      IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
        PERFORM return_zaiko_menge
                                 USING     wa_9100_bef-vbeln
                                           wa_9100_bef-posnr
                                           wa_9100_bef-kbn
                                 CHANGING  it_hikiate[]
                                           it_zaiko[].
      ENDIF.
    ENDLOOP.
  ELSE.
*EOC for MOD-034


    LOOP AT it_9100_bef INTO wa_9100_bef WHERE vbeln = wa_9100-vbeln AND posnr = wa_9100-posnr.
      PERFORM return_zaiko_menge
                               USING     wa_9100_bef-vbeln
                                         wa_9100_bef-posnr
                                         wa_9100_bef-kbn
                               CHANGING  it_hikiate[]
                                         it_zaiko[].
    ENDLOOP.

  ENDIF.
* 初期化
  REFRESH:
    it_9100_bef.   "変更前データ

ENDFORM.                    " RETURN_ZAIKO

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_SORT_ICON
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       ICONソートステータス取得
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->I_INPUT   入力アイコン
*      <--O_OUTPUT  順番
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_sort_icon  USING    i_input  TYPE c
                    CHANGING o_output TYPE n.

  CLEAR : o_output.

  CASE i_input.
    WHEN v_icon_red.
      o_output = 1.
    WHEN v_icon_warning.
      o_output = 2.
    WHEN v_icon_yellow.
      o_output = 3.
    WHEN v_icon_green.
      o_output = 4.
    WHEN v_icon_checked.
      o_output = 5.
    WHEN v_icon_lock.
      o_output = 6.
    WHEN OTHERS.
  ENDCASE.

ENDFORM.                    " GET_SORT_ICON
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_SORT_STATUS
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       処理ステータスソート順取得
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->I_INPUT   処理ステータス
*      <--O_OUTPUT  ソート順
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_sort_staus USING    i_input  TYPE char100
                    CHANGING o_output TYPE n.

  CLEAR : o_output.

  CASE i_input.
    WHEN text-010.
      o_output = 1.
    WHEN text-012.
      o_output = 2.
    WHEN text-011.
      o_output = 3.
    WHEN OTHERS.
  ENDCASE.

ENDFORM.                    " GET_SORT_STATUS
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  USER_COMMAND_9400
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       在庫一覧画面制御処理
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_user_command_9400 .

* 在庫一覧画面が呼び出された場合に当処理を行う
  CHECK sy-title = text-013.

  CASE sy-ucomm.
*   チェックボタン押下時(9400)
*   (ダブルクリックと同じ動作であるため同じサブルーチンを使用)
    WHEN c_entr.
      PERFORM f_command_pick_9400.
*   中止ボタン押下時(9400)
    WHEN c_canc.
      PERFORM f_command_canc_9400.
    WHEN OTHERS.
  ENDCASE.

ENDFORM.                    " USER_COMMAND_9400
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  F_COMMAND_PICK_9400
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       ダブルクリック(9400)
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_command_pick_9400 .

* ローカル定義
  DATA:
    l_it_taihi TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_9100,   "退避用テーブル
    l_wa_taihi TYPE ty_9100,                     "退避用テーブル
    l_wa_9100  TYPE ty_9100,                     "引当対象データ
    l_v_matnr TYPE mara-matnr.                  "品目コード

  DATA:
    l_it_9100_bef   TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_9100.
  FIELD-SYMBOLS:
    <l_fs_9100>     TYPE ty_9100.


* 初期化
  CLEAR v_line.


* *--- 選択された出荷ハブ一覧のデータが､引当済み or ロック中の場合､終了
  IF   wa_9100-icon = v_icon_checked
    OR wa_9100-icon = v_icon_lock.
    SET SCREEN 0.
    LEAVE SCREEN.
  ENDIF.


* カーソル行取得
  PERFORM f_get_line CHANGING v_line.

  CHECK v_line IS NOT INITIAL.

* 選択データの取得
  v_line = v_line - 1.
  READ TABLE it_9400 INTO wa_9400 INDEX v_line.

  CHECK sy-subrc = 0.

** 品目コード前ゼロ付与

  l_v_matnr = wa_9100-matnr.     "品目ｺｰﾄﾞ


* *--- 一覧画面に変更がある場合は､変更前の内容を【引き剥がし対象】
  IF wa_9100-icon = v_icon_warning.
    LOOP AT it_9100_bef INTO  l_wa_9100
                        WHERE kbn   =  wa_9100-kbn
                          AND vbeln =  wa_9100-vbeln
                          AND posnr =  wa_9100-posnr
                          AND icon  <> v_icon_checked.
      EXIT.
    ENDLOOP.
* *--- 一覧画面に変更がない場合､一覧のデータを【引き剥がし対象】
  ELSE.
*   *--- 在庫一覧で表示されたレコードが出荷ハブ一覧表示上データの場合
*   *--- (パレット種別ありとなしのデータがIT_9400に混在しない仕様)
    IF  wa_9400-matnr   = l_v_matnr          "品目コード

*    AND wa_9400-WERKS   = WA_9100-WERKS     "プラント
    AND wa_9400-werks   = p_werks

    AND wa_9400-lgort   = wa_9100-lgort     "保管場所
    AND wa_9400-edacode = wa_9100-edacode   "枝番
    AND wa_9400-syomi   = wa_9100-syomi.    "賞味期限
*   *--- 仮引当は行わず出荷ハブ一覧画面へ
      SET SCREEN 0.
      LEAVE SCREEN.
    ELSE.
      l_wa_9100 = wa_9100.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.
* *--- 引き剥がし&再引当の準備
  l_it_9100_bef[] = it_9100_bef[].
  REFRESH it_9100_bef.
  APPEND l_wa_9100 TO it_9100_bef.

* 仮引当データ編集
* (選択された保管場所と賞味期限を出荷ハブ一覧に反映)
  wa_9100-lgort = wa_9400-lgort.   "保管場所
  wa_9100-syomi = wa_9400-syomi.   "賞味期限
  MODIFY it_9100 FROM wa_9100
    TRANSPORTING lgort
                 syomi
    WHERE vbeln = wa_9100-vbeln    "伝票番号
      AND posnr = wa_9100-posnr.   "明細

* 在庫一覧で選択されたデータのみ仮引当を行うため一覧データ退避
  l_it_taihi = it_9100.
  REFRESH it_9100.
  APPEND wa_9100 TO it_9100.

* 仮引当判定前処理
  PERFORM edit_kari_init.

* 引当対象データのチェック（値の補填、チェック）
  PERFORM check_data_9100.

* 仮引当データの引き剥がし処理
  PERFORM return_zaiko.

* 引当対象データの編集処理（表示項目の補填、引当結果判定）
  PERFORM edit_9100_2.

* ソート処理
  SORT it_9100 BY vbeln   "伝票番号
                  posnr.  "明細番号

* 退避したデータをもとに戻す

* *--- 変更前データを戻し､一覧で選択されたデータのみ削除する
  it_9100_bef[] = l_it_9100_bef[].
  DELETE it_9100_bef WHERE kbn   = wa_9100-kbn
                       AND vbeln = wa_9100-vbeln
                       AND posnr = wa_9100-posnr.

*  LOOP AT L_IT_TAIHI INTO L_wa_TAIHI.
  LOOP AT l_it_taihi ASSIGNING <l_fs_9100>.
    l_wa_taihi = <l_fs_9100>.

**   伝票番号前ゼロ付与

    READ TABLE it_9100 INTO wa_9100 BINARY SEARCH
      WITH KEY vbeln = l_wa_taihi-vbeln    "伝票番号
               posnr = l_wa_taihi-posnr.   "明細
    IF sy-subrc = 0.

*      MODIFY L_IT_TAIHI FROM WA_9100.
      <l_fs_9100> = wa_9100.

    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.
  it_9100 = l_it_taihi.


* 引当残数の編集（品目、プラント、保管場所、枝番レベル）
  PERFORM edit_9100_3.

* 最新のソート順を考慮
  IF flg_sort = c_sort_init  "ソート：初期表示
  OR flg_sort IS INITIAL.
    SORT it_9100 BY kbn        "処理区分
                    vbeln      "購買依頼
                    posnr.     "明細番号
  ENDIF.

  IF flg_sort = c_sort_i.    "ソート：信号機
*   <補足>
*   "赤信号"の場合、信号機ソート制御 = 1
*   "変更入力あり"の場合、信号機ソート制御 = 2
*   "黄色信号"の場合、信号機ソート制御 = 3
*   "緑信号"の場合、信号機ソート制御 = 4
*   "引当済（通常/予定）"の場合、信号機ソート制御 = 5
    SORT it_9100 BY sort_icon  "信号機ソート制御
                    kbn        "処理区分
                    vbeln      "伝票番号
                    posnr.     "明細
  ENDIF.

  IF flg_sort = c_sort_s.    "ソート：処理ステータス
*   <補足>
*   "未引当"の場合、処理ステータスソート制御 = 1
*   "予定引当済"の場合、処理ステータスソート制御 = 2
*   "引当済"の場合、処理ステータスソート制御 = 3
    SORT it_9100 BY sort_sta   "処理ステータスソート制御
                    kbn        "処理区分
                    vbeln      "伝票番号
                    posnr.     "明細
  ENDIF.

  IF flg_sort = c_sort_m.    "ソート：品目コード
    SORT it_9100 BY matnr_s    "品目コード
                    kbn        "処理区分
                    vbeln      "伝票番号
                    posnr.     "明細
  ENDIF.

  IF flg_sort = c_sort_k.    "ソート：出荷先
    SORT it_9100 BY kunnr2_s   "出荷先
                    kbn        "処理区分
                    vbeln      "伝票番号
                    posnr.     "明細
  ENDIF.

* 出荷ハブ一覧画面へ
  SET SCREEN 0.
  LEAVE SCREEN.

ENDFORM.                    " F_COMMAND_PICK_9400
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  EDIT_KARI_INIT
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       仮引当判定前処理
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM edit_kari_init .


  FIELD-SYMBOLS:
    <l_fs_9100>    TYPE ty_9100.



*  LOOP AT IT_9100 INTO WA_9100.
  LOOP AT it_9100 ASSIGNING <l_fs_9100>.
    wa_9100 = <l_fs_9100>.

*   初期化
    CLEAR:
      wa_9100-status,            "処理ステータス
      wa_9100-icon,              "信号機
      wa_9100-sort_icon,         "信号機ソート制御
      wa_9100-sort_sta,          "処理ステータスソート制御
      wa_9100-message,           "処理メッセージ
      wa_9100-flg_sumi,          "引当済フラグ
      wa_9100-flg_letyp,         "保管単位考慮なしフラグ
      wa_9100-icon_bef,          "変更前信号機
      wa_9100-icon_lock,         "ロック前信号機
      wa_9100-icon_hiki,         "引当前信号機
      wa_9100-change,            "変更あり
      wa_9100-zan_out,           "引当残数
      wa_9100-zan_9300,          "引当残数(CS)
      wa_9100-kaku_9300.         "引当確定数(CS)

*   出荷ハブ一覧→仮引当
    wa_9100-hub = c_on.


*    MODIFY IT_9100 FROM WA_9100.
    <l_fs_9100> = wa_9100.


  ENDLOOP.

ENDFORM.                    " EDIT_KARI_INIT

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  EDIT_FLASH_RNG
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       フラッシュアウトレンジテーブルの作成
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM edit_flash_rng .

* ローカル定義
  DATA:
    l_wa_flash TYPE ty_auart.     "フラッシュアウト

* 初期化
  REFRESH rng_flash.


*  LOOP AT IT_FLASH INTO L_wa_FLASH.

**    wa_FLASH-LOW    = L_wa_FLASH-ZZDATA1+0(1).
*    IF L_wa_FLASH-ZZDATA1+0(1) = c_FLASH.
*      wa_FLASH-LOW    = c_FLASH.
*    ENDIF.

* *--- 特殊処理分のみRANGEに設定
  LOOP AT it_flash INTO  l_wa_flash
                   WHERE zzdata1 IS NOT INITIAL.
    wa_flash-low    = l_wa_flash-zzdata1+0(1).

    wa_flash-sign   = c_sign_i.
    wa_flash-option = c_opt_eq.
    APPEND wa_flash TO rng_flash.
  ENDLOOP.

ENDFORM.                    " EDIT_FLASH_RNG
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  CHECK_LGORT
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       保管場所の入力値チェック
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      <--O_WA_9100  引当対象データ
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM check_lgort  CHANGING o_wa_9100 TYPE ty_9100.

*  IF o_wa_9100-replaced <> c_on.
* 保管場所の検索
  SELECT SINGLE
         lgobe    "保管場所テキスト
    FROM t001l
    INTO o_wa_9100-lgobe
   WHERE werks = o_wa_9100-werks "p_werks           "プラント
     AND lgort = o_wa_9100-lgort.  "保管場所

* データが取得出来ない場合
  IF sy-subrc <> 0.

*   処理メッセージの編集
*  「プラント &1 / 保管場所 &2 の組み合わせは、保管場所マスタに存在しません」
    MESSAGE e115(/ccej/otc_atp) WITH o_wa_9100-werks "p_werks           "プラント
                              o_wa_9100-lgort   "保管場所
      INTO o_wa_9100-message.
*   信号機の編集
    o_wa_9100-icon     = v_icon_red.     "信号機
    PERFORM get_sort_icon  USING    o_wa_9100-icon
                           CHANGING o_wa_9100-sort_icon.
    o_wa_9100-status   = text-010.        "処理ステータス
    PERFORM get_sort_staus USING    o_wa_9100-status
                           CHANGING o_wa_9100-sort_sta.

  ENDIF.
*  ENDIF.
ENDFORM.                    " CHECK_LGORT
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  EDIT_STOCK_MINUS
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       引当済データの減算処理
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM edit_stock_minus .

* ローカル定義
  DATA:
    l_wa_lips TYPE ty_lips,   "引当済データ

    l_wa_marc TYPE ty_marc.   "品目プラントデータ

*BOC MOD-009
*Get Last Delivered BBE and MRSL Details
  PERFORM f_get_last_bbe_and_mrsl.

* BOC for MOD-030
  IF v_valid  IS INITIAL.
    SORT it_zaiko BY werks          "プラント
                       lgort          "保管場所
                       matnr          "品目コード
                       eda            "枝番
                       kiboshom.      "賞味期限
  ELSEIF v_valid = c_on.
    SORT it_zaiko BY werks          "プラント
                      lgort          "保管場所
                      matnr          "品目コード
                      eda            "枝番
                      kiboshom      "賞味期限
                      hsdat "MOD-030
                      verab."MOD-030

  ENDIF.
* EOC for MOD-030

*EOC MOD-009
  SORT it_lips BY matnr werks lgort eda kiboshom."MOD-019
*  LOOP AT IT_LIPS INTO L_wa_LIPS.
* *--- 通常引当分の減算
" Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_A Conversion **BEGIN OF CHANGE BY EJX9007359 03.06.2026  FOR ATC
SORT it_knvv_min BY vbeln .
" Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_A Conversion * *END OF CHANGE BY EJX9007359 03.06.2026 FOR ATC
  LOOP AT it_lips INTO  l_wa_lips
                  WHERE zzskyoseikb IS INITIAL.


    l_wa_lips-kiboshom = l_wa_lips-vfdat.
    l_wa_lips-eda = l_wa_lips-/scl/atwrt."MOD-019
*   ロット管理品判定
    READ TABLE it_marc INTO l_wa_marc BINARY SEARCH
      WITH KEY matnr = l_wa_lips-matnr     "品目コード
               werks = l_wa_lips-werks.    "プラント
*    READ TABLE IT_MCHB_MIN BINARY SEARCH TRANSPORTING NO FIELDS
*      WITH KEY MATNR = L_wa_LIPS-MATNR    "品目コード
*               WERKS = L_wa_LIPS-WERKS    "プラント
*               LGORT = L_wa_LIPS-LGORT.   "保管場所

*   ロット管理所要量区分が"X"の場合
    IF l_wa_marc-xchpf = c_on.
**   ロット管理品の場合
*    IF SY-SUBRC = 0.

*     賞味期限の編集（CDPに基づく賞味期限の算出）（減算用）
      PERFORM edit_syomi_minus
        CHANGING l_wa_lips.     "引当済データ

*   *--- ロット非管理品の場合､出荷伝票の枝番､賞味期限は考慮しない
    ELSE.
      CLEAR :
        l_wa_lips-eda,
        l_wa_lips-kiboshom,
        l_wa_lips-plcode.

    ENDIF.

*   減算対象の条件作成
    PERFORM edit_range_minus
      USING l_wa_lips.      "引当済データ

*   実在庫からの減算処理
*
*   *--- ﾊﾟﾚｯﾄ種別に入力がある場合､ﾊﾟﾚｯﾄ種別を指定して在庫減算
    IF l_wa_lips-plcode IS NOT INITIAL.
      PERFORM minus_jitu_letyp
                             USING     space
                                       space
                             CHANGING  l_wa_lips.
    ENDIF.
*   *--- ﾊﾟﾚｯﾄ種別未指定orﾊﾟﾚｯﾄ種別指定で在庫減算が完了しない場合､ﾊﾟﾚｯﾄ種別なしで在庫減算
    IF   l_wa_lips-plcode IS INITIAL
       OR ( l_wa_lips-plcode IS NOT INITIAL
        AND l_wa_lips-lfimg  >  0 ).
      PERFORM minus_jitu     USING     space
                                       space
                             CHANGING  l_wa_lips.
    ENDIF.

*   減算数量が0の場合は次レコードへ
    IF l_wa_lips-lfimg = 0.
      CONTINUE.
    ENDIF.


*   *--- 品検中在庫からの減算処理

*    PERFORM MINUS_JITU
*      USING    c_X        "予定在庫フラグ
*               c_BESTQ_Q  "在庫カテゴリ
*      CHANGING L_wa_LIPS.   "引当済データ
*   *--- ﾊﾟﾚｯﾄ種別に入力がある場合､ﾊﾟﾚｯﾄ種別指定で品検中在庫から減算
    IF l_wa_lips-plcode IS NOT INITIAL.
      PERFORM minus_jitu_letyp
                             USING     c_x               " 予定在庫
                                       c_bestq_q         " 品検中
                             CHANGING  l_wa_lips.
    ENDIF.
*   *--- ﾊﾟﾚｯﾄ種別未指定orﾊﾟﾚｯﾄ種別指定で在庫減算が完了しない場合､ﾊﾟﾚｯﾄ種別なしで在庫減算
    IF   l_wa_lips-plcode IS INITIAL
       OR ( l_wa_lips-plcode IS NOT INITIAL
        AND l_wa_lips-lfimg  >  0 ).
      PERFORM minus_jitu     USING     c_x
                                       c_bestq_q
                             CHANGING  l_wa_lips.
    ENDIF.


*   *--- 減算数量が0の場合は次レコードへ
    IF l_wa_lips-lfimg = 0.
      CONTINUE.
    ENDIF.


*   予定在庫からの減算処理
    PERFORM minus_yotei
      CHANGING l_wa_lips.   "引当済データ

  ENDLOOP.


* *--- 強制引当分の減算
  PERFORM minus_flashout.

* *--- 引当可能数量＝0の在庫ﾃﾞｰﾀを削除
  DELETE it_zaiko WHERE menge_cs = 0 AND stck_quality = 0.



  FREE :

    it_lips_ekpo,

    it_lips.


ENDFORM.                    " EDIT_STOCK_MINUS
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  SELECT_KNVV_MINUS
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       ボトラーアカウントコード(減算用)の取得
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM select_knvv_minus .


  DATA:
    l_it_lips      TYPE SORTED TABLE OF ty_lips
                        WITH NON-UNIQUE KEY vgbel.



  CHECK it_lips IS NOT INITIAL.

* 初期化
  REFRESH:

*    IT_KNVV.
    it_knvv_min.

  l_it_lips[] = it_lips[].
  DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM l_it_lips COMPARING vgbel.

* 販売伝票ヘッダ、得意先マスタ: 販売データの検索
  IF l_it_lips[] IS NOT INITIAL.
    SELECT vbak~vbeln        "販売伝票
           knvv~kunnr        "得意先コード
*         knvv~zkzbotacct   "ボトラーアカウントコード"NA
      INTO TABLE it_knvv_min
      FROM vbak
      INNER JOIN knvv
      ON vbak~kunnr = knvv~kunnr   "得意先コード

*    FOR ALL ENTRIES IN IT_LIPS
*   WHERE VBAK~VBELN = IT_LIPS-VGBEL    "販売伝票
      FOR ALL ENTRIES IN l_it_lips
     WHERE vbak~vbeln = l_it_lips-vgbel  "販売伝票

       AND knvv~vkorg = v_bukrs         "販売組織
       AND knvv~vtweg = c_vtweg_99     "流通チャネル
       AND knvv~spart = c_spart_10.    "製品部門
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    " SELECT_KNVV_MINUS
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  EDIT_SYOMI_MINUS
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       賞味期限の編集（CDPに基づく賞味期限の算出）（減算用）

*      <--O_wa_LIPS  引当済データ

FORM edit_syomi_minus  CHANGING o_wa_lips TYPE ty_lips.

* ローカル定義
  DATA:
    l_wa_knvv_min TYPE ty_knvv_min.   "ボトラーアカウントコード

  DATA:
    l_wa_lips_ekpo TYPE ty_lips_ekpo.  "在庫減算賞味期限算出用出荷先


* 賞味期限がブランクの場合に当処理を行う
  CHECK o_wa_lips-kiboshom IS INITIAL.

* 受注データの場合

*  IF O_wa_LIPS-VGTYP = TEXT-001.
  IF o_wa_lips-vgtyp = c_vbtyv_c.


*   ボトラーアカウントコードの取得

* Quick Fix Insert a SORT statement before the problematic statement
* 05.06.2026 10:37:09 EJX9007361
* Transport Q4RK902230 ATC Correction S4 HANA
    SORT IT_KNVV_MIN BY VBELN.

* End of Quick Fix

* Quick Fix Insert a SORT statement before the problematic statement
* 05.06.2026 10:54:41 EJX9007361
* Transport Q4RK902230 ATC Correction S4 HANA
    SORT IT_KNVV_MIN BY VBELN.

* End of Quick Fix

* Quick Fix Insert a SORT statement before the problematic statement
* 05.06.2026 10:56:26 EJX9007361
* Transport Q4RK902230 ATC Correction S4 HANA
    SORT IT_KNVV_MIN BY VBELN.

* End of Quick Fix
    READ TABLE it_knvv_min INTO l_wa_knvv_min BINARY SEARCH
      WITH KEY vbeln = o_wa_lips-vgbel.   "伝票番号

*      EXPORTING
*        im_vbtyv    = c_vbtyv_c               "販売管理伝票カテゴリ
*        im_kunag    = l_wa_knvv_min-kunnr       "受注先

*      IMPORTING
*        ex_lastship = o_wa_lips-kiboshom.       "最終出荷賞味

* 転送発注データの場合
  ELSE.


*   *--- 在庫減算賞味期限算出用出荷先からﾌﾟﾗﾝﾄ&保管場所を取得
    l_wa_lips_ekpo-ebeln = o_wa_lips-vgbel.
    l_wa_lips_ekpo-ebelp = o_wa_lips-vgpos.
    READ TABLE it_lips_ekpo INTO l_wa_lips_ekpo
                   WITH TABLE KEY ebeln = l_wa_lips_ekpo-ebeln
                                  ebelp = l_wa_lips_ekpo-ebelp

                                  lgort.

*      IMPORTING
*        ex_lastship = o_wa_lips-kiboshom.       "最終出荷賞味

  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    " EDIT_SYOMI_MINUS

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  EDIT_RANGE_MINUS
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       減算対象の条件作成
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->I_wa_LIPS  引当済データ
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM edit_range_minus  USING    i_wa_lips TYPE ty_lips.

* ローカル定義
  DATA:
    l_wa_eda   LIKE LINE OF rng_eda,     "枝番レンジテーブル
    l_wa_syomi LIKE LINE OF rng_syomi.   "賞味期限レンジテーブル

  DATA:
    l_wa_wadat LIKE LINE OF rng_wadat.   "出庫予定日レンジテーブル


* 初期化
  REFRESH:
    rng_eda,     "枝番レンジテーブル
    rng_syomi.   "賞味期限レンジテーブル

  REFRESH:
    rng_wadat.   "出庫予定日レンジテーブル


* 枝番レンジテーブル
* 枝番がある場合
  IF i_wa_lips-eda IS NOT INITIAL.
    l_wa_eda-sign   = c_sign_i.
    l_wa_eda-option = c_opt_eq.
    l_wa_eda-low    = i_wa_lips-eda.
    APPEND l_wa_eda TO rng_eda.
* 枝番がない場合
* (枝番がブランクの場合は全枝番が対象となる)
  ELSE.
    l_wa_eda-sign   = c_sign_i.
    l_wa_eda-option = c_opt_cp.
    l_wa_eda-low    = c_asta.
    APPEND l_wa_eda TO rng_eda.
  ENDIF.

* 賞味期限レンジテーブル
*BOC MOD-009
*  l_wa_syomi-sign   = c_sign_i.
*  l_wa_syomi-option = c_opt_ge.
*  l_wa_syomi-low    = i_wa_lips-kiboshom.
*  APPEND l_wa_syomi TO rng_syomi.

  IF i_wa_lips-kiboshom IS INITIAL  AND i_wa_lips-/lot/tempkz = c_spe_bbe .
    l_wa_syomi-sign   = c_sign_i.
    l_wa_syomi-option = c_opt_ge.
    l_wa_syomi-low    = i_wa_lips-last_bbe.
    APPEND l_wa_syomi TO rng_syomi.
  ENDIF.

  IF i_wa_lips-kiboshom IS INITIAL  AND i_wa_lips-/lot/tempkz = c_after_bbe.
    l_wa_syomi-sign   = c_sign_i.
    l_wa_syomi-option = c_opt_ge.
    l_wa_syomi-low    = i_wa_lips-last_bbe.
    APPEND l_wa_syomi TO rng_syomi.
  ENDIF.

  IF i_wa_lips-kiboshom IS INITIAL  AND ( i_wa_lips-/lot/tempkz IS INITIAL OR i_wa_lips-/lot/tempkz = c_non  ).
    l_wa_syomi-sign   = c_sign_i.
    l_wa_syomi-option = c_opt_ge.
    l_wa_syomi-low    = i_wa_lips-mrsl_date.
    APPEND l_wa_syomi TO rng_syomi.
  ENDIF.

  IF i_wa_lips-kiboshom IS NOT INITIAL  AND i_wa_lips-/lot/tempkz = c_spe_bbe.
    l_wa_syomi-sign   = c_sign_i.
    l_wa_syomi-option = c_opt_eq.
    l_wa_syomi-low    = i_wa_lips-vfdat.
    APPEND l_wa_syomi TO rng_syomi.
  ENDIF.

  IF i_wa_lips-kiboshom IS NOT INITIAL  AND i_wa_lips-/lot/tempkz = c_after_bbe.
    l_wa_syomi-sign   = c_sign_i.
    l_wa_syomi-option = c_opt_ge.

    IF i_wa_lips-vfdat GE i_wa_lips-last_bbe.
      l_wa_syomi-low    = i_wa_lips-vfdat.
    ELSE.
      l_wa_syomi-low    = i_wa_lips-last_bbe.
    ENDIF.

    APPEND l_wa_syomi TO rng_syomi.
  ENDIF.

  IF i_wa_lips-kiboshom IS NOT INITIAL  AND ( i_wa_lips-/lot/tempkz IS INITIAL OR i_wa_lips-/lot/tempkz = c_non  ).
    l_wa_syomi-sign   = c_sign_i.
    l_wa_syomi-option = c_opt_ge.
    IF i_wa_lips-mrsl_date IS NOT INITIAL.
      l_wa_syomi-low    = i_wa_lips-mrsl_date.
    ELSE.
      l_wa_syomi-low    = i_wa_lips-vfdat.
    ENDIF.
    APPEND l_wa_syomi TO rng_syomi.
  ENDIF.


*EOC MOD-009

* *--- 出庫予定日レンジテーブル
  l_wa_wadat-sign   = c_sign_i.
  l_wa_wadat-option = c_opt_le.
  l_wa_wadat-low    = i_wa_lips-wadat - v_factr.
  APPEND l_wa_wadat TO rng_wadat.


ENDFORM.                    " EDIT_RANGE_MINUS
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  MINUS_JITU
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       実在庫からの減算処理
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      <--O_wa_LIPS  引当済データ
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*

*FORM MINUS_JITU  CHANGING O_wa_LIPS TYPE TY_LIPS.
FORM minus_jitu  USING    i_v_flg_y   TYPE ty_zaiko-flg_y
                          i_v_bestq   TYPE ty_zaiko-bestq
                 CHANGING o_wa_lips    TYPE ty_lips.


* ローカル定義
  DATA:
    l_wa_zaiko TYPE ty_zaiko.   "在庫内部テーブル

  FIELD-SYMBOLS:
    <l_fs_zaiko>   TYPE ty_zaiko,
    <l_fs_yojitu>  TYPE any.


* ソート処理
*BOC MOD-009
*  SORT it_zaiko BY werks          "プラント
*                   lgort          "保管場所
*                   matnr          "品目コード
*                   eda            "枝番
*                   kiboshom.      "賞味期限
*EOC MOD-009

* 品目／プラント／保管場所／枝番／賞味期限レベルでの減算

*  LOOP AT IT_ZAIKO INTO L_wa_ZAIKO
  LOOP AT it_zaiko ASSIGNING <l_fs_zaiko>

                   WHERE matnr    =  o_wa_lips-matnr   "品目コード
                     AND werks    =  o_wa_lips-werks   "プラント
                     AND lgort    =  o_wa_lips-lgort   "保管場所
                     AND eda      IN rng_eda           "枝番
                     AND kiboshom IN rng_syomi         "賞味期限

*                     AND FLG_Y    =  SPACE.            "予定在庫フラグ

                     AND bestq    =  i_v_bestq        "在庫カテゴリ
                     AND flg_y    =  i_v_flg_y.       "予定在庫フラグ



**   品質検査中在庫は減算対象外とする
*    IF L_wa_ZAIKO-BESTQ = c_BESTQ_Q.     "在庫カテゴリ
*      CONTINUE.
*    ENDIF.
    l_wa_zaiko = <l_fs_zaiko>.
*   *--- 在庫カテゴリによって､減算する引当可能数量の項目を決定
*   *--- (ASSIGN 割当不可は想定しない)
    CASE i_v_bestq.
      WHEN c_bestq_q.      "品検中
" Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_APS Conversion **BEGIN OF CHANGE BY EJX9007359 11.06.2026  for ATC
*         ASSIGN COMPONENT c_fld_yotei_cs OF STRUCTURE l_wa_zaiko TO <l_fs_yojitu>.
        ASSIGN COMPONENT c_fld_yotei_cs OF STRUCTURE l_wa_zaiko TO <l_fs_yojitu>. "#EC CI_FLDEXT_OK[0002250183]
" Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_APS Conversion * *END OF CHANGE BY EJX9007359 11.06.2026 for ATC
      WHEN OTHERS.           "品検中以外
" Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_APS Conversion **BEGIN OF CHANGE BY EJX9007359 11.06.2026  for ATC
*         ASSIGN COMPONENT c_fld_jitu_cs  OF STRUCTURE l_wa_zaiko TO <l_fs_yojitu>.
        ASSIGN COMPONENT c_fld_jitu_cs  OF STRUCTURE l_wa_zaiko TO <l_fs_yojitu>. "#EC CI_FLDEXT_OK[0002250183]
" Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_APS Conversion * *END OF CHANGE BY EJX9007359 11.06.2026 for ATC
    ENDCASE.


*   在庫データの引当可能数量が0以下の場合は減算対象外とする
    IF l_wa_zaiko-menge_cs <= 0.
      CONTINUE.
    ENDIF.
    IF i_v_bestq = c_bestq_q.
      v_alloc_seq = v_alloc_seq + 1.
      l_wa_zaiko-stck_alloc_seq = v_alloc_seq.
    ENDIF.

*   在庫データの引当可能数量　＞＝　引当済数量
*   (実在庫のみで減算が終わるデータを想定)
    IF l_wa_zaiko-menge_cs >= o_wa_lips-lfimg.

*     引当可能数量(実在庫＋予定在庫－引当済数量)の算出
      l_wa_zaiko-menge_cs = l_wa_zaiko-menge_cs     "引当可能数量
                          - o_wa_lips-lfimg.        "引当済数量

*     実在庫引当可能数量(実在庫－引当済数量)の算出

*      L_wa_ZAIKO-JITU_CS  = L_wa_ZAIKO-JITU_CS      "引当可能数量
      <l_fs_yojitu>       = <l_fs_yojitu>           "引当可能数量

                          - o_wa_lips-lfimg.        "引当済数量

*     引当済数量残の算出
      o_wa_lips-lfimg     = 0.                      "引当済数量

*   在庫データの引当可能数量　＜　引当済数量
*   (実在庫と予定在庫の減算があるデータを想定)
    ELSE.

*     引当済数量残の算出
      o_wa_lips-lfimg     = o_wa_lips-lfimg         "引当済数量
                          - l_wa_zaiko-menge_cs.    "引当可能数量

*     引当可能数量(実在庫＋予定在庫－引当済数量)の算出
      l_wa_zaiko-menge_cs = 0.                      "引当可能数量

*     実在庫引当可能数量(実在庫－引当済数量)の算出

*      L_wa_ZAIKO-JITU_CS  = 0.                      "引当可能数量
      <l_fs_yojitu>       = 0.                      "引当可能数量


    ENDIF.

    IF i_v_bestq = c_bestq_q.
      l_wa_zaiko-stck_qrem = l_wa_zaiko-menge_cs.
    ENDIF.

    <l_fs_zaiko> = l_wa_zaiko.
*   *--- 引当済数量 = 0 の場合は､LOOPを抜ける
    IF o_wa_lips-lfimg = 0.
      EXIT.
    ENDIF.


  ENDLOOP.
  IF i_v_bestq = c_bestq_q.
*  Removing Open Delievery From Stock Under Quality Inpection found from MCHB Table
    LOOP AT it_zaiko ASSIGNING <l_fs_zaiko>

                    WHERE matnr    =  o_wa_lips-matnr   "品目コード
                      AND werks    =  o_wa_lips-werks   "プラント
*                     AND lgort    =  o_wa_lips-lgort   "保管場所
                      AND stck_ind = c_sch_stck
                      AND eda      IN rng_eda           "枝番
                      AND kiboshom IN rng_syomi         "賞味期限
                      AND stck_quality GT 0.
*                     AND FLG_Y    =  SPACE.            "予定在庫フラグ

*                     AND bestq    =  i_v_bestq        "在庫カテゴリ
*                     AND flg_y    =  i_v_flg_y.       "予定在庫フラグ


      l_wa_zaiko = <l_fs_zaiko>.
      v_alloc_seq = v_alloc_seq + 1.
      l_wa_zaiko-stck_alloc_seq = v_alloc_seq.

*   在庫データの引当可能数量　＞＝　引当済数量
*   (実在庫のみで減算が終わるデータを想定)
      IF l_wa_zaiko-stck_quality >= o_wa_lips-lfimg.

*     引当可能数量(実在庫＋予定在庫－引当済数量)の算出
        l_wa_zaiko-stck_quality = l_wa_zaiko-stck_quality     "引当可能数量
                            - o_wa_lips-lfimg.        "引当済数量


*     引当済数量残の算出
        o_wa_lips-lfimg     = 0.                      "引当済数量

*   在庫データの引当可能数量　＜　引当済数量
*   (実在庫と予定在庫の減算があるデータを想定)
      ELSE.

*     引当済数量残の算出
        o_wa_lips-lfimg     = o_wa_lips-lfimg         "引当済数量
                            - l_wa_zaiko-stck_quality.    "引当可能数量

*     引当可能数量(実在庫＋予定在庫－引当済数量)の算出
        l_wa_zaiko-stck_quality = 0.                      "引当可能数量


      ENDIF.

      IF i_v_bestq = c_bestq_q.
        l_wa_zaiko-stck_qrem = l_wa_zaiko-stck_quality.
      ENDIF.

      <l_fs_zaiko> = l_wa_zaiko.
*   *--- 引当済数量 = 0 の場合は､LOOPを抜ける
      IF o_wa_lips-lfimg = 0.
        EXIT.
      ENDIF.


    ENDLOOP.
  ENDIF.
ENDFORM.                    " MINUS_JITU
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  MINUS_YOTEI
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       予定在庫からの減算処理
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      <--O_wa_LIPS  引当済データ
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM minus_yotei  CHANGING o_wa_lips TYPE ty_lips.

* ローカル定義
  DATA:
    l_wa_zaiko TYPE ty_zaiko.   "在庫内部テーブル

  FIELD-SYMBOLS:
    <l_fs_zaiko> TYPE ty_zaiko. "在庫内部テーブル



* *--- Sort
*BOC MOD-009
*  SORT it_zaiko BY matnr ASCENDING  "品目コード
*                   werks ASCENDING  "プラント
*                   lgort ASCENDING  "保管場所
*                   eindt DESCENDING "入荷予定日
*                   eda   ASCENDING. "枝番
*EOC MOD-009

* 品目／プラント／保管場所レベルでの減算
* (必ず減算数量が0になる想定)

*  LOOP AT IT_ZAIKO INTO L_wa_ZAIKO
  LOOP AT it_zaiko ASSIGNING <l_fs_zaiko>

                   WHERE matnr    =  o_wa_lips-matnr   "品目コード
                     AND werks    =  o_wa_lips-werks   "プラント
                     AND lgort    =  o_wa_lips-lgort   "保管場所

*                     AND eindt    IN rng_wadat         "入荷予定日
                     AND eda      IN rng_eda           "枝番
                     AND bestq    =  space             "在庫カテゴリ
*--Begin of Changes - MOD-017 -  INC4248111 - 13/10/2016.
                     AND kiboshom IN rng_syomi         "賞味期限
*--End of Changes - MOD-017 -  INC4248111 - 13/10/2016.
                     AND flg_y    =  c_on.           "予定在庫フラグ



    l_wa_zaiko = <l_fs_zaiko>.

*   在庫データの引当可能数量が0以下の場合は減算対象外とする
    IF l_wa_zaiko-menge_cs <= 0.
      CONTINUE.
    ENDIF.

    v_alloc_seq = v_alloc_seq + 1.
    l_wa_zaiko-stck_alloc_seq = v_alloc_seq.

*   *--- 在庫データの引当可能数量　＞＝　引当済数量
    IF l_wa_zaiko-menge_cs >= o_wa_lips-lfimg.

*   引当可能数量(実在庫＋予定在庫－引当済数量)の算出
      l_wa_zaiko-menge_cs = l_wa_zaiko-menge_cs     "引当可能数量
                          - o_wa_lips-lfimg.        "引当済数量

*   予定在庫引当可能数量(予定在庫－引当済数量)の算出
      l_wa_zaiko-yotei_cs  = l_wa_zaiko-yotei_cs    "引当可能数量
                           - o_wa_lips-lfimg.       "引当済数量

*   引当済数量残の算出
      o_wa_lips-lfimg     = 0.                      "引当済数量


    ELSE.
*     *--- 引当済数量残の算出
      o_wa_lips-lfimg       = o_wa_lips-lfimg
                            - l_wa_zaiko-menge_cs.
      l_wa_zaiko-menge_cs = 0.
      l_wa_zaiko-yotei_cs = 0.
    ENDIF.



**   在庫内部テーブルの更新
*    MODIFY IT_ZAIKO FROM L_wa_ZAIKO.
*
**   LOOPを抜ける
*    EXIT.
    l_wa_zaiko-stck_srem = l_wa_zaiko-menge_cs.
    <l_fs_zaiko> = l_wa_zaiko.
*   *--- 引当済数量残 = 0 の場合､LOOPを抜ける
    IF o_wa_lips-lfimg = 0.
      EXIT.
    ENDIF.

  ENDLOOP.

ENDFORM.                    " MINUS_YOTEI
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  CONV_MEINS
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       基本数量単位変換ロジック
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->I_MATNR   品目コード
*      -->I_MEINH   基本数量単位（変更前）
*      -->I_MEINS   基本数量単位（変更後）
*      -->I_KWMENG  引当数量（CS）
*      <--O_KWMENG  引当数量（CS）
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM conv_meins  USING    i_matnr  TYPE mara-matnr
                          i_meinh  TYPE mara-meins
                          i_meins  TYPE mara-meins
                          i_kwmeng TYPE eban-menge
                 CHANGING o_kwmeng TYPE eban-menge.

* ローカル定義
  DATA:
    l_v_menge TYPE eban-menge.

*  CALL FUNCTION 'Z_ZMM1_CONVMEINS'
*    EXPORTING
*      i_matnr         = i_matnr     "品目コード
*      i_meinh         = i_meinh     "基本数量単位（変更前）
*      i_meins         = i_meins     "基本数量単位（変更後）
*      i_menge         = i_kwmeng    "引当数量（CS）
*    IMPORTING
*      o_menge         = l_v_menge  "引当数量（CS）
*    EXCEPTIONS
*      meins_not_exist = 1
*      OTHERS          = 2.
*  IF sy-subrc = 0.
*    o_kwmeng = l_v_menge.
*  ENDIF.


  CALL FUNCTION '/CCEJ/OTC_UNIT_CONVERT'
    EXPORTING
      im_matnr        = i_matnr     "品目コード
      im_meinh        = i_meinh     "基本数量単位（変更前）
      im_meins        = i_meins     "基本数量単位（変更後）
      im_menge        = i_kwmeng    "引当数量（CS）
    IMPORTING
      ex_menge        = l_v_menge  "引当数量（CS）
    EXCEPTIONS
      meins_not_exist = 1
      OTHERS          = 2.
  IF sy-subrc = 0.
    o_kwmeng = l_v_menge.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    " CONV_MEINS
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  CHECK_DATA_9100
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       引当対象データのチェック（値の補填、チェック）
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM check_data_9100 .

* ローカル定義
  DATA:

*    L_WA_9100         TYPE TY_9100,   "引当対象データ
*    L_FLG_LOT(1)      TYPE C.         "ロット管理フラグ
    l_wa_9100         TYPE ty_9100.   "引当対象データ

  FIELD-SYMBOLS:
    <l_fs_9100>       TYPE ty_9100.   "引当対象データ


* 各種テーブルソート処理
  PERFORM sort_9100.


*  LOOP AT IT_9100 INTO L_WA_9100.
  LOOP AT it_9100 ASSIGNING <l_fs_9100>.
    l_wa_9100 = <l_fs_9100>.



**   初期化
*    CLEAR:
*      L_FLG_LOT.           "ロット管理フラグ

*   出荷保管場所決定
    PERFORM get_khobcd
      CHANGING l_wa_9100.  "引当対象データ


**   ロット管理品判定
*    PERFORM CHECK_LOT
*      USING    L_WA_9100   "引当対象データ
*      CHANGING L_FLG_LOT.  "ロット管理フラグ

*   値の補填
    PERFORM add_9100
    CHANGING l_wa_9100.



    READ TABLE i_knvv INTO wa_knvv WITH KEY kunnr = l_wa_9100-kunnr2.
    IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL AND sy-ucomm <> c_kari.
      l_wa_9100-delprio = wa_knvv-delprio.
      l_wa_9100-flash_kbn = wa_knvv-/ccej/bbe_ind.
    ENDIF.

    READ TABLE it_marc_non TRANSPORTING NO FIELDS WITH KEY matnr = l_wa_9100-matnr werks = l_wa_9100-werks.
    IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
      CLEAR l_wa_9100-flash_kbn.
    ENDIF.


*      USING    L_FLG_LOT   "ロット管理フラグ

    "引当対象データ

*   引当対象データの更新

*    MODIFY IT_9100 FROM L_WA_9100.
    <l_fs_9100> = l_wa_9100.

    CLEAR  l_wa_9100.

  ENDLOOP.

  IF s_delpri[] IS NOT INITIAL.
    DELETE it_9100 WHERE delprio NOT IN s_delpri.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    " CHECK_DATA_9100

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  SELECT_ZNN0001_HUB
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       伝票タイプの取得(海貨業者要否判定あり)
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->I_KEY      取得キー
*      <--O_IT_DATA  伝票タイプ格納用
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM select_znn0001_hub
  USING     i_key                 TYPE any
  CHANGING  o_it_data             TYPE tbl_ty_auart_hub.

* 共通テーブルの検索


  IF i_key = c_so.
    IF s_auart[] IS NOT INITIAL.
      SELECT auart
        FROM /ccej/t_ordr_typ
        INTO TABLE o_it_data
        WHERE ktopl = v_ktopl
          AND /ccej/sls_ctgr = i_key
          AND auart IN s_auart.
      IF sy-subrc IS NOT INITIAL.
        MESSAGE e011(/ccej/otc_atp).
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
  ELSEIF i_key = c_po.

    IF s_bst_k[] IS NOT INITIAL.
      SELECT bsart
        FROM /ccej/t_ordr_typ
        INTO TABLE o_it_data
        WHERE ktopl = v_ktopl
          AND /ccej/sls_ctgr = i_key
          AND bsart IN s_bst_k.
      IF sy-subrc IS NOT INITIAL.
        MESSAGE e011(/ccej/otc_atp).
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    " SELECT_ZNN0001_HUB
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  CHECK_PARVW_CR_YOUHI
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       海貨業者要否判定
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->I_v_KBN           処理区分
*      -->I_v_AUART         伝票タイプ
*      <--O_FLG_CR           海貨業者要(on：要､off：不要)
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM check_parvw_cr_youhi
  USING     i_v_kbn              TYPE ty_9100-kbn
            i_v_auart            TYPE ty_9100-auart
  CHANGING  o_flg_cr              TYPE xfeld.

  DATA:
    l_v_zzdata1        TYPE char10."NA znn0001-zzdata1.

  CLEAR o_flg_cr.
  l_v_zzdata1 = i_v_auart.
* *--- 共通マスタの海貨業者要否確認
  CASE i_v_kbn.
    WHEN text-001.   "受注
*      READ TABLE it_art_v TRANSPORTING NO FIELDS BINARY SEARCH
*                          WITH KEY zzdata1  = l_v_zzdata1
*                                   zkzchlen = space.
    WHEN text-002.   "転送発注
*      READ TABLE it_BST_k TRANSPORTING NO FIELDS BINARY SEARCH
*                          WITH KEY zzdata1  = l_v_zzdata1
*                                   zkzchlen = space.
    WHEN OTHERS.
  ENDCASE.
* *--- 海貨業者要の伝票タイプの場合にフラグを設定
  CHECK sy-subrc = 0.
  o_flg_cr = c_x.

ENDFORM.                    " CHECK_PARVW_CR_YOUHI

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  SELECT_LIPS_VGBEL
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       在庫減算賞味期限算出用出荷先取得
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM select_lips_vgbel .

  TYPES :
    BEGIN OF l_ty_ekpo,
      ebeln        TYPE ekpo-ebeln,
      ebelp        TYPE ekpo-ebelp,
    END   OF l_ty_ekpo.
  DATA :
    l_it_ekpo      TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF l_ty_ekpo,
    l_wa_lips      TYPE ty_lips,
    l_wa_ekpo      TYPE l_ty_ekpo.

  REFRESH :
    it_lips_ekpo.
* *--- 参照元が購買発注で賞味期限未設定のﾃﾞｰﾀのみ検索条件に設定
  LOOP AT it_lips INTO  l_wa_lips
                  WHERE vgtyp      =  c_vbtyv_v
                    AND ( kiboshom IS INITIAL
                       OR kiboshom =  space ).
    l_wa_ekpo-ebeln = l_wa_lips-vgbel.
    l_wa_ekpo-ebelp = l_wa_lips-vgpos.
    APPEND l_wa_ekpo TO l_it_ekpo.
  ENDLOOP.
  SORT l_it_ekpo ASCENDING BY ebeln
                              ebelp.
  DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM l_it_ekpo COMPARING table_line.
* *--- 参照元取得
  CHECK l_it_ekpo[] IS NOT INITIAL.
  SELECT ebeln
         ebelp
         werks
         lgort
    INTO TABLE it_lips_ekpo
    FROM ekpo
     FOR ALL ENTRIES IN l_it_ekpo
   WHERE ebeln = l_it_ekpo-ebeln
     AND ebelp = l_it_ekpo-ebelp.

ENDFORM.                    " SELECT_LIPS_VGBEL

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  SET_LGOBE_9100
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       出荷保管場所名称取得
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->I_v_LGORT_BEF          前保管場所
*      -->I_v_LGORT_SCR          画面保管場所
*      <--O_v_LGOBE              保管場所名称
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM set_lgobe_9100
  USING     i_v_lgort_bef        TYPE ty_9100-lgort
            i_v_lgort_scr        TYPE ty_9100-lgort
            i_v_werks        TYPE ty_9100-werks
  CHANGING  o_v_lgobe            TYPE ty_9100-lgobe.

* *--- 出荷保管場所入力値に変更がある場合のみ実施
  CHECK i_v_lgort_bef <> i_v_lgort_scr.
  SELECT SINGLE lgobe
    INTO o_v_lgobe
    FROM t001l
   WHERE werks   =  i_v_werks"p_werks
     AND lgort   =  i_v_lgort_scr.
*   *--- 該当ﾃﾞｰﾀなし→保管場所名称初期化
  CHECK sy-subrc <> 0.
  CLEAR o_v_lgobe.

ENDFORM.                    " SET_LGOBE_9100

*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM set_tdlnr_name1_9100

  CHECK i_v_tdlnr_bef <> i_v_tdlnr_scr.

    INTO o_v_tdlnr_name1
    FROM lfa1
   WHERE lifnr   =  i_v_tdlnr_scr.

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  EDIT_OVERFLOW_9100
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       引当overflow時処理
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      <--O_WA_9100               引当可能一覧
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM edit_overflow_9100
  CHANGING  o_wa_9100             TYPE ty_9100.

* *--- 処理メッセージの編集
* *--- 「オーバーフローが発生しました。」
  MESSAGE e083(/ccej/otc_atp) WITH space
                       INTO o_wa_9100-message.

  o_wa_9100-icon     = v_icon_red.     "信号機

                             CHANGING  o_wa_9100-sort_icon.

  PERFORM get_sort_staus     USING     o_wa_9100-status
                             CHANGING  o_wa_9100-sort_sta.
* *--- 枝番&賞味期限を入力状態に戻す
  o_wa_9100-edacode = o_wa_9100-edacode_wrk.
