*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  MINUS_FLASHOUT
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       出荷済数量の減算：強制引き当て分
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM minus_flashout .

  DATA :
    l_wa_lips           TYPE ty_lips.                 " 出荷伝票
  FIELD-SYMBOLS :
    <l_fs_zaiko>        TYPE ty_zaiko,                " 在庫ﾃﾞｰﾀ
    <l_fs_yojitsu>      TYPE ty_zaiko-menge_cs.       " 引当可能数(予/実)

  SORT it_zaiko BY werks ASCENDING
                   lgort ASCENDING
                   matnr ASCENDING
                   eindt DESCENDING
                   flg_y ASCENDING   " 実在庫→予定在庫
                   bestq DESCENDING. " 品検中→入荷/製造予定

* *--- 強制引当区分を設定した出荷を対象とする
  LOOP AT it_lips INTO  l_wa_lips
                  WHERE zzskyoseikb IS NOT INITIAL.
    LOOP AT it_zaiko ASSIGNING <l_fs_zaiko>
                     WHERE werks   =  l_wa_lips-werks "p_werks
                       AND lgort   =  l_wa_lips-lgort
                       AND matnr   =  l_wa_lips-matnr
                       AND eindt   <= l_wa_lips-wadat    " 出庫日以前に入荷予定となる在庫を対象とする
                       AND menge_cs > 0.

*     *--- 予/実により､減算先を決定
      IF <l_fs_zaiko>-flg_y = c_on.       " 予定在庫
" Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_APS Conversion **BEGIN OF CHANGE BY EJX9007359 11.06.2026  for ATC
*         ASSIGN COMPONENT c_fld_yotei_cs OF STRUCTURE <l_fs_zaiko>
        ASSIGN COMPONENT c_fld_yotei_cs OF STRUCTURE <l_fs_zaiko> "#EC CI_FLDEXT_OK[0002250183]
" Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_APS Conversion * *END OF CHANGE BY EJX9007359 11.06.2026 for ATC
                                          TO <l_fs_yojitsu>.
      ELSE.                                 " 実在庫
" Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_APS Conversion **BEGIN OF CHANGE BY EJX9007359 11.06.2026  for ATC
*         ASSIGN COMPONENT c_fld_jitu_cs  OF STRUCTURE <l_fs_zaiko>
        ASSIGN COMPONENT c_fld_jitu_cs  OF STRUCTURE <l_fs_zaiko> "#EC CI_FLDEXT_OK[0002250183]
" Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_APS Conversion * *END OF CHANGE BY EJX9007359 11.06.2026 for ATC
                                          TO <l_fs_yojitsu>.
      ENDIF.
*     *--- 引当可能数=>出荷数量
      IF   <l_fs_zaiko>-menge_cs >= l_wa_lips-lfimg.
        <l_fs_zaiko>-menge_cs = <l_fs_zaiko>-menge_cs
                              - l_wa_lips-lfimg.
        <l_fs_yojitsu>        = <l_fs_yojitsu>
                              - l_wa_lips-lfimg.
        l_wa_lips-lfimg       = 0.
*     *--- 引当可能数< 出荷数量
      ELSE.
        l_wa_lips-lfimg       = l_wa_lips-lfimg
                              - <l_fs_zaiko>-menge_cs.
        <l_fs_zaiko>-menge_cs = 0.
        <l_fs_yojitsu>        = 0.
      ENDIF.
      UNASSIGN <l_fs_yojitsu>.
*     *--- 出荷数量の減算が完了したら在庫減算終了
      CHECK l_wa_lips-lfimg = 0.
      EXIT.
    ENDLOOP.

  ENDLOOP.

ENDFORM.                    " MINUS_FLASHOUT
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  MINUS_JITU_LETYP
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       出荷済数量減算(ﾊﾟﾚｯﾄ種別考慮)
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->I_FLG_Y            予定在庫ﾌﾗｸﾞ
*      -->I_v_BESTQ         在庫ｶﾃｺﾞﾘ
*      <--O_wa_LIPS          出荷伝票
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM minus_jitu_letyp
  USING     i_flg_y               TYPE ty_zaiko-flg_y
            i_v_bestq            TYPE ty_zaiko-bestq
  CHANGING  o_wa_lips             TYPE ty_lips.

* ローカル定義
  FIELD-SYMBOLS:
    <l_fs_zaiko>   TYPE ty_zaiko,           " 在庫ﾃﾞｰﾀ
    <l_fs_yojitu>  TYPE any.                " 予定or実績引当済数量

  SORT it_zaiko BY werks          " ﾌﾟﾗﾝﾄ
                   lgort          " 保管場所
                   matnr          " 品目
                   eda            " 枝番
                   kiboshom       " 賞味期限
                   letyp.         " ﾊﾟﾚｯﾄ種別

* *--- 品目&ﾌﾟﾗﾝﾄ&保管場所&枝番&賞味期限&ﾊﾟﾚｯﾄ種別
  LOOP AT it_zaiko ASSIGNING <l_fs_zaiko>
                   WHERE matnr    =  o_wa_lips-matnr       " 品目
                     AND werks    =  o_wa_lips-werks       " ﾌﾟﾗﾝﾄ
                     AND lgort    =  o_wa_lips-lgort       " 保管場所
                     AND eda      IN rng_eda               " 枝番
                     AND kiboshom IN rng_syomi             " 賞味期限
                     AND letyp    =  o_wa_lips-plcode      " ﾊﾟﾚｯﾄ種別
                     AND bestq    =  i_v_bestq            " 在庫ｶﾃｺﾞﾘ
                     AND flg_y    =  i_flg_y.              " 予定在庫

*   *--- 在庫ｶﾃｺﾞﾘによって､減算する引当可能数量の項目を決定(ASSIGN割当不可は想定しない)
    CASE i_v_bestq.
      WHEN c_bestq_q.      "品検中
" Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_APS Conversion **BEGIN OF CHANGE BY EJX9007359 11.06.2026  for ATC
*         ASSIGN COMPONENT c_fld_yotei_cs OF STRUCTURE <l_fs_zaiko> TO <l_fs_yojitu>.
        ASSIGN COMPONENT c_fld_yotei_cs OF STRUCTURE <l_fs_zaiko> TO <l_fs_yojitu>. "#EC CI_FLDEXT_OK[0002250183]
" Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_APS Conversion * *END OF CHANGE BY EJX9007359 11.06.2026 for ATC
      WHEN OTHERS.           "品検中以外
" Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_APS Conversion **BEGIN OF CHANGE BY EJX9007359 11.06.2026  for ATC
*         ASSIGN COMPONENT c_fld_jitu_cs  OF STRUCTURE <l_fs_zaiko> TO <l_fs_yojitu>.
        ASSIGN COMPONENT c_fld_jitu_cs  OF STRUCTURE <l_fs_zaiko> TO <l_fs_yojitu>. "#EC CI_FLDEXT_OK[0002250183]
" Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_APS Conversion * *END OF CHANGE BY EJX9007359 11.06.2026 for ATC
    ENDCASE.

*   *--- 引当可能数量>0の在庫から減算
    CHECK <l_fs_zaiko>-menge_cs > 0.
    IF i_v_bestq = c_bestq_q.
      v_alloc_seq = v_alloc_seq + 1.
      <l_fs_zaiko>-stck_alloc_seq = v_alloc_seq.
    ENDIF.
*   *--- 在庫数量>=出荷数量
    IF <l_fs_zaiko>-menge_cs >= o_wa_lips-lfimg.

*     *--- 在庫数量から出荷数量を減算
      <l_fs_zaiko>-menge_cs =  <l_fs_zaiko>-menge_cs
                            -  o_wa_lips-lfimg.
      <l_fs_yojitu>         =  <l_fs_yojitu>
                            -  o_wa_lips-lfimg.
*     *--- 出荷数量の減算完了
      o_wa_lips-lfimg       =  0.
*   *--- 在庫数量<出荷数量
    ELSE.
*     *--- 出荷数量から在庫数量を減算
      o_wa_lips-lfimg       =  o_wa_lips-lfimg
                            -  <l_fs_zaiko>-menge_cs.
*     *--- 在庫数量は0とする
      <l_fs_zaiko>-menge_cs =  0.
      <l_fs_yojitu>         =  0.
    ENDIF.

    IF i_v_bestq = c_bestq_q.
      <l_fs_zaiko>-stck_qrem = <l_fs_zaiko>-menge_cs.
    ENDIF.

*   *--- 引当済数量 = 0 の場合は､LOOPを抜ける
    CHECK o_wa_lips-lfimg = 0.
    EXIT.
  ENDLOOP.

ENDFORM.                    " MINUS_JITU_LETYP
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  RETURN_ZAIKO_MENGE
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       在庫返却
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->I_v_VBELN              伝票番号
*      -->I_v_POSNR              明細番号
*      -->I_v_KBN                区分
*      <--O_IT_HIKIATE            引当済ﾃﾞｰﾀ
*      <--O_IT_ZAIKO              在庫ﾃﾞｰﾀ
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM return_zaiko_menge
  USING     i_v_vbeln            TYPE ty_9100-vbeln
            i_v_posnr            TYPE ty_9100-posnr
            i_v_kbn              TYPE ty_9100-kbn
  CHANGING  o_it_hikiate          TYPE tbl_ty_hikiate
            o_it_zaiko            TYPE tbl_ty_zaiko.

  DATA :
    l_wa_hikiate        TYPE LINE OF tbl_ty_hikiate.
  FIELD-SYMBOLS :
    <l_fs_zaiko>        TYPE LINE OF tbl_ty_zaiko.

  LOOP AT o_it_hikiate INTO  l_wa_hikiate
                       WHERE vbeln = i_v_vbeln
                         AND posnr = i_v_posnr
                         AND kbn   = i_v_kbn.
    READ TABLE o_it_zaiko ASSIGNING <l_fs_zaiko>
                         WITH KEY werks    = l_wa_hikiate-werks
                                  lgort    = l_wa_hikiate-lgort
                                  matnr    = l_wa_hikiate-matnr
" Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_A Conversion **BEGIN OF CHANGE BY EJX9007359 03.06.2026  FOR ATC
*                                   eda      = l_wa_hikiate-edacode+0(4)
                                  eda      = l_wa_hikiate-edacode+0(4) "#EC CI_FLDEXT_OK[2215424]
" Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_A Conversion * *END OF CHANGE BY EJX9007359 03.06.2026 FOR ATC
                                  kiboshom = l_wa_hikiate-syomi
                                  eindt    = l_wa_hikiate-eindt
                                  letyp    = l_wa_hikiate-plcode+0(3)
                                  bestq    = l_wa_hikiate-bestq
                                  charg    = l_wa_hikiate-charg
                                  hsdat    = l_wa_hikiate-hsdat
                                  flg_y    = l_wa_hikiate-flg_y
                                  flg_ekpo = l_wa_hikiate-flg_ekpo
                                  flg_afpo = l_wa_hikiate-flg_afpo.
    CHECK <l_fs_zaiko> IS ASSIGNED.
    <l_fs_zaiko>-menge_cs = <l_fs_zaiko>-menge_cs + l_wa_hikiate-sumi_cs.
    <l_fs_zaiko>-sumi_cs  = <l_fs_zaiko>-sumi_cs  - l_wa_hikiate-sumi_cs.
    IF <l_fs_zaiko>-flg_y = c_on.
      <l_fs_zaiko>-yotei_cs = <l_fs_zaiko>-yotei_cs + l_wa_hikiate-sumi_cs.
    ELSE.
      <l_fs_zaiko>-jitu_cs  = <l_fs_zaiko>-jitu_cs  + l_wa_hikiate-sumi_cs.
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.
  DELETE o_it_hikiate WHERE vbeln = i_v_vbeln
                        AND posnr = i_v_posnr
                        AND kbn   = i_v_kbn.

ENDFORM.                    " RETURN_ZAIKO_MENGE
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  REWRITE_ZAIKO_EDIT_HIKIATE
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       在庫書き戻しと引当情報作成
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->I_v_VBELN              伝票番号
*      -->I_v_POSNR              明細
*      -->I_v_KBN                区分
*      -->I_v_LGORT              保管場所
*      -->I_v_MATNR              品目
*      -->I_IT_ZAIKO              引当処理後在庫
*      <--O_IT_HIKIATE            引当情報
*      <--O_IT_ZAIKO              引当処理前
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM rewrite_zaiko_edit_hikiate
  USING     i_v_vbeln            TYPE ty_9100-vbeln
            i_v_posnr            TYPE ty_9100-posnr
            i_v_kbn              TYPE ty_9100-kbn
            i_v_lgort            TYPE ty_9100-lgort
            i_v_matnr            TYPE ty_9100-matnr
            i_it_zaiko            TYPE tbl_ty_zaiko
            l_v_werks            TYPE ty_9100-werks
  CHANGING  o_it_zaiko            TYPE tbl_ty_zaiko.

  DATA :
    l_wa_start          TYPE LINE OF tbl_ty_zaiko,
    l_wa_zaiko          TYPE LINE OF tbl_ty_zaiko,
    l_wa_hikiate        TYPE LINE OF tbl_ty_hikiate.

* *--- 開始前のﾃﾞｰﾀを確認
  LOOP AT o_it_zaiko INTO  l_wa_start
                     WHERE werks = l_v_werks "p_werks
                       AND lgort = i_v_lgort
                       AND matnr = i_v_matnr.
*   *--- 開始時点の在庫と終了時点の在庫で差分があるものを引当情報に保持
    READ TABLE i_it_zaiko INTO l_wa_zaiko
                           WITH KEY werks    = l_wa_start-werks
                                    lgort    = l_wa_start-lgort
                                    matnr    = l_wa_start-matnr
                                    eda      = l_wa_start-eda
                                    kiboshom = l_wa_start-kiboshom
                                    eindt    = l_wa_start-eindt
                                    letyp    = l_wa_start-letyp
                                    bestq    = l_wa_start-bestq
                                    charg    = l_wa_start-charg
                                    hsdat    = l_wa_start-hsdat
                                    flg_y    = l_wa_start-flg_y
                                    flg_ekpo = l_wa_start-flg_ekpo
                                    flg_afpo = l_wa_start-flg_afpo
                           TRANSPORTING menge_cs
                                        jitu_cs
                                        yotei_cs
                                        sumi_cs.
*   *--- 数量項目に変更がない場合､何もしない
    IF    l_wa_start-menge_cs = l_wa_zaiko-menge_cs
      AND l_wa_start-jitu_cs  = l_wa_zaiko-jitu_cs
      AND l_wa_start-yotei_cs = l_wa_zaiko-yotei_cs
      AND l_wa_start-sumi_cs  = l_wa_zaiko-sumi_cs.
      CLEAR :
        l_wa_zaiko.
      CONTINUE.
*   *--- 数量項目に変更がある場合､変更情報を保持
    ELSE.
*    *--- 引当情報へ登録
      l_wa_hikiate-werks    =  l_wa_start-werks.
      l_wa_hikiate-lgort    =  l_wa_start-lgort.
      l_wa_hikiate-matnr    =  l_wa_start-matnr.
      l_wa_hikiate-edacode  =  l_wa_start-eda.
      l_wa_hikiate-syomi    =  l_wa_start-kiboshom.
      l_wa_hikiate-eindt    =  l_wa_start-eindt.
      l_wa_hikiate-flg_y    =  l_wa_start-flg_y.
      l_wa_hikiate-bestq    =  l_wa_start-bestq.
      l_wa_hikiate-plcode   =  l_wa_start-letyp.
      l_wa_hikiate-kbn      =  i_v_kbn.
      l_wa_hikiate-vbeln    =  i_v_vbeln.
      l_wa_hikiate-posnr    =  i_v_posnr.
      l_wa_hikiate-charg    =  l_wa_start-charg.
      l_wa_hikiate-hsdat    =  l_wa_start-hsdat.
      l_wa_hikiate-flg_ekpo =  l_wa_start-flg_ekpo.
      l_wa_hikiate-flg_afpo =  l_wa_start-flg_afpo.
*     *--- 開始時点の数量-終了時点の数量を引当数量とする
      l_wa_hikiate-sumi_cs = l_wa_start-menge_cs - l_wa_zaiko-menge_cs.
      APPEND l_wa_hikiate TO it_hikiate.
      CLEAR :
        l_wa_hikiate,
        l_wa_zaiko.
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.
  o_it_zaiko[] = i_it_zaiko[].

ENDFORM.                    " REWRITE_ZAIKO_EDIT_HIKIATE

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Module  SET_LISTBOX_VALUE_9100  OUTPUT
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       ListBox値の設定
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE set_listbox_value_9100 OUTPUT.

* *--- 強制引当区分ListBox値設定
  PERFORM set_listbox_value_flash_kbn
                             USING     it_flash[].

ENDMODULE.                 " SET_LISTBOX_VALUE_9100  OUTPUT
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  SET_LISTBOX_VALUE_FLASH_KBN
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       ListBox作成
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->I_IT_FLASH              強制引当区分値
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM set_listbox_value_flash_kbn
  USING     i_it_flash            TYPE ty_it_flash.

  DATA :
    l_it_vrm_values     TYPE vrm_values,
    l_wa_vrm_values     TYPE LINE OF vrm_values,
    l_wa_flash          TYPE LINE OF ty_it_flash,
    l_v_vrm_id         TYPE vrm_id.

* *--- ListBox値作成
  LOOP AT i_it_flash INTO l_wa_flash.
    l_wa_vrm_values-key  = l_wa_flash-zzdata1.
    l_wa_vrm_values-text = l_wa_flash-zzdata3.
    APPEND l_wa_vrm_values TO l_it_vrm_values.
    CLEAR l_wa_vrm_values.
  ENDLOOP.
  SORT l_it_vrm_values ASCENDING BY key.
  DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM l_it_vrm_values COMPARING ALL FIELDS.
* *--- ListBox割当
  l_v_vrm_id = 'WA_9100-FLASH_KBN'.
  CALL FUNCTION 'VRM_SET_VALUES'
    EXPORTING
      id              = l_v_vrm_id
      values          = l_it_vrm_values[]
    EXCEPTIONS
      id_illegal_name = 0
      OTHERS          = 0.

*  CLEAR:l_it_vrm_values,l_wa_vrm_values.

ENDFORM.                    " SET_LISTBOX_VALUE_FLASH_KBN
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_FLASH_KBN
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       強制引当区分取得
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->I_v_FLASH         引当区分 KEY1
*      -->I_v_ZZNUMC1       実行権限による分類
*      <--O_IT_FLASH         引当区分
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_flash_kbn
  USING     i_v_flash            TYPE char16 "NA znn0001-zzkey1
            i_v_zznumc1          TYPE numc10 " NA znn0001-zznumc1
  CHANGING  o_it_flash            TYPE ty_it_flash.


  DATA:l_wa_flash TYPE ty_flash.
  CONSTANTS:l_c_1 TYPE char1 VALUE '1',
            l_c_2 TYPE char1 VALUE '2'.

  l_wa_flash-zzdata1 = l_c_1.
  l_wa_flash-zzdata3 = text-025.
  APPEND l_wa_flash TO o_it_flash.
  CLEAR l_wa_flash.

  l_wa_flash-zzdata1 = l_c_2.
  l_wa_flash-zzdata3 = text-026.
  APPEND l_wa_flash TO o_it_flash.
  CLEAR l_wa_flash.

  l_wa_flash-zzdata1 = space.
* l_wa_flash-zzdata3 = text-026.
  APPEND l_wa_flash TO o_it_flash.
  CLEAR l_wa_flash.


*---------NA----------------------
* *--- 既存分は削除
*  DELETE o_it_flash WHERE zzkey2 IS INITIAL.

ENDFORM.                    " GET_FLASH_KBN
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  REVERSE_ZAIKO
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       賞味跨ぎの在庫戻し
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      <--O_v_ZAN                前回の引当可能数
*      <--O_v_MENGE_BEF          前回賞味引当開始時点の数量
*      <--O_MENGE_CS              引当可能残数
*      <--O_IT_ZAIKO              在庫
*      <--O_IT_ZAIKO_BEF          前回賞味期限の在庫
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM reverse_zaiko
  USING     i_v_menge_bef        TYPE ty_9100-menge_cs
  CHANGING  o_v_zan              TYPE vbap-kwmeng
            o_v_menge_cs         TYPE ty_9100-menge_cs
            o_it_zaiko            TYPE tbl_ty_zaiko
            o_it_zaiko_bef        TYPE tbl_ty_zaiko.

  DATA :
    l_wa_zaiko_bef      TYPE LINE OF tbl_ty_zaiko.
  FIELD-SYMBOLS :
    <l_fs_zaiko>        TYPE LINE OF tbl_ty_zaiko.

* *--- 前の賞味期限で引当てた数量を在庫に戻す
  LOOP AT o_it_zaiko_bef INTO l_wa_zaiko_bef.
    READ TABLE o_it_zaiko ASSIGNING <l_fs_zaiko>
                        WITH KEY werks      = l_wa_zaiko_bef-werks
                                 lgort      = l_wa_zaiko_bef-lgort
                                 matnr      = l_wa_zaiko_bef-matnr
                                 eda        = l_wa_zaiko_bef-eda
                                 kiboshom   = l_wa_zaiko_bef-kiboshom
                                 eindt      = l_wa_zaiko_bef-eindt
                                 letyp      = l_wa_zaiko_bef-letyp
                                 bestq      = l_wa_zaiko_bef-bestq
                                 charg      = l_wa_zaiko_bef-charg
                                 hsdat      = l_wa_zaiko_bef-hsdat
                                 flg_y      = l_wa_zaiko_bef-flg_y
                                 flg_hiki   = l_wa_zaiko_bef-flg_hiki
                                 num_hiki   = l_wa_zaiko_bef-num_hiki
                                 flg_ekpo   = l_wa_zaiko_bef-flg_ekpo
                                 flg_afpo   = l_wa_zaiko_bef-flg_afpo.
    CHECK <l_fs_zaiko> IS ASSIGNED.
    <l_fs_zaiko>  = l_wa_zaiko_bef.
    o_v_menge_cs = i_v_menge_bef.
    o_v_zan      = i_v_menge_bef.
    UNASSIGN : <l_fs_zaiko>.
  ENDLOOP.

  REFRESH :
    o_it_zaiko_bef.

ENDFORM.                    " REVERSE_ZAIKO

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  F_GET_VSTEL
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       出荷ポイントの決定処理
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->I_9100_DLV  出荷伝票作成用内部テーブル
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_get_vstel  TABLES i_9100_dlv LIKE it_9100.
  TYPES:BEGIN OF l_ty_vbak_tvak,
      vbeln TYPE vbak-vbeln,   "販売伝票番号
      vsbed TYPE vbak-vsbed,   "出荷条件
      lfarv TYPE tvak-lfarv,   "出荷伝票タイプ初期値
    END OF l_ty_vbak_tvak,
    BEGIN OF l_ty_marc,
      matnr TYPE marc-matnr,   "品目コード
      werks TYPE marc-werks,   "プラント
      ladgr TYPE marc-ladgr,   "積載グループ
    END OF l_ty_marc,
    BEGIN OF l_ty_ekko,
      ebeln TYPE ekko-ebeln,   "購買伝票番号
      bstyp TYPE ekko-bstyp,   "購買伝票カテゴリ
      bsart TYPE ekko-bsart,   "購買伝票タイプ
      reswk TYPE t161v-reswk,  "供給プラント
    END OF l_ty_ekko,
    BEGIN OF l_ty_t161v,
      reswk TYPE t161v-reswk,  "供給プラント
      bstyp TYPE t161v-bstyp,  "購買伝票カテゴリ
      bsart TYPE t161v-bsart,  "購買伝票タイプ
      lfart TYPE t161v-lfart,  "出荷伝票タイプ
    END OF l_ty_t161v,
    BEGIN OF l_ty_ekpv,
      ebeln TYPE ekpv-ebeln,   "購買伝票番号
      ebelp	TYPE ekpv-ebelp,   "購買伝票明細
      vsbed TYPE ekpv-vsbed,   "出荷条件
      ladgr TYPE ekpv-ladgr,   "積載グループ
    END OF l_ty_ekpv.
  DATA:l_it_9100 TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_9100,
       l_wa_9100 TYPE ty_9100,
       l_it_vbak_tvak TYPE SORTED TABLE OF l_ty_vbak_tvak
                      WITH UNIQUE KEY vbeln,
       l_wa_vbak_tvak TYPE l_ty_vbak_tvak,
       l_it_marc  TYPE SORTED TABLE OF l_ty_marc
                      WITH UNIQUE KEY matnr werks,
       l_wa_marc  TYPE  l_ty_marc,
       l_it_ekko  TYPE SORTED TABLE OF l_ty_ekko
                      WITH UNIQUE KEY ebeln,
       l_wa_ekko  TYPE l_ty_ekko,
       l_it_ekko_con  TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF l_ty_ekko,
       l_it_t161v TYPE SORTED TABLE OF l_ty_t161v
                      WITH UNIQUE KEY reswk bstyp bsart,
       l_wa_t161v TYPE l_ty_t161v,
       l_it_ekpv  TYPE SORTED TABLE OF l_ty_ekpv
                      WITH UNIQUE KEY ebeln ebelp,
       l_wa_ekpv  TYPE l_ty_ekpv,
       l_v_vstel TYPE tvstz-vstel.

  FIELD-SYMBOLS: <l_wa_ekko> TYPE l_ty_ekko,
                 <l_wa_9100> TYPE ty_9100.

  CHECK i_9100_dlv[] IS NOT INITIAL.

  IF wa_9100-kbn = text-001.
* 受注
*   受注伝票ヘッダ/販売伝票タイプテーブルから、出荷条件/出荷伝票タイプを取得
    l_it_9100[] = i_9100_dlv[].
    SORT l_it_9100 BY vbeln.
    DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM l_it_9100 COMPARING vbeln.
    IF l_it_9100[] IS NOT INITIAL.
      SELECT v~vbeln       "販売伝票番号
             v~vsbed       "出荷条件
             t~lfarv       "出荷伝票タイプ初期値
          INTO TABLE l_it_vbak_tvak
      FROM vbak AS v
        INNER JOIN tvak AS t
        ON v~auart = t~auart
      FOR ALL ENTRIES IN l_it_9100
      WHERE vbeln = l_it_9100-vbeln.
    ENDIF.

*   品目マスタ（プラントデータ）から、積載グループを取得
    l_it_9100[] = i_9100_dlv[].
    SORT l_it_9100 BY matnr werks.
    DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM l_it_9100 COMPARING matnr werks.
    IF l_it_9100[] IS NOT INITIAL.
      SELECT matnr     "品目コード
             werks     "プラント
             ladgr     "積載グループ
        INTO TABLE l_it_marc
      FROM marc
        FOR ALL ENTRIES IN l_it_9100
      WHERE matnr = l_it_9100-matnr
        AND werks = l_it_9100-werks.
    ENDIF.

    LOOP AT i_9100_dlv ASSIGNING <l_wa_9100>.
      CLEAR:l_wa_vbak_tvak,
            l_wa_marc,
            l_v_vstel.

*     出荷条件／出荷伝票タイプ取得
      READ TABLE l_it_vbak_tvak INTO l_wa_vbak_tvak
        WITH  KEY vbeln = <l_wa_9100>-vbeln
                       BINARY SEARCH.

*     積積グループ取得
      READ TABLE l_it_marc INTO l_wa_marc
        WITH  KEY matnr = <l_wa_9100>-matnr
                       werks = <l_wa_9100>-werks
                       BINARY SEARCH.

*     汎用モジュールを使用し、出荷ポイントを取得
      CALL FUNCTION 'VERSANDSTELLE_ERMITTELN'
        EXPORTING
          ladgr                  = l_wa_marc-ladgr         "イ)で取得した品目グループ
          lgort                  = <l_wa_9100>-lgort       "LOOPレコードの出荷元保管場所
          vsbed                  = l_wa_vbak_tvak-vsbed    "ア)で取得した出荷条件
          werks                  = <l_wa_9100>-werks       "LOOPレコードの出荷元プラント
          if_lfart               = l_wa_vbak_tvak-lfarv    "ア)で取得した出荷伝票タイプ初期値
          vstel_i                = space                   "空白
        IMPORTING
          vstel                  = l_v_vstel
        EXCEPTIONS
          fehler_bei_lesen_tvst  = 1
          fehler_bei_lesen_tvstz = 2
          vstel_nicht_erlaubt    = 3
          OTHERS                 = 4.

      IF sy-subrc = 0.
        <l_wa_9100>-vstel = l_v_vstel.   "出荷ポイント
      ENDIF.
    ENDLOOP.

  ELSEIF wa_9100-kbn = text-002.
* 転送発注

    l_it_9100[] = i_9100_dlv[].
    SORT l_it_9100 BY vbeln.
    DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM l_it_9100 COMPARING vbeln.
    IF l_it_9100[] IS NOT INITIAL.
      SELECT ebeln    "購買伝票番号
             bstyp    "購買伝票カテゴリ
             bsart    "購買伝票タイプ
        INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE l_it_ekko
      FROM ekko
        FOR ALL ENTRIES IN l_it_9100
      WHERE ebeln = l_it_9100-vbeln.
    ENDIF.

*   供給プラント設定
    LOOP AT l_it_ekko ASSIGNING <l_wa_ekko>.
      READ TABLE l_it_9100 INTO l_wa_9100
          WITH KEY vbeln = <l_wa_ekko>-ebeln
          BINARY SEARCH.
      IF sy-subrc = 0.
        <l_wa_ekko>-reswk = l_wa_9100-werks."供給プラント
      ENDIF.
    ENDLOOP.

*   供給プラント/購買伝票カテゴリ/購買伝票タイプでソート
    l_it_ekko_con[] = l_it_ekko[].
    SORT l_it_ekko_con BY reswk bstyp bsart.
    IF l_it_ekko_con[] IS NOT INITIAL.

* Quick Fix Append ORDER BY PRIMARY KEY to the SELECT statement
* 05.06.2026 08:05:57 EJX9007361
* Transport Q4RK902230 ATC Correction S4 HANA
* Replaced Code:
*      SELECT reswk    "供給プラント
*             bstyp    "購買伝票カテゴリ
*             bsart    "購買伝票タイプ
*             lfart    "出荷伝票タイプ
*        INTO TABLE l_it_t161v
*      FROM t161v
*        FOR ALL ENTRIES IN l_it_ekko_con
*      WHERE reswk = l_it_ekko_con-reswk
*        AND bstyp = l_it_ekko_con-bstyp
*        AND bsart = l_it_ekko_con-bsart.
      SELECT reswk    "供給プラント
             bstyp    "購買伝票カテゴリ
             bsart    "購買伝票タイプ
             lfart    "出荷伝票タイプ
        INTO TABLE l_it_t161v
      FROM t161v
        FOR ALL ENTRIES IN l_it_ekko_con
      WHERE reswk = l_it_ekko_con-reswk
        AND bstyp = l_it_ekko_con-bstyp
        AND bsart = l_it_ekko_con-bsart ORDER BY PRIMARY KEY.
* End of Quick Fix

    ENDIF.

*   購買伝票明細の出荷データ等から、出荷条件・積載グループ・出荷ポイントを取得

    l_it_9100[] = i_9100_dlv[].
    SORT l_it_9100 BY vbeln.
    DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM l_it_9100 COMPARING vbeln.
    IF l_it_9100[] IS NOT INITIAL.
      SELECT ebeln   "購買伝票番号
             ebelp   "購買伝票明細
             vsbed   "出荷条件
             ladgr   "積載グループ
        INTO TABLE l_it_ekpv
      FROM ekpv
        FOR ALL ENTRIES IN l_it_9100
      WHERE ebeln = l_it_9100-vbeln.
    ENDIF.


    LOOP AT i_9100_dlv ASSIGNING <l_wa_9100>.
      CLEAR:l_wa_ekko,
            l_wa_t161v,
            l_wa_ekpv,
            l_v_vstel.

*     出荷伝票タイプ取得
      READ TABLE l_it_ekko INTO l_wa_ekko
        WITH  KEY ebeln = <l_wa_9100>-vbeln
                  BINARY SEARCH.

      READ TABLE l_it_t161v INTO l_wa_t161v
        WITH KEY reswk = <l_wa_9100>-werks
                       bstyp = l_wa_ekko-bstyp
                       bsart = l_wa_ekko-bsart
                       BINARY SEARCH.

*     出荷条件・積載グループ・出荷ポイント取得
      READ TABLE l_it_ekpv INTO l_wa_ekpv
        WITH KEY ebeln = <l_wa_9100>-vbeln
                       ebelp = <l_wa_9100>-posnr+1(5)
                       BINARY SEARCH.

*     汎用モジュールを使用し、出荷ポイントを取得
      CALL FUNCTION 'VERSANDSTELLE_ERMITTELN'
        EXPORTING
          ladgr                  = l_wa_ekpv-ladgr        "イ)で取得した積載グループ
          lgort                  = <l_wa_9100>-lgort      "LOOPレコードの出荷元保管場所
          vsbed                  = l_wa_ekpv-vsbed        "イ)で取得した出荷条件
          werks                  = <l_wa_9100>-werks      "LOOPレコードの出荷元プラント
          if_lfart               = l_wa_t161v-lfart       "ア)で取得した出荷伝票タイプ
          vstel_i                = space                  "空白
        IMPORTING
          vstel                  = l_v_vstel
        EXCEPTIONS
          fehler_bei_lesen_tvst  = 1
          fehler_bei_lesen_tvstz = 2
          vstel_nicht_erlaubt    = 3
          OTHERS                 = 4.

      IF sy-subrc = 0.
        <l_wa_9100>-vstel = l_v_vstel.   "出荷ポイント
      ENDIF.
    ENDLOOP.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    " F_GET_VSTEL

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  f_get_vstel1
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->FP_BAPI_EKKO  text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_get_vstel1  USING fp_bapi_ekko TYPE ty_bapi_ekko.
  TYPES:BEGIN OF l_ty_vbak_tvak,
      vbeln TYPE vbak-vbeln,   "販売伝票番号
      vsbed TYPE vbak-vsbed,   "出荷条件
      lfarv TYPE tvak-lfarv,   "出荷伝票タイプ初期値
    END OF l_ty_vbak_tvak,
    BEGIN OF l_ty_marc,
      matnr TYPE marc-matnr,   "品目コード
      werks TYPE marc-werks,   "プラント
      ladgr TYPE marc-ladgr,   "積載グループ
    END OF l_ty_marc,
    BEGIN OF l_ty_ekko,
      ebeln TYPE ekko-ebeln,   "購買伝票番号
      bstyp TYPE ekko-bstyp,   "購買伝票カテゴリ
      bsart TYPE ekko-bsart,   "購買伝票タイプ
      reswk TYPE t161v-reswk,  "供給プラント
    END OF l_ty_ekko,
    BEGIN OF l_ty_t161v,
      reswk TYPE t161v-reswk,  "供給プラント
      bstyp TYPE t161v-bstyp,  "購買伝票カテゴリ
      bsart TYPE t161v-bsart,  "購買伝票タイプ
      lfart TYPE t161v-lfart,  "出荷伝票タイプ
    END OF l_ty_t161v,
    BEGIN OF l_ty_ekpv,
      ebeln TYPE ekpv-ebeln,   "購買伝票番号
      ebelp	TYPE ekpv-ebelp,   "購買伝票明細
      vsbed TYPE ekpv-vsbed,   "出荷条件
      ladgr TYPE ekpv-ladgr,   "積載グループ
    END OF l_ty_ekpv.

  DATA:l_it_9100 TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_9100,
       l_wa_9100 TYPE ty_9100,
       l_it_vbak_tvak TYPE SORTED TABLE OF l_ty_vbak_tvak
                      WITH UNIQUE KEY vbeln,
       l_wa_vbak_tvak TYPE l_ty_vbak_tvak,
       l_it_marc  TYPE SORTED TABLE OF l_ty_marc
                      WITH UNIQUE KEY matnr werks,
       l_wa_marc  TYPE  l_ty_marc,
       l_it_ekko  TYPE SORTED TABLE OF l_ty_ekko
                      WITH UNIQUE KEY ebeln,
       l_wa_ekko  TYPE l_ty_ekko,
       l_it_ekko_con  TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF l_ty_ekko,
       l_it_t161v TYPE SORTED TABLE OF l_ty_t161v
                      WITH UNIQUE KEY reswk bstyp bsart,
       l_wa_t161v TYPE l_ty_t161v,
       l_it_ekpv  TYPE SORTED TABLE OF l_ty_ekpv
                      WITH UNIQUE KEY ebeln ebelp,
       l_wa_ekpv  TYPE l_ty_ekpv,
       l_v_vstel TYPE tvstz-vstel.

  FIELD-SYMBOLS: <l_wa_ekko> TYPE l_ty_ekko,
                 <l_wa_9100> TYPE ty_9100.

  CLEAR:wa_bapi_po_data.
  READ TABLE i_bapi_po_data INTO wa_bapi_po_data WITH KEY vbeln = fp_bapi_ekko-ebeln.
  IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
    SELECT reswk    "供給プラント
           bstyp    "購買伝票カテゴリ
           bsart    "購買伝票タイプ
           lfart    "出荷伝票タイプ
      INTO TABLE l_it_t161v
    FROM t161v
    WHERE reswk = wa_bapi_po_data-werks
      AND bstyp = fp_bapi_ekko-bstyp
      AND bsart = fp_bapi_ekko-bsart.


    SELECT ebeln   "購買伝票番号
           ebelp   "購買伝票明細
           vsbed   "出荷条件
           ladgr   "積載グループ
      INTO TABLE l_it_ekpv
    FROM ekpv
    WHERE ebeln = fp_bapi_ekko-ebeln.



    CLEAR:l_wa_ekko,
          l_wa_t161v,
          l_wa_ekpv,
          l_v_vstel.


    READ TABLE l_it_t161v INTO l_wa_t161v
      WITH KEY reswk = wa_bapi_po_data-werks
                     bstyp = fp_bapi_ekko-bstyp
                     bsart = fp_bapi_ekko-bsart.


*     出荷条件・積載グループ・出荷ポイント取得
    READ TABLE l_it_ekpv INTO l_wa_ekpv
      WITH KEY ebeln = wa_bapi_po_data-vbeln
               ebelp = wa_bapi_po_data-posnr+1(5).

    IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
*     汎用モジュールを使用し、出荷ポイントを取得
      CALL FUNCTION 'VERSANDSTELLE_ERMITTELN'
        EXPORTING
          ladgr                  = l_wa_ekpv-ladgr        "イ)で取得した積載グループ
          lgort                  = wa_bapi_po_data-lgort      "LOOPレコードの出荷元保管場所
          vsbed                  = l_wa_ekpv-vsbed        "イ)で取得した出荷条件
          werks                  = wa_bapi_po_data-werks      "LOOPレコードの出荷元プラント
          if_lfart               = l_wa_t161v-lfart       "ア)で取得した出荷伝票タイプ
          vstel_i                = space                  "空白
        IMPORTING
          vstel                  = l_v_vstel
        EXCEPTIONS
          fehler_bei_lesen_tvst  = 1
          fehler_bei_lesen_tvstz = 2
          vstel_nicht_erlaubt    = 3
          OTHERS                 = 4.

      IF sy-subrc = 0.
        v_wanst = l_v_vstel.   "出荷ポイント
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    " F_GET_VSTEL


*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_ERDAT
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       出荷伝票登録日の取得
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_erdat .

  DATA: l_v_bukrs    TYPE bukrs,
        l_v_zzdata1  TYPE char10,"NA znn0001-zzdata1,
        l_v_days     TYPE t5a4a-dlydy.

* 仙台・四国の場合
  CHECK v_routine_007 = c_kosha.

  CLEAR: v_likp_erdat.

* 会社コードの取得
*  CALL FUNCTION 'Z_ZMA1_GET_USER_ORG_INFO'
*    EXPORTING
*      i_uname                    = sy-uname
*      i_date                     = sy-datlo
*    IMPORTING
*      e_bukrs                    = l_v_bukrs     "会社コード
*    EXCEPTIONS
*      user_not_found             = 1
*      bottler_not_found          = 2
*      company_no_org_information = 3
*      OTHERS                     = 4.
  IF sy-subrc <> 0.
*    MESSAGE ID sy-msgid TYPE sy-msgty NUMBER sy-msgno
*            WITH sy-msgv1 sy-msgv2 sy-msgv3 sy-msgv4.
  ELSE.

*   出荷伝票抽出日数の取得
*---------------------NA----------------------
*    SELECT SINGLE zzdata1
*      FROM znn0001
*      INTO l_v_zzdata1
*     WHERE zzkey1 = sy-cprog            "プログラムID
*       AND zzkey2 = l_v_bukrs          "会社コード
*       AND zzkey3 = c_zzkey3_ebdat    "固定値‘EBDAT’
    .
*---------------------NA----------------------
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      l_v_days = l_v_zzdata1.

*     出荷伝票登録日の取得
      CALL FUNCTION 'RP_CALC_DATE_IN_INTERVAL'
        EXPORTING
          date      = sy-datlo        "システム日付
          days      = l_v_days       "出荷伝票抽出日数
          months    = 0
          signum    = c_minus       "‘-’
          years     = 0
        IMPORTING
          calc_date = v_likp_erdat.   "出荷伝票登録日
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    " GET_ERDAT

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  SET_ATTR_SELSCR_AUTH
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       個社別実行権限による選択画面表示/非表示設定
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->I_FLG_AUTH              個社別実行権限
*      -->I_v_ZZSEQNO            ﾙｰﾁﾝ番号(修正grp1に設定)
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_set_attr_selscr_auth
  USING     i_flg_auth                 TYPE c
            i_v_zzseqno               TYPE seqn3."NA ztma0004-zzseqno.
  DATA :
    l_v_group1         TYPE screen-group1.      " 画面修正grp1

  l_v_group1 = i_v_zzseqno.                    " 型変換
  LOOP AT SCREEN.
*   *--- 修正grp1とﾙｰﾁﾝ番号が一致する場合
    CHECK screen-group1 = l_v_group1.
*     *--- 個社別実行権限あり…有効
    IF i_flg_auth   = c_kosha.
      screen-active = c_active.
*     *--- 個社別実行権限なし…無効
    ELSE.
      screen-active = c_non_active.
    ENDIF.
    MODIFY SCREEN.
  ENDLOOP.

*BOC for MOD-030
*Set Default one months date
  IF s_erdat[] IS INITIAL.
    s_erdat-sign   = c_sign_i.
    s_erdat-option = c_opt_eq.
    s_erdat-low = sy-datlo - 30.
    s_erdat-high = sy-datlo.
    APPEND s_erdat TO s_erdat.
  ENDIF.

  IF s_aedat[] IS INITIAL.
    s_aedat-sign   = c_sign_i.
    s_aedat-option = c_opt_eq.
    s_aedat-low = sy-datlo - 30.
    s_aedat-high = sy-datlo.
    APPEND s_aedat TO s_aedat.
  ENDIF.
*EOC for MOD-030


ENDFORM.                    " SET_ATTR_SELSCR_AUTH

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  EDIT_COND_DELIV_RTN_001
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       未引当ﾃﾞｰﾀ 取置区分による絞込み条件作成
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      <--O_RNG_ZSZSHUKKA         絞込み対象出荷先
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM edit_cond_deliv_rtn_001
  CHANGING  o_rng_zszshukka       TYPE ty_rng_zszshukka_001.

  DATA :
    l_wa_rng_zszshukka  TYPE LINE OF ty_rng_zszshukka_001, " 取置区分ﾏｽﾀ
    l_wa_zstmm0068      TYPE LINE OF ty_it_zstmm0068_001.  " 条件

* *--- CCW&仙台&四国 個別の実行権限がある場合のみ実施
  CHECK v_routine_001 = c_kosha.
  REFRESH : o_rng_zszshukka.
* *--- 選択画面のﾁｪｯｸﾎﾞｯｸｽにより絞込み対象を決定
*  CASE space.
**   *--- 通常出荷先引当をしない場合
**    WHEN ck_nmdlv.
*      l_wa_rng_zszshukka-sign   = c_sign_e.    " 特定荷主以外を削除対象とする
*      l_wa_rng_zszshukka-option = c_opt_eq.
**   *--- 特定荷主引当をしない場合
**    WHEN ck_spdlv.
*      l_wa_rng_zszshukka-sign   = c_sign_i.    " 特定荷主を削除対象とする
*      l_wa_rng_zszshukka-option = c_opt_eq.
**   *--- 通常出荷先引当,特定荷主引当とも行う場合
*    WHEN OTHERS.
*      RETURN.
*  ENDCASE.
* *--- 取置区分ﾏｽﾀの出荷先をRangeへ追加
  LOOP AT it_zstmm0068_001 INTO l_wa_zstmm0068.
    l_wa_rng_zszshukka-low = l_wa_zstmm0068-zszshukka.
    APPEND l_wa_rng_zszshukka TO o_rng_zszshukka.
  ENDLOOP.

ENDFORM.                    " EDIT_COND_DELIV_RTN_001
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  LOCK_TRAN_RTN_001
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       CCW&仙台&四国 ﾄﾗﾝｻﾞｸｼｮﾝﾛｯｸ
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      <--O_IT_9100               未引当ﾃﾞｰﾀ
*      <--O_IT_LOCKED             ﾛｯｸ中未引当ﾃﾞｰﾀ
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM lock_tran_rtn_001
  CHANGING  o_it_9100             TYPE tbl_ty_9100
            o_it_locked           TYPE tbl_ty_9100.

  DATA :
    l_it_9100           TYPE SORTED   TABLE OF ty_9100     " ﾛｯｸ対象
                             WITH NON-UNIQUE KEY matnr kunnr2,
    l_wa_9100           TYPE ty_9100,                      " 処理対象ﾃﾞｰﾀ
    l_wa_locked         TYPE ty_9100,                      " ﾛｯｸ中未引当ﾃﾞｰﾀ
    l_v_param1         TYPE funfd,"NA zmm70-param1,                 " 出荷ﾌﾟﾗﾝﾄ
    l_v_param2         TYPE funfd,"NA zmm70-param2,                 " 品目ｺｰﾄﾞ
    l_v_param3         TYPE funfd,"NA zmm70-param3,                 " 出荷先
    l_v_message        TYPE string.                       " ﾒｯｾｰｼﾞﾃｷｽﾄ

* *--- CCW&仙台&四国 個別の実行権限がある場合のみ実施
  CHECK v_routine_001 = c_kosha.
* *--- 未引当ﾃﾞｰﾀを品目&出荷先の一意に編集
  l_it_9100[] = o_it_9100[].
  DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM l_it_9100 COMPARING matnr   " 品目
                                                      kunnr2. " 出荷先

* *--- 品目と出荷先の単位でﾄﾗﾝｻﾞｸｼｮﾝﾛｯｸを実施する
  LOOP AT l_it_9100 INTO l_wa_9100.
    l_v_param1 = p_werks.             " 出荷元ﾌﾟﾗﾝﾄ
    l_v_param2 = l_wa_9100-matnr.     " 品目
    l_v_param3 = l_wa_9100-kunnr2.    " 出荷先
    CALL FUNCTION '/CCEJ/OTC_TCODE_LOCK'
      EXPORTING
        im_tcode              = sy-tcode
        im_param1             = l_v_param1
        im_param2             = l_v_param2
        im_param3             = l_v_param3
        im_kubun              = c_on          " ﾛｯｸ設定
      EXCEPTIONS
        foreign_lock          = 1
        system_failure        = 2
        transaction_not_found = 3
        i_kubun_err           = 4
        OTHERS                = 5.
*   *--- ﾛｯｸｴﾗｰ時
    CHECK sy-subrc <> 0.
*     *--- 返却されたﾒｯｾｰｼﾞのﾃｷｽﾄを生成
    MESSAGE ID     sy-msgid
            TYPE   sy-msgty
            NUMBER sy-msgno
            WITH   sy-msgv1  sy-msgv2  sy-msgv3  sy-msgv4
            INTO   l_v_message.
*     *--- ﾛｯｸ失敗となる未引当ﾃﾞｰﾀをﾛｯｸ中未引当ﾃﾞｰﾀへ移動する
    LOOP AT o_it_9100 INTO  l_wa_locked
                      WHERE matnr  = l_wa_9100-matnr
                        AND kunnr2 = l_wa_9100-kunnr2.
      l_wa_locked-status   = text-010.           " 処理ｽﾃｰﾀｽ:未引当
      l_wa_locked-icon     = v_icon_red.        " ｱｲｺﾝ:赤信号
      l_wa_locked-message  = l_v_message.       " ﾛｯｸｴﾗｰのﾒｯｾｰｼﾞ
      PERFORM get_sort_icon                      " iconでのSORT用の値設定
                           USING     l_wa_locked-icon
                           CHANGING  l_wa_locked-sort_icon.
      PERFORM get_sort_staus                     " 処理ｽﾃｰﾀｽでのSORT用の値設定
                           USING     l_wa_locked-status
                           CHANGING  l_wa_locked-sort_sta.
*       *--- 数量項目設定
      l_wa_locked-zan_out   = 0.     " 引当残数(一覧表示用)
      l_wa_locked-zan_9300  = 0.     " 引当残数(詳細画面用)
      l_wa_locked-kaku_9300 = 0.     " 引当確定数(詳細画面用)
      l_wa_locked-jitu_9300 = 0.     " 在庫数量(詳細画面用)
      APPEND l_wa_locked TO o_it_locked.
    ENDLOOP.
*     *--- ﾛｯｸ中未引当に設定したﾃﾞｰﾀを未引当ﾃﾞｰﾀから削除
    DELETE o_it_9100 WHERE matnr  = l_wa_9100-matnr
                       AND kunnr2 = l_wa_9100-kunnr2.
  ENDLOOP.

ENDFORM.                    " LOCK_TRAN_RTN_001
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  UNLOCK_TRAN_RTN_001
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       CCW&仙台&四国 ﾄﾗﾝｻﾞｸｼｮﾝﾛｯｸ
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM unlock_tran_rtn_001 .

  DATA :
    l_it_9100           TYPE SORTED   TABLE OF ty_9100     " ﾛｯｸ対象
                             WITH NON-UNIQUE KEY matnr kunnr2,
    l_wa_9100           TYPE ty_9100,                      " 処理対象ﾃﾞｰﾀ
    l_v_param1         TYPE funfd,"NA zmm70-param1,                 " 出荷ﾌﾟﾗﾝﾄ
    l_v_param2         TYPE funfd,"NA zmm70-param2,                 " 品目ｺｰﾄﾞ
    l_v_param3         TYPE funfd."NA zmm70-param3.                 " 出荷先

* *--- CCW&仙台&四国 個別の実行権限がある場合のみ実施
  CHECK v_routine_001 = c_kosha.

  l_it_9100[] = it_9100[].
  DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM l_it_9100 COMPARING matnr   " 品目
                                                      kunnr2. " 出荷先
* *--- ﾛｯｸ解除
  LOOP AT l_it_9100 INTO l_wa_9100.
    l_v_param1 = p_werks.             " 出荷元ﾌﾟﾗﾝﾄ
    l_v_param2 = l_wa_9100-matnr.     " 品目
    l_v_param3 = l_wa_9100-kunnr2.    " 出荷先
    CALL FUNCTION '/CCEJ/OTC_TCODE_LOCK'
      EXPORTING
        im_tcode              = sy-tcode
        im_param1             = l_v_param1
        im_param2             = l_v_param2
        im_param3             = l_v_param3
        im_kubun              = space           " ﾛｯｸ解除
      EXCEPTIONS
        foreign_lock          = 0
        system_failure        = 0
        transaction_not_found = 0
        i_kubun_err           = 0
        OTHERS                = 0.
  ENDLOOP.

ENDFORM.                    " UNLOCK_TRAN_RTN_001
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  SELECT_RESERVE_RTN_001
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       在庫･ﾘｻﾞｰﾌﾞﾃﾞｰﾀの取得

FORM select_reserve_rtn_001
  USING     i_rng_lgort           TYPE STANDARD TABLE.

  DATA :
    l_it_9100           TYPE tbl_ty_9100.              " 在庫･ｵｰﾀﾞｰﾘｻﾞｰﾌﾞﾃｰﾌﾞﾙ 検索条件値

* *--- CCW&仙台&四国 個別の実行権限がある場合のみ実施
  CHECK v_routine_001 = c_kosha.
  REFRESH :
    it_zstmm0069_001.
* *--- 在庫･ｵｰﾀﾞｰﾘｻﾞｰﾌﾞﾃｰﾌﾞﾙのﾃﾞｰﾀ取得
  l_it_9100[] = it_9100[].
  SORT l_it_9100 ASCENDING BY matnr.
  DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM l_it_9100 COMPARING matnr.
  CHECK lines( l_it_9100 ) > 0.
* -----------------------NA----------------------------
*  SELECT zszsymwerks            " 出荷ﾌﾟﾗﾝﾄ
*         zszsymlgort            " 出荷保管場所
*         matnr                  " 品目ｺｰﾄﾞ

*         zsztorikbn             " 取置区分

*         vbeln                  " 出荷伝票
*         posnr                  " 出荷明細

*   INTO TABLE it_zstmm0069_001

*   WHERE zszsymwerks  =  p_werks
*     AND zszsymlgort  IN i_rng_lgort

**       AND MATNR        =  L_IT_9100-MATNR.
*     AND matnr        =  l_it_9100-matnr
*     AND zzloevm      =  space.

* *--- 指定賞味分を削除
* -----------------------NA----------------------------
  DELETE it_zstmm0069_001 WHERE zsztorikbn = space.

ENDFORM.                    " SELECT_RESERVE_RTN_001
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  SELECT_HIKIATE_RTN_001
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       CCW･仙台･四国 個社 引当済出荷伝票取得
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM select_hikiate_rtn_001 .

* *--- CCW&仙台&四国 個別の実行権限がある場合のみ実施
  CHECK v_routine_001 = c_kosha.


** *--- 引当済伝票ヘッダ情報取得
*  PERFORM SELECT_VBUK.
*
** *--- 引当済伝票明細情報取得
*  PERFORM SELECT_VBUP.


* *--- 出荷伝票情報取得
  PERFORM select_lips_rtn_001
                             CHANGING  it_lips_001[].

* *--- 在庫転送源泉の場合､発注から転送先情報を取得

* *--- 仙台/四国 実行権限あり
  IF v_routine_002 = c_kosha.
    PERFORM select_lips_vgbel_rtn_002
                             USING     it_lips_001[]
                             CHANGING  it_lips_ekpo[]
                                       it_bednr_002[].
* *--- 仙台/四国 実行権限なし
  ELSE.

    PERFORM select_lips_vgbel_rtn_001
                               USING     it_lips_001[]
                               CHANGING  it_lips_ekpo[].

  ENDIF.

*  FREE :
*    IT_VBUK,
*    IT_VBUP.


ENDFORM.                    " SELECT_HIKIATE_RTN_001
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  SELECT_LIPS_RTN_001
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       CCW･仙台･四国個別 引当済出荷 出荷ﾃﾞｰﾀ取得
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->I_IT_VBUP               明細ｽﾃｰﾀｽ
*      <--O_IT_LIPS               出荷明細
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM select_lips_rtn_001
  CHANGING  o_it_lips             TYPE ty_it_lips_001.

  TYPES:
    BEGIN OF l_ty_vbeln,
      vbeln       TYPE likp-vbeln,        " 出荷伝票
      kunnr       TYPE likp-kunnr,        " 出荷先
      wadat       TYPE likp-wadat,        " 出庫予定日付
    END OF l_ty_vbeln.


  DATA :
    l_it_9100           TYPE tbl_ty_9100.             " 引当対象(検索条件用)

  DATA:
    l_wa_lips_001       TYPE ty_lips_001,
    l_v_tabix          TYPE i.

  DATA:
    l_it_tmp    TYPE ty_it_vbup,
    l_wa_tmp    TYPE ty_vbup,
    l_it_vbuk   TYPE ty_it_vbuk,
    l_it_vbup   TYPE ty_it_vbup.

  DATA:
    l_it_vbeln    TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF l_ty_vbeln,
    l_it_lips     TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_lips_001,
    l_wa_vbeln    TYPE l_ty_vbeln.

  FIELD-SYMBOLS:
    <fs_wa_lips>       TYPE ty_lips_001.


* *--- CCW&仙台&四国 個別の実行権限がある場合のみ実施
  CHECK v_routine_001 = c_kosha.

  REFRESH :
    o_it_lips.


*  CHECK IT_VBUP[] IS NOT INITIAL.

* *--- 引当対象を品目で一意に編集
  l_it_9100[] = it_9100[].
  SORT l_it_9100 ASCENDING BY matnr.
  DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM l_it_9100 COMPARING matnr.


** *--- 品目毎に引当済の出荷伝票取得
*  LOOP AT L_IT_9100 INTO L_WA_9100.



* 仙台・四国の場合
  IF v_routine_007 = c_kosha.

    SELECT vbeln         "出荷伝票
           kunnr         "出荷先
           wadat         "出庫予定日付
      INTO TABLE l_it_vbeln
      FROM likp
     WHERE likp~erdat >= v_likp_erdat      "レコード登録日
       AND likp~lfart IN rng_lfart.         "除外出荷伝票タイプ
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      SORT l_it_vbeln BY vbeln.
      SELECT vbeln         " 出荷伝票
             posnr         " 出荷明細
             matnr         " 品目ｺｰﾄﾞ
             werks         " ﾌﾟﾗﾝﾄ
             lgort         " 保管場所
             lgmng   AS lfimg       " 出荷数量実績(在庫単位)
             meins         " 基本数量単位
             vrkme         " 販売単位
             umvkz         " 販売数量単位から基本数量単位への変換係数の分子
             umvkn         " 販売数量を在庫単位に換算する分母 (除数)
             vgbel         " 参照伝票
             vgpos         " 参照明細
             vgtyp         " 伝票ｶﾃｺﾞﾘ

*             ZZSEDA        " 枝番
*             ZZSKIBOSHOM   " 賞味期限
*             ZZSPLCODE     " ﾊﾟﾚｯﾄ種別
*             zzseda       AS eda       " 枝番"NA
*             zzskiboshom  AS kiboshom  " 賞味期限"Na
*             zzsplcode    AS plcode    " ﾊﾟﾚｯﾄ種別"NA

*             zzskyoseikb   " 強制引当区分"NA
        INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE l_it_lips
        FROM lips
         FOR ALL ENTRIES IN l_it_vbeln
       WHERE vbeln      =  l_it_vbeln-vbeln   "出荷伝票
         AND lips~werks IN s_werks "=  p_werks            "ﾌﾟﾗﾝﾄ
         AND lips~lgort IN rng_lgort.         "保管場所
      LOOP AT l_it_lips ASSIGNING  <fs_wa_lips>.
        READ TABLE l_it_vbeln INTO l_wa_vbeln WITH KEY vbeln = <fs_wa_lips>-vbeln
                                              BINARY SEARCH.
        IF sy-subrc = 0.
          <fs_wa_lips>-kunnr  = l_wa_vbeln-kunnr.        " 出荷先
          <fs_wa_lips>-wadat  = l_wa_vbeln-wadat.        " 出庫予定日付
        ENDIF.
      ENDLOOP.
      APPEND LINES OF l_it_lips TO o_it_lips.
    ENDIF.

  ELSE.



    SELECT lips~vbeln         " 出荷伝票
           lips~posnr         " 出荷明細
           lips~matnr         " 品目ｺｰﾄﾞ
           lips~werks         " ﾌﾟﾗﾝﾄ
           lips~lgort         " 保管場所

*           LIPS~LFIMG         " 出荷数量実績(販売単位)
           lips~lgmng   AS lfimg       " 出荷数量実績(在庫単位)

           lips~meins         " 基本数量単位
           lips~vrkme         " 販売単位
           lips~umvkz         " 販売数量単位から基本数量単位への変換係数の分子
           lips~umvkn         " 販売数量を在庫単位に換算する分母 (除数)
           lips~vgbel         " 参照伝票
           lips~vgpos         " 参照明細
           lips~vgtyp         " 伝票ｶﾃｺﾞﾘ
*           lips~zzseda        " 枝番"NA
*           lips~zzskiboshom   " 賞味期限"NA
*           lips~zzsplcode     " ﾊﾟﾚｯﾄ種別"NA
           likp~wadat         " 出庫予定日付
*           lips~zzskyoseikb   " 強制引当区分"NA
           likp~kunnr         " 出荷先
      APPENDING CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE o_it_lips
      FROM lips
     INNER JOIN likp
        ON lips~vbeln = likp~vbeln   "出荷伝票

*      FOR ALL ENTRIES IN IT_VBUP
*     WHERE LIPS~VBELN =  IT_VBUP-VBELN    "出荷伝票
*       AND LIPS~POSNR =  IT_VBUP-POSNR    "出荷明細


*       AND LIPS~MATNR =  L_WA_9100-MATNR    "品目ｺｰﾄﾞ

*       AND LIPS~WERKS =  P_WERKS            "ﾌﾟﾗﾝﾄ
     WHERE lips~werks IN s_werks "=  p_werks            "ﾌﾟﾗﾝﾄ

       AND likp~lfart IN rng_lfart          "除外出荷伝票タイプ

       AND lips~lgort IN rng_lgort.         "保管場所

  ENDIF.

  LOOP AT o_it_lips INTO l_wa_lips_001.
    l_wa_tmp-vbeln = l_wa_lips_001-vbeln.
    l_wa_tmp-posnr = l_wa_lips_001-posnr.
    APPEND l_wa_tmp TO l_it_tmp.
  ENDLOOP.
* 伝票ステータスの取得
  PERFORM f_get_vbukp USING     l_it_tmp      "出荷伝票
                      CHANGING  l_it_vbuk     "ヘッダステータス
                                l_it_vbup.    "明細ステータス

  LOOP AT o_it_lips INTO l_wa_lips_001.

*
    l_v_tabix  = sy-tabix.


*   ヘッダステータスのチェック
    READ TABLE l_it_vbuk TRANSPORTING NO FIELDS
                         WITH KEY vbeln = l_wa_lips_001-vbeln   "出荷伝票
                         BINARY SEARCH.
    IF sy-subrc <> 0.
      DELETE o_it_lips INDEX l_v_tabix.
      CONTINUE.
    ENDIF.

*   明細ステータスのチェック
    READ TABLE l_it_vbup TRANSPORTING NO FIELDS
                         WITH KEY vbeln = l_wa_lips_001-vbeln   "出荷伝票
                                  posnr = l_wa_lips_001-posnr   "出荷明細
                         BINARY SEARCH.
    IF sy-subrc <> 0.
      DELETE o_it_lips INDEX l_v_tabix.
      CONTINUE.
    ENDIF.

*
    READ TABLE l_it_9100 TRANSPORTING NO FIELDS
               WITH KEY matnr =  l_wa_lips_001-matnr    "品目ｺｰﾄﾞ
                        BINARY SEARCH.
*
    IF sy-subrc <> 0.

*
      DELETE o_it_lips INDEX l_v_tabix.

    ENDIF.

  ENDLOOP.

* 品目、プラント、保管場所、強制引当区分（降順）、希望賞味期限（昇順）、枝番（降順）
  SORT o_it_lips BY matnr       ASCENDING
                    werks       ASCENDING
                    lgort       ASCENDING
                    zzskyoseikb DESCENDING
                    kiboshom    ASCENDING
                    eda         DESCENDING.

ENDFORM.                    " SELECT_LIPS_RTN_001
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  SELECT_LIPS_VGBEL_RTN_001
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       発注源泉出荷伝票の供給元情報取得
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->I_IT_LIPS               引当済出荷伝票
*      <--O_IT_LIPS_EKPO          発注源泉の出荷の参照伝票情報
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM select_lips_vgbel_rtn_001
  USING     i_it_lips             TYPE ty_it_lips_001
  CHANGING  o_it_lips_ekpo        TYPE HASHED TABLE.

  DATA :
    l_it_vgbel     TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_vgbel_001,
    l_wa_lips      TYPE LINE OF ty_it_lips_001,
    l_wa_vgbel     TYPE ty_vgbel_001.

* *--- CCW&仙台&四国 個別の実行権限がある場合のみ実施
  CHECK v_routine_001 = c_kosha.

  REFRESH :
    o_it_lips_ekpo.
* *--- 参照元が購買発注で賞味期限未設定のﾃﾞｰﾀのみ検索条件に設定
  LOOP AT i_it_lips INTO  l_wa_lips
                    WHERE vgtyp      =  c_vbtyv_v
                      AND ( kiboshom IS INITIAL
                         OR kiboshom =  space ).
    l_wa_vgbel-vgbel = l_wa_lips-vgbel.
    l_wa_vgbel-vgpos = l_wa_lips-vgpos.
    APPEND l_wa_vgbel TO l_it_vgbel.
  ENDLOOP.
  SORT l_it_vgbel ASCENDING BY table_line.
  DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM l_it_vgbel COMPARING table_line.
* *--- 参照元取得
  CHECK l_it_vgbel[] IS NOT INITIAL.
  SELECT ebeln
         ebelp
         werks
         lgort
    INTO TABLE o_it_lips_ekpo
    FROM ekpo
     FOR ALL ENTRIES IN l_it_vgbel
   WHERE ebeln = l_it_vgbel-vgbel
     AND ebelp = l_it_vgbel-vgpos.

ENDFORM.                    " SELECT_LIPS_VGBEL_RTN_001
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  EDIT_RESERVE_RTN_001
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       ﾘｻﾞｰﾌﾞ在庫作成
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM edit_reserve_rtn_001 .

  DATA :
    l_wa_zstmm0069      TYPE LINE OF ty_it_zstmm0069_001,  " ﾘｻﾞｰﾌﾞ在庫
    l_v_zsztorisu      TYPE menge13.                      " 取置数量

* *--- CCW&仙台&四国 個別の実行権限がある場合のみ実施
  CHECK v_routine_001 = c_kosha.

  REFRESH :
    it_reserve_001,
    it_hiki_reserve_001.

  SORT it_lips_001 ASCENDING BY vbeln
                                posnr.
  SORT it_zaiko              BY werks     ASCENDING
                                lgort     ASCENDING
                                matnr     ASCENDING
                                kiboshom  ASCENDING
                                eda       ASCENDING
                                eindt     ASCENDING
                                flg_y     ASCENDING
                                bestq     DESCENDING.
  LOOP AT it_zstmm0069_001 INTO l_wa_zstmm0069.
*   *--- 出荷明細に存在するﾘｻﾞｰﾌﾞ在庫は除外して､取置数量を集計
    READ TABLE it_lips_001 TRANSPORTING NO FIELDS BINARY SEARCH
                        WITH KEY vbeln = l_wa_zstmm0069-vbeln
                                 posnr = l_wa_zstmm0069-posnr.
    IF sy-subrc <> 0.
      l_v_zsztorisu = l_v_zsztorisu + l_wa_zstmm0069-zsztorisu.
    ENDIF.
    AT END OF zsztorikbn.
      IF l_v_zsztorisu > 0.
*       *--- ﾘｻﾞｰﾌﾞ在庫と一致する実在庫数量分の移動
        PERFORM adjuwa_stock_reserve_rtn_001
                             USING     l_wa_zstmm0069
                                       space
                                       space
                             CHANGING  l_v_zsztorisu.
*       *--- 品検中在庫から取置数量の移動
        PERFORM adjuwa_stock_reserve_rtn_001
                             USING     l_wa_zstmm0069
                                       c_x
                                       c_bestq_q
                             CHANGING  l_v_zsztorisu.
      ENDIF.
      CLEAR :
        l_v_zsztorisu.
    ENDAT.
  ENDLOOP.
* *--- sort
  SORT it_reserve_001 ASCENDING BY werks
                                   lgort
                                   matnr
                                   kiboshom
                                   eda
                                   zsztorikbn
                                   letyp
                                   flg_y
                                   bestq.
  FREE : it_zstmm0069_001,
         it_lips.

ENDFORM.                    " EDIT_RESERVE_RTN_001
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  EDIT_STOCK_MINUS_RTN_001
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       CCW･仙台･四国 引当済出荷の減算
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM edit_stock_minus_rtn_001 .

  DATA :
    l_it_lips_sp        TYPE ty_it_lips_001,
    l_it_lips_nm        TYPE ty_it_lips_001.

* *--- CCW&仙台&四国 個別の実行権限がある場合のみ実施
  CHECK v_routine_001 = c_kosha.

* *-- 出荷伝票を特定荷主と通常出荷先に分ける
  PERFORM edit_lips_spdlv_nmdlv_rtn_001
                             CHANGING  l_it_lips_sp[]
                                       l_it_lips_nm[].
* *--- 通常出荷先の分の減算
  PERFORM stock_minus_nmdlv_rtn_001
                             USING     l_it_lips_nm[].
* *--- ﾘｻﾞｰﾌﾞ在庫ﾃﾞｰﾀの作成
  PERFORM edit_reserve_rtn_001.

* *--- 特定荷主分の減算
  PERFORM stock_minus_spdlv_rtn_001
                             USING     l_it_lips_sp[].
* *--- 在庫から数量0のものを削除
  DELETE : it_zaiko       WHERE menge_cs = 0,
           it_reserve_001 WHERE menge_cs = 0.
  FREE:

    it_bednr_002,

    it_lips_ekpo,
    it_knvv_min.

ENDFORM.                    " EDIT_STOCK_MINUS_RTN_001
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Module  SET_ATTR_SCREEN_9100_AUTH  OUTPUT
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       標準/個別の画面制御
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE set_attr_screen_9100_auth OUTPUT.

  PERFORM set_attr_screen_9100
                             USING     v_routine_001
                                       c_routine_001
                             CHANGING  tc9100.

ENDMODULE.                 " SET_ATTR_SCREEN_9100_AUTH  OUTPUT
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  SET_ATTR_SCREEN_9100
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       引当可一覧 画面属性設定
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->I_FLG_KOSHA             実行権限ﾌﾗｸﾞ
*      -->I_v_ZZSEQNO            連番
*      <--O_TC_9100               TableControl
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM set_attr_screen_9100
  USING     i_flg_kosha           TYPE c
            i_v_zzseqno          TYPE seqnr "ztma0004-zzseqno
  CHANGING  o_tc_9100             TYPE cxtab_control.

  DATA :
    l_v_group1         TYPE screen-group1.
  FIELD-SYMBOLS :
    <l_fs_cols>         TYPE LINE OF cxtab_control-cols.

  l_v_group1 = i_v_zzseqno.
  LOOP AT o_tc_9100-cols ASSIGNING <l_fs_cols>.
*-------Invisible the column
    IF "<l_fs_cols>-screen-name = 'WA_9100-EDACODE' OR
      <l_fs_cols>-screen-name = 'WA_9100-PLCODE' OR
      <l_fs_cols>-screen-name = 'WA_9100-TDLNR' OR
      <l_fs_cols>-screen-name = 'WA_9100-TDLNR_NAME1' .
      <l_fs_cols>-invisible = c_on.
    ENDIF.

*   BOC for MOD-029
*   Display only if new batch is active for plant
    IF v_valid IS INITIAL.
      IF <l_fs_cols>-screen-name = 'WA_9100-HSDAT' OR
         <l_fs_cols>-screen-name = 'WA_9100-CHARG'.
        <l_fs_cols>-invisible = c_on.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
*   EOC for MOD-029

*   *--- 修正grp1とﾙｰﾁﾝ番号が一致する場合
    CHECK <l_fs_cols>-screen-group1 = l_v_group1.
*     *--- 個別対応の場合
    IF i_flg_kosha = c_kosha.
      <l_fs_cols>-invisible = space.
    ELSE.
      <l_fs_cols>-invisible = c_on.
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.

ENDFORM.                    " SET_ATTR_SCREEN_9100
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  EDIT_DLV_EXTENSION_RTN_001
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       CCW･仙台･四国 個別 出荷 拡張項目設定
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->I_v_POSNR              明細番号
*      -->I_WA_9100_DLV           出荷伝票登録対象
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM edit_dlv_extension_rtn_001
  USING     i_v_posnr            TYPE lips-posnr
            i_wa_9100             TYPE ty_9100.

  DATA :
    l_wa_mm0012         TYPE ty_zssmm0012,          " 拡張項目
    l_wa_extensionin    TYPE bapiparex.          " 拡張項目設定

* *--- CCW&仙台&四国 個別の実行権限がある場合のみ実施
  CHECK v_routine_001 = c_kosha.

  l_wa_mm0012-posnr       = i_v_posnr.          "出荷明細
  l_wa_mm0012-zzseda      = i_wa_9100-edacode.   "枝番
  l_wa_mm0012-zzskiboshom = i_wa_9100-syomi.     "賞味期限
  l_wa_mm0012-zzsplcode   = i_wa_9100-plcode.    "パレット種別
  l_wa_mm0012-lgort       = i_wa_9100-lgort.     "保管場所
  l_wa_mm0012-zzssyukadt  = i_wa_9100-wadat.     "出荷予定日
* *--- 引当区分 "A" の場合は、"X" を設定
  IF i_wa_9100-flash_kbn = c_flash_a_001.
    l_wa_mm0012-zzskyoseikb = c_on.
* *--- 引当区分 "A" 以外は一覧の値を設定
  ELSE.
    l_wa_mm0012-zzskyoseikb = i_wa_9100-flash_kbn.
  ENDIF.

  l_wa_extensionin-structure  = c_bapi_te_zssmm0012.    "BAPIテーブル拡張の構造名
  l_wa_extensionin+30         = l_wa_mm0012.
  APPEND l_wa_extensionin TO it_extensionin.

ENDFORM.                    " EDIT_DLV_EXTENSION_RTN_001
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  CHECK_9100_2_RTN_001
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       CCW･仙台･四国 引当対象データの編集処理（引当結果判定）
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       <--O_WA_9100              引当対象ﾃﾞｰﾀ
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM check_9100_rtn_001
  CHANGING  o_wa_9100             TYPE ty_9100.

* *--- CCW&仙台&四国 個別の実行権限がある場合のみ実施
  CHECK v_routine_001 = c_kosha.

*   *--- 通常出荷先or特定荷主判定
  READ TABLE it_zstmm0068_001 TRANSPORTING NO FIELDS
                      WITH TABLE KEY zszshukka = o_wa_9100-kunnr2.
*   *--- ﾛｯﾄ管理品で特定荷主の場合､取置在庫から引当

*    IF    SY-SUBRC = 0
  IF  (  sy-subrc = 0    "特定荷主
     OR ( v_routine_002 = c_kosha                "仙台&四国 個別
      AND  sy-subrc <> 0                             "通常出荷先
      AND  o_wa_9100-kbn = text-002                  "転送発注
      AND  o_wa_9100-zsztorikbn IS NOT INITIAL ) )   "取置区分あり

    AND o_wa_9100-kakunin = v_icon_location.
    PERFORM check_9100_torioki_rtn_001
                           CHANGING  o_wa_9100.
*   *--- ﾛｯﾄ管理品で通常出荷先､非ﾛｯﾄ管理品は通常在庫から引当
  ELSE.
    PERFORM check_9100_zaiko_rtn_001
                           CHANGING  o_wa_9100.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    " CHECK_9100_RTN_001
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  RETURN_ZAIKO_RTN_001
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       CCW･仙台･四国 引き剥がし
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM return_zaiko_rtn_001 .

  DATA :
    l_wa_9100_bef       TYPE LINE OF tbl_ty_9100,
    l_flg_reserve       TYPE xfeld.

* *--- CCW&仙台&四国 個別の実行権限がある場合のみ実施
  CHECK v_routine_001 = c_kosha.

  LOOP AT it_9100_bef INTO l_wa_9100_bef.
    PERFORM check_reserve_zaiko_rtn_001
                             USING     l_wa_9100_bef-werks"p_werks
                                       l_wa_9100_bef-matnr
                                       l_wa_9100_bef-kunnr2
                             CHANGING  l_flg_reserve.
*   *--- 取置在庫から引当てた分

*    IF L_FLG_RESERVE = c_ON.
    IF l_flg_reserve = c_on    "特定荷主
      OR ( v_routine_002 = c_kosha                "仙台&四国 個別
      AND  l_wa_9100_bef-kbn = text-002               "転送発注
      AND  l_wa_9100_bef-zsztorikbn IS NOT INITIAL ). "取置区分あり

      PERFORM return_reserve_rtn_001
                             USING     l_wa_9100_bef-vbeln
                                       l_wa_9100_bef-posnr
                                       l_wa_9100_bef-kbn
                             CHANGING  it_hiki_reserve_001[]
                                       it_reserve_001[].
*   *--- 通常在庫から引当てた分
    ELSE.
      PERFORM return_zaiko_menge
                             USING     l_wa_9100_bef-vbeln
                                       l_wa_9100_bef-posnr
                                       l_wa_9100_bef-kbn
                             CHANGING  it_hikiate[]
                                       it_zaiko[].
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.

  FREE :
    it_9100_bef.

ENDFORM.                    " RETURN_ZAIKO_RTN_001
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  STOCK_MINUS_SPDLV_RTN_001
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       CCW･四国･仙台 特定荷主出荷伝票数量を在庫から減算する
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      <--I_IT_LIPS               特定荷主出荷伝票
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM stock_minus_spdlv_rtn_001
  USING     i_it_lips             TYPE ty_it_lips_001.

  DATA :
    l_wa_lips           TYPE LINE OF ty_it_lips_001,
    l_wa_marc           TYPE ty_marc.

* *--- CCW&仙台&四国 個別の実行権限がある場合のみ実施
  CHECK v_routine_001 = c_kosha.

  LOOP AT i_it_lips INTO  l_wa_lips.
*   *--- ﾛｯﾄ管理品の判定
    READ TABLE it_marc INTO l_wa_marc BINARY SEARCH
                     WITH KEY matnr = l_wa_lips-matnr
                              werks = l_wa_lips-werks
                     TRANSPORTING xchpf.
*     *--- ﾛｯﾄ管理品&引当区分=SPACEの場合､賞味期限決定
    IF     l_wa_marc-xchpf       = c_on
       AND l_wa_lips-zzskyoseikb = space.
      PERFORM edit_syomi_minus
                           CHANGING  l_wa_lips-lips_stnd.
*    *--- ﾛｯﾄ非管理品の場合､枝番&賞味&ﾊﾟﾚｯﾄ種別を初期化
    ELSEIF l_wa_marc-xchpf       = space.
      CLEAR :
        l_wa_lips-eda,
        l_wa_lips-kiboshom,
        l_wa_lips-plcode.
    ENDIF.

*   *--- 減算対象の条件作成
    PERFORM edit_range_rtn_001
                             USING     l_wa_lips-eda
                                       l_wa_lips-kiboshom
                                       l_wa_lips-wadat
                                       l_wa_lips-zzskyoseikb.
*   *--- ﾊﾟﾚｯﾄ種別に入力あり->ﾊﾟﾚｯﾄ種別指定で実在庫減算
    IF l_wa_lips-plcode IS NOT INITIAL.
      PERFORM minus_jitu_letyp_rtn_001
                             USING     space
                                       space
                             CHANGING  l_wa_lips.
    ENDIF.
*   *--- 減算未完了orﾊﾟﾚｯﾄ種別未入力->ﾊﾟﾚｯﾄ種別未指定で実在庫減算
    CHECK l_wa_lips-plcode IS INITIAL
       OR ( l_wa_lips-plcode IS NOT INITIAL
        AND l_wa_lips-lfimg  >  0 ).
    PERFORM minus_jitu_rtn_001
                           USING     space
                                     space
                           CHANGING  l_wa_lips.
*   *--- 減算未完了andﾊﾟﾚｯﾄ種別に入力あり->ﾊﾟﾚｯﾄ種別指定で品検中在庫減算
    CHECK l_wa_lips-plcode IS INITIAL
       OR ( l_wa_lips-plcode IS NOT INITIAL
        AND l_wa_lips-lfimg  >  0 ).
    IF l_wa_lips-plcode IS NOT INITIAL.
      PERFORM minus_jitu_letyp_rtn_001
                             USING     c_on
                                       c_bestq_q
                             CHANGING  l_wa_lips.
    ENDIF.
*   *--- 減算未完了orﾊﾟﾚｯﾄ種別未入力->ﾊﾟﾚｯﾄ種別未指定で品検中在庫減算
    CHECK l_wa_lips-plcode IS INITIAL
       OR ( l_wa_lips-plcode IS NOT INITIAL
        AND l_wa_lips-lfimg  >  0 ).
    PERFORM minus_jitu_rtn_001
                           USING     c_on
                                     c_bestq_q
                           CHANGING  l_wa_lips.
  ENDLOOP.

ENDFORM.                    " STOCK_MINUS_SPDLV_RTN_001
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  STOCK_MINUS_NMDLV_RTN_001
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       CCW･仙台･四国 通常出荷先の減算
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->I_IT_LIPS               出荷伝票
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM stock_minus_nmdlv_rtn_001
  USING     i_it_lips             TYPE ty_it_lips_001.

  DATA :
    l_wa_lips           TYPE LINE OF ty_it_lips_001.

* *--- CCW&仙台&四国 個別の実行権限がある場合のみ実施
  CHECK v_routine_001 = c_kosha.

* *--- 指定賞味出荷の減算
  LOOP AT i_it_lips INTO  l_wa_lips
                    WHERE zzskyoseikb = c_flash_b_001.
    PERFORM minus_nm_zaiko_rtn_001
                             CHANGING  l_wa_lips-lips_stnd.
  ENDLOOP.
* *--- 以降賞味(逆転考慮なし)出荷の減算
  LOOP AT i_it_lips INTO  l_wa_lips
                    WHERE zzskyoseikb = c_x.
    PERFORM minus_nm_zaiko_rtn_001
                             CHANGING  l_wa_lips-lips_stnd.
  ENDLOOP.
* *--- 以降賞味(逆転考慮)出荷の減算
  LOOP AT i_it_lips INTO  l_wa_lips
                    WHERE zzskyoseikb = space.
    PERFORM minus_nm_zaiko_rtn_001
                             CHANGING  l_wa_lips-lips_stnd.
  ENDLOOP.

ENDFORM.                    " STOCK_MINUS_NMDLV_RTN_001
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  SELECT_KNVV_MINUS_RTN_001
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       CCW･四国･仙台　通常出荷先 CDP算出用 ﾎﾞﾄﾗｰｱｶｳﾝﾄｺｰﾄﾞ取得
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM select_knvv_minus_rtn_001 .

  DATA:
    l_it_lips      TYPE SORTED TABLE OF ty_lips_001
                        WITH NON-UNIQUE KEY vgbel.

* *--- CCW&仙台&四国 個別の実行権限がある場合のみ実施
  CHECK v_routine_001 = c_kosha.

* 初期化
  REFRESH:
    it_knvv_min.
  l_it_lips[] = it_lips_001[].
  DELETE : l_it_lips WHERE vgtyp <> c_vbtyv_c,
           ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM l_it_lips COMPARING vgbel.

  CHECK l_it_lips[] IS NOT INITIAL.
* 販売伝票ヘッダ、得意先マスタ: 販売データの検索
  SELECT vbak~vbeln        "販売伝票
         knvv~kunnr        "得意先コード
*         knvv~zkzbotacct   "ボトラーアカウントコード"NA
    INTO TABLE it_knvv_min
    FROM vbak
    INNER JOIN knvv
    ON vbak~kunnr = knvv~kunnr   "得意先コード
    FOR ALL ENTRIES IN l_it_lips
   WHERE vbak~vbeln = l_it_lips-vgbel  "販売伝票
     AND knvv~vkorg = v_bukrs         "販売組織
     AND knvv~vtweg = c_vtweg_99     "流通チャネル
     AND knvv~spart = c_spart_10.    "製品部門

ENDFORM.                    " SELECT_KNVV_MINUS_RTN_001
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  MINUS_NM_ZAIKO_RTN_001
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       通常在庫の減算
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      <--O_wa_LIPS          出荷ﾃﾞｰﾀ
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM minus_nm_zaiko_rtn_001
  CHANGING  o_wa_lips             TYPE ty_lips.

  DATA :
    l_wa_marc           TYPE ty_marc.

* *--- CCW&仙台&四国 個別の実行権限がある場合のみ実施
  CHECK v_routine_001 = c_kosha.

* *--- ﾛｯﾄ管理品の判定
  READ TABLE it_marc INTO l_wa_marc BINARY SEARCH
                     WITH KEY matnr = o_wa_lips-matnr
                              werks = o_wa_lips-werks
                     TRANSPORTING xchpf.
*   *--- ﾛｯﾄ管理品の場合､賞味期限決定
  IF l_wa_marc-xchpf = c_on.
    PERFORM edit_syomi_minus
                           CHANGING  o_wa_lips.
*   *--- ﾛｯﾄ非管理品の場合､枝番&賞味&ﾊﾟﾚｯﾄ種別を初期化
  ELSE.
    CLEAR :
      o_wa_lips-eda,
      o_wa_lips-kiboshom,
      o_wa_lips-plcode.
  ENDIF.

*   *--- 減算対象の条件作成
  PERFORM edit_range_rtn_001
                           USING     o_wa_lips-eda
                                     o_wa_lips-kiboshom
                                     o_wa_lips-wadat
                                     o_wa_lips-zzskyoseikb.
*   *--- ﾊﾟﾚｯﾄ種別に入力がある場合､ﾊﾟﾚｯﾄ種別指定で実在庫から減算
  IF o_wa_lips-plcode IS NOT INITIAL.
    PERFORM minus_jitu_letyp
                           USING     space
                                     space
                           CHANGING  o_wa_lips.
  ENDIF.
*   *--- ﾊﾟﾚｯﾄ種別未指定orﾊﾟﾚｯﾄ種別指定で在庫減算が完了しない場合､ﾊﾟﾚｯﾄ種別なしで在庫減算
  IF   o_wa_lips-plcode IS INITIAL
     OR ( o_wa_lips-plcode IS NOT INITIAL
      AND o_wa_lips-lfimg  >  0 ).
    PERFORM minus_jitu     USING     space
                                     space
                           CHANGING  o_wa_lips.
  ENDIF.
*   *--- 減算未完了の場合のみ続行
  CHECK o_wa_lips-lfimg > 0.
*   *--- ﾊﾟﾚｯﾄ種別に入力がある場合､ﾊﾟﾚｯﾄ種別指定で品検中在庫から減算
  IF o_wa_lips-plcode IS NOT INITIAL.
    PERFORM minus_jitu_letyp
                           USING     c_x               " 予定在庫
                                     c_bestq_q         " 品検中
                           CHANGING  o_wa_lips.
  ENDIF.
*   *--- ﾊﾟﾚｯﾄ種別未指定orﾊﾟﾚｯﾄ種別指定で在庫減算が完了しない場合､ﾊﾟﾚｯﾄ種別なしで在庫減算
  IF   o_wa_lips-plcode IS INITIAL
     OR ( o_wa_lips-plcode IS NOT INITIAL
      AND o_wa_lips-lfimg  >  0 ).
    PERFORM minus_jitu     USING     c_x
                                     c_bestq_q
                           CHANGING  o_wa_lips.
  ENDIF.
*   *--- 減算未完了の場合のみ続行

*    CHECK O_wa_LIPS-LFIMG > 0.
*   *--- 以降賞味の場合のみ､予定在庫からの
  CHECK o_wa_lips-lfimg > 0
    AND o_wa_lips-zzskyoseikb <> c_flash_b_001.

*   *--- 予定在庫からの減算処理
  PERFORM minus_yotei      CHANGING  o_wa_lips.

ENDFORM.                    " MINUS_NM_ZAIKO_RTN_001
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  ADD_EDA_ZSZSYOMI_RTN_001
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       CCW･仙台･四国  仮引当時の枝番&賞味の決定
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->I_v_XCHPF              ﾛｯﾄ管理区分
*      <--O_WA_9100               引当対象ﾃﾞｰﾀ
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM add_eda_zszsyomi_rtn_001
  USING     i_v_xchpf            TYPE marc-xchpf
  CHANGING  o_wa_9100             TYPE ty_9100.


  DATA:
    l_flg_auto_plt TYPE char01,
    l_v_pallet    TYPE lvs_letyp.


* *--- CCW&仙台&四国 個別の実行権限がある場合のみ実施
  CHECK v_routine_001 = c_kosha.

  CHECK o_wa_9100-message IS INITIAL.
* *--- 枝番&賞味期限を退避
  o_wa_9100-edacode_wrk = o_wa_9100-edacode.
  o_wa_9100-syomi_wrk = o_wa_9100-syomi.
* *--- 一覧からの仮引当時に賞味期限未設定⇒ｵｰﾀﾞの賞味でCDP実行
  IF    o_wa_9100-hub   =  c_on
    AND o_wa_9100-syomi IS INITIAL.
    o_wa_9100-syomi = o_wa_9100-syomi_org.
  ENDIF.


  l_flg_auto_plt = c_on.
* CCW社個別権限の場合
  IF flg_rout_006 = c_kosha.

*   転送オーダー明細テキストのパレット種別を取得
    PERFORM f_get_text_pallet USING o_wa_9100
                           CHANGING l_v_pallet.
*   明細テキストのパレット種別が取得できた場合、自動提案をスキップする
    IF l_v_pallet IS NOT INITIAL.
      o_wa_9100-plcode = l_v_pallet.
      CLEAR l_flg_auto_plt.
    ENDIF.
  ELSE.
*   ﾛｯﾄ非管理品の場合、自動提案をスキップする
    IF i_v_xchpf IS INITIAL.
      CLEAR l_flg_auto_plt.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.
  IF l_flg_auto_plt = c_on.
*   *--- ﾊﾟﾚｯﾄ種別の編集
    PERFORM edit_plcode      CHANGING  o_wa_9100.
    CHECK o_wa_9100-message IS INITIAL.
  ENDIF.


*  *--- ﾛｯﾄ非管理品 枝番&賞味未入力に設定
  IF     i_v_xchpf IS INITIAL.
    CLEAR :
      o_wa_9100-edacode,
      o_wa_9100-syomi,
      o_wa_9100-zsztorikbn.
*  *--- ﾛｯﾄ管理品でﾌﾗｯｼｭｱｳﾄ以外

*   ELSEIF I_v_XCHPF IS NOT INITIAL
*      AND O_WA_9100-FLASH_KBN NOT IN RNG_FLASH.
  ELSEIF i_v_xchpf IS NOT INITIAL.

*   *--- 枝番ﾏｽﾀの存在ﾁｪｯｸ
    PERFORM edit_edacode     CHANGING  o_wa_9100.

    CHECK o_wa_9100-message IS INITIAL.


**   *--- ﾊﾟﾚｯﾄ種別の編集
*    PERFORM EDIT_PLCODE      CHANGING  O_WA_9100.
*
*    CHECK O_WA_9100-MESSAGE IS INITIAL.


*   *--- 最終出荷賞味期限基点or初期起動の場合はCDPによる最終賞味算出
    IF o_wa_9100-flash_kbn NOT IN rng_flash.
      PERFORM edit_syomi     CHANGING  o_wa_9100.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    " ADD_EDA_ZSZSYOMI_RTN_001
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  CHECK_FLASH_KBN_SYOMI_RTN_001
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       CCW･仙台･四国  引当区分と賞味期限のﾁｪｯｸ
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->I_v_XCHPF              ﾛｯﾄ管理区分
*      <--O_WA_9100               引当対象ﾃﾞｰﾀ
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM check_flash_kbn_syomi_rtn_001
  USING     i_v_xchpf            TYPE marc-xchpf
  CHANGING  o_wa_9100             TYPE ty_9100.

* *--- CCW&仙台&四国 個別の実行権限がある場合のみ実施
  CHECK v_routine_001 = c_kosha.

* *--- 引当可一覧から仮引当が押下された場合のみ実施する
  CHECK o_wa_9100-hub     =  c_on
    AND o_wa_9100-message IS INITIAL.

* *--- 出荷先判定
  READ TABLE it_zstmm0068_001 TRANSPORTING NO FIELDS
                        WITH TABLE KEY zszshukka = o_wa_9100-kunnr2.
*   *--- 特定荷主&指定賞味->ｴﾗｰ
  IF     sy-subrc = 0

*       AND O_WA_9100-FLASH_KBN =  c_FLASH_B_001.
     AND o_wa_9100-flash_kbn =  c_flash_b_001
*     *--- 仙台/四国以外 ｴﾗｰ
     AND v_routine_002     <> c_kosha.

    MESSAGE s281(/ccej/otc_atp) INTO o_wa_9100-message.
*   *--- ﾛｯﾄ管理品&引当区分入力あり&賞味期限未入力->ｴﾗｰ
  ELSEIF i_v_xchpf          =  c_on
     AND o_wa_9100-flash_kbn IS NOT INITIAL
     AND o_wa_9100-syomi     IS     INITIAL.
    MESSAGE s054(/ccej/otc_atp) WITH text-s20 INTO o_wa_9100-message.
  ELSE.
    RETURN.
  ENDIF.
* *--- ﾒｯｾｰｼﾞが設定された場合､ｽﾃｰﾀｽ､iconを設定
  o_wa_9100-icon     = v_icon_red.         " 引当不可
  o_wa_9100-status   = text-010.            " 未引当
  PERFORM get_sort_icon      USING     o_wa_9100-icon
                             CHANGING  o_wa_9100-sort_icon.
  PERFORM get_sort_staus     USING     o_wa_9100-status
                             CHANGING  o_wa_9100-sort_sta.

ENDFORM.                    " CHECK_FLASH_KBN_SYOMI_RTN_001
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  HIKIATE_TORIOKI_RTN_001
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       特定荷主の引当(ﾊﾟﾚｯﾄ種別任意)
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->I_FLG_Y                 予定在庫
*      -->I_v_BESTQ              在庫ｶﾃｺﾞﾘ
*      <--O_FLG_OVERFLOW          ｵｰﾊﾞｰﾌﾛｰﾌﾗｸﾞ
*      <--O_WA_9100               引当対象ﾃﾞｰﾀ
*      <--O_IT_RESERVE            ﾘｻﾞｰﾌﾞ在庫
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM hikiate_torioki_rtn_001
  USING     i_flg_y               TYPE ty_reserve_001-flg_y
            i_v_bestq            TYPE ty_reserve_001-bestq
  CHANGING  o_flg_overflow        TYPE xfeld
            o_wa_9100             TYPE ty_9100
            o_it_reserve          TYPE ty_it_reserve_001.

  DATA :
    l_it_reserve_prv    TYPE ty_it_reserve_001,               " 引当済分
    l_v_zszsyomi_prv   TYPE ty_reserve_001-kiboshom,         " 前loop賞味
    l_v_menge_prv      TYPE ty_9100-menge_cs,                " 賞味開始時点の引当残数
    l_v_edacode        TYPE ty_9100-edacode,                 " 引当開始時点の枝番
    l_v_syomi          TYPE ty_9100-syomi,                   " 引当開始時点の賞味期限
    l_v_zan            TYPE mengv13,"NAzstmm0069-zsztorisu,             " 引当残数量
    l_v_kano_zan       TYPE mengv13,"NAzstmm0069-zsztorisu,             " 引当可能残数量
    l_v_sumi_su        TYPE ty_reserve_001-sumi_cs,          " 引当済数量計算用
    l_cnt_loop          TYPE syst-tabix.                      " loopｶｳﾝﾀ
  FIELD-SYMBOLS :
    <l_fs_yojitu>       TYPE any,                             " 予定 or 実在庫
    <l_fs_reserve>      TYPE LINE OF ty_it_reserve_001.       " 引当対象ﾘｻﾞｰﾌﾞ在庫

* *--- CCW&仙台&四国 個別の実行権限がある場合のみ実施
  CHECK v_routine_001 = c_kosha.

* *--- 引当が完了していない場合のみ実施
  CHECK o_wa_9100-flg_sumi = space.
* *--- 引当対象の条件作成
  PERFORM edit_range_rtn_001 USING     o_wa_9100-edacode
                                       o_wa_9100-syomi
                                       o_wa_9100-wadat
                                       o_wa_9100-flash_kbn.
* *--- ﾃﾞｰﾀ退避
  l_v_edacode   = o_wa_9100-edacode.
  l_v_syomi     = o_wa_9100-syomi.
  l_v_zan       = o_wa_9100-menge_cs.
  l_v_menge_prv = o_wa_9100-menge_cs.
* *--- 引当処理
  LOOP AT o_it_reserve ASSIGNING <l_fs_reserve>
                       WHERE werks      =  o_wa_9100-werks"p_werks
                         AND lgort      =  o_wa_9100-lgort
                         AND matnr      =  o_wa_9100-matnr
                         AND eda        IN rng_eda
                         AND kiboshom   IN rng_syomi
                         AND zsztorikbn =  o_wa_9100-zsztorikbn
                         AND ( flg_y    =  i_flg_y
                            OR flg_y    =  space )
                         AND ( bestq    =  i_v_bestq
                            OR flg_y    =  space )
                         AND menge_cs   >  0.
    l_cnt_loop = l_cnt_loop + 1.
    IF l_cnt_loop = 1.
      l_v_zszsyomi_prv = <l_fs_reserve>-kiboshom.
    ENDIF.
*   *--- 前loopで引当てたﾃﾞｰﾀと賞味期限が異なる場合(賞味跨ぎ)
    IF l_v_zszsyomi_prv <> <l_fs_reserve>-kiboshom.
      l_v_zszsyomi_prv = <l_fs_reserve>-kiboshom.
      PERFORM reverse_reserve_data_rtn_001
                             CHANGING  l_v_zan
                                       l_v_menge_prv
                                       o_wa_9100-menge_cs
                                       o_it_reserve
                                       l_it_reserve_prv.
    ENDIF.
*   *--- 次loopで賞味跨ぎとなった場合の戻し用にﾃﾞｰﾀ保持
    APPEND <l_fs_reserve> TO  l_it_reserve_prv.
    l_v_kano_zan = <l_fs_reserve>-menge_cs.
*   *--- 引当可能残数>0の場合のみ実施
    CHECK l_v_kano_zan > 0.

*    CASE I_v_BESTQ.
    CASE <l_fs_reserve>-bestq.

      WHEN c_bestq_q.      "品検中
" Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_APS Conversion **BEGIN OF CHANGE BY EJX9007359 11.06.2026  for ATC
*         ASSIGN COMPONENT c_fld_yotei_cs OF STRUCTURE <l_fs_reserve> TO <l_fs_yojitu>.
        ASSIGN COMPONENT c_fld_yotei_cs OF STRUCTURE <l_fs_reserve> TO <l_fs_yojitu>. "#EC CI_FLDEXT_OK[0002250183]
" Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_APS Conversion * *END OF CHANGE BY EJX9007359 11.06.2026 for ATC
      WHEN OTHERS.           "品検中以外
