" Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_APS Conversion **BEGIN OF CHANGE BY EJX9007359 11.06.2026  for ATC
*         ASSIGN COMPONENT c_fld_jitu_cs  OF STRUCTURE <l_fs_reserve> TO <l_fs_yojitu>.
        ASSIGN COMPONENT c_fld_jitu_cs  OF STRUCTURE <l_fs_reserve> TO <l_fs_yojitu>. "#EC CI_FLDEXT_OK[0002250183]
" Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_APS Conversion * *END OF CHANGE BY EJX9007359 11.06.2026 for ATC
    ENDCASE.
    IF l_v_zan > l_v_kano_zan.
      <l_fs_reserve>-menge_cs = 0.
      <l_fs_yojitu>           = 0.
      TRY.
          l_v_sumi_su = <l_fs_reserve>-sumi_cs + l_v_kano_zan.
          <l_fs_reserve>-sumi_cs = l_v_sumi_su.
        CATCH cx_root.
          o_flg_overflow      = c_on.
          o_wa_9100-edacode   = l_v_edacode.
          o_wa_9100-syomi     = l_v_syomi.
          PERFORM edit_overflow_9100
                             CHANGING  o_wa_9100.
          RETURN.
      ENDTRY.
      o_wa_9100-menge_cs  = o_wa_9100-menge_cs - l_v_kano_zan.
      l_v_zan            = l_v_zan           - l_v_kano_zan.
      o_wa_9100-edacode   = <l_fs_reserve>-eda.            " 枝番
      o_wa_9100-syomi     = <l_fs_reserve>-kiboshom.       " 賞味期限
    ELSE.
      <l_fs_reserve>-menge_cs = <l_fs_reserve>-menge_cs - l_v_zan.
      <l_fs_yojitu>           = <l_fs_yojitu>           - l_v_zan.
      TRY.
          l_v_sumi_su = <l_fs_reserve>-sumi_cs + l_v_zan.
          <l_fs_reserve>-sumi_cs = l_v_sumi_su.
        CATCH cx_root.
          o_flg_overflow      = c_on.
          o_wa_9100-edacode   = l_v_edacode.
          o_wa_9100-syomi     = l_v_syomi.
          PERFORM edit_overflow_9100
                             CHANGING  o_wa_9100.
          RETURN.
      ENDTRY.
      o_wa_9100-status    = text-010.               " 処理ステータス
      CASE i_v_bestq.
*       *--- 在庫ｶﾃｺﾞﾘ＝SPACEの場合､通常引当可
        WHEN space.
          o_wa_9100-icon = v_icon_green.
*       *--- 在庫ｶﾃｺﾞﾘ≠SPACEの場合､予定引当可
        WHEN OTHERS.
          o_wa_9100-icon = v_icon_yellow.
      ENDCASE.
      o_wa_9100-flg_sumi  = c_on.                 " 引当済フラグ
      PERFORM get_sort_icon  USING     o_wa_9100-icon
                             CHANGING  o_wa_9100-sort_icon.
      PERFORM get_sort_staus USING     o_wa_9100-status
                             CHANGING  o_wa_9100-sort_sta.
      o_wa_9100-edacode   = <l_fs_reserve>-eda.            " 枝番
      o_wa_9100-syomi     = <l_fs_reserve>-kiboshom.       " 賞味期限
      EXIT.
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.
* *--- 引当済の数量を算出する
  IF o_wa_9100-flg_sumi = c_on.
*   *--- ﾘｻﾞｰﾌﾞ在庫(引当処理用)をﾘｻﾞｰﾌﾞ在庫に更新(引当結果の在庫増減を保持)
    PERFORM rewrite_reserve_rtn_001
                             USING     o_wa_9100-vbeln
                                       o_wa_9100-posnr
                                       o_wa_9100-kbn
                                       o_wa_9100-lgort
                                       o_wa_9100-matnr
                                       o_wa_9100-zsztorikbn
                                       o_it_reserve[]
                                       o_wa_9100-werks
*                             CHANGING  IT_RESERVE_001[].
                             CHANGING  o_wa_9100-bestq_txt
                                       it_reserve_001[].

*   *--- 引き剝がし用に引当開始時点の枝番と賞味を保持しておく
    o_wa_9100-edacode_wrk = l_v_edacode.
    o_wa_9100-syomi_wrk   = l_v_syomi.
  ELSE.
*   *--- 引当できない場合､引当処理内で変更されている可能性がある項目を引当前の状態に戻す
    o_wa_9100-edacode   = l_v_edacode.
    o_wa_9100-syomi     = l_v_syomi.
    PERFORM reverse_reserve_data_rtn_001
                             CHANGING  l_v_zan
                                       l_v_menge_prv
                                       o_wa_9100-menge_cs
                                       o_it_reserve
                                       l_it_reserve_prv.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    " HIKIATE_TORIOKI_RTN_001
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  EDIT_9100_3_RTN_001
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       引当残数の編集(品目､ﾌﾟﾗﾝﾄ､保管場所､枝番)
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM edit_9100_3_rtn_001 .

  DATA :
    l_it_9100           TYPE SORTED   TABLE OF ty_9100
                             WITH NON-UNIQUE KEY kunnr2
                                                 matnr,
    l_wa_9100           TYPE ty_9100,
    l_it_zaiko_sum      TYPE ty_it_sum_001,
    l_it_reserve_sum    TYPE ty_it_sum_001,
    l_wa_mara_9100      TYPE ty_mara_9100,
    l_flg_reserve       TYPE xfeld.
  FIELD-SYMBOLS :
    <l_fs_9100>         TYPE LINE OF tbl_ty_9100.

* *--- CCW&仙台&四国 個別の実行権限がある場合のみ実施
  CHECK v_routine_001 = c_kosha.

* *--- 在庫の集計
  PERFORM sum_quant_zaiko_rtn_001
                             CHANGING  l_it_zaiko_sum[].
  PERFORM sum_quant_reserve_rtn_001
                             CHANGING  l_it_reserve_sum[].
* *--- 編集準備
  SORT it_mara_9100 ASCENDING BY matnr.
  l_it_9100[] = it_9100[].
  DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM l_it_9100 COMPARING kunnr2
                                                      matnr.

  LOOP AT l_it_9100 INTO l_wa_9100.
    READ TABLE it_mara_9100 INTO l_wa_mara_9100 BINARY SEARCH
                            WITH KEY matnr = l_wa_9100-matnr
                            TRANSPORTING meins.
    PERFORM check_reserve_zaiko_rtn_001
                             USING     l_wa_9100-werks "p_werks
                                       l_wa_9100-matnr
                                       l_wa_9100-kunnr2
                             CHANGING  l_flg_reserve.
*   *--- 特定荷主&ﾛｯﾄ管理品

*    IF L_FLG_RESERVE = c_ON.
    IF l_flg_reserve = c_on                     "特定荷主
      OR ( v_routine_002 = c_kosha            "仙台&四国 個別
      AND  l_wa_9100-kbn = text-002               "転送発注
      AND  l_wa_9100-zsztorikbn IS NOT INITIAL ). "取置区分あり
      l_flg_reserve = c_on.   "通常出荷先の場合も特定荷主と同様

      PERFORM edit_quant_9100_9300_rtn_001
                             USING     l_wa_9100-kunnr2
                                       l_wa_9100-matnr
                                       l_wa_mara_9100-meins
                                       l_flg_reserve
                             CHANGING  l_it_reserve_sum[].
*   *--- 通常出荷先､特定荷主&非ﾛｯﾄ管理品
    ELSE.
      PERFORM edit_quant_9100_9300_rtn_001
                             USING     l_wa_9100-kunnr2
                                       l_wa_9100-matnr
                                       l_wa_mara_9100-meins
                                       l_flg_reserve
                             CHANGING  l_it_zaiko_sum[].
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.

* *--- 数量項目がﾌﾞﾗﾝｸの場合は"0"を設定する
  LOOP AT it_9100 ASSIGNING <l_fs_9100>
                  WHERE zan_9300  IS INITIAL       "引当残数
                     OR zan_out   IS INITIAL       "引当残数(9100表示用)
                     OR kaku_9300 IS INITIAL       "引当確定数
                     OR jitu_9300 IS INITIAL.      "在庫数量
    IF <l_fs_9100>-zan_9300 IS INITIAL.
      <l_fs_9100>-zan_9300 = 0.       "引当残数
    ENDIF.
    IF <l_fs_9100>-zan_out  IS INITIAL.
      <l_fs_9100>-zan_out = 0.        "引当残数(9100表示用)
    ENDIF.
    IF <l_fs_9100>-kaku_9300 IS INITIAL.
      <l_fs_9100>-kaku_9300 = 0.      "引当確定数
    ENDIF.
    IF <l_fs_9100>-jitu_9300 IS INITIAL.
      <l_fs_9100>-jitu_9300 = 0.      "在庫数量
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.

ENDFORM.                    " EDIT_9100_3_RTN_001
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  SUM_QUANT_ZAIKO_RTN_001
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       通常在庫の集計
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      <--O_IT_SUM                通常在庫の集計(実在庫のみ)
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM sum_quant_zaiko_rtn_001
  CHANGING  o_it_sum              TYPE ty_it_sum_001.

  DATA :
    l_wa_zaiko          TYPE LINE OF tbl_ty_zaiko,
    l_wa_sum            TYPE LINE OF ty_it_sum_001.

* *--- CCW&仙台&四国 個別の実行権限がある場合のみ実施
  CHECK v_routine_001 = c_kosha.

* *--- ﾌﾟﾗﾝﾄ/保管場所/品目/枝番/賞味期限毎の実在庫合計
  SORT it_zaiko ASCENDING BY werks
                             lgort
                             matnr
                             eda
                             kiboshom.
  LOOP AT it_zaiko INTO l_wa_zaiko.
*   *--- 実在庫のみ集計
    IF    l_wa_zaiko-bestq = space
      AND l_wa_zaiko-flg_y = space.
      l_wa_sum-zaiko_cs = l_wa_sum-zaiko_cs + l_wa_zaiko-menge_cs.
      l_wa_sum-hiki_cs  = l_wa_sum-hiki_cs  + l_wa_zaiko-sumi_cs.
    ENDIF.
*   *--- 合計数量を作成
    AT END OF kiboshom.
      l_wa_sum-matnr     =  l_wa_zaiko-matnr.
      l_wa_sum-werks     =  l_wa_zaiko-werks.
      l_wa_sum-lgort     =  l_wa_zaiko-lgort.
      l_wa_sum-eda       =  l_wa_zaiko-eda.
      l_wa_sum-kiboshom  =  l_wa_zaiko-kiboshom.
      APPEND l_wa_sum TO o_it_sum.
      CLEAR l_wa_sum.
    ENDAT.
  ENDLOOP.

ENDFORM.                    " SUM_QUANT_ZAIKO_RTN_001
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  SUM_QUANT_RESERVE_RTN_001
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       取置在庫の集計
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      <--O_IT_SUM                取置在庫の集計
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM sum_quant_reserve_rtn_001
  CHANGING  o_it_sum              TYPE ty_it_sum_001.

  DATA :
    l_wa_reserve        TYPE LINE OF ty_it_reserve_001,
    l_wa_sum            TYPE LINE OF ty_it_sum_001,
    l_v_tabix_next     TYPE syst-tabix.

  DATA :
    l_flg_avail         TYPE xfeld,                   " 利用可能
    l_flg_check         TYPE xfeld.                   " 検査中


* *--- CCW&仙台&四国 個別の実行権限がある場合のみ実施
  CHECK v_routine_001 = c_kosha.

* *--- ﾌﾟﾗﾝﾄ/保管場所/品目/枝番/賞味期限毎の実在庫合計
  SORT it_reserve_001 ASCENDING BY werks
                                   lgort
                                   matnr
                                   kiboshom
                                   eda
                                   zsztorikbn.
  LOOP AT it_reserve_001 INTO l_wa_reserve.
    l_v_tabix_next   = sy-tabix + 1.
    l_wa_sum-zaiko_cs = l_wa_sum-zaiko_cs + l_wa_reserve-menge_cs.
    l_wa_sum-hiki_cs  = l_wa_sum-hiki_cs  + l_wa_reserve-sumi_cs.

    CASE l_wa_reserve-bestq.
      WHEN space.                      " 利用可能
        l_flg_avail = c_on.
      WHEN c_bestq_q.                " 品検中
        l_flg_check = c_on.
      WHEN OTHERS.
    ENDCASE.

*   *--- ﾌﾟﾗﾝﾄ/保管場所/品目/枝番/賞味期限/取置区分単位で集計
    READ TABLE it_reserve_001 INTO l_wa_reserve
                              TRANSPORTING NO FIELDS
                              INDEX     l_v_tabix_next
                              COMPARING werks
                                        lgort
                                        matnr
                                        eda
                                        kiboshom
                                        zsztorikbn.
    CHECK sy-subrc <> 0.
    l_wa_sum-matnr      =  l_wa_reserve-matnr.
    l_wa_sum-werks      =  l_wa_reserve-werks.
    l_wa_sum-lgort      =  l_wa_reserve-lgort.
    l_wa_sum-eda        =  l_wa_reserve-eda.
    l_wa_sum-kiboshom   =  l_wa_reserve-kiboshom.
    l_wa_sum-zsztorikbn =  l_wa_reserve-zsztorikbn.

*       *--- 集計値が利用可能在庫のみの場合
    IF     l_flg_avail = c_on
       AND l_flg_check = space.
      PERFORM edit_bestq_txt
                         USING     space
                         CHANGING  l_wa_sum-bestq_txt.
*       *--- 集計値が品検中在庫のみの場合
    ELSEIF l_flg_avail = space
       AND l_flg_check = c_on.
      PERFORM edit_bestq_txt
                         USING     c_bestq
                         CHANGING  l_wa_sum-bestq_txt.
*       *--- 集計値に利用可能在庫と品検中在庫が混在している場合
    ELSE.
      CLEAR l_wa_sum-bestq_txt.
    ENDIF.

    APPEND l_wa_sum TO o_it_sum.

*        CLEAR L_wa_SUM.
    CLEAR :
      l_wa_sum,
      l_flg_avail,
      l_flg_check.

  ENDLOOP.

ENDFORM.                    " SUM_QUANT_RESERVE_RTN_001
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  EDIT_QUANT_9100_9300_RTN_001
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       引当可一覧画面(9100)と詳細画面(9300)の数量項目の編集
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->I_v_KUNNR              出荷先
*      -->I_v_MATNR              品目
*      -->I_v_MEINS              基本数量単位
*      -->I_FLG_RESERVE           ﾘｻﾞｰﾌﾞ在庫("X"=ﾘｻﾞｰﾌﾞ在庫､" "=通常在庫)
*      -->I_IT_SUM                集計ﾃﾞｰﾀ
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM edit_quant_9100_9300_rtn_001
  USING     i_v_kunnr            TYPE ty_9100-kunnr2
            i_v_matnr            TYPE ty_9100-matnr
            i_v_meins            TYPE mara-meins

            i_flg_reserve         TYPE xfeld

  CHANGING  i_it_sum              TYPE ty_it_sum_001.

  DATA :
    l_wa_sum            TYPE LINE OF ty_it_sum_001,
    l_wa_9100           TYPE ty_9100,
    l_v_jitu           TYPE lqua-gesme.

* *--- CCW&仙台&四国 個別の実行権限がある場合のみ実施
  CHECK v_routine_001 = c_kosha.

  LOOP AT i_it_sum INTO l_wa_sum.
    CHECK l_wa_sum-matnr = i_v_matnr.
*   *--- 引当残数
    IF l_wa_sum-zaiko_cs = 0.
      l_wa_9100-zan_9300 = 0.
      l_wa_9100-zan_out  = 0.
    ELSE.
      WRITE l_wa_sum-zaiko_cs TO   l_wa_9100-zan_9300
                              UNIT i_v_meins.
      l_wa_9100-zan_out = l_wa_9100-zan_9300.
    ENDIF.
*   *--- 引当確定数
    IF l_wa_sum-hiki_cs = 0.
      l_wa_9100-kaku_9300 = 0.
    ELSE.
      WRITE l_wa_sum-hiki_cs  TO   l_wa_9100-kaku_9300
                              UNIT i_v_meins.
    ENDIF.
*   *--- 在庫数量
    IF    l_wa_sum-zaiko_cs = 0
      AND l_wa_sum-hiki_cs  = 0.
      l_wa_9100-jitu_9300 = 0.
    ELSE.
      l_v_jitu = l_wa_sum-zaiko_cs + l_wa_sum-hiki_cs.
      WRITE l_v_jitu         TO   l_wa_9100-jitu_9300
                              UNIT i_v_meins.
    ENDIF.
*   *--- 引当残数､引当残数､引当確定数､在庫数量の反映
    CASE i_flg_reserve.
*     *--- 特定荷主&ﾛｯﾄ管理品の場合､通常と予定在庫で数量反映
      WHEN c_on.
        MODIFY it_9100 FROM l_wa_9100
          TRANSPORTING zan_out
                       zan_9300
                       kaku_9300
                       jitu_9300
          WHERE kunnr2     =  i_v_kunnr           " 出荷先
            AND matnr      =  l_wa_sum-matnr       " 品目
            AND lgort      =  l_wa_sum-lgort       " 保管場所
" Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_APS Conversion **BEGIN OF CHANGE BY EJX9007359 11.06.2026  for ATC
*             AND edacode    =  l_wa_sum-eda         " 枝番
AND edacode = l_wa_sum-eda "#EC CI_FLDEXT_OK[0002250183]
" Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_APS Conversion * *END OF CHANGE BY EJX9007359 11.06.2026 for ATC
            AND syomi      =  l_wa_sum-kiboshom    " 賞味期限
            AND zsztorikbn =  l_wa_sum-zsztorikbn  " 取置区分
*           *--- 引当不可以外のｽﾃｰﾀｽの在庫数量を更新する
            AND icon       <> v_icon_red.         " 引当不可
*     *--- 通常出荷先or特定荷主&非ﾛｯﾄ管理品の場合､通常引当済の場合に在庫数量反映
      WHEN OTHERS.
        MODIFY it_9100 FROM l_wa_9100
          TRANSPORTING zan_out
                       zan_9300
                       kaku_9300
                       jitu_9300
          WHERE kunnr2     =  i_v_kunnr           " 出荷先
            AND matnr      =  l_wa_sum-matnr       " 品目
            AND lgort      =  l_wa_sum-lgort       " 保管場所
" Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_APS Conversion **BEGIN OF CHANGE BY EJX9007359 11.06.2026  for ATC
*             AND edacode    =  l_wa_sum-eda         " 枝番
AND edacode = l_wa_sum-eda "#EC CI_FLDEXT_OK[0002250183]
" Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_APS Conversion * *END OF CHANGE BY EJX9007359 11.06.2026 for ATC
            AND syomi      =  l_wa_sum-kiboshom    " 賞味期限
            AND zsztorikbn =  l_wa_sum-zsztorikbn  " 取置区分
*           *--- 予定引当可or引当不可以外のｽﾃｰﾀｽの在庫数量を更新する
            AND icon       <> v_icon_yellow       " 予定引当
            AND icon       <> v_icon_red.         " 引当不可
    ENDCASE.

  ENDLOOP.

ENDFORM.                    " EDIT_QUANT_9100_9300_RTN_001
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  RETURN_RESERVE_RTN_001
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       特定荷主の在庫引き剥がし
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->I_v_VBELN              伝票番号
*      -->I_v_POSNR              明細番号
*      -->I_v_KBN                区分
*      <--O_IT_HIKIATE            引当済ﾃﾞｰﾀ
*      <--O_IT_RESERVE            ﾘｻﾞｰﾌﾞ在庫
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM return_reserve_rtn_001
  USING     i_v_vbeln            TYPE ty_9100-vbeln
            i_v_posnr            TYPE ty_9100-posnr
            i_v_kbn              TYPE ty_9100-kbn
  CHANGING  o_it_hikiate          TYPE ty_it_hikiate_001
            o_it_reserve          TYPE ty_it_reserve_001.

  DATA :
    l_wa_hikiate        TYPE LINE OF ty_it_hikiate_001.
  FIELD-SYMBOLS :
    <l_fs_reserve>      TYPE LINE OF ty_it_reserve_001.

* *--- CCW&仙台&四国 個別の実行権限がある場合のみ実施
  CHECK v_routine_001 = c_kosha.

  LOOP AT o_it_hikiate INTO  l_wa_hikiate
                       WHERE vbeln = i_v_vbeln
                         AND posnr = i_v_posnr
                         AND kbn   = i_v_kbn.
    READ TABLE o_it_reserve ASSIGNING <l_fs_reserve>
                         WITH KEY werks      = l_wa_hikiate-werks
                                  lgort      = l_wa_hikiate-lgort
                                  matnr      = l_wa_hikiate-matnr
" Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_A Conversion **BEGIN OF CHANGE BY EJX9007359 03.06.2026  FOR ATC
*                                   eda        = l_wa_hikiate-edacode+0(4)
                                  eda        = l_wa_hikiate-edacode+0(4) "#EC CI_FLDEXT_OK[2215424]
" Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_A Conversion * *END OF CHANGE BY EJX9007359 03.06.2026 FOR ATC
                                  kiboshom   = l_wa_hikiate-syomi
                                  eindt      = l_wa_hikiate-eindt
                                  letyp      = l_wa_hikiate-plcode+0(3)
                                  bestq      = l_wa_hikiate-bestq
                                  charg      = l_wa_hikiate-charg
                                  hsdat      = l_wa_hikiate-hsdat
                                  flg_y      = l_wa_hikiate-flg_y
                                  flg_ekpo   = l_wa_hikiate-flg_ekpo
                                  flg_afpo   = l_wa_hikiate-flg_afpo
                                  zsztorikbn = l_wa_hikiate-zsztorikbn.
    <l_fs_reserve>-menge_cs = <l_fs_reserve>-menge_cs + l_wa_hikiate-sumi_cs.
    <l_fs_reserve>-sumi_cs  = <l_fs_reserve>-sumi_cs  - l_wa_hikiate-sumi_cs.
    IF <l_fs_reserve>-flg_y = c_on.
      <l_fs_reserve>-yotei_cs = <l_fs_reserve>-yotei_cs + l_wa_hikiate-sumi_cs.
    ELSE.
      <l_fs_reserve>-jitu_cs  = <l_fs_reserve>-jitu_cs  + l_wa_hikiate-sumi_cs.
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.
  DELETE o_it_hikiate WHERE vbeln = i_v_vbeln
                        AND posnr = i_v_posnr
                        AND kbn   = i_v_kbn.

ENDFORM.                    " RETURN_RESERVE_RTN_001
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  EDIT_9400_RTN_001
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       在庫一覧画面ﾃﾞｰﾀ編集
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM edit_9400_rtn_001 .

  DATA :
    l_flg_reserve       TYPE xfeld.              " 取置在庫使用

* *--- CCW&仙台&四国 個別の実行権限がある場合のみ実施
  CHECK v_routine_001 = c_kosha.

* *--- 選択行の出荷先の判定
  PERFORM check_reserve_zaiko_rtn_001
                             USING     wa_9100-werks"p_werks
                                       wa_9100-matnr
                                       wa_9100-kunnr2
                             CHANGING  l_flg_reserve.
*   *--- 特定荷主&ﾛｯﾄ管理品

*    IF L_FLG_RESERVE = c_ON.
  IF l_flg_reserve = c_on                   "特定荷主
    OR ( v_routine_002 = c_kosha          "仙台&四国 個別
    AND  wa_9100-kbn = text-002               "転送発注
    AND  wa_9100-zsztorikbn IS NOT INITIAL ). "取置区分あり

    PERFORM edit_reserve_9400_rtn_001.
*   *--- 通常出荷先&特定荷主で非ﾛｯﾄ管理品
  ELSE.
    PERFORM edit_9400.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    " EDIT_9400_RTN_001
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  EDIT_RESERVE_9400_RTN_001
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       特定荷主の在庫一覧表示ﾃﾞｰﾀ編集
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM edit_reserve_9400_rtn_001 .

  DATA :
    l_it_sum            TYPE ty_it_sum_001,
    l_wa_sum            TYPE LINE OF ty_it_sum_001,
    l_wa_mara_9100      TYPE ty_mara_9100,
    l_wa_9400           TYPE ty_9400,
    l_v_zaiko_cs       TYPE ty_sum-zaiko_cs.

* *--- CCW&仙台&四国 個別の実行権限がある場合のみ実施
  CHECK v_routine_001 = c_kosha.

* *--- 集計
  PERFORM sum_quant_reserve_rtn_001
                             CHANGING  l_it_sum[].

* *--- 初期化
  REFRESH it_9400.

* *--- 品目の基本数量単位取得
  READ TABLE it_mara_9100 INTO l_wa_mara_9100 BINARY SEARCH
                        WITH KEY matnr = wa_9100-matnr
                        TRANSPORTING meins.
* *--- 品目&枝番&取置区分が一致するﾃﾞｰﾀのみ表示対象
  LOOP AT l_it_sum INTO  l_wa_sum
                   WHERE werks      =  wa_9100-werks "p_werks
                     AND matnr      =  wa_9100-matnr
" Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_APS Conversion **BEGIN OF CHANGE BY EJX9007359 11.06.2026  for ATC
*                      AND eda        =  wa_9100-edacode
                     AND eda        =  wa_9100-edacode "#EC CI_FLDEXT_OK[0002250183]
" Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_APS Conversion * *END OF CHANGE BY EJX9007359 11.06.2026 for ATC
                     AND zsztorikbn =  wa_9100-zsztorikbn.

*   *--- 賞味期限設定済の場合は画面賞味期限以降､賞味期限未設定の場合は未設定分のみ表示
    CHECK ( wa_9100-syomi     <> c_syomi_0
        AND l_wa_sum-kiboshom >= wa_9100-syomi )
       OR ( wa_9100-syomi     =  c_syomi_0
        AND l_wa_sum-kiboshom <> c_syomi_0 ).
*   *--- 引当数量より引当残数が大きい在庫のみ表示対象とする
    CHECK l_wa_sum-zaiko_cs >= wa_9100-menge_cs.
    l_wa_9400-werks     = l_wa_sum-werks.      " ﾌﾟﾗﾝﾄ
    l_wa_9400-lgort     = l_wa_sum-lgort.      " 保管場所
    l_wa_9400-matnr     = l_wa_sum-matnr.      " 品目
    l_wa_9400-edacode   = l_wa_sum-eda.        " 枝番
    l_wa_9400-syomi     = l_wa_sum-kiboshom.   " 賞味期限
    l_wa_9400-bestq_txt = l_wa_sum-bestq_txt.  " 在庫ｶﾃｺﾞﾘ
    l_v_zaiko_cs = l_wa_sum-zaiko_cs + l_wa_sum-hiki_cs.
    WRITE : l_v_zaiko_cs     TO l_wa_9400-zaiko_suu UNIT l_wa_mara_9100-meins,
            l_wa_sum-zaiko_cs TO l_wa_9400-zan_suu   UNIT l_wa_mara_9100-meins.
    APPEND l_wa_9400 TO it_9400.
    CLEAR l_wa_9400.
  ENDLOOP.

ENDFORM.                    " EDIT_RESERVE_9400_RTN_001
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  SET_ZSZTORIKBN_RTN_001
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       CCW･仙台･四国個別取置区分設定
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->I_v_KUNNR              出荷先
*      <--O_v_ZSZTORIKBN         取置区分
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM set_zsztorikbn_rtn_001
  USING     i_v_kunnr            TYPE ty_9100-kunnr

            i_v_bednr            TYPE ekpo-bednr

  CHANGING  o_v_zsztorikbn       TYPE ty_9100-zsztorikbn.

  DATA :
    l_wa_zstmm0068      TYPE LINE OF ty_it_zstmm0068_001.

* *--- CCW&仙台&四国 個別の実行権限がある場合のみ実施
  CHECK v_routine_001 = c_kosha.

  CLEAR o_v_zsztorikbn.
* *--- 取置区分ﾏｽﾀより取置区分を取得
  READ TABLE it_zstmm0068_001 INTO l_wa_zstmm0068
                        WITH TABLE KEY zszshukka = i_v_kunnr
                        TRANSPORTING zsztorikbn.
*   *--- 取置区分ﾏｽﾀがある場合､取置区分を設定
  IF sy-subrc = 0.

*      O_v_ZSZTORIKBN = L_wa_ZSTMM0068-ZSZTORIKBN.
    PERFORM set_zsztorikbn_rtn_002
                           USING     i_v_bednr
                                     l_wa_zstmm0068-zsztorikbn
                           CHANGING  o_v_zsztorikbn.

  ELSE.

*      RETURN.

*     通常出荷先＆在庫転送オーダーの取置区分が設定された場合
*     仙台&四国実行権限がある場合
    IF v_routine_002 = c_kosha
      AND i_v_bednr IS NOT INITIAL.
*
      PERFORM set_zsztorikbn_rtn_002
                             USING     i_v_bednr
                                       space
                             CHANGING  o_v_zsztorikbn.

    ENDIF.

  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    " SET_ZSZTORIKBN_RTN_001
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  EDIT_LIPS_SPDLV_NMDLV_RTN_001
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       出荷伝票を特定荷主分と通常出荷分に分ける
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      <--O_IT_LIPS_SP            特定荷主の出荷伝票
*      <--O_IT_LIPS_NM            通常出荷先の出荷伝票
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM edit_lips_spdlv_nmdlv_rtn_001
  CHANGING  o_it_lips_sp          TYPE ty_it_lips_001
            o_it_lips_nm          TYPE ty_it_lips_001.

  DATA :
    l_wa_lips           TYPE LINE OF ty_it_lips_001.

  DATA :
    l_v_ebeln          TYPE ekpo-ebeln,
    l_v_ebelp          TYPE ekpo-ebelp,
    l_v_bednr          TYPE ty_bednr_002-bednr, " 追跡No.
    l_flg_reserve       TYPE xfeld.              " 特定荷主or通常出荷先


* *--- CCW&仙台&四国 個別の実行権限がある場合のみ実施
  CHECK v_routine_001 = c_kosha.

  REFRESH :
    o_it_lips_sp,
    o_it_lips_nm.

* *--- 非ﾛｯﾄ管理品
  SORT it_marc_non ASCENDING BY matnr
                                werks.
* *--- ﾛｯﾄ管理区分と出荷先によりﾃﾞｰﾀをわける
  LOOP AT it_lips_001 INTO l_wa_lips.

**   *--- ﾛｯﾄ管理品判定
*    READ TABLE IT_MARC_NON TRANSPORTING NO FIELDS BINARY SEARCH
*                        WITH KEY MATNR = L_wa_LIPS-MATNR
*                                 WERKS = L_wa_LIPS-WERKS.
**   *--- 非ﾛｯﾄ管理品->通常出荷先として処理
*    IF SY-SUBRC = 0.
*      APPEND L_wa_LIPS TO O_IT_LIPS_NM.
**   *--- ﾛｯﾄ管理品
*    ELSE.
*      PERFORM SET_ZSZTORIKBN_RTN_001
*                             USING     L_wa_LIPS-KUNNR
*                             CHANGING  L_wa_LIPS-ZSZTORIKBN.
**     *--- 取置区分 SPACE->通常出荷先として処理
*      IF L_wa_LIPS-ZSZTORIKBN = SPACE.
*        APPEND L_wa_LIPS TO O_IT_LIPS_NM.
*      ELSE.
*        APPEND L_wa_LIPS TO O_IT_LIPS_SP.
*      ENDIF.
*    ENDIF.
*   *--- 特定荷主or通常出荷先判定
    PERFORM check_reserve_zaiko_rtn_001
                             USING     l_wa_lips-werks
                                       l_wa_lips-matnr
                                       l_wa_lips-kunnr
                             CHANGING  l_flg_reserve.
*     *--- 特定荷主
    IF     l_flg_reserve = c_on.
      CASE l_wa_lips-vgtyp.
        WHEN c_vbtyv_v.
          l_v_ebeln = l_wa_lips-vgbel.
          l_v_ebelp = l_wa_lips-vgpos.
*           *--- 発注参照の場合､発注の購買依頼追跡番号を取得
          PERFORM get_bednr_rtn_002
                           USING     l_v_ebeln
                                     l_v_ebelp
                           CHANGING  l_v_bednr.
          CLEAR :
            l_v_ebeln,
            l_v_ebelp.
*         *--- 発注以外参照の場合､追跡番号初期値で取置区分判定へ
        WHEN OTHERS.
          CLEAR l_v_bednr.
      ENDCASE.
*       *--- 取置区分判定
      PERFORM set_zsztorikbn_rtn_001
                           USING     l_wa_lips-kunnr
                                     l_v_bednr
                           CHANGING  l_wa_lips-zsztorikbn.
      APPEND l_wa_lips TO o_it_lips_sp.
*     *--- 通常出荷先
    ELSE.

      l_wa_lips-zsztorikbn = space.       " 取置区分はSPACEに変更

*       仙台&四国 個別の実行権限がある場合のみ実施
      IF v_routine_002 = c_kosha.

        CLEAR l_v_bednr.
        IF l_wa_lips-vgtyp = c_vbtyv_v.

          l_v_ebeln = l_wa_lips-vgbel.
          l_v_ebelp = l_wa_lips-vgpos.
*           *--- 発注参照の場合､発注の購買依頼追跡番号を取得
          PERFORM get_bednr_rtn_002
                           USING     l_v_ebeln
                                     l_v_ebelp
                           CHANGING  l_v_bednr.
          CLEAR :
            l_v_ebeln,
            l_v_ebelp.
*
          IF l_v_bednr IS NOT INITIAL.

*             取置区分判定
            PERFORM set_zsztorikbn_rtn_001
                                 USING     l_wa_lips-kunnr
                                           l_v_bednr
                                 CHANGING  l_wa_lips-zsztorikbn.
*             有効な追跡番号の場合
            IF l_wa_lips-zsztorikbn IS NOT INITIAL.
              APPEND l_wa_lips TO o_it_lips_sp.
              CONTINUE.
            ENDIF.

          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.

      APPEND l_wa_lips TO o_it_lips_nm.
    ENDIF.

  ENDLOOP.

ENDFORM.                    " EDIT_LIPS_SPDLV_NMDLV_RTN_001
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  ADJUwa_STOCK_RESERVE_RTN_001
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       在庫から取置分を移動する
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->I_wa_ZSTMM0069          ﾘｻﾞｰﾌﾞ在庫
*      -->I_FLG_Y                 在庫ﾀｲﾌﾟ(実在庫/予定在庫)
*      -->I_v_BESTQ              在庫ｶﾃｺﾞﾘ
*      <--O_v_ZSZTORISU          移動未了分の取置数量
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM adjuwa_stock_reserve_rtn_001
  USING     i_wa_zstmm0069        TYPE LINE OF ty_it_zstmm0069_001
            i_flg_y               TYPE ty_zaiko-flg_y
            i_v_bestq            TYPE ty_zaiko-bestq
  CHANGING  o_v_zsztorisu        TYPE menge13.

  DATA :
    l_wa_reserve        TYPE LINE OF ty_it_reserve_001,
    l_v_tabix          TYPE syst-tabix.
  FIELD-SYMBOLS :
    <l_fs_zaiko>        TYPE LINE OF tbl_ty_zaiko.

* *--- CCW&仙台&四国 個別の実行権限がある場合のみ実施
  CHECK v_routine_001 = c_kosha.

  CHECK o_v_zsztorisu > 0.
  LOOP AT it_zaiko ASSIGNING <l_fs_zaiko>
                   WHERE werks     =  i_wa_zstmm0069-zszsymwerks
" Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_APS Conversion **BEGIN OF CHANGE BY EJX9007359 11.06.2026  for ATC
*                      AND lgort     =  i_wa_zstmm0069-zszsymlgort
                     AND lgort     =  i_wa_zstmm0069-zszsymlgort "#EC CI_FLDEXT_OK[0002250183]
" Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_APS Conversion * *END OF CHANGE BY EJX9007359 11.06.2026 for ATC
                     AND matnr     =  i_wa_zstmm0069-matnr
                     AND eda       =  i_wa_zstmm0069-eda
                     AND kiboshom  =  i_wa_zstmm0069-zszsyomi
                     AND flg_y     =  i_flg_y
                     AND bestq     =  i_v_bestq
                     AND menge_cs  >  0.
    l_v_tabix = sy-tabix.
*     *--- 実在庫数量<=取置数量
    IF <l_fs_zaiko>-menge_cs <= o_v_zsztorisu.
*       *--- 在庫をﾘｻﾞｰﾌﾞ在庫に移動
      l_wa_reserve-zaiko_stnd  = <l_fs_zaiko>.
      o_v_zsztorisu = o_v_zsztorisu - <l_fs_zaiko>-menge_cs.
      DELETE it_zaiko INDEX l_v_tabix.
*     *--- 在庫数量>取置数量
    ELSE.
      l_wa_reserve-zaiko_stnd  = <l_fs_zaiko>.
      l_wa_reserve-menge_cs    = o_v_zsztorisu.
      CASE <l_fs_zaiko>-flg_y.
*         *--- 予定在庫の場合､予定在庫数量へ追加
        WHEN c_on.
          l_wa_reserve-yotei_cs = o_v_zsztorisu.
          <l_fs_zaiko>-yotei_cs = <l_fs_zaiko>-menge_cs - o_v_zsztorisu.
          CLEAR : l_wa_reserve-jitu_cs.
*         *--- 利用可能在庫の場合､実在庫数量を設定
        WHEN OTHERS.
          l_wa_reserve-jitu_cs  = o_v_zsztorisu.
          <l_fs_zaiko>-jitu_cs  = <l_fs_zaiko>-menge_cs - o_v_zsztorisu.
          CLEAR : l_wa_reserve-yotei_cs.
      ENDCASE.
      <l_fs_zaiko>-menge_cs    = <l_fs_zaiko>-menge_cs - o_v_zsztorisu.
      o_v_zsztorisu = 0.
    ENDIF.
*     *--- 取置区分を付加してﾘｻﾞｰﾌﾞ在庫に追加する
    l_wa_reserve-zsztorikbn = i_wa_zstmm0069-zsztorikbn.
    APPEND l_wa_reserve TO it_reserve_001.
    CLEAR :
      l_wa_reserve.
    CHECK o_v_zsztorisu = 0.
    EXIT.
  ENDLOOP.

ENDFORM.                    " ADJUwa_STOCK_RESERVE_RTN_001
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  REWRITE_RESERVE_RTN_001
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       ﾘｻﾞｰﾌﾞ在庫書き戻しと引当済情報作成
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->I_v_VBELN              伝票番号
*      -->I_v_POSNR              明細番号
*      -->I_v_KBN                区分
*      -->I_v_LGORT              保管場所
*      -->I_v_MATNR              品目
*      -->I_IT_RESERVE            引当処理後ﾘｻﾞｰﾌﾞ在庫
*      <--O_IT_RESERVE            引当前ﾘｻﾞｰﾌﾞ在庫
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM rewrite_reserve_rtn_001
  USING     i_v_vbeln            TYPE ty_9100-vbeln
            i_v_posnr            TYPE ty_9100-posnr
            i_v_kbn              TYPE ty_9100-kbn
            i_v_lgort            TYPE ty_reserve_001-lgort
            i_v_matnr            TYPE ty_reserve_001-matnr
            i_v_zsztorikbn       TYPE ty_reserve_001-zsztorikbn
            i_it_reserve          TYPE ty_it_reserve_001
            i_v_werks              TYPE ty_9100-werks

*  CHANGING  O_IT_RESERVE          TYPE TY_IT_RESERVE_001.
  CHANGING  o_v_bestq_txt        TYPE ty_9100-bestq_txt
            o_it_reserve          TYPE ty_it_reserve_001.


  DATA :
    l_wa_reserve        TYPE LINE OF ty_it_reserve_001,
    l_wa_start          TYPE LINE OF ty_it_reserve_001,
    l_wa_hikiate        TYPE LINE OF ty_it_hikiate_001.

  DATA :
    l_flg_avail         TYPE xfeld,
    l_flg_check         TYPE xfeld.


* *--- CCW&仙台&四国 個別の実行権限がある場合のみ実施
  CHECK v_routine_001 = c_kosha.

* *--- 引当前の情報から引当結果を取得する
  LOOP AT o_it_reserve INTO  l_wa_start
                       WHERE werks      =  i_v_werks"p_werks
                         AND lgort      =  i_v_lgort
                         AND matnr      =  i_v_matnr
                         AND zsztorikbn =  i_v_zsztorikbn.
*   *--- 引当結果を取得する
    READ TABLE i_it_reserve INTO l_wa_reserve
                           WITH KEY werks      = l_wa_start-werks
                                    lgort      = l_wa_start-lgort
                                    matnr      = l_wa_start-matnr
                                    eda        = l_wa_start-eda
                                    kiboshom   = l_wa_start-kiboshom
                                    eindt      = l_wa_start-eindt
                                    letyp      = l_wa_start-letyp
                                    bestq      = l_wa_start-bestq
                                    charg      = l_wa_start-charg
                                    hsdat      = l_wa_start-hsdat
                                    flg_y      = l_wa_start-flg_y
                                    flg_ekpo   = l_wa_start-flg_ekpo
                                    flg_afpo   = l_wa_start-flg_afpo
                                    zsztorikbn = l_wa_start-zsztorikbn

*                           TRANSPORTING MENGE_CS
                           TRANSPORTING flg_y
                                        bestq
                                        menge_cs

                                        jitu_cs
                                        yotei_cs
                                        sumi_cs.
*   *--- 数量項目に変更がない場合､当該在庫からの引当はないので対象外
    IF    l_wa_start-menge_cs = l_wa_reserve-menge_cs
      AND l_wa_start-jitu_cs  = l_wa_reserve-jitu_cs
      AND l_wa_start-yotei_cs = l_wa_reserve-yotei_cs
      AND l_wa_start-sumi_cs  = l_wa_reserve-sumi_cs.
      CLEAR :
        l_wa_reserve.
      CONTINUE.
*   *--- 引当情報を退避
    ELSE.
*    *--- 引当情報へ登録
      l_wa_hikiate-werks      =  l_wa_start-werks.
      l_wa_hikiate-lgort      =  l_wa_start-lgort.
      l_wa_hikiate-matnr      =  l_wa_start-matnr.
      l_wa_hikiate-edacode    =  l_wa_start-eda.
      l_wa_hikiate-syomi      =  l_wa_start-kiboshom.
      l_wa_hikiate-eindt      =  l_wa_start-eindt.
      l_wa_hikiate-flg_y      =  l_wa_start-flg_y.
      l_wa_hikiate-bestq      =  l_wa_start-bestq.
      l_wa_hikiate-plcode     =  l_wa_start-letyp.
      l_wa_hikiate-kbn        =  i_v_kbn.
      l_wa_hikiate-vbeln      =  i_v_vbeln.
      l_wa_hikiate-posnr      =  i_v_posnr.
      l_wa_hikiate-charg      =  l_wa_start-charg.
      l_wa_hikiate-hsdat      =  l_wa_start-hsdat.
      l_wa_hikiate-flg_ekpo   =  l_wa_start-flg_ekpo.
      l_wa_hikiate-flg_afpo   =  l_wa_start-flg_afpo.
      l_wa_hikiate-zsztorikbn = l_wa_start-zsztorikbn.
*     *--- 開始時点の数量-終了時点の数量を引当数量とする
      l_wa_hikiate-sumi_cs = l_wa_start-menge_cs - l_wa_reserve-menge_cs.
      APPEND l_wa_hikiate TO  it_hiki_reserve_001.

*     *--- 引当在庫のｶﾃｺﾞﾘﾁｪｯｸ
      IF     l_wa_reserve-flg_y = space          " 利用可能
         AND l_wa_reserve-bestq = space.
        l_flg_avail = c_on.
      ELSEIF l_wa_reserve-bestq = c_bestq.     " 品検中
        l_flg_check = c_on.
      ENDIF.

      CLEAR :
        l_wa_hikiate,
        l_wa_reserve.
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.
* *--- 引当後のﾘｻﾞｰﾌﾞ在庫を引当前のﾘｻﾞｰﾌﾞ在庫へ返却
  o_it_reserve[] = i_it_reserve[].

  IF     l_flg_avail = c_on
     AND l_flg_check = c_on.
    CONCATENATE text-004 text-014 text-005
           INTO o_v_bestq_txt.             " '良品＋品検中'
  ELSEIF l_flg_avail = c_on.
    o_v_bestq_txt = text-004.              " '良品'
  ELSEIF l_flg_check = c_on.
    o_v_bestq_txt = text-005.              " '品検中'
  ENDIF.


ENDFORM.                    " REWRITE_RESERVE_RTN_001
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  CHECK_9100_TORIOKI_RTN_001
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       取置在庫の引当
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      <--O_WA_9100               引当対象
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM check_9100_torioki_rtn_001
  CHANGING  o_wa_9100             TYPE ty_9100.

  DATA :
    l_it_reserve        TYPE ty_it_reserve_001,
    l_v_syomi          TYPE ty_9100-syomi,
    l_v_edacode        TYPE ty_9100-edacode,
    l_flg_overflow      TYPE xfeld.

* *--- CCW&仙台&四国 個別の実行権限がある場合のみ実施
  CHECK v_routine_001 = c_kosha.

* *--- 引当前の状態を退避
  l_it_reserve[] = it_reserve_001[].
  l_v_syomi     = o_wa_9100-syomi.
  l_v_edacode   = o_wa_9100-edacode.
* *--- ﾊﾟﾚｯﾄ種別あり
  IF o_wa_9100-plcode IS NOT INITIAL.
*   *--- ﾊﾟﾚｯﾄ種別一致で取置在庫(利用可能)からの引当
    PERFORM hikiate_torioki_plcode_rtn_001
                             USING     space
                                       space
                                       o_wa_9100-plcode
                             CHANGING  l_flg_overflow
                                       o_wa_9100
                                       l_it_reserve[].
    IF   l_flg_overflow = c_on.
      RETURN.
    ENDIF.
*   *--- ﾊﾟﾚｯﾄ種別一致で利用可能在庫引当不可→ﾒｯｾｰｼﾞを設定しておく
    IF o_wa_9100-flg_sumi = space.
      o_wa_9100-syomi   = l_v_syomi.
      o_wa_9100-edacode = l_v_edacode.
*     *--- ﾊﾟﾚｯﾄ種別任意で取置在庫(利用可能)から引当て
      PERFORM hikiate_torioki_rtn_001
                             USING     space
                                       space
                             CHANGING  l_flg_overflow
                                       o_wa_9100
                                       l_it_reserve[].
*       *--- 引当完了⇒混在ﾒｯｾｰｼﾞ設定
      IF o_wa_9100-flg_sumi = c_on.
*         *--- MSG:指定されたパレット種別の在庫がありません
*        o_wa_9100-message   = text-s19.
        o_wa_9100-flg_letyp = c_on.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
* *--- ﾊﾟﾚｯﾄ種別未設定で引当
  ELSE.
*   *--- ﾊﾟﾚｯﾄ種別任意で取置在庫(利用可能)から引当て
    PERFORM hikiate_torioki_rtn_001
                             USING     space
                                       space
                             CHANGING  l_flg_overflow
                                       o_wa_9100
                                       l_it_reserve[].
    IF l_flg_overflow = c_on.
      RETURN.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.
* *--- 利用可能在庫で引当が完了したら､"良品"を設定
  IF    o_wa_9100-flg_sumi  = c_on.

*    O_WA_9100-BESTQ_TXT = TEXT-004.

    RETURN.
  ELSE.
    o_wa_9100-syomi   = l_v_syomi.
    o_wa_9100-edacode = l_v_edacode.
  ENDIF.
* *--- ﾊﾟﾚｯﾄ種別一致で取置在庫(品検中)から引当て
  PERFORM hikiate_torioki_plcode_rtn_001
                             USING     c_x
                                       c_bestq_q
                                       o_wa_9100-plcode
                             CHANGING  l_flg_overflow
                                       o_wa_9100
                                       l_it_reserve[].
  IF l_flg_overflow = c_on.
    RETURN.
  ENDIF.
* *--- ﾊﾟﾚｯﾄ種別任意で取置在庫(品検中)から引当て
  IF o_wa_9100-flg_sumi = space.
    o_wa_9100-syomi   = l_v_syomi.
    o_wa_9100-edacode = l_v_edacode.
  ENDIF.
  PERFORM hikiate_torioki_rtn_001
                             USING     c_x
                                       c_bestq_q
                             CHANGING  l_flg_overflow
                                       o_wa_9100
                                       l_it_reserve[].
  IF l_flg_overflow = c_on.
    RETURN.
  ENDIF.
* *--- 引当不可
  CHECK o_wa_9100-flg_sumi = space.
  o_wa_9100-edacode  = o_wa_9100-edacode_wrk.
  o_wa_9100-syomi    = o_wa_9100-syomi_wrk.
* *--- 引当不可の処理
  PERFORM set_sts_hiki_ng_rtn_001
                             CHANGING  o_wa_9100.

ENDFORM.                    " CHECK_9100_TORIOKI_RTN_001
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  CHECK_9100_ZAIKO_RTN_001
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       通常在庫からの引当
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      <--O_WA_9100               引当対象
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM check_9100_zaiko_rtn_001
  CHANGING  o_wa_9100             TYPE ty_9100.

  DATA :
    l_v_edacode        TYPE ty_9100-edacode,
    l_v_syomi          TYPE ty_9100-syomi,       " 引当開始時点の賞味期限
    l_v_menge          TYPE ty_9100-menge_cs,    " 予定在庫引当開始時点の数量
    l_v_flash_kbn      TYPE ty_9100-flash_kbn,   " 引当処理区分
    l_it_zaiko          TYPE tbl_ty_zaiko.        " 在庫(退避用)

* *--- CCW&仙台&四国 個別の実行権限がある場合のみ実施
  CHECK v_routine_001 = c_kosha.

* *--- 初期化
  CLEAR flg_over.

* *--- 引当対象の条件作成
  PERFORM edit_range_rtn_001 USING     o_wa_9100-edacode
                                       o_wa_9100-syomi
                                       o_wa_9100-wadat
                                       o_wa_9100-flash_kbn.

* CCW社個別権限の場合
  IF flg_rout_006 = c_kosha AND
     o_wa_9100-syomi_org IS NOT INITIAL AND
     o_wa_9100-hub IS INITIAL.
*   引当条件(賞味き期限)の再作成
    PERFORM f_reser_rng_syomi USING o_wa_9100-syomi_org.
    o_wa_9100-flash_kbn = c_flash_b_001.
  ENDIF.

* *--- 開始前の在庫を退避
  l_it_zaiko[] = it_zaiko[].
  l_v_edacode   = o_wa_9100-edacode.
  l_v_syomi     = o_wa_9100-syomi.
  l_v_flash_kbn = o_wa_9100-flash_kbn.
  CLEAR o_wa_9100-flash_kbn.

* *--- ﾊﾟﾚｯﾄ種別未入力の場合の引当処理
  IF o_wa_9100-plcode IS INITIAL.
*   *--- ﾊﾟﾚｯﾄ種別条件なしで実在庫の引当
    PERFORM hikiate_jitu     USING     space     " 予定在庫flag
                                       space     " 在庫ｶﾃｺﾞﾘ
                             CHANGING  o_wa_9100
                                       l_it_zaiko[].
*   *--- overflow->終了
    IF flg_over = c_on.
      o_wa_9100-flash_kbn = l_v_flash_kbn.
      RETURN.
    ENDIF.
* *--- ﾊﾟﾚｯﾄ種別に入力がある場合の引当処理
  ELSE.
*   *--- ﾊﾟﾚｯﾄ種別が一致する実在庫の引当
    PERFORM hikiate_jitu_letyp
                             USING     space     " 予定在庫flag
                                       space     " 在庫ｶﾃｺﾞﾘ
                             CHANGING  o_wa_9100
                                       l_it_zaiko[].
*   *--- overflow->終了
    IF flg_over = c_on.
      o_wa_9100-flash_kbn = l_v_flash_kbn.
      RETURN.
    ENDIF.
*   *--- ﾊﾟﾚｯﾄ種別一致の在庫で引当が完了しない場合
    IF o_wa_9100-flg_sumi IS INITIAL.

*     *--- 開始時点の枝番がﾌﾞﾗﾝｸの場合は､開始時点の枝番に戻す
      IF l_v_edacode IS INITIAL.
        o_wa_9100-edacode = l_v_edacode.
      ENDIF.
*     *--- ﾊﾟﾚｯﾄ種別条件なしで実在庫の引当
      PERFORM hikiate_jitu   USING     space     " 予定在庫flag
                                       space     " 在庫ｶﾃｺﾞﾘ
                             CHANGING  o_wa_9100
                                       l_it_zaiko[].
*     *--- overflow->終了
      IF flg_over = c_on.
        o_wa_9100-flash_kbn = l_v_flash_kbn.
        RETURN.
      ENDIF.
*     *--- ﾊﾟﾚｯﾄ種別条件なしで引当が完了した場合
      IF o_wa_9100-flg_sumi = c_on.
*       *--- ﾊﾟﾚｯﾄ種別指定なしのﾒｯｾｰｼﾞ設定
*        o_wa_9100-message = text-s19.     " '指定されたパレット種別の在庫がありません'
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.
* *--- 引当完了
  IF o_wa_9100-flg_sumi = c_on.
*   *--- 引当結果を在庫ﾃﾞｰﾀと引当情報に設定
    PERFORM rewrite_zaiko_edit_hikiate
                             USING     o_wa_9100-vbeln
                                       o_wa_9100-posnr
                                       o_wa_9100-kbn
                                       o_wa_9100-lgort
                                       o_wa_9100-matnr
                                       l_it_zaiko[]
                                       o_wa_9100-werks
                             CHANGING  it_zaiko[].
*   *--- 引当済の場合は､引き剝がしの為に､引当開始時点の枝番と引き当てた在庫の賞味を保持しておく
    o_wa_9100-edacode_wrk = l_v_edacode.
    o_wa_9100-syomi_wrk   = o_wa_9100-syomi.
    o_wa_9100-flash_kbn = l_v_flash_kbn.
    RETURN.
* *--- 引当未完了
  ELSE.
*   *--- 引当できない場合､引当処理内で変更されている可能性がある項目を引当前の状態に戻す
    o_wa_9100-edacode   = l_v_edacode.
    o_wa_9100-syomi     = l_v_syomi.
  ENDIF.

* *--- ﾊﾟﾚｯﾄ種別未入力の場合の引当処理
  IF o_wa_9100-plcode IS INITIAL.
*   *--- ﾊﾟﾚｯﾄ種別条件なしで品検中在庫の引当
    PERFORM hikiate_jitu     USING     c_x          " 予定在庫flag
                                       c_bestq_q    " 在庫ｶﾃｺﾞﾘ
                             CHANGING  o_wa_9100
                                       l_it_zaiko[].
*   *--- overflow->終了
    IF flg_over = c_on.
      o_wa_9100-flash_kbn = l_v_flash_kbn.
      RETURN.
    ENDIF.
* *--- ﾊﾟﾚｯﾄ種別に入力がある場合の引当処理
  ELSE.
*   *--- ﾊﾟﾚｯﾄ種別が一致する品検中在庫の引当
    PERFORM hikiate_jitu_letyp
                             USING     c_x          " 予定在庫flag
                                       c_bestq_q    " 在庫ｶﾃｺﾞﾘ
                             CHANGING  o_wa_9100
                                       l_it_zaiko[].
*   *--- overflow->終了
    IF flg_over = c_on.
      o_wa_9100-flash_kbn = l_v_flash_kbn.
      RETURN.
    ENDIF.
*   *--- ﾊﾟﾚｯﾄ種別一致の在庫で引当が完了しない場合
    IF o_wa_9100-flg_sumi IS INITIAL.
*     *--- 引当開始時点の数量を保持
      l_v_menge = o_wa_9100-menge_cs.
*     *--- ﾊﾟﾚｯﾄ種別条件なしで品検中在庫の引当
      PERFORM hikiate_jitu   USING     c_x          " 予定在庫flag
                                       c_bestq_q    " 在庫ｶﾃｺﾞﾘ
                             CHANGING  o_wa_9100
                                       l_it_zaiko[].

*     *--- overflow->終了
      IF flg_over = c_on.
        o_wa_9100-flash_kbn = l_v_flash_kbn.
        RETURN.
      ENDIF.
*     *--- ﾊﾟﾚｯﾄ種別条件なしで一部でも引当が実施された場合
      IF    o_wa_9100-flg_sumi <> c_on
        AND o_wa_9100-menge_cs <> l_v_menge.
        o_wa_9100-flg_letyp = c_on.     " 保管単位考慮なしのﾌﾗｸﾞを設定
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

* *--- 引当完了
  IF o_wa_9100-flg_sumi = c_on.
*   *--- 引当結果を在庫ﾃﾞｰﾀと引当情報に設定
    PERFORM rewrite_zaiko_edit_hikiate
                             USING     o_wa_9100-vbeln
                                       o_wa_9100-posnr
                                       o_wa_9100-kbn
                                       o_wa_9100-lgort
                                       o_wa_9100-matnr
                                       l_it_zaiko[]
                                       o_wa_9100-werks
                             CHANGING  it_zaiko[].
*   *--- 一覧表示される枝番と賞味を引当前の表示内容(初期処理はｵｰﾀﾞ)に戻す
    o_wa_9100-edacode     = o_wa_9100-edacode_wrk.
    o_wa_9100-syomi       = o_wa_9100-syomi_wrk.
*   *--- 引き剝がし用に引当開始時点の枝番と賞味を保持しておく
    o_wa_9100-edacode_wrk = l_v_edacode.
    o_wa_9100-syomi_wrk   = l_v_syomi.
    o_wa_9100-flash_kbn = l_v_flash_kbn.
    RETURN.
* *--- 引当未完了
  ELSE.
*   *--- 引当できない場合､引当処理内で変更されている可能性がある項目を引当前の状態に戻す
    o_wa_9100-edacode   = l_v_edacode.
    o_wa_9100-syomi     = l_v_syomi.
  ENDIF.


* CCW社個別権限の場合
  IF flg_rout_006 = c_kosha AND

     o_wa_9100-hub IS INITIAL AND

     o_wa_9100-syomi_org IS NOT INITIAL.
*   *--- 一覧表示される枝番と賞味を引当前の表示内容(初期処理はｵｰﾀﾞ)に戻す
    o_wa_9100-edacode     = o_wa_9100-edacode_wrk.
    o_wa_9100-syomi       = o_wa_9100-syomi_wrk.
    o_wa_9100-flg_letyp   = space.
    o_wa_9100-flash_kbn = l_v_flash_kbn.
  ELSE.


* *--- 指定賞味の場合､入荷予定･製造予定からの引当は行わない
    IF l_v_flash_kbn = c_flash_b_001.
*   *--- 一覧表示される枝番と賞味を引当前の表示内容(初期処理はｵｰﾀﾞ)に戻す
      o_wa_9100-edacode     = o_wa_9100-edacode_wrk.
      o_wa_9100-syomi       = o_wa_9100-syomi_wrk.
      o_wa_9100-flg_letyp   = space.
      o_wa_9100-flash_kbn = l_v_flash_kbn.
* *--- 入力値が以降賞味の場合のみ､入荷予定･製造予定からの引当
    ELSE.

* *--- 入荷予定・製造予定からの引当
      PERFORM hikiate_yotei      CHANGING  o_wa_9100
                                           l_it_zaiko[].
*   *--- overflow->終了
      IF flg_over = c_on.
        o_wa_9100-flash_kbn = l_v_flash_kbn.
        RETURN.
      ENDIF.
* *--- 引当完了
      IF o_wa_9100-flg_sumi = c_on.
*   *--- 引当結果を在庫ﾃﾞｰﾀと引当情報に設定
        PERFORM rewrite_zaiko_edit_hikiate
                                 USING     o_wa_9100-vbeln
                                           o_wa_9100-posnr
                                           o_wa_9100-kbn
                                           o_wa_9100-lgort
                                           o_wa_9100-matnr
                                           l_it_zaiko[]
                                           o_wa_9100-werks
                                 CHANGING  it_zaiko[].
*   *--- 一覧表示される枝番と賞味を引当前の表示内容(初期処理はｵｰﾀﾞ)に戻す
        o_wa_9100-edacode     = o_wa_9100-edacode_wrk.
        o_wa_9100-syomi       = o_wa_9100-syomi_wrk.
*   *--- 引き剝がし用に引当開始時点の枝番と賞味を保持しておく
        o_wa_9100-edacode_wrk = l_v_edacode.
        o_wa_9100-syomi_wrk   = l_v_syomi.
        o_wa_9100-flash_kbn = l_v_flash_kbn.
        RETURN.
* *--- 引当不可となる場合
      ELSE.
*   *--- 一覧表示される枝番と賞味を引当前の表示内容(初期処理はｵｰﾀﾞ)に戻す
        o_wa_9100-edacode     = o_wa_9100-edacode_wrk.
        o_wa_9100-syomi       = o_wa_9100-syomi_wrk.
        o_wa_9100-flg_letyp   = space.
        o_wa_9100-flash_kbn = l_v_flash_kbn.
      ENDIF.

    ENDIF.

  ENDIF.

* *--- 引当不可の処理
  PERFORM set_sts_hiki_ng_rtn_001
                             CHANGING  o_wa_9100.

ENDFORM.                    " CHECK_9100_ZAIKO_RTN_001
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  EDIT_RANGE_RTN_001
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       引当or減算対象の条件設定
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->I_v_EDA                枝番
*      -->I_v_KIBOSHOM           賞味期限
*      -->I_v_WADAT              出荷予定日
*      -->I_v_ZZSKYOSEIKB        引当区分
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM edit_range_rtn_001
  USING     i_v_eda              TYPE any
            i_v_kiboshom         TYPE any
            i_v_wadat            TYPE d
            i_v_zzskyoseikb      TYPE any.

  DATA :
    l_wa_eda            LIKE LINE OF rng_eda,
    l_wa_syomi          LIKE LINE OF rng_syomi,
    l_wa_wadat          LIKE LINE OF rng_wadat.

* *--- CCW&仙台&四国 個別の実行権限がある場合のみ実施
  CHECK v_routine_001 = c_kosha.

  REFRESH :
    rng_eda,
    rng_syomi,
    rng_wadat.

* *--- 枝番が初期値以外の場合､指定枝番とする
  IF i_v_eda IS NOT INITIAL.
    l_wa_eda-sign    =  c_sign_i.
    l_wa_eda-option  =  c_opt_eq.
    l_wa_eda-low     =  i_v_eda.
    APPEND l_wa_eda TO rng_eda.
  ENDIF.

* *--- 指定賞味の場合､指定の賞味期限のみ
  IF i_v_zzskyoseikb = c_flash_b_001.
    l_wa_syomi-sign   = c_sign_i.
    l_wa_syomi-option = c_opt_eq.
    l_wa_syomi-low    = i_v_kiboshom.
    APPEND l_wa_syomi TO rng_syomi.
* *--- 以降賞味の場合､指定の賞味期限以降
  ELSE.
    l_wa_syomi-sign   = c_sign_i.
    l_wa_syomi-option = c_opt_ge.
    l_wa_syomi-low    = i_v_kiboshom.
    APPEND l_wa_syomi TO rng_syomi.
  ENDIF.

* *--- 出荷予定
  l_wa_wadat-sign   = c_sign_i.
  l_wa_wadat-option = c_opt_le.
  l_wa_wadat-low    = i_v_wadat - v_factr.
  APPEND l_wa_wadat TO rng_wadat.
ENDFORM.                    " EDIT_RANGE_RTN_001
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  SET_STS_HIKI_NG_RTN_001
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       引当不可時のｽﾃｰﾀｽ設定
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      <-O_WA_9100                引当対象
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM set_sts_hiki_ng_rtn_001
  CHANGING  o_wa_9100             TYPE ty_9100.

* *--- CCW&仙台&四国 個別の実行権限がある場合のみ実施
  CHECK v_routine_001 = c_kosha.

  o_wa_9100-status   = text-010.            " 処理Stat
  o_wa_9100-icon     = v_icon_red.         " 信号機
  o_wa_9100-message  = text-e10.            " 引当不可（在庫数量不足）
  PERFORM get_sort_icon      USING     o_wa_9100-icon
                             CHANGING  o_wa_9100-sort_icon.
  PERFORM get_sort_staus     USING     o_wa_9100-status
                             CHANGING  o_wa_9100-sort_sta.

ENDFORM.                    " SET_STS_HIKI_NG_RTN_001
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  REVERSE_RESERVE_DATA_RTN_001
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       賞味跨ぎの在庫戻し
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      <--O_v_ZAN                前回の引当可能数
*      <--O_v_MENGE_PRV          前回賞味の引当開始前数量
*      <--O_MENGE_CS              引当可能残数
*      <--O_IT_RESERVE            ﾘｻﾞｰﾌﾞ在庫
*      <--O_IT_RESERVE_PRV        前回賞味期限のﾘｻﾞｰﾌﾞ在庫
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM reverse_reserve_data_rtn_001
  CHANGING  o_v_zan              TYPE mengv13"NA zstmm0069-zsztorisu
            o_v_menge_prv        TYPE ty_9100-menge_cs
            o_menge_cs            TYPE ty_9100-menge_cs
            o_it_reserve          TYPE ty_it_reserve_001
            o_it_reserve_prv      TYPE ty_it_reserve_001.

  DATA :
    l_wa_reserve        TYPE LINE OF ty_it_reserve_001.
  FIELD-SYMBOLS :
    <l_fs_reserve>      TYPE LINE OF ty_it_reserve_001.

* *--- CCW&仙台&四国 個別の実行権限がある場合のみ実施
  CHECK v_routine_001 = c_kosha.

* *--- 在庫戻し
  LOOP AT o_it_reserve_prv INTO l_wa_reserve.
    READ TABLE o_it_reserve ASSIGNING <l_fs_reserve>
                        WITH KEY werks      = l_wa_reserve-werks
                                 lgort      = l_wa_reserve-lgort
                                 matnr      = l_wa_reserve-matnr
                                 eda        = l_wa_reserve-eda
                                 kiboshom   = l_wa_reserve-kiboshom
                                 eindt      = l_wa_reserve-eindt
                                 letyp      = l_wa_reserve-letyp
                                 bestq      = l_wa_reserve-bestq
                                 charg      = l_wa_reserve-charg
                                 hsdat      = l_wa_reserve-hsdat
                                 flg_y      = l_wa_reserve-flg_y
                                 flg_hiki   = l_wa_reserve-flg_hiki
                                 num_hiki   = l_wa_reserve-num_hiki
                                 flg_ekpo   = l_wa_reserve-flg_ekpo
                                 flg_afpo   = l_wa_reserve-flg_afpo
                                 zsztorikbn = l_wa_reserve-zsztorikbn.
    CHECK <l_fs_reserve> IS ASSIGNED.
    <l_fs_reserve> = l_wa_reserve.
    UNASSIGN <l_fs_reserve>.
  ENDLOOP.
  o_menge_cs = o_v_menge_prv.
  o_v_zan = o_menge_cs.
  REFRESH o_it_reserve_prv.

ENDFORM.                    " REVERSE_RESERVE_DATA_RTN_001
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  HIKIATE_TORIOKI_PLCODE_RTN_001
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       特定荷主の引当(ﾊﾟﾚｯﾄ種別指定)
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->I_FLG_Y                 予定在庫
*      -->I_v_BESTQ              在庫ｶﾃｺﾞﾘ
*      -->I_v_PLCODE             ﾊﾟﾚｯﾄ種別
*      <--O_FLG_OVERFLOW          ｵｰﾊﾞｰﾌﾛｰﾌﾗｸﾞ
*      <--O_WA_9100               引当対象ﾃﾞｰﾀ
*      <--O_IT_RESERVE            ﾘｻﾞｰﾌﾞ在庫
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM hikiate_torioki_plcode_rtn_001
  USING     i_flg_y               TYPE ty_reserve_001-flg_y
            i_v_bestq            TYPE ty_reserve_001-bestq
            i_v_plcode           TYPE ty_9100-plcode
  CHANGING  o_flg_overflow        TYPE xfeld
            o_wa_9100             TYPE ty_9100
            o_it_reserve          TYPE ty_it_reserve_001.

  DATA :
    l_it_reserve_prv    TYPE ty_it_reserve_001,               " 引当済分
    l_v_zszsyomi_prv   TYPE ty_reserve_001-kiboshom,         " 前loop賞味
    l_v_edacode        TYPE ty_9100-edacode,                 " 引当開始時点の枝番
    l_v_syomi          TYPE ty_9100-syomi,                   " 引当開始時点の賞味期限
    l_v_menge_prv      TYPE ty_9100-menge_cs,                " 前賞味引当開始時点の残数
    l_v_zan            TYPE mengv13,"NAzstmm0069-zsztorisu,             " 引当残数量
    l_v_kano_zan       TYPE mengv13,"NAzstmm0069-zsztorisu,             " 引当可能残数量
    l_v_sumi_su        TYPE ty_reserve_001-sumi_cs,          " 引当済数量計算用
    l_cnt_loop          TYPE syst-tabix.                      " loopｶｳﾝﾀ
  FIELD-SYMBOLS :
    <l_fs_yojitu>       TYPE any,                             " 予定 or 実在庫
    <l_fs_reserve>      TYPE LINE OF ty_it_reserve_001.       " 引当対象ﾘｻﾞｰﾌﾞ在庫

* *--- CCW&仙台&四国 個別の実行権限がある場合のみ実施
  CHECK v_routine_001 = c_kosha.

* *--- 引当が完了していない場合のみ実施
  CHECK o_wa_9100-flg_sumi = space.
* *--- 引当対象の条件作成
  PERFORM edit_range_rtn_001 USING     o_wa_9100-edacode
                                       o_wa_9100-syomi
                                       o_wa_9100-wadat
                                       o_wa_9100-flash_kbn.
* *--- ﾃﾞｰﾀ退避
  l_v_edacode   = o_wa_9100-edacode.
  l_v_syomi     = o_wa_9100-syomi.
  l_v_zan       = o_wa_9100-menge_cs.
  l_v_menge_prv = o_wa_9100-menge_cs.
* *--- 引当処理
  LOOP AT o_it_reserve ASSIGNING <l_fs_reserve>
                       WHERE werks      =  o_wa_9100-werks"p_werks
                         AND lgort      =  o_wa_9100-lgort
                         AND matnr      =  o_wa_9100-matnr
                         AND eda        IN rng_eda
                         AND kiboshom   IN rng_syomi
                         AND zsztorikbn =  o_wa_9100-zsztorikbn
                         AND letyp      =  i_v_plcode
                         AND ( flg_y    =  i_flg_y
                            OR flg_y    =  space )
                         AND ( bestq    =  i_v_bestq
                            OR bestq    =  space )
                         AND menge_cs   >  0.
    l_cnt_loop = l_cnt_loop + 1.
    IF l_cnt_loop = 1.
      l_v_zszsyomi_prv = <l_fs_reserve>-kiboshom.
    ENDIF.
*   *--- 前loopで引当てたﾃﾞｰﾀと賞味期限が異なる場合(賞味跨ぎ)
    IF l_v_zszsyomi_prv <> <l_fs_reserve>-kiboshom.
      l_v_zszsyomi_prv = <l_fs_reserve>-kiboshom.
      PERFORM reverse_reserve_data_rtn_001
                             CHANGING  l_v_zan
                                       l_v_menge_prv
                                       o_wa_9100-menge_cs
                                       o_it_reserve
                                       l_it_reserve_prv.
    ENDIF.
*   *--- 次loopで賞味跨ぎとなった場合の戻し用にﾃﾞｰﾀ保持
    APPEND <l_fs_reserve> TO  l_it_reserve_prv.
    l_v_kano_zan = <l_fs_reserve>-menge_cs.
*   *--- 引当可能残数>0の場合のみ実施
    CHECK l_v_kano_zan > 0.

*    CASE I_v_BESTQ.
    CASE <l_fs_reserve>-bestq.

      WHEN c_bestq_q.      "品検中
" Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_APS Conversion **BEGIN OF CHANGE BY EJX9007359 11.06.2026  for ATC
*         ASSIGN COMPONENT c_fld_yotei_cs OF STRUCTURE <l_fs_reserve> TO <l_fs_yojitu>.
        ASSIGN COMPONENT c_fld_yotei_cs OF STRUCTURE <l_fs_reserve> TO <l_fs_yojitu>. "#EC CI_FLDEXT_OK[0002250183]
" Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_APS Conversion * *END OF CHANGE BY EJX9007359 11.06.2026 for ATC
      WHEN OTHERS.           "品検中以外
" Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_APS Conversion **BEGIN OF CHANGE BY EJX9007359 11.06.2026  for ATC
*         ASSIGN COMPONENT c_fld_jitu_cs  OF STRUCTURE <l_fs_reserve> TO <l_fs_yojitu>.
        ASSIGN COMPONENT c_fld_jitu_cs  OF STRUCTURE <l_fs_reserve> TO <l_fs_yojitu>. "#EC CI_FLDEXT_OK[0002250183]
" Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_APS Conversion * *END OF CHANGE BY EJX9007359 11.06.2026 for ATC
    ENDCASE.
    IF l_v_zan > l_v_kano_zan.
      <l_fs_reserve>-menge_cs = 0.
      <l_fs_yojitu>           = 0.
      TRY.
          l_v_sumi_su = <l_fs_reserve>-sumi_cs + l_v_kano_zan.
          <l_fs_reserve>-sumi_cs = l_v_sumi_su.
        CATCH cx_root.
          o_flg_overflow      = c_on.
          o_wa_9100-edacode   = l_v_edacode.
          o_wa_9100-syomi     = l_v_syomi.
          PERFORM edit_overflow_9100
                             CHANGING  o_wa_9100.
          RETURN.
      ENDTRY.
      o_wa_9100-menge_cs  = o_wa_9100-menge_cs - l_v_kano_zan.
      l_v_zan            = l_v_zan           - l_v_kano_zan.
      o_wa_9100-edacode   = <l_fs_reserve>-eda.            " 枝番
      o_wa_9100-syomi     = <l_fs_reserve>-kiboshom.       " 賞味期限
    ELSE.
      <l_fs_reserve>-menge_cs = <l_fs_reserve>-menge_cs - l_v_zan.
      <l_fs_yojitu>           = <l_fs_yojitu>           - l_v_zan.
      TRY.
          l_v_sumi_su = <l_fs_reserve>-sumi_cs + l_v_zan.
          <l_fs_reserve>-sumi_cs = l_v_sumi_su.
        CATCH cx_root.
          o_flg_overflow      = c_on.
          o_wa_9100-edacode   = l_v_edacode.
          o_wa_9100-syomi     = l_v_syomi.
          PERFORM edit_overflow_9100
                             CHANGING  o_wa_9100.
          RETURN.
      ENDTRY.
      o_wa_9100-status    = text-010.               " 処理ステータス
      CASE i_v_bestq.
*       *--- 在庫ｶﾃｺﾞﾘ＝SPACEの場合､通常引当可
        WHEN space.
          o_wa_9100-icon = v_icon_green.
*       *--- 在庫ｶﾃｺﾞﾘ≠SPACEの場合､予定引当可
        WHEN OTHERS.
          o_wa_9100-icon = v_icon_yellow.
      ENDCASE.
      o_wa_9100-flg_sumi  = c_on.                 " 引当済フラグ
      PERFORM get_sort_icon  USING     o_wa_9100-icon
                             CHANGING  o_wa_9100-sort_icon.
      PERFORM get_sort_staus USING     o_wa_9100-status
                             CHANGING  o_wa_9100-sort_sta.
      o_wa_9100-edacode   = <l_fs_reserve>-eda.            " 枝番
      o_wa_9100-syomi     = <l_fs_reserve>-kiboshom.       " 賞味期限
      EXIT.
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.
* *--- 引当済の数量を算出する
  IF o_wa_9100-flg_sumi = c_on.
*   *--- ﾘｻﾞｰﾌﾞ在庫(引当処理用)をﾘｻﾞｰﾌﾞ在庫に更新(引当結果の在庫増減を保持)
    PERFORM rewrite_reserve_rtn_001
                             USING     o_wa_9100-vbeln
                                       o_wa_9100-posnr
                                       o_wa_9100-kbn
                                       o_wa_9100-lgort
                                       o_wa_9100-matnr
                                       o_wa_9100-zsztorikbn
                                       o_it_reserve[]
                                       o_wa_9100-werks
*                             CHANGING  IT_RESERVE_001[].
                             CHANGING  o_wa_9100-bestq_txt
                                       it_reserve_001[].

*   *--- 引き剝がし用に引当開始時点の枝番と賞味を保持しておく
    o_wa_9100-edacode_wrk = l_v_edacode.
    o_wa_9100-syomi_wrk   = l_v_syomi.
  ELSE.
*   *--- 引当できない場合､引当処理内で変更されている可能性がある項目を引当前の状態に戻す
    o_wa_9100-edacode   = l_v_edacode.
    o_wa_9100-syomi     = l_v_syomi.
    PERFORM reverse_reserve_data_rtn_001
                             CHANGING  l_v_zan
                                       l_v_menge_prv
                                       o_wa_9100-menge_cs
                                       o_it_reserve
                                       l_it_reserve_prv.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    " HIKIATE_TORIOKI_PLCODE_RTN_001
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  CHECK_RESERVE_ZAIKO_RTN_001
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       使用する在庫のﾁｪｯｸ
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->I_v_WERKS              出荷元ﾌﾟﾗﾝﾄ
*      -->I_v_MATNR              品目
*      -->I_v_KUNNR              出荷先
*      <--O_FLG_RESERVE           取置在庫使用("X"取置在庫､" "通常在庫)
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM check_reserve_zaiko_rtn_001
  USING     i_v_werks            TYPE marc-werks
            i_v_matnr            TYPE ty_9100-matnr
            i_v_kunnr            TYPE ty_9100-kunnr2
  CHANGING  o_flg_reserve         TYPE xfeld.

* *--- CCW&仙台&四国 個別の実行権限がある場合のみ実施
  CHECK v_routine_001 = c_kosha.

  CLEAR :
    o_flg_reserve.
* *--- 非ﾛｯﾄ管理品かどうか
  READ TABLE it_marc_non TRANSPORTING NO FIELDS BINARY SEARCH
                        WITH KEY matnr = i_v_matnr
                                  werks = i_v_werks.
* *--- ﾛｯﾄ管理品の場合
  CHECK sy-subrc <> 0.
  READ TABLE it_zstmm0068_001 TRANSPORTING NO FIELDS
                        WITH TABLE KEY zszshukka = i_v_kunnr.
*   *--- 特定荷主の場合､取置在庫使用
  CHECK sy-subrc = 0.
  o_flg_reserve = c_on.

ENDFORM.                    " CHECK_RESERVE_ZAIKO_RTN_001
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  MINUS_JITU_LETYP_RTN_001
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       出荷済数量減算(ﾊﾟﾚｯﾄ種別考慮)
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->I_FLG_Y            予定在庫ﾌﾗｸﾞ
*      -->I_v_BESTQ         在庫ｶﾃｺﾞﾘ
*      <--O_wa_LIPS          出荷伝票
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM minus_jitu_letyp_rtn_001
  USING     i_flg_y               TYPE ty_reserve_001-flg_y
            i_v_bestq            TYPE ty_reserve_001-bestq
  CHANGING  o_wa_lips             TYPE LINE OF ty_it_lips_001.

* ローカル定義
  FIELD-SYMBOLS:
    <l_fs_zaiko>        TYPE LINE OF ty_it_reserve_001,
    <l_fs_yojitu>       TYPE any.

* *--- CCW&仙台&四国 個別の実行権限がある場合のみ実施
*  CHECK v_routine_001 = c_kosha.

  SORT it_reserve_001 BY werks
                         lgort
                         matnr
                         kiboshom
                         eda
                         letyp
                         zsztorikbn.

* *--- 品目&ﾌﾟﾗﾝﾄ&保管場所&枝番&賞味期限&ﾊﾟﾚｯﾄ種別
  LOOP AT it_reserve_001 ASSIGNING <l_fs_zaiko>
                         WHERE matnr      =  o_wa_lips-matnr       " 品目
                           AND werks      =  o_wa_lips-werks       " ﾌﾟﾗﾝﾄ
                           AND lgort      =  o_wa_lips-lgort       " 保管場所
                           AND eda        IN rng_eda               " 枝番
                           AND kiboshom   IN rng_syomi             " 賞味期限
                           AND zsztorikbn =  o_wa_lips-zsztorikbn  " 取置区分
                           AND letyp      =  o_wa_lips-plcode      " ﾊﾟﾚｯﾄ種別
                           AND bestq      =  i_v_bestq            " 在庫ｶﾃｺﾞﾘ
                           AND flg_y      =  i_flg_y.              " 予定在庫

*   *--- 在庫ｶﾃｺﾞﾘによって､減算する引当可能数量の項目を決定(ASSIGN割当不可は想定しない)
    CASE i_v_bestq.
      WHEN c_bestq_q.      "品検中
" Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_APS Conversion **BEGIN OF CHANGE BY EJX9007359 11.06.2026  for ATC
*         ASSIGN COMPONENT c_fld_yotei_cs OF STRUCTURE <l_fs_zaiko> TO <l_fs_yojitu>.
        ASSIGN COMPONENT c_fld_yotei_cs OF STRUCTURE <l_fs_zaiko> TO <l_fs_yojitu>. "#EC CI_FLDEXT_OK[0002250183]
" Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_APS Conversion * *END OF CHANGE BY EJX9007359 11.06.2026 for ATC
      WHEN OTHERS.           "品検中以外
" Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_APS Conversion **BEGIN OF CHANGE BY EJX9007359 11.06.2026  for ATC
*         ASSIGN COMPONENT c_fld_jitu_cs  OF STRUCTURE <l_fs_zaiko> TO <l_fs_yojitu>.
        ASSIGN COMPONENT c_fld_jitu_cs  OF STRUCTURE <l_fs_zaiko> TO <l_fs_yojitu>. "#EC CI_FLDEXT_OK[0002250183]
" Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_APS Conversion * *END OF CHANGE BY EJX9007359 11.06.2026 for ATC
    ENDCASE.

*   *--- 引当可能数量>0の在庫から減算
    CHECK <l_fs_zaiko>-menge_cs > 0.

*   *--- 在庫数量>=出荷数量
    IF <l_fs_zaiko>-menge_cs >= o_wa_lips-lfimg.

*     *--- 在庫数量から出荷数量を減算
      <l_fs_zaiko>-menge_cs =  <l_fs_zaiko>-menge_cs
                            -  o_wa_lips-lfimg.
      <l_fs_yojitu>         =  <l_fs_yojitu>
                            -  o_wa_lips-lfimg.
*     *--- 出荷数量の減算完了
      o_wa_lips-lfimg       =  0.
*   *--- 在庫数量<出荷数量
    ELSE.
*     *--- 出荷数量から在庫数量を減算
      o_wa_lips-lfimg       =  o_wa_lips-lfimg
                            -  <l_fs_zaiko>-menge_cs.
*     *--- 在庫数量は0とする
      <l_fs_zaiko>-menge_cs =  0.
      <l_fs_yojitu>         =  0.
    ENDIF.
*   *--- 引当済数量 = 0 の場合は､LOOPを抜ける
    CHECK o_wa_lips-lfimg = 0.
    EXIT.
  ENDLOOP.

ENDFORM.                    " MINUS_JITU_LETYP_RTN_001
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  MINUS_JITU_RTN_001
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       実在庫からの減算処理
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      <--O_wa_LIPS  引当済データ
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM minus_jitu_rtn_001
  USING     i_v_flg_y            TYPE ty_reserve_001-flg_y
            i_v_bestq            TYPE ty_reserve_001-bestq
  CHANGING  o_wa_lips             TYPE LINE OF ty_it_lips_001.

* ローカル定義
  FIELD-SYMBOLS:
    <l_fs_zaiko>        TYPE LINE OF ty_it_reserve_001,
    <l_fs_yojitu>       TYPE any.

* *--- CCW&仙台&四国 個別の実行権限がある場合のみ実施
*  CHECK v_routine_001 = c_kosha.

* sort処理
  SORT it_reserve_001 BY werks
                         lgort
                         matnr
                         kiboshom
                         eda
                         zsztorikbn.

* 品目/ﾌﾟﾗﾝﾄ/保管場所/枝番/賞味期限での減算
  LOOP AT it_reserve_001 ASSIGNING <l_fs_zaiko>
                         WHERE matnr      =  o_wa_lips-matnr
                           AND werks      =  o_wa_lips-werks
                           AND lgort      =  o_wa_lips-lgort
                           AND eda        IN rng_eda
                           AND kiboshom   IN rng_syomi
                           AND zsztorikbn = o_wa_lips-zsztorikbn
                           AND bestq      =  i_v_bestq
                           AND flg_y      =  i_v_flg_y.

*   *--- 在庫ｶﾃｺﾞﾘによって､減算する引当可能数量の項目を決定
*   *--- (ASSIGN 割当不可は想定しない)
    CASE i_v_bestq.
      WHEN c_bestq_q.      "品検中
" Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_APS Conversion **BEGIN OF CHANGE BY EJX9007359 11.06.2026  for ATC
*         ASSIGN COMPONENT c_fld_yotei_cs OF STRUCTURE <l_fs_zaiko> TO <l_fs_yojitu>.
        ASSIGN COMPONENT c_fld_yotei_cs OF STRUCTURE <l_fs_zaiko> TO <l_fs_yojitu>. "#EC CI_FLDEXT_OK[0002250183]
" Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_APS Conversion * *END OF CHANGE BY EJX9007359 11.06.2026 for ATC
      WHEN OTHERS.           "品検中以外
" Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_APS Conversion **BEGIN OF CHANGE BY EJX9007359 11.06.2026  for ATC
*         ASSIGN COMPONENT c_fld_jitu_cs  OF STRUCTURE <l_fs_zaiko> TO <l_fs_yojitu>.
        ASSIGN COMPONENT c_fld_jitu_cs  OF STRUCTURE <l_fs_zaiko> TO <l_fs_yojitu>. "#EC CI_FLDEXT_OK[0002250183]
" Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_APS Conversion * *END OF CHANGE BY EJX9007359 11.06.2026 for ATC
    ENDCASE.

*   *--- 在庫の引当可能数量が0以下の場合は減算対象外とする
    IF <l_fs_zaiko>-menge_cs <= 0.
      CONTINUE.
    ENDIF.

*   *--- 在庫の引当可能数量≧引当済数量
    IF <l_fs_zaiko>-menge_cs >= o_wa_lips-lfimg.
*     *--- 引当可能数量(実在庫+予定在庫-引当済数量)の算出
      <l_fs_zaiko>-menge_cs = <l_fs_zaiko>-menge_cs
                            - o_wa_lips-lfimg.

*     *--- 実在庫引当可能数量(実在庫-引当済数量)の算出
      <l_fs_yojitu>       = <l_fs_yojitu>
                          - o_wa_lips-lfimg.
*     *--- 引当済数量残の算出
      o_wa_lips-lfimg     = 0.
*   *--- 在庫の引当可能数量<引当済数量
    ELSE.
*     *--- 引当済数量残の算出
      o_wa_lips-lfimg     = o_wa_lips-lfimg
                          - <l_fs_zaiko>-menge_cs.
*     *--- 引当可能数量(実在庫+予定在庫-引当済数量)の算出
      <l_fs_zaiko>-menge_cs = 0.
*     *--- 実在庫引当可能数量(実在庫-引当済数量)の算出
      <l_fs_yojitu>       = 0.
    ENDIF.
*   *--- 引当済数量 = 0 の場合は､LOOPを抜ける
    IF o_wa_lips-lfimg = 0.
      EXIT.
    ENDIF.

  ENDLOOP.

ENDFORM.                    " MINUS_JITU_RTN_001

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  EDIT_9300_HUB_RTN_001
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       出荷ﾊﾌﾞ 引当可能一覧 詳細画面データの編集
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->I_WA_9100          一覧ﾃﾞｰﾀ
*      <--O_wa_9300          詳細ﾃﾞｰﾀ
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM edit_9300_hub_rtn_001
  USING     i_wa_9100             TYPE ty_9100
  CHANGING  o_wa_9300             TYPE ty_9300.


  DATA :
    l_flg_reserve       TYPE xfeld.


* *--- CCW&仙台&四国 個別の実行権限がある場合のみ実施
*  CHECK v_routine_001 = c_kosha.

* *--- 選択画面 出荷ﾊﾌﾞ選択時
*  IF rb_hub = c_on.


  PERFORM check_reserve_zaiko_rtn_001
                           USING     i_wa_9100-werks"p_werks
                                     i_wa_9100-matnr
                                     i_wa_9100-kunnr2
                           CHANGING  l_flg_reserve.

  o_wa_9300-kbn        = i_wa_9100-kbn.             " 処理区分(ﾃｷｽﾄ)
  o_wa_9300-vbeln      = i_wa_9100-vbeln.           " 伝票番号
  o_wa_9300-icon       = i_wa_9100-icon.            " 伝票番号
  o_wa_9300-posnr      = i_wa_9100-posnr.           " 明細
  o_wa_9300-werks      = i_wa_9100-werks."p_werks.                   " ﾌﾟﾗﾝﾄ
  PERFORM exit_alpha_output                         " 出荷先
                           USING     i_wa_9100-kunnr2
                           CHANGING  o_wa_9300-kunnr.
  o_wa_9300-matnr      = i_wa_9100-matnr.           " 品目ｺｰﾄﾞ
  o_wa_9300-maktx      = i_wa_9100-maktx.           " 品目名称
  WRITE i_wa_9100-menge_cs TO   o_wa_9300-menge     " 入出荷予定数
                           UNIT i_wa_9100-meins.
  o_wa_9300-meins      = i_wa_9100-meins.           " 基本数量単位
  o_wa_9300-vrkme      = i_wa_9100-vrkme.           " 販売/発注単位
  o_wa_9300-eda        = i_wa_9100-edacode.         " 枝番
  o_wa_9300-youki      = i_wa_9100-youki.           " 材質
  o_wa_9300-lgort      = i_wa_9100-lgort.           " 出荷保管場所
  o_wa_9300-wadat      = i_wa_9100-wadat.           " 出荷予定日
  o_wa_9300-tdlnr      = i_wa_9100-tdlnr.           " 輸送業者
  o_wa_9300-edatu      = i_wa_9100-edatu.           " 納入期日
*   *--- 伝票登録日
  IF i_wa_9100-kbn     = text-001.
    o_wa_9300-erdat    = i_wa_9100-erdat.           " 受注伝票登録日
*   転送発注の場合
  ELSE.
    o_wa_9300-aedat    = i_wa_9100-erdat.           " 転送発注登録日
  ENDIF.
  o_wa_9300-syomi      = i_wa_9100-syomi.           " 賞味期限
  o_wa_9300-hsdat      = i_wa_9100-hsdat.           " 製造年月日
  IF i_wa_9100-plcode  = c_pl_cc.
    o_wa_9300-pltext   = text-008.                  " 'CCパレット'
  ENDIF.
  IF i_wa_9100-plcode  = c_pl_t09.
    o_wa_9300-pltext   = text-009.                  " 'T-09パレット'
  ENDIF.
  WRITE i_wa_9100-menge_cs TO   wa_9300-hiki_cs     " 引当数量
                           UNIT o_wa_9300-meins.
  CASE i_wa_9100-flash_kbn.
*     *--- 指定賞味の場合､【指定値のみ】のICON
    WHEN c_flash_b_001.
      o_wa_9300-icon_syomi = v_equal.
*     *--- 以降賞味の場合､【指定値以上】のICON
    WHEN OTHERS.
      o_wa_9300-icon_syomi = v_greater_equal.
  ENDCASE.
  o_wa_9300-bestq_txt  = i_wa_9100-bestq_txt.       " 良品・不良品区分


**   *--- 通常引当済
*    IF I_WA_9100-ICON = v_icon_green.
*   *--- 通常引当可と特定荷主&ﾛｯﾄ管理品で予定引当の場合､数量表示
  IF    i_wa_9100-icon = v_icon_green
     OR ( i_wa_9100-icon = v_icon_yellow

*        AND L_FLG_RESERVE  = c_ON ).
      AND l_flg_reserve  = c_on )
       OR ( v_routine_002 = c_kosha           "仙台&四国 個別
      AND  l_flg_reserve     = space              "通常出荷先
      AND  i_wa_9100-icon = v_icon_yellow
      AND  i_wa_9100-kbn = text-002               "転送発注
      AND  i_wa_9100-zsztorikbn IS NOT INITIAL ). "取置区分あり

    o_wa_9300-zan_cs   = i_wa_9100-zan_9300.        " 引当残数
    o_wa_9300-kak_cs   = i_wa_9100-kaku_9300.       " 引当確定数
    o_wa_9300-zai_cs   = i_wa_9100-jitu_9300.       " 在庫数量
*   *--- その他
  ELSE.
    o_wa_9300-zan_cs   = 0.                         " 引当残数
    o_wa_9300-kak_cs   = 0.                         " 引当確定数
    o_wa_9300-zai_cs   = 0.                         " 在庫数量
  ENDIF.

*  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    " EDIT_9300_HUB_RTN_001
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Module  M_SET_ATTRIBUTE_9300  OUTPUT
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       詳細画面 画面属性制御
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE m_set_attribute_9300 OUTPUT.

  PERFORM set_attribute_9300_rtn_001
                             USING     v_routine_001
                                       c_routine_001.

ENDMODULE.                 " M_SET_ATTRIBUTE_9300  OUTPUT
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  SET_ATTRIBUTE_9300_RTN_001
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       詳細画面 画面属性制御
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->I_v_routine           個別権限有無
*      -->I_v_ROUTINE            ﾙｰﾁﾝ番号
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM set_attribute_9300_rtn_001
  USING     i_v_routine         TYPE c
            i_v_seq          TYPE seqnr."NA ztma0004-zzseqno.

  DATA :
    l_v_group1         TYPE screen-group1.

  l_v_group1 = i_v_seq.
  LOOP AT SCREEN.
    CHECK screen-group1 = l_v_group1.
    IF i_v_routine = c_kosha.
      CONTINUE.
    ELSE.
      screen-length = screen-length / 2.
    ENDIF.
    MODIFY SCREEN.
  ENDLOOP.

ENDFORM.                    " SET_ATTRIBUTE_9300_RTN_001

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  EDIT_LIwa_ZSZTORIKBN_RTN_002
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       取置区分値の編集
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->I_IT_ZSTMM0068          取置区分ﾏｽﾀ
*      <--O_IT_ZSZTORIKBN         取置区分値
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM edit_liwa_zsztorikbn_rtn_002
  USING     i_it_zstmm0068        TYPE ty_it_zstmm0068_001
  CHANGING  o_it_zsztorikbn       TYPE ty_it_zsztorikbn_002.

  DATA :
    l_wa_zstmm0068      TYPE LINE OF ty_it_zstmm0068_001,
    l_wa_zsztorikbn     TYPE LINE OF ty_it_zsztorikbn_002.

  REFRESH :
    o_it_zsztorikbn.
* *--- 仙台&四国実行権限がある場合のみ取置区分値作成
  CHECK v_routine_002 = c_kosha.
  LOOP AT i_it_zstmm0068 INTO l_wa_zstmm0068.
    l_wa_zsztorikbn = l_wa_zstmm0068-zsztorikbn.
    APPEND l_wa_zsztorikbn TO o_it_zsztorikbn.
  ENDLOOP.
  SORT o_it_zsztorikbn ASCENDING BY table_line.
  DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM o_it_zsztorikbn
                  COMPARING  table_line.

ENDFORM.                    " EDIT_LIwa_ZSZTORIKBN_RTN_002
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  SELECT_EKPO_RTN_002
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       仙台&四国 実行権限がある場合の購買伝票明細取得
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       -->I_IT_EKKO              購買伝票ﾍｯﾀﾞ
*       <--O_IT_EKPO              購買伝票明細
*       <--O_IT_BEDNR             購買依頼追跡番号
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM select_ekpo_rtn_002
  USING     i_it_ekko             LIKE it_ekko
  CHANGING  o_it_ekpo             TYPE SORTED TABLE
            o_it_bednr            TYPE ty_it_bednr_002.

  DATA :
    l_it_ekpo_002       TYPE ty_it_ekpo_002,
    l_wa_ekpo_002       TYPE LINE OF ty_it_ekpo_002.

* *--- 仙台&四国実行権限がある場合のみ実施
  CHECK v_routine_002 = c_kosha.

* *--- ﾍｯﾀﾞﾃﾞｰﾀがある場合のみ実施
  CHECK i_it_ekko IS NOT INITIAL.
  REFRESH :
    o_it_ekpo,
    o_it_bednr.
* *--- 出荷対象の在庫転送ﾃﾞｰﾀを取得する
  SELECT ekpo~ebeln                    " 購買伝票番号
         ekpo~ebelp                    " 購買伝票の明細番号
         ekpo~matnr                    " 品目ｺｰﾄﾞ
         ekpo~werks                    " ﾌﾟﾗﾝﾄ
         ekpo~lgort                    " 保管場所
         ekpo~menge                    " 購買発注量
         ekpo~meins                    " 発注単位
         ekpo~umrez                    " 発注単位から基本単位への換算式の分子
         ekpo~umren                    " 発注単位から基本単位への換算式の分母
         ekpo~lmein                    " 基本数量単位
         ekpo~reslo                    " 出庫保管場所: 在庫転送ｵｰﾀﾞｰ
         ekpo~/scl/atwrt               " 特性値
*         ekpo~zzssyomi                 " 希望賞味期限"NA
