         eket~eindt                    " 納入期日
         eket~wadat                    " 出庫日付
         ekpv~kunnr                    " 得意先ｺｰﾄﾞ1(出荷先)

         ekpo~afnam                    " 購買依頼者名/要求者名
*         EKPO~BEDNR AS ZZBEDNR         " 購買依頼追跡番号
         ekpo~banfn                    " 購買依頼番号
         ekpo~bnfpo                    " 購買依頼の明細番号
         ekpo~insmk                    " 在庫タイプ
         ekpo~/scl/charg               " ロット番号
         ekpo~/scl/prueflos            " 品質検査ロット番号
         eket~mbdat                    " 品目供給/利用可能日
         eket~tddat                    " 輸送計画日付
         eket~lddat                    " 積載日付
         eket~eldat                    " 入庫終了日付

         ekpo~bednr                    " 購買依頼追跡No
*****Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1 Conversion **begin of change by EJX9007364 2026.05.26  for ATC
*    INTO TABLE l_it_ekpo_002
    INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE l_it_ekpo_002
*****Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1 Conversion **End of change by EJX9007364 2026.05.26 for ATC
    FROM ekpo
   INNER JOIN eket
      ON ekpo~ebeln = eket~ebeln       " 購買伝票番号
     AND ekpo~ebelp = eket~ebelp       " 購買伝票の明細番号
   INNER JOIN ekpv
      ON ekpo~ebeln = ekpv~ebeln       " 購買伝票番号
     AND ekpo~ebelp = ekpv~ebelp       " 購買伝票の明細番号
     FOR ALL ENTRIES IN i_it_ekko
   WHERE ekpo~ebeln =  i_it_ekko-ebeln " 購買伝票番号
     AND ekpo~elikz <> c_on          " 納入完了"区分
     AND ekpo~loekz <> c_loelz       " 購買伝票の削除ﾌﾗｸﾞ
     AND ekpo~matnr IN s_matnr         " 品目ｺｰﾄﾞ
     AND ekpv~kunnr IN s_kunnr         " 得意先ｺｰﾄﾞ1(出荷先)
     AND eket~eindt IN s_edatu         " 納入期日
     AND eket~wadat IN s_wadat.        " 出庫日付(出荷予定日)

* *--- 購買明細ﾃﾞｰﾀ､購買依頼追跡No.ﾃﾞｰﾀに分割
  LOOP AT l_it_ekpo_002 INTO l_wa_ekpo_002.
*   *--- 購買明細ﾃﾞｰﾀ設定
    APPEND l_wa_ekpo_002-ekpo_stnd TO o_it_ekpo.
*   *--- 購買依頼追跡No.ﾃﾞｰﾀ設定
    PERFORM edit_po_bednr_rtn_002
                             USING     l_wa_ekpo_002-ebeln
                                       l_wa_ekpo_002-ebelp
                                       l_wa_ekpo_002-bednr
                             CHANGING  o_it_bednr[].
  ENDLOOP.

ENDFORM.                    " SELECT_EKPO_RTN_002
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  SET_ZSZTORIKBN_RTN_002
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       仙台/四国 実行権限あり 取置区分決定
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->I_v_BEDNR              購買依頼追跡No.
*      -->I_v_ZSZTORIKBN         ﾏｽﾀ取置区分
*      <--O_v_ZSZTORIKBN         取置区分
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM set_zsztorikbn_rtn_002
  USING     i_v_bednr            TYPE ty_bednr_002-bednr
            i_v_zsztorikbn       TYPE ty_zstmm0068_001-zsztorikbn
  CHANGING  o_v_zsztorikbn       TYPE ty_9100-zsztorikbn.

* *--- 仙台&四国実行権限がある場合
  IF v_routine_002 = c_kosha.

*   *--- 購買依頼追跡No.なし→ﾏｽﾀ値返却
    IF i_v_bednr IS INITIAL.
      o_v_zsztorikbn = i_v_zsztorikbn.
*   *--- 購買依頼追跡No.あり→取置区分値ﾁｪｯｸ
    ELSE.
      READ TABLE it_zsztorikbn_002 TRANSPORTING NO FIELDS
                        WITH KEY table_line = i_v_bednr
                        BINARY SEARCH.
*       *--- 購買依頼追跡No.が区分値にある場合､追跡No.を返却
      IF sy-subrc = 0.
        o_v_zsztorikbn = i_v_bednr.
*       *--- 購買依頼追跡No.が区分値にない場合､ﾏｽﾀ値を返却
      ELSE.
        o_v_zsztorikbn = i_v_zsztorikbn.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
* *--- 仙台/四国実行権限がない場合､ﾏｽﾀ値を返却
  ELSE.
    o_v_zsztorikbn = i_v_zsztorikbn.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    " SET_ZSZTORIKBN_RTN_002
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  SELECT_LIPS_VGBEL_RTN_002
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->I_IT_LIPS               引当済出荷伝票
*      <--O_IT_LIPS_EKPO          発注源泉の出荷の参照伝票情報
*      <--O_IT_BEDNR              購買依頼追跡番号
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM select_lips_vgbel_rtn_002
  USING     i_it_lips             TYPE ty_it_lips_001
  CHANGING  o_it_lips_ekpo        LIKE it_lips_ekpo
            o_it_bednr            TYPE ty_it_bednr_002.

  DATA :
    l_it_vgbel          TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_vgbel_001,
    l_it_lips_ekpo      TYPE ty_it_lips_ekpo_002,
    l_wa_lips           TYPE LINE OF ty_it_lips_001,
    l_wa_vgbel          TYPE ty_vgbel_001,
    l_wa_lips_ekpo      TYPE LINE OF ty_it_lips_ekpo_002.

* *--- 仙台&四国実行権限がある場合のみ実施
  CHECK v_routine_002 = c_kosha.

  REFRESH :
    o_it_lips_ekpo,
    o_it_bednr.
* *--- 参照元が購買発注のﾃﾞｰﾀのみ検索条件に設定
  LOOP AT i_it_lips INTO  l_wa_lips
                    WHERE vgtyp = c_vbtyv_v.
    l_wa_vgbel-vgbel = l_wa_lips-vgbel.
    l_wa_vgbel-vgpos = l_wa_lips-vgpos.
    APPEND l_wa_vgbel TO l_it_vgbel.
  ENDLOOP.
  SORT l_it_vgbel ASCENDING BY table_line.
  DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM l_it_vgbel COMPARING table_line.
* *--- 参照元取得
  CHECK l_it_vgbel[] IS NOT INITIAL.
  SELECT ebeln
         ebelp
         werks
         lgort
         bednr
    INTO TABLE l_it_lips_ekpo
    FROM ekpo
     FOR ALL ENTRIES IN l_it_vgbel
   WHERE ebeln = l_it_vgbel-vgbel
     AND ebelp = l_it_vgbel-vgpos.

* *--- 参照元情報と追跡No.情報に切り分ける
  LOOP AT l_it_lips_ekpo INTO l_wa_lips_ekpo.
    INSERT l_wa_lips_ekpo-lips_ekpo_stnd INTO TABLE o_it_lips_ekpo.
    PERFORM edit_po_bednr_rtn_002
                             USING     l_wa_lips_ekpo-ebeln
                                       l_wa_lips_ekpo-ebelp
                                       l_wa_lips_ekpo-bednr
                             CHANGING  o_it_bednr[].
  ENDLOOP.

ENDFORM.                    " SELECT_LIPS_VGBEL_RTN_002
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  EDIT_PO_BEDNR_RTN_002
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       仙台/四国 実行権限あり 購買依頼追跡Noﾃﾞｰﾀ編集
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->I_v_EBELN              購買伝票番号
*      -->I_v_EBELP              購買伝票明細
*      -->I_v_BEDNR              購買依頼追跡No
*      <--O_IT_BEDNR              購買依頼追跡Noﾃﾞｰﾀ
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM edit_po_bednr_rtn_002
  USING     i_v_ebeln            TYPE ekpo-ebeln
            i_v_ebelp            TYPE ekpo-ebelp
            i_v_bednr            TYPE ekpo-bednr
  CHANGING  o_it_bednr            TYPE ty_it_bednr_002.

  DATA :
    l_wa_bednr          TYPE LINE OF ty_it_bednr_002.

* *--- 仙台&四国実行権限がある場合のみ実施
  CHECK v_routine_002 = c_kosha.

  l_wa_bednr-ebeln = i_v_ebeln.                 " 購買伝票番号
  l_wa_bednr-ebelp = i_v_ebelp.                 " 購買伝票明細
  l_wa_bednr-bednr = i_v_bednr.                 " 購買依頼追跡No
  INSERT l_wa_bednr INTO TABLE o_it_bednr.

ENDFORM.                    " EDIT_PO_BEDNR_RTN_002
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_BEDNR_RTN_002
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       購買依頼追跡ﾃﾞｰﾀから購買依頼追跡Noを取得する
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->I_v_EBELN              購買伝票番号
*      -->I_v_EBELP              購買伝票明細
*      <--O_v_BEDNR              購買依頼追跡No.
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_bednr_rtn_002
  USING     i_v_ebeln            TYPE ty_bednr_002-ebeln
            i_v_ebelp            TYPE ty_bednr_002-ebelp
  CHANGING  o_v_bednr            TYPE ty_bednr_002-bednr.

  DATA :
    l_wa_bednr          TYPE LINE OF ty_it_bednr_002.

* *--- 仙台/四国 実行権限あり
  IF v_routine_002 = c_kosha.
    READ TABLE it_bednr_002 INTO l_wa_bednr
                            WITH TABLE KEY ebeln = i_v_ebeln
                                           ebelp = i_v_ebelp
                            TRANSPORTING bednr.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      o_v_bednr = l_wa_bednr-bednr.
    ELSE.
      CLEAR o_v_bednr.
    ENDIF.
* *--- 仙台/四国 実行権限なし->初期値返却
  ELSE.
    CLEAR o_v_bednr.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    " GET_BEDNR_RTN_002


*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  F_GET_BANFN
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       購買依頼番号の取得
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_get_banfn .

* ローカル変数定義
  DATA:
    l_it_eket     TYPE TABLE OF ty_banfn,
    l_wa_eket     TYPE ty_banfn,
    l_wa_9100     TYPE ty_9100.

* 初期化
  REFRESH:
    it_banfn[],
    it_ziv0067[],
    it_zstmm0075[].

* 四国権限の場合
  CHECK v_routine_004 = c_kosha.

* 抽出条件を作成
  LOOP AT it_ins_9100 INTO l_wa_9100
          WHERE kbn = text-002.

*
    CLEAR l_wa_eket.
    l_wa_eket-ebeln   = l_wa_9100-vbeln.
    l_wa_eket-ebelp   = l_wa_9100-posnr.
    APPEND l_wa_eket TO l_it_eket.

  ENDLOOP.

* 実行条件
  CHECK l_it_eket[] IS NOT INITIAL.

* 分納契約納入日程行
  SELECT
          ebeln           "購買伝票番号
          ebelp           "購買伝票の明細番号
          banfn           "購買依頼番号
          bnfpo           "購買依頼の明細番号
    INTO TABLE it_banfn
    FROM eket
     FOR ALL ENTRIES IN l_it_eket
   WHERE ebeln  = l_it_eket-ebeln
     AND ebelp  = l_it_eket-ebelp.

* 購買依頼を取得できる場合
*-------------------NA-------------------------------
*  IF it_banfn[] IS NOT INITIAL.
*
**   確定引当R/3項目テーブル
*    SELECT
*            banfn           "購買依頼番号
*            bnfpo           "購買依頼の明細番号
*            seqnr           "連番
*      INTO TABLE it_ziv0067
*      FROM ziv0067
*       FOR ALL ENTRIES IN it_banfn
*     WHERE banfn  = it_banfn-banfn
*       AND bnfpo  = it_banfn-bnfpo.
*
**    取得できる場合
*    IF sy-subrc = 0.
*
**      ソート
*      SORT it_ziv0067
*        BY banfn ASCENDING
*           bnfpo ASCENDING
*           seqnr DESCENDING.
*
**      同一購買依頼番号＆購買依頼の明細番号のうち、連番が最大のデータのみ残す
*      DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM  it_ziv0067
*             COMPARING   banfn bnfpo.
*
*    ENDIF.
*
*  ENDIF.

* 確定引当データを取得できる場合
*  IF it_ziv0067[] IS NOT INITIAL.
*
**   確定引当テーブル更新情報
*    SELECT
*            zszbanfn           "購買依頼番号
*            zszbnfpo           "購買依頼の明細番号
*            zszseqnr           "連番
*            zzernam            "オブジェクト登録者名
*            zzersda            "登録日付
*            zzerzzt            "登録時刻
*      INTO TABLE it_zstmm0075
*      FROM zstmm0075
*       FOR ALL ENTRIES IN it_ziv0067
*     WHERE zszbanfn   =  it_ziv0067-banfn        "購買依頼番号
*       AND zszbnfpo   =  it_ziv0067-bnfpo        "購買依頼の明細番号
*       AND zszseqnr   =  it_ziv0067-seqnr        "連番
*            .
*
*  ENDIF.
*-------------------NA-------------------------------

ENDFORM.                    " F_GET_BANFN
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  F_ZSTMM0075_MODIFY
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       確定引当テーブル更新
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_zstmm0075_modify .

* ローカル変数定義
  DATA:
    l_wa_items           LIKE LINE OF it_created_items,
    l_wa_banfn           LIKE LINE OF it_banfn,
    l_wa_ziv0067         LIKE LINE OF it_ziv0067,
    l_wa_zstmm0075       LIKE LINE OF it_zstmm0075,
    l_it_zstmm0075_mod   TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_zstmm0075,
    l_wa_zstmm0075_mod   TYPE ty_zstmm0075.

* 四国権限の場合
  CHECK v_routine_004 = c_kosha.

* 内部TBL_登録出荷明細
  LOOP AT it_created_items INTO l_wa_items.

*   初期化
    CLEAR:
      l_wa_banfn,
      l_wa_ziv0067,
      l_wa_zstmm0075,
      l_wa_zstmm0075_mod.


*   内部TBL_購買依頼番号より、購買依頼番号を抽出する
    READ TABLE it_banfn INTO l_wa_banfn
               WITH KEY ebeln  = l_wa_items-ref_doc
                        ebelp  = l_wa_items-ref_item
                        BINARY SEARCH.

*   取得できる場合
    CHECK sy-subrc = 0.

*   確定引当
    READ TABLE it_ziv0067 INTO l_wa_ziv0067
               WITH KEY banfn  = l_wa_banfn-banfn
                        bnfpo  = l_wa_banfn-bnfpo
                        BINARY SEARCH.

*   取得できる場合
    CHECK sy-subrc = 0.

*   確定引当テーブル更新情報
    READ TABLE it_zstmm0075 INTO l_wa_zstmm0075
               WITH KEY zszbanfn     = l_wa_ziv0067-banfn
                        zszbnfpo     = l_wa_ziv0067-bnfpo
                        zszseqnr     = l_wa_ziv0067-seqnr
                        .

*   内部TBL_確定引当更新情報登録/更新 へ追加する
    l_wa_zstmm0075_mod-zszbanfn    =  l_wa_ziv0067-banfn.       " 購買依頼番号
    l_wa_zstmm0075_mod-zszbnfpo    =  l_wa_ziv0067-bnfpo.       " 購買依頼明細
    l_wa_zstmm0075_mod-zszseqnr    =  l_wa_ziv0067-seqnr.       " 連番
    l_wa_zstmm0075_mod-zszebeln    =  l_wa_banfn-ebeln.         " 購買伝票番号
    l_wa_zstmm0075_mod-zszebelp    =  l_wa_banfn-ebelp.         " 購買伝票明細
    l_wa_zstmm0075_mod-zszvbeln    =  l_wa_items-deliv_numb.    " 出荷伝票番号
    l_wa_zstmm0075_mod-zszposnr    =  l_wa_items-deliv_item.    " 出荷伝票明細

*
    IF l_wa_zstmm0075 IS INITIAL.

      l_wa_zstmm0075_mod-zzernam    =  sy-uname.    " オブジェクト登録者名
      l_wa_zstmm0075_mod-zzersda    =  sy-datlo.    " 登録日付
      l_wa_zstmm0075_mod-zzerzzt    =  sy-uzeit.    " 登録時刻

    ELSE.

      l_wa_zstmm0075_mod-zzernam    =  l_wa_zstmm0075-zzernam.    " オブジェクト登録者名
      l_wa_zstmm0075_mod-zzersda    =  l_wa_zstmm0075-zzersda.    " 登録日付
      l_wa_zstmm0075_mod-zzerzzt    =  l_wa_zstmm0075-zzerzzt.    " 登録時刻
      l_wa_zstmm0075_mod-zzaenam    =  sy-uname.    " オブジェクトの変更者名
      l_wa_zstmm0075_mod-zzupdat    =  sy-datlo.    " 対象の最終変更日
      l_wa_zstmm0075_mod-zzuptim    =  sy-uzeit.    " 最終変更時刻


    ENDIF.

*
    APPEND l_wa_zstmm0075_mod TO l_it_zstmm0075_mod.


  ENDLOOP.

*
  CHECK l_it_zstmm0075_mod[] IS NOT INITIAL.

* 更新＆登録
*  MODIFY zstmm0075 FROM TABLE l_it_zstmm0075_mod."Table Update NA


ENDFORM.                    " F_ZSTMM0075_MODIFY

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  F_WAIT_RETRY_GET
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       WAIT秒数､RETRY回数取得
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_wait_retry_get .

* 初期化
  CLEAR wa_wait.
*-------------NA----------------------
* 共通テーブルの検索
*  SELECT SINGLE
*         zzdata1                    "WAIT時間(秒)
*         zznumc1                    "リトライ回数
*    FROM znn0001
*    INTO wa_wait
*   WHERE zzkey1 = sy-cprog                                  "長さ 16 の項目
*     AND zzkey2 = c_key2                                  "文字項目長 = 10
*     AND zzkey3 = c_key3.                                 "文字項目長 = 10
*-------------NA----------------------
* 取得失敗の場合
  IF sy-subrc <> 0.

    wa_wait-zzdata1  = 0.
    wa_wait-zznumc1  = 0.

  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    " F_WAIT_RETRY_GET
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  F_EDIT_DATA_BAPI_ITEM
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       数量変換
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_edit_data_bapi_item USING i_it_9100  TYPE tbl_ty_9100.

* ローカル変数定義
  DATA:
    l_wa_9100            TYPE ty_9100,
    l_wa_items           LIKE LINE OF it_created_items,
    l_wa_item_data       LIKE LINE OF it_item_data,
    l_wa_item_control    LIKE LINE OF it_item_control.

* 実行権限判断
  CHECK v_routine_003 = c_kosha.

* 初期化
  REFRESH:
    it_item_data,
    it_item_control.

* 内部TBL_出荷登録明細
  LOOP AT it_created_items INTO l_wa_items.

*   初期化
    CLEAR:
      l_wa_9100,
      l_wa_item_data,
      l_wa_item_control.

*   出荷伝票作成用内部テーブルより､データを抽出する
    READ TABLE i_it_9100 INTO l_wa_9100
               WITH KEY vbeln   = l_wa_items-ref_doc             "伝票番号
                        posnr   = l_wa_items-ref_item.           "明細

*
    CHECK sy-subrc = 0.
*
    CHECK l_wa_9100-meins  <>  l_wa_items-sales_unit.

*   出荷明細変更用の内部テーブルへ以下の値を追加する
    l_wa_item_data-deliv_numb    = l_wa_items-deliv_numb.   "出荷伝票
    l_wa_item_data-deliv_item    = l_wa_items-deliv_item.   "出荷明細
    l_wa_item_data-dlv_qty       = l_wa_9100-menge_cs.      " 引当数量(基本数量単位数量)
    l_wa_item_data-fact_unit_nom   = c_1.                 " 分子
    l_wa_item_data-fact_unit_denom = c_1.                 " 分母
    l_wa_item_data-sales_unit    = l_wa_9100-meins.         " 出荷単位(基本数量単位)
    APPEND l_wa_item_data TO it_item_data.

*   ITEM_CONTROL(内部テーブル)
    l_wa_item_control-deliv_numb    = l_wa_items-deliv_numb.   "出荷伝票
    l_wa_item_control-deliv_item    = l_wa_items-deliv_item.   "出荷明細
    l_wa_item_control-chg_delqty    = c_x.                   "出荷数量変更
    APPEND l_wa_item_control TO it_item_control.


  ENDLOOP.


ENDFORM.                    " F_EDIT_DATA_BAPI_ITEM
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  F_LOCK_LIKP_READ
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       出荷伝票のロックエントリ確認
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_lock_likp_read  CHANGING o_flg_err  TYPE any
                                o_flg_lock TYPE any.

* ローカル変数定義
  DATA:
    l_v_index       TYPE i,
    l_v_gname       TYPE seqg3-gname,
    l_v_garg        TYPE seqg3-garg,
    l_it_seqg3       TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF seqg3.

* パラメータ設定
  l_v_gname   = c_likp.
  CONCATENATE sy-mandt
              v_delibery
         INTO l_v_garg.


* WHILE 確認回数≦RETRY回数
  WHILE l_v_index  <= wa_wait-zznumc1.

*   初期化
    REFRESH:
      l_it_seqg3.

*   出荷伝票のロックエントリ確認
    CALL FUNCTION 'ENQUEUE_READ'
      EXPORTING
        gname                 = l_v_gname
        garg                  = l_v_garg
      TABLES
        enq                   = l_it_seqg3
      EXCEPTIONS
        communication_failure = 1
        system_failure        = 2
        OTHERS                = 3.
    IF sy-subrc = 0 AND l_it_seqg3[] IS INITIAL.

*     処理終了
      RETURN.

    ELSEIF sy-subrc <> 0 OR l_it_seqg3[] IS NOT INITIAL.

*
      WAIT UP TO wa_wait-zzdata1 SECONDS.

*
      l_v_index = l_v_index + 1.

    ENDIF.

  ENDWHILE.

* ロックエラーあるとする
  o_flg_err   = c_x.
  o_flg_lock  = c_x.


ENDFORM.                    " F_LOCK_LIKP_READ

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  F_LFART_OFF_GET
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       抽出対象外出荷伝票タイプ取得
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_lfart_off_get .

* ローカル変数定義
  FIELD-SYMBOLS:
    <wa_lfart>   LIKE LINE OF rng_lfart.

* 初期化
  REFRESH:
    rng_lfart.

*-----------------------NA--------------------------
*  SELECT zzdata1  AS low
*    INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE rng_lfart
*    FROM znn0001
*   WHERE zzkey1 = sy-cprog                                  "長さ 16 の項目
*     AND zzkey2 = c_lfart                                 "文字項目長 = 10
  .
*-----------------------NA--------------------------
*
  LOOP AT rng_lfart ASSIGNING <wa_lfart>.

*
    <wa_lfart>-sign     = c_sign_e.
    <wa_lfart>-option   = c_opt_eq.

  ENDLOOP.


ENDFORM.                    " F_LFART_OFF_GET

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  F_EKPO_EDIT_CCW_005
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       CCW個別社のサマリ処理
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_ekpo_edit_ccw_005 .

* ローカル変数定義
  TYPES:
    BEGIN OF ty_ekpo_hkk,
      ebeln   TYPE ekpo-ebeln,        "購買伝票番号
      afnam   TYPE ekpo-afnam,        "購買依頼者名/要求者名
      matnr   TYPE ekpo-matnr,        "品目コード
      eindt   TYPE eket-eindt,        "納入期日
      werks   TYPE ekpo-werks,        "プラント
      lgort   TYPE ekpo-lgort,        "保管場所
      banfn   TYPE ekpo-banfn,        "購買依頼番号
      bnfpo   TYPE ekpo-bnfpo,        "購買依頼の明細番号
      reslo   TYPE ekpo-reslo,        "出庫保管場所: 在庫転送オーダー
      insmk   TYPE ekpo-insmk,        "在庫タイプ
      /scl/charg      TYPE ekpo-/scl/charg,         "ロット番号
      atwrt   TYPE ekpo-/scl/atwrt,   "特性値
      syomi   TYPE dats,"NA ekpo-zzssyomi,     "希望賞味期限
      /scl/prueflos   TYPE ekpo-/scl/prueflos,      "品質検査ロット番号
      mbdat   TYPE eket-mbdat,        "品目供給/利用可能日
      tddat   TYPE eket-tddat,        "輸送計画日付
      lddat   TYPE eket-lddat,        "積載日付
      wadat   TYPE eket-wadat,        "出庫日付
      eldat   TYPE eket-eldat,        "入庫終了日付
*      ZZBEDNR TYPE EKPO-BEDNR,        "購買依頼追跡番号
    END OF ty_ekpo_hkk.

  DATA:
    l_wa_ekpomae    TYPE ty_ekpo,
    l_wa_ekpoato    TYPE ty_ekpo,
    l_wa_ekpomt     TYPE ty_ekpo,
    l_v_index      TYPE i,
    l_v_ebelp      TYPE ebelp,
    l_it_ekpo       TYPE ty_it_ekpo_mt,
    l_it_ekpo_mt    TYPE ty_it_ekpo_mt,
    l_v_menge      TYPE ekpo-menge,         "購買発注量(合計)
    l_wa_ekpo_mae   TYPE ty_ekpo_hkk,        "判断前レコード
    l_wa_ekpo_ato   TYPE ty_ekpo_hkk,        "判断後レコード
    l_wa_ekpo_mt    TYPE ty_ekpo_hkk.        "判断元レコード

* 初期化
  REFRESH:
    it_ekpo_bk[].

* 実行権限判断
  CHECK v_routine_005 = c_kosha.

* 対象データ
  l_it_ekpo[]   = it_ekpo[].

* 分割されないレコードを削除
  DELETE l_it_ekpo WHERE afnam IS INITIAL.

* 分割されるレコードを削除
  DELETE it_ekpo WHERE afnam IS NOT INITIAL.

* 重複キーより、ソート
  SORT l_it_ekpo
    BY ebeln          "購買伝票番号
       afnam          "購買依頼者名/要求者名
       matnr          "品目コード
       eindt          "納入期日
       werks          "プラント
       lgort          "保管場所
       banfn          "購買依頼番号
       bnfpo          "購買依頼の明細番号
       reslo          "出庫保管場所: 在庫転送オーダー
       insmk          "在庫タイプ
       /scl/charg     "ロット番号

  " BOC | MOD-016| S180184 | Gap 3389 | Subcode changes | 29/07/2016
   /scl/atwrt          "特性値
*   atwrt          "特性値
 " EOC |MOD-016 | S180184 | Gap 3389 | Subcode changes | 29/07/2016

       syomi          "希望賞味期限
       /scl/prueflos  "品質検査ロット番号
       mbdat          "品目供給/利用可能日
       tddat          "輸送計画日付
       lddat          "積載日付
       wadat          "出庫日付
       eldat          "入庫終了日付
*       ZZBEDNR        "購買依頼追跡番号
       ebelp          "購買伝票の明細番号
       .

* 実行条件
  CHECK l_it_ekpo[] IS NOT INITIAL.

* 元明細の情報を取得
  PERFORM f_ekpo_ebelp_mt
                    USING l_it_ekpo
                 CHANGING l_it_ekpo_mt.

* 分割データをマージ
  LOOP AT l_it_ekpo INTO l_wa_ekpomae.

*
    l_v_index  = sy-tabix.

*   初期化
    CLEAR:
      l_v_ebelp,
      l_wa_ekpomt,
      l_wa_ekpo_mae,
      l_wa_ekpo_ato,
      l_wa_ekpo_mt,
      l_wa_ekpoato.

*   分割前明細番号を変換
    l_v_ebelp  = l_wa_ekpomae-afnam.

*   元明細の取得
    READ TABLE l_it_ekpo_mt INTO l_wa_ekpomt
               WITH KEY ebeln =  l_wa_ekpomae-ebeln   "購買伝票番号
                        ebelp =  l_v_ebelp           "購買伝票明細番号
                        BINARY SEARCH.
    MOVE-CORRESPONDING l_wa_ekpomt TO l_wa_ekpo_mt.
    l_wa_ekpo_mt-afnam   = l_wa_ekpomae-afnam.

*   当前レコードの比較用構造
    MOVE-CORRESPONDING l_wa_ekpomae TO l_wa_ekpo_mae.

*   次レコードの比較用構造
    l_v_index  = l_v_index + 1.
    READ TABLE l_it_ekpo INTO l_wa_ekpoato
               INDEX l_v_index.
    MOVE-CORRESPONDING l_wa_ekpoato TO l_wa_ekpo_ato.

*   前レコードと判断
    IF l_wa_ekpo_mae  = l_wa_ekpo_ato AND
       l_wa_ekpo_mae  = l_wa_ekpo_mt.

*     数量合計
      l_v_menge  = l_v_menge +   l_wa_ekpomae-menge.

*     マージされたデータを退避
      l_wa_ekpomae-afnam  = l_v_ebelp.
      INSERT l_wa_ekpomae INTO TABLE it_ekpo_bk.

    ELSE.

*     数量更新
*     合計数量あり
      IF l_v_menge > 0.

*       マージされたデータを退避
        l_wa_ekpomae-afnam  = l_v_ebelp.
        INSERT l_wa_ekpomae INTO TABLE it_ekpo_bk.

        l_wa_ekpomae-menge  =  l_wa_ekpomae-menge + l_v_menge.

*       購買伝票の明細番号を変換
        l_wa_ekpomae-ebelp  = l_wa_ekpomae-afnam.

*     合計数量なし
      ELSE.

*       マージされない場合、元明細番号を初期化
        CLEAR l_wa_ekpomae-afnam.

      ENDIF.

*     マージしたレコードを追加
      INSERT l_wa_ekpomae INTO TABLE it_ekpo.

*     初期化
      CLEAR:
        l_v_menge.

    ENDIF.


  ENDLOOP.


ENDFORM.                    " F_EKPO_EDIT_CCW_005
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  F_EDIT_DATA_BAPI_EKPO_005
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       発注伝票明細の編集(仙台社)
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_edit_data_bapi_ekpo_005 .

* ローカル変数定義
  DATA:
    l_wa_ekpo_bk           TYPE ty_ekpo.

* 実行権限判断
  CHECK v_routine_005 = c_kosha.

*
  LOOP AT it_ekpo_bk INTO l_wa_ekpo_bk
           WHERE ebeln = wa_9100-vbeln
             AND afnam = wa_9100-afnam.

*   初期化
    CLEAR :
      wa_poitem,
      wa_poitemx.

*   明細データの編集
    wa_poitem-po_item      = l_wa_ekpo_bk-ebelp.    "購買発注明細
    wa_poitem-suppl_stloc  = wa_9100-lgort.         "保管場所
    APPEND wa_poitem TO it_poitem.

*   明細データ (変更ツールバー)の編集
    wa_poitemx-po_item     = l_wa_ekpo_bk-ebelp.    "購買発注明細
*   *--- 保管場所が明細の保管場所と異なる場合に更新
    wa_poitemx-po_itemx = c_on.
    IF wa_9100-lgort <> wa_9100-lgort_org.
      wa_poitemx-suppl_stloc = c_on.
    ENDIF.
    APPEND wa_poitemx TO it_poitemx.

  ENDLOOP.


ENDFORM.                    " F_EDIT_DATA_BAPI_EKPO_005

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  F_EKPO_EBELP_MT
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       元明細の情報を取得
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_ekpo_ebelp_mt  USING    i_it_ekpo     TYPE ty_it_ekpo_mt
                      CHANGING o_it_ekpo_mt  TYPE ty_it_ekpo_mt.

  DATA:
    l_wa_ekpo       TYPE ty_ekpo,
    l_v_ebelp      TYPE ebelp,
    l_it_ekpo       TYPE ty_it_ekpo_mt.

*
  LOOP AT i_it_ekpo INTO l_wa_ekpo.

    l_v_ebelp       = l_wa_ekpo-afnam.
    l_wa_ekpo-ebelp  = l_v_ebelp.
    APPEND l_wa_ekpo TO l_it_ekpo.

  ENDLOOP.

*
  SORT l_it_ekpo
    BY ebeln
       ebelp.
  DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM l_it_ekpo
         COMPARING ebeln ebelp.

*
  CHECK l_it_ekpo[] IS NOT INITIAL.

*
  SELECT ekpo~ebeln        "購買伝票番号
         ekpo~ebelp        "購買伝票の明細番号
         ekpo~matnr        "品目コード
         ekpo~werks        "プラント
         ekpo~lgort        "保管場所
         ekpo~menge        "購買発注量
         ekpo~meins        "発注単位
         ekpo~umrez        "発注単位から基本単位への換算式の分子
         ekpo~umren        "発注単位から基本単位への換算式の分母
         ekpo~lmein        "基本数量単位
         ekpo~reslo        "出庫保管場所: 在庫転送オーダー
         ekpo~/scl/atwrt   "特性値
*         ekpo~zzssyomi     "希望賞味期限"NA
         eket~eindt        "納入期日
         eket~wadat        "出庫日付
         ekpv~kunnr                                         "得意先コード1
         ekpo~afnam                 "購買依頼者名/要求者名
*         EKPO~BEDNR AS ZZBEDNR      "購買依頼追跡番号
         ekpo~banfn                 "購買依頼番号
         ekpo~bnfpo                 "購買依頼の明細番号
         ekpo~insmk                 "在庫タイプ
         ekpo~/scl/charg            "ロット番号
         ekpo~/scl/prueflos         "品質検査ロット番号
         eket~mbdat                 "品目供給/利用可能日
         eket~tddat                 "輸送計画日付
         eket~lddat                 "積載日付
         eket~eldat                 "入庫終了日付
*****Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1 Conversion **begin of change by EJX9007364 2026.05.26  for ATC
    INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE o_it_ekpo_mt
*****Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1 Conversion **End of change by EJX9007364 2026.05.26 for ATC
    FROM ekpo
    INNER JOIN eket
    ON  ekpo~ebeln = eket~ebeln   "購買伝票番号
    AND ekpo~ebelp = eket~ebelp   "購買伝票の明細番号
    INNER JOIN ekpv
    ON  ekpo~ebeln = ekpv~ebeln   "購買伝票番号
    AND ekpo~ebelp = ekpv~ebelp   "購買伝票の明細番号
    FOR ALL ENTRIES IN l_it_ekpo
   WHERE ekpo~ebeln =  l_it_ekpo-ebeln   "購買伝票番号
     AND ekpo~ebelp =  l_it_ekpo-ebelp   "購買伝票明細番号
         .

*
  SORT o_it_ekpo_mt
    BY ebeln
       ebelp.

ENDFORM.                    " F_EKPO_EBELP_MT

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  F_GET_VBUKP
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       伝票ステータスの取得
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->I_IT_IN     出荷伝票
*      <--O_IT_VBUK   ヘッダステータス
*      <--O_IT_VBUP   明細ステータス
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_get_vbukp  USING    i_it_in    TYPE ty_it_vbup
                  CHANGING o_it_vbuk  TYPE ty_it_vbuk
                           o_it_vbup  TYPE ty_it_vbup.

  DATA: l_it_h  TYPE ty_it_vbup,
        l_it_m  TYPE ty_it_vbup.

  CHECK lines( i_it_in ) > 0.

  CLEAR:  o_it_vbuk,
          o_it_vbup.

* 販売伝票: ヘッダステータスの取得
  l_it_h[] = i_it_in[].
  SORT l_it_h BY vbeln.
  DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM l_it_h COMPARING vbeln.
  IF lines( l_it_h ) > 0.

    SELECT vbeln   "販売管理伝票番号
      FROM vbuk
      INTO TABLE o_it_vbuk
       FOR ALL ENTRIES IN l_it_h
     WHERE vbeln  =   l_it_h-vbeln    "販売管理伝票番号
       AND vbtyp  =   c_vbtyp_j     "販売管理伝票カテゴリ
       AND wbstk  IN (c_wbstk_a,    "合計在庫移動ステータス
                      c_wbstk_b)
       AND abstk  IN (space,          "全伝票明細の拒否ステータス
                      c_abstk_a,
                      c_abstk_b)
       AND vbobj  =   c_vbobj_l.    "販売管理伝票対象
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      SORT o_it_vbuk BY vbeln.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

* 販売伝票: 明細ステータスの取得
  l_it_m[] = i_it_in[].
  SORT l_it_m BY vbeln posnr.
  DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM l_it_m COMPARING vbeln posnr.
  IF lines( l_it_m ) > 0.

    SELECT vbeln   "販売管理伝票番号
           posnr   "明細番号(販売管理伝票)
      FROM vbup
      INTO TABLE o_it_vbup
       FOR ALL ENTRIES IN l_it_m
     WHERE vbeln =  l_it_m-vbeln        "販売管理伝票番号
       AND posnr =  l_it_m-posnr        "明細番号 (販売管理伝票)
       AND wbsta IN (c_wbsta_a,       "在庫移動ステータス
                     c_wbsta_b)
       AND absta IN (space,             "販売伝票明細の拒否ステータス
                     c_absta_a,
                     c_absta_b).
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      SORT o_it_vbup BY vbeln posnr.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    " F_GET_VBUKP

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  F_GET_TEXT_PALLET
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       転送オーダー明細テキストのパレット種別を取得
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->I_WA_9100  text
*      <--O_v_PALLET  text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_get_text_pallet  USING    i_wa_9100   TYPE ty_9100
                        CHANGING o_v_pallet TYPE any.
  DATA:
    l_v_name  TYPE thead-tdname,
    l_v_ebeln TYPE ebeln,
    l_v_ebelp TYPE ebelp,
    l_wa_line  TYPE tline,
    l_it_line  TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF tline.
  CLEAR o_v_pallet.

* 初期画面からの仮引当時
  CHECK i_wa_9100-hub IS INITIAL.

  PERFORM exit_alpha_input  USING i_wa_9100-vbeln
                         CHANGING l_v_ebeln.
  PERFORM exit_alpha_input  USING i_wa_9100-posnr
                         CHANGING l_v_ebelp.

  CONCATENATE l_v_ebeln l_v_ebelp INTO l_v_name.

  CALL FUNCTION 'READ_TEXT'
    EXPORTING
      client                  = sy-mandt
      id                      = c_id
      language                = sy-langu
      name                    = l_v_name
      object                  = c_objectr
    TABLES
      lines                   = l_it_line
    EXCEPTIONS
      id                      = 1
      language                = 2
      name                    = 3
      not_found               = 4
      object                  = 5
      reference_check         = 6
      wrong_access_to_archive = 7
      OTHERS                  = 8.
  IF sy-subrc = 0.
    READ TABLE l_it_line INTO l_wa_line INDEX 1.
    IF sy-subrc = 0 AND (
       l_wa_line-tdline = c_pl_cc OR
       l_wa_line-tdline = c_pl_t09 ).
      o_v_pallet = l_wa_line-tdline.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    " F_GET_TEXT_PALLET
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  EXIT_ALPHA_INPUT
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       内部形式変換
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->I_DATA
*      <--O_DATA
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM exit_alpha_input  USING i_data TYPE any
                    CHANGING o_data TYPE any.

  CALL FUNCTION 'CONVERSION_EXIT_ALPHA_INPUT'
    EXPORTING
      input  = i_data
    IMPORTING
      output = o_data.

ENDFORM.                    " EXIT_ALPHA_INPUT

FORM f_fill_item_data .
  DATA:l_wa_item        TYPE bapimepoitem,
        l_wa_itemx       TYPE bapimepoitemx,
        l_wa_schedule    TYPE bapimeposchedule,
        l_wa_schedulex   TYPE bapimeposchedulx,
        l_v_tracking_num TYPE trackn.

  CONSTANTS: l_c_sep    TYPE c VALUE ','.

  " BOC | MOD-016 | S180184 | Gap 3389 | Subcode changes | 29/07/2016

  DATA : l_wa_jscale TYPE ty_jscalecode,
         l_wa_extentionin TYPE bapiparex ,
         l_wa_bape_pox TYPE bapi_te_mepoitemx,
         l_wa_bape_po TYPE bapi_te_mepoitem.

  CONSTANTS : l_c_po TYPE te_struc VALUE 'BAPI_TE_MEPOITEM',
  l_c_pox TYPE te_struc VALUE 'BAPI_TE_MEPOITEMX'.
*  CLEAR i_extentionin."MOD-023

  " BOC | MOD-016 | S180184 | Gap 3389 | Subcode changes | 29/07/2016

**Item Number
  v_item = v_item + 00010.
  l_wa_item-po_item   = v_item.
  l_wa_itemx-po_item  = v_item.
  l_wa_itemx-po_itemx = abap_true.

**item number for schdule line item

*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_call_bapi .
  CLEAR:wa_return_po.

  CONSTANTS:l_c_s TYPE bapi_mtype VALUE 'S'.
**call the bapi to create Purchase Order
" Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_A Conversion **BEGIN OF CHANGE BY EJX9007359 03.06.2026  FOR ATC
  CALL FUNCTION 'BAPI_PO_CREATE1'"#EC CI_USAGE_OK[2438131]
" Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_A Conversion * *END OF CHANGE BY EJX9007359 03.06.2026 FOR ATC
    EXPORTING
      poheader         = wa_header
      poheaderx        = wa_headerx
    IMPORTING
      exppurchaseorder = v_po_num
    TABLES
      return           = i_return_po
      poitem           = i_item_data
      poitemx          = i_item_datax
      poschedule       = i_schedule
      poschedulex      = i_schedulex
                                                                                                                                                                                          " BOC | MOD-016 | S180184 | Gap 3389 | Subcode changes | 29/07/2016
      extensionin      = i_extentionin.
  " EOC | MOD-016 | S180184 | Gap 3389 | Subcode changes | 29/07/2016

  IF v_po_num IS NOT INITIAL .

    CALL FUNCTION 'BAPI_TRANSACTION_COMMIT'
      EXPORTING
        wait = abap_true.
    v_msg_new_po = text-018.
    REPLACE c_a1 WITH v_po_num INTO v_msg_new_po.
*    DELETE i_return_po WHERE  type <> l_c_s.
***write enddate and end time
*    v_edate = sy-datlo.
*    v_etime = sy-uzeit.
***numv=ber of PO created
*    v_records = v_records + 1.
***add the successful records to the log table
*    PERFORM f_get_poitem_number.

**refresh all the error tables.
    REFRESH : i_schedulex, i_schedule, i_item_datax, i_item_data,i_return_po[].
    CLEAR   : wa_headerx, wa_header.
*    wa_return_po-type = l_c_s.
*    wa_return_po-message = v_msg_new_po.
    wa_return_po-type = c_s.
    wa_return_po-id = c_msg_class.
    wa_return_po-number = c_003.
    wa_return_po-message_v1 = v_po_num .
    APPEND wa_return_po TO i_return_po.
    CLEAR wa_return_po.
  ENDIF.

  IF i_return_po[] IS NOT INITIAL.
    LOOP AT i_return_po INTO wa_errlog.
      APPEND wa_errlog TO i_errlog.
      CLEAR wa_errlog.
    ENDLOOP.
  ENDIF.

  CLEAR:i_return_po[].
ENDFORM.                    " F_CALL_BAPI

*&      Form  F_CREATE_DELIVERY
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_create_delivery .
* 初期化
  CLEAR v_delibery.
  REFRESH it_return.
  CLEAR:wa_return_po.
  REFRESH: it_created_items.

  LOOP AT it_items INTO wa_items.
    wa_items-ref_doc = v_po_num.
    MODIFY it_items FROM wa_items.
    CLEAR:wa_items.
  ENDLOOP.

*BOC MOD-011
  LOOP AT i_sled_dtl INTO wa_sled_dtl.
    wa_sled_dtl-vbeln = v_po_num.
    MODIFY i_sled_dtl FROM wa_sled_dtl.
    CLEAR:wa_sled_dtl.
  ENDLOOP.

  wa_indx-srtfd = v_po_num.
*EOC MOD-011

*Export BBE Date to update in LIPS table into Delivery BADI : LE_SHP_DELIVERY_PROC Implementaion Name : /CCEJ/OTC_UPD_BBE
  IF i_sled_dtl[] IS NOT INITIAL.
    DELETE FROM DATABASE indx(at) ID wa_indx-srtfd.
    EXPORT i_sled_dtl FROM i_sled_dtl TO DATABASE indx(at) ID wa_indx-srtfd.
  ENDIF.

* Generates Delivery for Stock Transport Order
" Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_A Conversion **BEGIN OF CHANGE BY EJX9007359 03.06.2026  FOR ATC
  CALL FUNCTION 'BAPI_OUTB_DELIVERY_CREATE_STO'"#EC CI_USAGE_OK[2438131]
" Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_A Conversion * *END OF CHANGE BY EJX9007359 03.06.2026 FOR ATC
    EXPORTING
      ship_point        = v_wanst         "出荷ポイント
    IMPORTING
      delivery          = v_delibery      "出荷伝票番号
    TABLES
      stock_trans_items = it_items         "出荷伝票明細
      extension_in      = it_extensionin   "EXTENSIONIN
      created_items     = it_created_items
      return            = it_return.        "リターンコード

  REFRESH:it_items[].
  IF v_delibery IS NOT INITIAL.
    CALL FUNCTION 'BAPI_TRANSACTION_COMMIT'
      EXPORTING
        wait = c_on.

    CLEAR v_msg_new_po.
    v_msg_new_po = text-019.
    REPLACE c_a1 WITH v_delibery INTO v_msg_new_po.
    CLEAR it_return[].
    wa_return_po-type = c_s.
    wa_return_po-id = c_msg_class.
    wa_return_po-number = c_004.
    wa_return_po-message_v1 = v_delibery .
    APPEND wa_return_po TO it_return.
    CLEAR wa_return_po.
  ELSE.
    flg_err = c_on.
  ENDIF.

  IF it_return[] IS NOT INITIAL.
    LOOP AT it_return INTO wa_errlog.
      APPEND wa_errlog TO i_errlog.
      CLEAR wa_errlog.
    ENDLOOP.
  ENDIF.
ENDFORM.                    " F_CREATE_DELIVERY

*&      Form  F_DISPLAY_ERRLOG
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*

*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_display_errlog .

  DATA: gr_table TYPE REF TO cl_salv_table.
  DATA: gr_funct TYPE REF TO cl_salv_functions.
  DATA: gr_columns TYPE REF TO cl_salv_columns_table.
  DATA: gr_column TYPE REF TO cl_salv_column_table.

  CONSTANTS:l_c_i TYPE bapi_mtype VALUE 'I',
            l_c_s TYPE bapi_mtype VALUE 'S',
            l_c_06 TYPE symsgid VALUE '06',
            l_c_22 TYPE symsgno VALUE '022',
            l_c_17 TYPE symsgno VALUE '017'.

*TYPE	1 Types	BAPI_MTYPE
*ID	1 Types	SYMSGID
*NUMBER	1 Types	SYMSGNO
*MESSAGE  1 Types BAPI_MSG

  DELETE i_errlog WHERE type = l_c_i AND id IS INITIAL.
  DELETE i_errlog WHERE type = l_c_i AND id = l_c_06 AND number <> l_c_17.
  DELETE i_errlog WHERE type = l_c_s AND id = l_c_06 AND number = l_c_22.
*  DELETE i_errlog WHERE type = l_c_i AND id = l_c_06.

  TRY.
      CALL METHOD cl_salv_table=>factory
        IMPORTING
          r_salv_table = gr_table
        CHANGING
          t_table      = i_errlog.
    CATCH cx_salv_msg .
  ENDTRY.

  gr_funct = gr_table->get_functions( ).
  gr_funct->set_all( abap_true ).

  gr_columns = gr_table->get_columns( ).
  gr_column ?= gr_columns->get_column( 'MESSAGE' ).
  gr_column->set_visible( abap_true ).

*  gr_table->display( ).
  DATA:l_wa_applog TYPE bal_s_log,
          l_wa_msg   TYPE bal_s_msg,
          l_v_handle TYPE balloghndl,
          l_v_handl  TYPE bal_t_logh,
          l_c_obj TYPE balobj_d VALUE '/CCEJ/OTC',
          l_c_atp(6) TYPE c VALUE 'ATP' ,
          l_c_moded TYPE balmode VALUE 'D',
          l_c_modeb TYPE balmode VALUE 'B',
          l_wa_return      TYPE bapireturn1,
          l_wa_display_profile TYPE bal_s_prof,
          l_c_msgid TYPE symsgid VALUE '/CCEJ/OTC_ATP'.
*-----Display Applciation Log

* Application Log Creation
*  l_wa_applog-object = l_c_obj.
*  l_wa_applog-subobject = l_c_atp.
  l_wa_applog-aldate = sy-datlo.
  l_wa_applog-altime = sy-uzeit.
  l_wa_applog-aluser = sy-uname.
  l_wa_applog-altcode = sy-tcode.
  l_wa_applog-alprog = sy-repid.

  IF sy-batch IS INITIAL.
    l_wa_applog-almode = l_c_moded.
  ELSE.
    l_wa_applog-almode = l_c_modeb.
  ENDIF.
*  l_wa_applog-aldate_del = sy-datlo + 10.

  CALL FUNCTION 'BAL_LOG_CREATE'
    EXPORTING
      i_s_log      = l_wa_applog
    IMPORTING
      e_log_handle = l_v_handle.
  IF sy-subrc <> 0.
* Implement suitable error handling here
  ENDIF.


  LOOP AT i_errlog INTO l_wa_return.

    l_wa_msg-msgty = l_wa_return-type.
    IF l_wa_return-id IS INITIAL.
      l_wa_msg-msgid = l_c_msgid.
    ELSE.
      l_wa_msg-msgid = l_wa_return-id.
    ENDIF.
    l_wa_msg-msgno = l_wa_return-number.
    l_wa_msg-msgv1 = l_wa_return-message_v1.
    l_wa_msg-msgv2 = l_wa_return-message_v2.
    l_wa_msg-msgv3 = l_wa_return-message_v3.
    l_wa_msg-msgv4 = l_wa_return-message_v4.

    CALL FUNCTION 'BAL_LOG_MSG_ADD'
      EXPORTING
        i_log_handle     = l_v_handle
        i_s_msg          = l_wa_msg
      EXCEPTIONS
        log_not_found    = 1
        msg_inconsistent = 2
        log_is_full      = 3
        OTHERS           = 4.
    IF sy-subrc <> 0.
* Implement suitable error handling here
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.


  APPEND l_v_handle TO l_v_handl.

* get a prepared profile
  CALL FUNCTION 'BAL_DSP_PROFILE_POPUP_GET'
    IMPORTING
      e_s_display_profile = l_wa_display_profile
    EXCEPTIONS
      OTHERS              = 1.
  IF sy-subrc <> 0.
    MESSAGE ID sy-msgid TYPE sy-msgty NUMBER sy-msgno
             WITH sy-msgv1 sy-msgv2 sy-msgv3 sy-msgv4.
  ENDIF.

  IF i_errlog[] IS NOT INITIAL.
    CALL FUNCTION 'BAL_DSP_LOG_DISPLAY'
      EXPORTING
        i_s_display_profile  = l_wa_display_profile
        i_t_log_handle       = l_v_handl
      EXCEPTIONS
        profile_inconsistent = 1
        internal_error       = 2
        no_data_available    = 3
        no_authority         = 4
        OTHERS               = 5.
    IF sy-subrc <> 0.
* Implement suitable error handling here
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.
ENDFORM.                    " F_DISPLAY_ERRLOG

      l_wa_9100-new_werks = wa_stk-werks.

      "BOC for MOD-029

        l_wa_9100-flash_kbn = c_1.

      ENDIF.

  ENDLOOP.

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  f_shchedule_allocation
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_shchedule_allocation USING fp_9100 TYPE ty_9100.

  FIELD-SYMBOLS :<l_fs_plan> TYPE ty_plan_stck.
  DATA:l_v_order_menge TYPE vbap-kwmeng,
       l_v_index TYPE sy-tabix,
       l_v_index2 TYPE sy-tabix.

  CLEAR :v_total,l_v_order_menge.
  l_v_order_menge = fp_9100-menge_cs.
  v_counter = v_counter + 1.

  LOOP AT i_plan_stck ASSIGNING <l_fs_plan>  WHERE check = c_on.
    wa_plan_stck = <l_fs_plan>.

    v_total = v_total + wa_plan_stck-planned_stock.

    IF wa_plan_stck-planned_stock GE l_v_order_menge.
      wa_plan_stck-planned_stock = wa_plan_stck-planned_stock - l_v_order_menge.
      wa_plan_stck-old_stock = wa_plan_stck-old_stock + l_v_order_menge.
      l_v_order_menge = 0.
    ELSE.
      l_v_order_menge = l_v_order_menge - wa_plan_stck-planned_stock.
      wa_plan_stck-old_stock = wa_plan_stck-old_stock + wa_plan_stck-planned_stock.
      wa_plan_stck-planned_stock = 0.
    ENDIF.
    wa_plan_stck-ordr_qty = fp_9100-menge_cs.
    wa_plan_stck-vbeln = fp_9100-vbeln.
    wa_plan_stck-posnr = fp_9100-posnr.
    CLEAR wa_plan_stck-check.
    wa_plan_stck-stck_ind = v_counter.
    <l_fs_plan> = wa_plan_stck.

    APPEND wa_plan_stck TO i_plan_stck_allocated.


    IF l_v_order_menge = 0.
      EXIT.
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.

  DELETE i_plan_stck_reserve  WHERE matnr = fp_9100-matnr  AND werks = fp_9100-werks AND lgort = fp_9100-lgort AND ( eindt LE fp_9100-wadat OR eindt IS INITIAL )."MOD-006

  LOOP AT i_plan_stck INTO wa_plan_stck WHERE check = c_on.
    l_v_index = sy-tabix.
    CLEAR wa_plan_stck-check.
    MODIFY i_plan_stck FROM wa_plan_stck INDEX l_v_index TRANSPORTING check.
  ENDLOOP.

*  DELETE i_plan_stck WHERE planned_stock = 0."MOD-001

  LOOP AT i_plan_stck INTO wa_plan_stck.
    APPEND wa_plan_stck TO i_plan_stck_reserve.
  ENDLOOP.


ENDFORM.                    " F_REPLACE_MATERIAL

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  f_shchedule_allocation
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_shchedule_allocation1 USING fp_9100 TYPE ty_9100.

  FIELD-SYMBOLS :<l_fs_plan> TYPE ty_plan_stck.
  DATA:l_v_order_menge TYPE vbap-kwmeng,
       l_v_index TYPE sy-tabix,
       l_v_index2 TYPE sy-tabix.

  CLEAR :l_v_order_menge.
  l_v_order_menge = fp_9100-menge_cs.

  LOOP AT i_plan_stck ASSIGNING <l_fs_plan>  WHERE check = c_on.
    wa_plan_stck = <l_fs_plan>.

    wa_plan_stck-ordr_qty = fp_9100-menge_cs.
    wa_plan_stck-vbeln = fp_9100-vbeln.
    wa_plan_stck-posnr = fp_9100-posnr.
    CLEAR wa_plan_stck-check.

    LOOP AT i_schedule_stock INTO wa_schedule_stock WHERE matnr = fp_9100-matnr
                                                      AND werks = fp_9100-werks
                                                      AND lgort = fp_9100-lgort
                                                      AND sub_code = wa_plan_stck-eda " MOD-018
                                                      AND bbe = wa_plan_stck-kiboshom " MOD-014
                                                      AND eindt = wa_plan_stck-eindt
                                                      AND current_qty = wa_plan_stck-planned_stock.

      l_v_index = sy-tabix.
      wa_schedule_stock-vbeln = fp_9100-vbeln.
      wa_schedule_stock-posnr = fp_9100-posnr.
      wa_schedule_stock-wadat = fp_9100-wadat.
      wa_schedule_stock-old_qty = wa_schedule_stock-current_qty.


      IF wa_schedule_stock-current_qty GE l_v_order_menge .
        wa_schedule_stock-current_qty = wa_schedule_stock-current_qty - l_v_order_menge.
        CLEAR l_v_order_menge.

      ELSEIF wa_schedule_stock-current_qty LT l_v_order_menge .
        l_v_order_menge = l_v_order_menge - wa_schedule_stock-current_qty.
        CLEAR wa_schedule_stock-current_qty.
      ENDIF.

      MODIFY i_schedule_stock FROM wa_schedule_stock INDEX l_v_index.
      CLEAR l_v_index.

      READ TABLE i_schedule_stock1 INTO wa_schedule_stock1 WITH KEY vbeln = fp_9100-vbeln posnr = fp_9100-posnr eindt = wa_schedule_stock-eindt.
      IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
        wa_schedule_stock1-current_qty = wa_schedule_stock1-current_qty + fp_9100-menge_cs.
      ELSE.
        wa_schedule_stock1 = wa_schedule_stock.
        wa_schedule_stock1-current_qty = fp_9100-menge_cs.
        APPEND wa_schedule_stock1 TO i_schedule_stock1.
        CLEAR:wa_schedule_stock1,wa_schedule_stock.
      ENDIF.

      IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
        MODIFY i_schedule_stock1 FROM wa_schedule_stock1 INDEX sy-tabix.
      ENDIF.

      CLEAR:wa_schedule_stock.

    ENDLOOP.

    IF l_v_order_menge = 0.
      EXIT.
    ENDIF.

  ENDLOOP.



ENDFORM.                    " F_REPLACE_MATERIAL

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  F_GET_PLANT
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_get_plant .
  DATA:l_v_bukrs TYPE bukrs.

  CLEAR:l_v_bukrs.
*Find Company Code based on Plant Entered
*  SELECT SINGLE bukrs FROM
*    t001k INTO l_v_bukrs
*    WHERE bwkey = p_werks.

*Find All Plants based on company code
  CLEAR:i_werks[].
*  IF l_v_bukrs IS NOT INITIAL.
*    SELECT bwkey bukrs FROM
*      t001k INTO TABLE i_werks
*      WHERE bukrs = l_v_bukrs.
*  ENDIF.

  IF p_werks IS NOT INITIAL.
    SELECT a_werks FROM
        /ccej/t_atp_plnt INTO TABLE i_werks
        WHERE ktopl = v_ktopl AND werks = p_werks.
  ENDIF.


  REFRESH s_werks.

  s_werks-sign   = c_sign_i.
  s_werks-option = c_opt_eq.
  s_werks-low    = p_werks.
  APPEND s_werks TO s_werks.

  LOOP AT i_werks INTO wa_werks.
    s_werks-sign   = c_sign_i.
    s_werks-option = c_opt_eq.
    s_werks-low    = wa_werks-werks.
    APPEND s_werks TO s_werks.
  ENDLOOP.



ENDFORM.                    " F_GET_PLANT

FORM f_search_planned_stock1 USING fp_9100 TYPE ty_9100.

  DATA:l_v_flag TYPE char1.
  DATA: l_i_fieldcat  TYPE slis_t_fieldcat_alv,
        l_wa_fiedcat TYPE slis_fieldcat_alv,
        l_v_layout    TYPE slis_layout_alv,
        l_v_title TYPE string ,
        l_cnt_pos TYPE int2 VALUE 1,
        l_v_stock TYPE kwmeng,
        l_wa_syomi LIKE LINE OF rng_syomi,
        l_i_temp_zaiko TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_zaiko,
        l_v_index TYPE sy-tabix.


  SORT i_schedule_stock BY matnr werks lgort stck_typ.
  CLEAR:i_plan_stck[],v_total,v_plan_pd,
        wa_plan_stck."MOD-043
  " BOC | MOD-016 | S180184 | Gap 3389 | Subcode changes | 29/07/2016
*  LOOP AT i_schedule_stock INTO wa_schedule_stock WHERE matnr = fp_9100-matnr
  LOOP AT i_schedule_stock INTO wa_schedule_stock WHERE matnr+0(14) = fp_9100-matnr+0(14)
      " EOC | MOD-016 | S180184 | Gap 3389 | Subcode changes | 29/07/2016
                                       AND werks = fp_9100-werks
                                       AND lgort = fp_9100-lgort
" Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_APS Conversion **BEGIN OF CHANGE BY EJX9007359 11.06.2026  for ATC
*                                        AND sub_code = fp_9100-edacode "MOD-018
AND sub_code = fp_9100-edacode "#EC CI_FLDEXT_OK[0002250183]
" Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_APS Conversion * *END OF CHANGE BY EJX9007359 11.06.2026 for ATC
                                       AND ( eindt LE fp_9100-wadat OR stck_typ = c_qsr ).

    wa_plan_stck-matnr = wa_schedule_stock-matnr.
    wa_plan_stck-werks = wa_schedule_stock-werks.
    wa_plan_stck-lgort  = wa_schedule_stock-lgort.
    wa_plan_stck-kiboshom = wa_schedule_stock-bbe.
    wa_plan_stck-eda = wa_schedule_stock-sub_code."MOD-018
    wa_plan_stck-eindt = wa_schedule_stock-eindt.

    IF wa_schedule_stock-stck_typ = c_qsr.
      CLEAR wa_plan_stck-eindt1.
    ELSE.
      wa_plan_stck-eindt1 = wa_schedule_stock-eindt.
    ENDIF.

    wa_plan_stck-wadat = fp_9100-wadat.

    WRITE wa_schedule_stock-bbe TO wa_plan_stck-bbe.
    CLEAR wa_plan_stck-check.
    wa_plan_stck-ordr_qty = fp_9100-menge_cs.
    wa_plan_stck-planned_stock = wa_schedule_stock-current_qty.

    IF  wa_schedule_stock-stck_typ = c_qsr.
      wa_plan_stck-stck_typ = text-005.
      APPEND wa_plan_stck TO i_plan_stck.
    ENDIF.

    IF  wa_schedule_stock-stck_typ = c_sto.
      wa_plan_stck-stck_typ = text-022.
      APPEND wa_plan_stck TO i_plan_stck.
    ENDIF.

    IF  wa_schedule_stock-stck_typ = c_pro.
*     BOC for MOD-043
      wa_plan_stck-plan_pd = wa_plan_stck-eindt.
*     EOC for MOD-043
      wa_plan_stck-stck_typ = text-023.
      APPEND wa_plan_stck TO i_plan_stck.
    ENDIF.

*BOC MOD-014
    IF  wa_schedule_stock-stck_typ = c_sumstopro.
      CONCATENATE text-022 c_slash text-023 INTO wa_plan_stck-stck_typ.
      APPEND wa_plan_stck TO i_plan_stck.
    ENDIF.
*EOC MOD-014

    CLEAR:wa_plan_stck,wa_zaiko.

  ENDLOOP.



  IF i_plan_stck[] IS NOT INITIAL.

*    l_wa_fiedcat-tabname = c_tabname.
*    l_wa_fiedcat-fieldname = c_check.
*    l_wa_fiedcat-seltext_m = text-h12.
*    l_wa_fiedcat-col_pos = l_cnt_pos.
*    APPEND l_wa_fiedcat TO l_i_fieldcat .
*    CLEAR: l_wa_fiedcat.
*    l_cnt_pos = l_cnt_pos + 1.

    l_wa_fiedcat-tabname = c_tabname.
    l_wa_fiedcat-fieldname = c_material.
    l_wa_fiedcat-seltext_m = text-h01.
    l_wa_fiedcat-col_pos = l_cnt_pos.
    APPEND l_wa_fiedcat TO l_i_fieldcat .
    CLEAR: l_wa_fiedcat.
    l_cnt_pos = l_cnt_pos + 1.

    l_wa_fiedcat-tabname = c_tabname.
    l_wa_fiedcat-fieldname = c_werks.
    l_wa_fiedcat-seltext_m = text-h07.
    l_wa_fiedcat-col_pos = l_cnt_pos.
    APPEND l_wa_fiedcat TO l_i_fieldcat .
    CLEAR: l_wa_fiedcat.
    l_cnt_pos = l_cnt_pos + 1.

    l_wa_fiedcat-tabname = c_tabname.
    l_wa_fiedcat-fieldname = c_lgort.
    l_wa_fiedcat-seltext_m = text-h08.
    l_wa_fiedcat-col_pos = l_cnt_pos.
    APPEND l_wa_fiedcat TO l_i_fieldcat .
    CLEAR: l_wa_fiedcat.
    l_cnt_pos = l_cnt_pos + 1.

    l_wa_fiedcat-tabname = c_tabname.
    l_wa_fiedcat-fieldname = c_eda.
    l_wa_fiedcat-seltext_m = text-h02.
    l_wa_fiedcat-col_pos = l_cnt_pos.
    APPEND l_wa_fiedcat TO l_i_fieldcat .
    CLEAR: l_wa_fiedcat.
    l_cnt_pos = l_cnt_pos + 1.

*    BOC for MOD-046
    "Planned Production Date
    l_wa_fiedcat-tabname = c_tabname.
    l_wa_fiedcat-fieldname = c_plan_pd.
    l_wa_fiedcat-seltext_m = text-s24.
    l_wa_fiedcat-col_pos = l_cnt_pos.
    APPEND l_wa_fiedcat TO l_i_fieldcat .
    CLEAR: l_wa_fiedcat.
    l_cnt_pos = l_cnt_pos + 1.

*    EOC for MOD-046

    l_wa_fiedcat-tabname = c_tabname.
    l_wa_fiedcat-fieldname = c_bbe.
    l_wa_fiedcat-seltext_m = text-s25."text-s20.MOD-043
    l_wa_fiedcat-col_pos = l_cnt_pos.
    APPEND l_wa_fiedcat TO l_i_fieldcat .
    CLEAR: l_wa_fiedcat.
    l_cnt_pos = l_cnt_pos + 1.

    l_wa_fiedcat-tabname = c_tabname.
    l_wa_fiedcat-fieldname = c_deldt.
    l_wa_fiedcat-seltext_m = text-s23.
    l_wa_fiedcat-col_pos = l_cnt_pos.
    APPEND l_wa_fiedcat TO l_i_fieldcat .
    CLEAR: l_wa_fiedcat.
    l_cnt_pos = l_cnt_pos + 1.

    l_wa_fiedcat-tabname = c_tabname.
    l_wa_fiedcat-fieldname = c_qty.
*    l_wa_fiedcat-qfieldname = c_qty.
    l_wa_fiedcat-seltext_m = text-h10.
    l_wa_fiedcat-col_pos = l_cnt_pos.
    l_wa_fiedcat-outputlen = 20.
    APPEND l_wa_fiedcat TO l_i_fieldcat .

    CLEAR: l_wa_fiedcat.
    l_cnt_pos = l_cnt_pos + 1.

    l_wa_fiedcat-tabname = c_tabname.
    l_wa_fiedcat-fieldname = c_type.
    l_wa_fiedcat-seltext_m = text-h11.
    l_wa_fiedcat-seltext_l = text-h11.
    l_wa_fiedcat-seltext_s = text-h11.
    l_wa_fiedcat-col_pos = l_cnt_pos.
    l_wa_fiedcat-outputlen = 60."MOD-014
    APPEND l_wa_fiedcat TO l_i_fieldcat .
    CLEAR: l_wa_fiedcat.
    l_cnt_pos = l_cnt_pos + 1.


    l_v_title = text-017.
*---Beging of MOD-001
    CLEAR:v_total.
    LOOP AT i_plan_stck INTO wa_plan_stck.
      v_total = v_total + wa_plan_stck-planned_stock.
    ENDLOOP.

    DELETE i_plan_stck WHERE planned_stock = 0.

    IF v_total LT fp_9100-menge_cs.

      v_msg = text-024.
      MESSAGE v_msg TYPE c_s.
    ELSE.
*---End of MOD-001
      CALL FUNCTION 'REUSE_ALV_POPUP_TO_SELECT'
        EXPORTING
          i_title               = l_v_title
*         i_selection           = ' '
          i_tabname             = 'I_PLAN_STCK'
          i_screen_start_column = 2
          i_screen_start_line   = 2
          i_screen_end_column   = 160"120"MOD-014
          i_screen_end_line     = 10
          i_checkbox_fieldname  = 'CHECK'
*         i_structure_name      = '/CCEJ/SLSS_STOCK'
          it_fieldcat           = l_i_fieldcat
        TABLES
          t_outtab              = i_plan_stck
        EXCEPTIONS
          program_error         = 1
          OTHERS                = 2.

    ENDIF.

    CLEAR :v_total,v_bbe,"MOD-014
           v_plan_pd.
    LOOP AT i_plan_stck INTO wa_plan_stck  WHERE check = c_on.
      v_total = v_total + wa_plan_stck-planned_stock.
      v_bbe = wa_plan_stck-kiboshom."MOD-014
      v_plan_pd = wa_plan_stck-plan_pd."MOD-046
    ENDLOOP.


    IF  v_total LT fp_9100-menge_cs AND v_total <> 0.
      v_msg = text-024.
      MESSAGE v_msg TYPE c_s.
    ELSEIF v_total GE fp_9100-menge_cs.
*      PERFORM f_shchedule_allocation USING fp_9100 ."MOD-006
      PERFORM f_shchedule_allocation1 USING fp_9100 .
    ENDIF.

  ELSE.
    v_msg = text-e10.
    MESSAGE v_msg TYPE c_s.
  ENDIF.



ENDFORM.                    " F_SEARCH_PLANNED_STOCK

  ENDIF.

  SELECT SINGLE werks

  IF it_lips[] IS NOT INITIAL.

        ENDIF.

                                                                    material = wa_jscale-matnr BINARY SEARCH.

            ENDLOOP.

          l_wa_lips-last_bbe = l_wa_shdate-last_sled.

        ELSE.

          l_wa_lips-last_bbe = wa_9100-last_bbe.

        ENDIF.

    " BOC | s180184| MOD-023 | Gap 4276 | 21/07/2017
  ELSE.

      l_v_index1 = sy-tabix.

        " BOC | MOD - 037 | S180184 | 12/10/2017 | defect 4069
        IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
          " EOC | MOD - 037 | S180184 | 12/10/2017 | defect 4069
          l_wa_lips-last_bbe = l_wa_shdate-last_sled.
          " BOC | MOD - 037 | S180184 | 12/10/2017 | defect 4069

      ELSE.

      ENDIF.

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  sort_table
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->I_FLG_KOSHA  text
*      -->I_V_ZZSEQNO  text
*      <--O_TC_9100    text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_sort_details
  USING     fp_ucom TYPE sy-ucomm
  CHANGING  fp_tc_9100 TYPE cxtab_control.

  DATA :
    l_v_group1         TYPE screen-group1.
  FIELD-SYMBOLS :
    <l_fs_cols>         TYPE LINE OF cxtab_control-cols.

  DATA: l_i_otab TYPE abap_sortorder_tab,
        l_wa_oline TYPE abap_sortorder.

  LOOP AT fp_tc_9100-cols ASSIGNING <l_fs_cols> WHERE selected = c_on.

    <l_fs_cols>-selected = ' '.

    IF fp_ucom = c_asc.
      l_wa_oline-name = <l_fs_cols>-screen-name+8. " Add column name
      APPEND l_wa_oline TO l_i_otab.
    ENDIF.

    IF fp_ucom = c_dsc.
      l_wa_oline-descending = c_on.
      l_wa_oline-name = <l_fs_cols>-screen-name+8. " Add column name
      APPEND l_wa_oline TO l_i_otab.
    ENDIF.

    IF fp_ucom = c_hid.
      <l_fs_cols>-invisible = c_on.
    ENDIF.

  ENDLOOP.

  IF l_i_otab[] IS NOT INITIAL.
    SORT it_9100 BY (l_i_otab) .
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    " SET_ATTR_SCREEN_9100

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  F_BATCH_CHECK
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       Batch Check
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_batch_check .

  DATA : l_v_valid TYPE flag,
         l_v_state TYPE flag.

  IF p_werks IS NOT INITIAL.

    CLEAR : v_valid.
    CALL FUNCTION '/CCBJI/FTD_BATCH_CHECK'
      EXPORTING
        im_werks       = p_werks
        im_hsdat       = sy-datlo
      IMPORTING
        ex_valid       = l_v_valid
        ex_state       = l_v_state
      EXCEPTIONS
        import_missing = 1
        OTHERS         = 2.
    IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
      IF l_v_valid IS NOT INITIAL AND l_v_state IS NOT INITIAL.
        v_valid = abap_true.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    " F_BATCH_CHECK
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  F_CHECK_SAME_ITEM
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_check_same_item .

  TYPES:BEGIN OF l_ty_matnr,
          matnr TYPE matnr,
        END OF l_ty_matnr.
  DATA:l_i_matnr TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF l_ty_matnr,
       l_wa_matnr TYPE l_ty_matnr,
       l_wa_9100_tmp TYPE ty_9100,
       l_v_items TYPE sy-tabix.

  CLEAR:l_i_matnr[],l_wa_matnr,l_v_items.

  IF it_9100[] IS NOT INITIAL.

    "Select on items selected
    LOOP AT it_9100 INTO l_wa_9100_tmp WHERE check = c_on.
      l_wa_matnr-matnr = l_wa_9100_tmp-matnr.
      APPEND l_wa_matnr TO l_i_matnr.
      CLEAR:l_wa_matnr,l_wa_9100_tmp.
    ENDLOOP.

    "Check if different materials are selected
    SORT l_i_matnr BY matnr.
    DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM l_i_matnr COMPARING matnr.
    DESCRIBE TABLE l_i_matnr LINES l_v_items.
    IF l_v_items GT 1.
      v_err_same_matnr = c_on.
    ENDIF.

  ENDIF.


ENDFORM.                    " F_CHECK_SAME_ITEM
